param(
    [string]$ProjectName = 'ActiveDirectoryExplorer',
    [ValidateSet('Auto','Console','Avalonia')][string]$ProjectType = 'Avalonia',
    [string]$BaseDir = '',
    [switch]$NoLaunch,
    [int]$MaxRetries = 3
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$ProgressPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
$Script:StageName = 'Initialization'
$Script:InstallerPath = ''
$Script:DotnetDir = Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'Microsoft\dotnet'
function Write-Info([string]$m)  { Write-Host $m -ForegroundColor Cyan }
function Write-Ok([string]$m)    { Write-Host $m -ForegroundColor Green }
function Write-Warn2([string]$m) { Write-Host $m -ForegroundColor Yellow }
function Write-Err2([string]$m)  { Write-Host $m -ForegroundColor Red }
function Throw-Code([int]$c, [string]$m) { throw "[$c] $m" }
function Write-Stage([string]$n, [string]$m) { Write-Host ''; Write-Host "[$n] $m" -ForegroundColor Cyan }
function Initialize-Tls {
    try { [Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol = [Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol -bor [Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls12 } catch {}
    try { [Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol = [Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol -bor [Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls13 } catch {}
}
function Get-SafeName([string]$n) {
    $n = [regex]::Replace($n, '[^A-Za-z0-9_]', '')
    if ($n.Length -eq 0) { return 'GeneratedApp' }
    if ($n -match '^\d') { $n = 'App' + $n }
    $n = $n.Substring(0,1).ToUpperInvariant() + $n.Substring(1)
    $kw = @('abstract','as','async','await','base','bool','break','byte','case','catch','char','checked','class','const','continue','decimal','default','delegate','do','double','else','enum','event','explicit','extern','false','finally','fixed','float','for','foreach','goto','if','implicit','in','init','int','interface','internal','is','lock','long','namespace','new','null','object','operator','out','override','params','private','protected','public','readonly','record','ref','return','sbyte','sealed','short','sizeof','stackalloc','static','string','struct','switch','this','throw','true','try','typeof','uint','ulong','unchecked','unsafe','ushort','using','var','virtual','void','volatile','while')
    if ($kw -contains $n.ToLowerInvariant()) { $n = $n + 'App' }
    return $n
}
function Remove-Folder([string]$Path) {
    $p = $Path.TrimEnd('\')
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $p)) { return $true }
    for ($i = 1; $i -le 3; $i++) {
        try {
            Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $p -Force -Recurse -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object {
                try { $_.Attributes = [IO.FileAttributes]::Normal } catch {}
            }
            Remove-Item -LiteralPath $p -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction Stop
        } catch {
            Start-Sleep -Seconds ([math]::Min(30, 5 * $i))
        }
        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $p)) { return $true }
    }
    try { & cmd.exe /c rd /s /q "$p" | Out-Null } catch {}
    $gone = -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $p)
    if (-not $gone) { Write-Warn2 "cmd rd /s /q exited with code $LASTEXITCODE but '$p' still exists." }
    return $gone
}
function Find-DotNetSdk {
    $cand = @()
    $local = Join-Path $Script:DotnetDir 'dotnet.exe'
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $local) { $cand += $local }
    $resolved = Get-Command dotnet.exe -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($resolved -and ($cand -notcontains $resolved.Source)) { $cand += $resolved.Source }
    foreach ($d in $cand) {
        $prev = $ErrorActionPreference
        $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
        try {
            $null = (& $d --version 2>$null)
            if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) {
                $sdks = @(& $d --list-sdks 2>$null)
                if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0 -and (@($sdks | Where-Object { $_ -match '^8\.' }).Count -gt 0)) {
                    return $d
                }
            }
        } catch {} finally { $ErrorActionPreference = $prev }
    }
    return $null
}
function Save-FileWithRetry([string]$Url, [string]$Dest) {
    for ($i = 1; $i -le $MaxRetries; $i++) {
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Dest) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $Dest -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
        try {
            if ($env:HTTPS_PROXY) {
                Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $Url -OutFile $Dest -UseBasicParsing -TimeoutSec 120 -Proxy $env:HTTPS_PROXY -ErrorAction Stop
            } else {
                Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $Url -OutFile $Dest -UseBasicParsing -TimeoutSec 120 -ErrorAction Stop
            }
            if ((Test-Path -LiteralPath $Dest) -and ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $Dest).Length -gt 1000)) {
                $head = ((Get-Content -LiteralPath $Dest -TotalCount 5 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) -join ' ')
                if ($head -notmatch '(?i)<\s*html|<!doctype') { return $true }
            }
        } catch {}
        Write-Warn2 "Download attempt $i failed for: $Url"
        if ($i -lt $MaxRetries) {
            Write-Warn2 "Retrying in $([math]::Min(30, 5 * $i)) seconds..."
            Start-Sleep -Seconds ([math]::Min(30, 5 * $i))
        }
    }
    return $false
}
function Install-DotNetSdk {
    $Script:InstallerPath = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'dotnet-install.ps1'
    $urls = @(
        'https://dot.net/v1/dotnet-install.ps1',
        'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dotnet/install-scripts/main/src/dotnet-install.ps1'
    )
    $got = $false
    foreach ($u in $urls) {
        if (Save-FileWithRetry -Url $u -Dest $Script:InstallerPath) { $got = $true; break }
    }
    if (-not $got) {
        Throw-Code 2 "Could not download dotnet-install.ps1 from any known mirror after repeated retries."
    }
    try { Unblock-File -LiteralPath $Script:InstallerPath -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue } catch {}
    Write-Info "Installing the .NET 8 SDK user-local (no elevation) under: $($Script:DotnetDir)"
    & powershell.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File $Script:InstallerPath -Channel 8.0 -Architecture x64 -InstallDir $Script:DotnetDir
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        Throw-Code 3 "dotnet-install.ps1 exited with code $LASTEXITCODE."
    }
}
function Set-DotNetEnv([string]$Dir) {
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Dir)) {
        Throw-Code 3 "Expected dotnet directory '$Dir' does not exist after installation."
    }
    $env:DOTNET_ROOT = $Dir
    $env:DOTNET_MULTILEVEL_LOOKUP = '0'
    $env:DOTNET_NOLOGO = '1'
    $env:DOTNET_SKIP_FIRST_TIME_EXPERIENCE = '1'
    $env:DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT = '1'
    $env:MSBUILDDISABLENODEREUSE = '1'
    $env:NUGET_INTERACTIVE = 'false'
    if (@($env:Path -split ';') -notcontains $Dir) {
        $env:Path = "$Dir;$env:Path"
    }
}
function Invoke-DotNet {
    param(
        [string]$ExePath,
        [int]$FailCode = 5,
        [string[]]$CliArgs = @()
    )
    & $ExePath @CliArgs
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        Throw-Code $FailCode "dotnet $($CliArgs -join ' ') failed with exit code $LASTEXITCODE. Review the output above for the exact cause."
    }
}
function Test-DiskSpace([string]$Path) {
    $gb = $null
    try {
        $di = New-Object IO.DriveInfo($Path.Substring(0,1))
        if ($di.IsReady) { $gb = [math]::Round($di.AvailableFreeSpace / 1GB, 2) }
    } catch {}
    if ($null -eq $gb) {
        Write-Warn2 'Could not determine free disk space; continuing.'
        return
    }
    $L = $Path.Substring(0,1)
    if ($gb -lt 0.5) {
        Throw-Code 1 "Only $gb GB free on drive ${L}: - at least 0.5 GB is required."
    } elseif ($gb -lt 2) {
        Write-Warn2 "Low disk space: $gb GB free on drive ${L}:"
    } else {
        Write-Ok "Disk space OK: $gb GB free on drive ${L}:"
    }
}

        /// <summary>
        /// Themed chrome for popup dialogs only (not the main window).
        /// Win32 ignores Window.BorderBrush with system decorations, so we wrap the
        /// dialog content in a visible Avalonia Border for light/dark contrast.
        /// </summary>
        private void StyleDialogWindow(Window dlg)
        {
            if (dlg == null) return;
            try
            {
                IBrush? TryBrush(string key)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var app = Application.Current;
                        if (app == null) return null;
                        if (app.TryGetResource(key, app.ActualThemeVariant, out var obj) && obj is IBrush b)
                            return b;
                    }
                    catch { }
                    return null;
                }

                var borderBrush = TryBrush("AdeDialogBorderBrush")
                    ?? TryBrush("AdeAccentBrush")
                    ?? TryBrush("AdeBorderBrush");
                var bg = TryBrush("AdeCardBrush");
                var fg = TryBrush("AdePrimaryTextBrush");

                if (bg != null) dlg.Background = bg;
                if (fg != null) dlg.Foreground = fg;
                // Still set window border for platforms that honor it
                dlg.BorderThickness = new Thickness(2);
                if (borderBrush != null) dlg.BorderBrush = borderBrush;

                void WrapContent()
                {
                    try
                    {
                        if (dlg.Content is Border existing
                            && existing.Tag as string == "AdeDialogChrome")
                            return;
                        var content = dlg.Content as Control;
                        if (content == null) return;
                        dlg.Content = null;
                        var frame = new Border
                        {
                            Tag = "AdeDialogChrome",
                            BorderThickness = new Thickness(3),
                            BorderBrush = borderBrush,
                            Background = bg,
                            Child = content,
                            Margin = new Thickness(0)
                        };
                        dlg.Content = frame;
                        if (fg != null)
                            content.SetValue(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, fg);
                    }
                    catch { }
                }

                // Content is often assigned after StyleDialogWindow
                dlg.Opened += (_, _) => WrapContent();
                dlg.PropertyChanged += (_, e) =>
                {
                    try
                    {
                        if (e.Property == ContentControl.ContentProperty
                            && dlg.Content is not Border { Tag: "AdeDialogChrome" })
                            WrapContent();
                    }
                    catch { }
                };
                if (dlg.Content != null)
                    WrapContent();
            }
            catch { }
        }

        private async Task<string?> PromptTextAsync(string title, string message, string defaultValue, bool password, string? example, bool compactPin = false, bool widePath = false)
        {
            string? result = null;
            bool confirmed = false;
            // compact PIN ~400; normal ~500; long UNC paths ~760
            double w = compactPin ? 400 : (widePath ? 760 : 500);
            double minW = compactPin ? 340 : (widePath ? 640 : 420);
            var dlg = new Window
            {
                Title = title,
                Width = w,
                MinWidth = minW,
                SizeToContent = SizeToContent.Height,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                CanResize = widePath
            };
            StyleDialogWindow(dlg);
            var root = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(20), Spacing = 10 };
            root.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = title, FontSize = 18, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold });
            root.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = message, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap, Opacity = 0.9 });
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(example))
            {
                root.Children.Add(Tb("Example: " + example, size: 12, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas")));
            }
            var box = new TextBox
            {
                Text = defaultValue ?? "",
                MinHeight = 36,
                FontSize = 14,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            if (password) box.PasswordChar = '\u2022';
            root.Children.Add(box);
            var btns = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 10, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right };
            var cancel = new Button { Content = "Cancel", MinWidth = 100, Classes = { "action" } };
            var ok = new Button { Content = "Continue", MinWidth = 100, Classes = { "action" } };
            void Accept() { result = box.Text ?? ""; confirmed = true; dlg.Close(); }
            void Reject() { confirmed = false; dlg.Close(); }
            cancel.Click += (_, _) => Reject();
            ok.Click += (_, _) => Accept();
            // Enter = Continue, Escape = Cancel
            box.KeyDown += (_, e) =>
            {
                if (e.Key == Key.Enter) { Accept(); e.Handled = true; }
                else if (e.Key == Key.Escape) { Reject(); e.Handled = true; }
            };
            dlg.KeyDown += (_, e) =>
            {
                if (e.Key == Key.Enter) { Accept(); e.Handled = true; }
                else if (e.Key == Key.Escape) { Reject(); e.Handled = true; }
            };
            btns.Children.Add(cancel);
            btns.Children.Add(ok);
            root.Children.Add(btns);
            dlg.Content = root;
            dlg.Opened += (_, _) =>
            {
                box.Focus();
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(box.Text))
                    box.CaretIndex = box.Text.Length;
            };
            try { await dlg.ShowDialog(this); } catch { }
            return confirmed ? result : null;
        }

        private static List<string> ParseBrokerList(string raw)
        {
            var list = new List<string>();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw)) return list;
            foreach (string part in raw.Split(new[] { '\r', '\n', ',', ';' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries))
            {
                string h = part.Trim();
                if (h.Length == 0) continue;
                // strip protocol if pasted
                if (h.StartsWith("https://", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) h = h.Substring(8);
                if (h.StartsWith("http://", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) h = h.Substring(7);
                h = h.TrimEnd('/');
                if (h.Length > 0 && !list.Contains(h, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    list.Add(h);
            }
            return list;
        }

        public MainWindow()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            WireChromeIcons();
            WindowState = WindowState.Maximized;
            _isInitialized = true;
            LoadAppSettings();
            LoadThemePreference(); // prefer local theme file over defaults
            ApplyPreferredTheme();
            // No directory calls in the constructor: the old IsDomainAdminAccount
            // probe ran a synchronous RootDSE bind + subtree searches on the UI
            // thread here, and its result was never even used (Title is fixed).
            // On a machine with no reachable domain, DC discovery can block for
            // minutes, which froze the app before the window could open
            // (user report 2026-10-27). The method is removed entirely.
            Title = "Active Directory Explorer";
            UpdateThemeButtonIcon();
            this.KeyDown += OnWindowKeyDown;
            this.Closed += (_, _) =>
            {
                _toastTimer?.Stop();
                _loadCts?.Cancel();
                CleanupLaunchedRdpProcesses();
            };
            // Vault unlock / first-run wizard must run after the window is shown
            bool startupRan = false;
            this.Opened += async (_, _) =>
            {
                if (startupRan) return;
                startupRan = true;
                UpdateVaultBadge();
                await EnsureVaultReadyAsync();
                UpdateVaultBadge();
                ApplyPreferredTheme();
                UpdateThemeButtonIcon();
                // Domain tree + dashboard often takes several seconds  -  show progress
                BeginProgressiveLoad(showProgress: true);
            };
        }


        // Track mstsc processes we started so we can close them with the app



        private void InitializeComponent() => AvaloniaXamlLoader.Load(this);

        /// <summary>
        /// Normalize Horizon user display: user@domain.mil or domain.mil\user -> DOMAIN\user or short sam.
        /// Avoids noisy reversed UPN forms like internal.oln-afmc.af.mil\user.
        /// </summary>
        private static string FormatHorizonUserName(string? raw)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw)) return "";
            string s = raw.Trim();
            // UPN: user@domain
            int at = s.IndexOf('@');
            if (at > 0)
            {
                string user = s.Substring(0, at);
                string domain = s.Substring(at + 1);
                string netbios = domain.Contains('.') ? domain.Split('.')[0].ToUpperInvariant() : domain.ToUpperInvariant();
                return netbios + "\\" + user;
            }
            // DOMAIN\user or fqdn\user
            int slash = s.IndexOf('\\');
            if (slash > 0)
            {
                string left = s.Substring(0, slash);
                string user = s.Substring(slash + 1);
                // If left looks like a DNS name (has dots), use first label as netbios
                if (left.Contains('.'))
                    left = left.Split('.')[0];
                return left.ToUpperInvariant() + "\\" + user;
            }
            return s;
        }

        // Profile Fix (CIFS profile share) - defaults match Profile Reset Tool

        // Cached OU list for Move-to-OU search (name + DN)
        private List<(string Name, string Dn)>? _ouCache;
        private DateTime _ouCacheLoadedUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
        private static readonly TimeSpan OuCacheTtl = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(30);
        private readonly object _ouCacheLock = new();

        private List<(string Name, string Dn)> EnsureOuCache()
        {
            lock (_ouCacheLock)
            {
                if (_ouCache != null && (DateTime.UtcNow - _ouCacheLoadedUtc) < OuCacheTtl)
                    return _ouCache;
                var list = new List<(string Name, string Dn)>();
                bool ok = false;
                try
                {
                    using var searcher = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + _domainDn))
                    {
                        Filter = "(objectClass=organizationalUnit)",
                        SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                        PageSize = 500,
                        PropertiesToLoad = { "distinguishedName", "name", "ou" }
                    };
                    using var results = searcher.FindAll();
                    foreach (SearchResult sr in results)
                    {
                        string ouDn = (Prop(sr, "distinguishedName"));
                        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ouDn)) continue;
                        string ouName = sr.Properties["name"]?[0]?.ToString()
                                     ?? sr.Properties["ou"]?[0]?.ToString()
                                     ?? ouDn;
                        list.Add((ouName, ouDn));
                    }
                    ok = true;
                }
                catch { }
                if (ok)
                {
                    _ouCache = list;
                    _ouCacheLoadedUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
                    return list;
                }
                // A failed load is NOT cached: return the stale list (or empty)
                // and let the next call retry.
                return _ouCache ?? list;
            }
        }

        private static string Truncate(string s, int max)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(s)) return "";
            s = s.Replace("\r", " ").Replace("\n", " ").Trim();
            return s.Length <= max ? s : s.Substring(0, max) + "...";
        }

        /// <summary>Bind an LDAP path with the app's standard read flags (this
        /// construction was previously repeated at every call site).</summary>
        private static DirectoryEntry BindLdap(string dn) =>
            new("LDAP://" + dn, null, null,
                AuthenticationTypes.Secure | AuthenticationTypes.FastBind | AuthenticationTypes.ReadonlyServer);

        /// <summary>Read a string property from a search result, "" when absent or
        /// unreadable. Replaces seven identical local Prop functions and the inlined
        /// Contains-guarded ternary reads.</summary>
        private static string Prop(SearchResult sr, string name)
        {
            try
            {
                if (sr.Properties.Contains(name) && sr.Properties[name].Count > 0)
                    return sr.Properties[name][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
            }
            catch { }
            return "";
        }

        private Task<bool> ConfirmDialogAsync(string title, string message)
        {

            {

            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                Spacing = 10,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right
            };

            var ok = new Button { Content = "Yes", MinWidth = 90, Classes = { "action" } };

            ok.Click += (_, _) => { tcs.TrySetResult(true); dlg.Close(); };

            catch { dlg.Show(); }

                private void RenderGroupPanel(StackPanel panel, DirectoryEntry de, AdNode node)
        {
            var allGroups = GetAllMultiValues(de, "memberOf");
            try
            {
                var pgidObj = de.Properties["primaryGroupID"]?.Value;
                if (pgidObj != null)
                {
                    int pgid = Convert.ToInt32(pgidObj);
                    string primaryDn = ResolvePrimaryGroupDn(de, pgid);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(primaryDn) &&
                        !allGroups.Any(g => string.Equals(g, primaryDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                        allGroups.Insert(0, primaryDn);
                }
            }
            catch { }
            string nodeDn = node.Dn;
            string nodeName = node.Name;

            // Split view: LEFT = Add to group search, RIGHT = membership list with Remove
            var split = new Grid
            {
                ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,12,*"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            var left = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            var right = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };

            left.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Add to group", FontSize = 15, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left });
            var groupBox = new TextBox
            {
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Watermark = "Type to live-search groups...",
                MinHeight = 34,
                FontSize = 14
            };
            var suggestList = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };
            var suggestHost = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Padding = new Thickness(4),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0),
                IsVisible = false,
                MaxHeight = 140,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Child = new ScrollViewer
                {
                    Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0),
                    AllowAutoHide = false,
                    Content = suggestList,
                    MaxHeight = 130,
                    VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
                }
            };
            groupBox.TextChanged += (_, _) =>
            {
                string raw = groupBox.Text?.Trim() ?? "";
                suggestList.Children.Clear();
                if (raw.Length < 1) { suggestHost.IsVisible = false; return; }
                try
                {
                    using var searcher = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + _domainDn))
                    {
                        Filter = $"(&(objectClass=group)(|(sAMAccountName={EscapeLdap(raw)}*)(cn={EscapeLdap(raw)}*)))",
                        SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                        SizeLimit = 3,
                        PropertiesToLoad = { "distinguishedName", "cn", "sAMAccountName", "name" }
                    };
                    using var results = searcher.FindAll();
                    int shown = 0;
                    foreach (SearchResult sr in results)
                    {
                        if (shown >= 3) break;
                        string gDn = (Prop(sr, "distinguishedName"));
                        string cn = sr.Properties["cn"]?[0]?.ToString()
                                 ?? sr.Properties["name"]?[0]?.ToString()
                                 ?? gDn;
                        string sam = (Prop(sr, "sAMAccountName"));
                        var content = new StackPanel { Spacing = 1 };
                        content.Children.Add(MakeIconLine("Group", cn + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sam) ? "" : "  (" + sam + ")"), 13, 13, bold: false));
                        content.Children.Add(Tb(gDn, size: 11, opacity: 0.7, wrap: true));
                        var btn = new Button
                        {
                            Content = content,
                            Classes = { "suggestItem" },
                            HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                            HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left
                        };
                        string capturedCn = cn;
                        string capturedDn = gDn;
                        btn.Click += (_, _) =>
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                using var group = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + capturedDn);
                                using var user = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDn);
                                string userDn = user.Properties["distinguishedName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                                var existing = GetAllMultiValues(group, "member");
                                if (existing.Any(m => string.Equals(m, userDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                                {
                                    ShowNotification($"The group \"{capturedCn}\" already exists on this account (already a member).", "error");
                                    return;
                                }
                                group.Invoke("Add", new object[] { user.Path });
                                group.CommitChanges();
                                ShowNotification($"Group \"{capturedCn}\" has been added to {nodeName}", "success");
                                groupBox.Text = "";
                                suggestList.Children.Clear();
                                suggestHost.IsVisible = false;
                                ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                            }
                            catch (Exception ex)
                            {
                                string msg = ex.Message ?? "";
                                if (msg.IndexOf("already", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0 ||
                                    msg.IndexOf("exists", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                                    ShowNotification($"The group \"{capturedCn}\" already exists on this account (already a member).", "error");
                                else
                                    ShowNotification("Add to group failed: " + msg, "error");
                            }
                        };
                        suggestList.Children.Add(btn);
                        shown++;
                    }
                    suggestHost.IsVisible = shown > 0;
                }
                catch { suggestHost.IsVisible = false; }
            };
            left.Children.Add(groupBox);
            left.Children.Add(suggestHost);

            right.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = $"Membership ({allGroups.Count})", FontSize = 15, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left });
            if (allGroups.Count == 0)
            {
                right.Children.Add(Tb("(no group memberships found or insufficient rights)", opacity: 0.7, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));
            }
            else
            {
                var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4 };
                int rowIndex = 0;
                foreach (var gDn in allGroups.OrderBy(g => g, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    string display = gDn;
                    var cnPart = gDn.Split(',').FirstOrDefault(p => p.StartsWith("CN=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                    if (cnPart != null) display = cnPart.Substring(3);
                    var row = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto") };
                    row.Children.Add(MakeIconLine("Group", display, 13, 13, bold: false));
                    var rmBtn = new Button
                    {
                        Content = "Remove",
                        Classes = { "action" },
                        Padding = new Thickness(8, 2),
                        Margin = new Thickness(0, 2, 4, 2),
                        FontSize = 13
                    };
                    string capturedDisplay = display;
                    string capturedGdn = gDn;
                    rmBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            using var group = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + capturedGdn);
                            using var user = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDn);
                            group.Invoke("Remove", new object[] { user.Path });
                            group.CommitChanges();
                            ShowNotification($"Removed \"{capturedDisplay}\" from {nodeName}", "success");
                            ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                        }
                        catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Remove failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
                    };
                    Grid.SetColumn(rmBtn, 1);
                    row.Children.Add(rmBtn);
                    var rowBorder = new Border
                    {
                        Child = row,
                        CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(4),
                        Padding = new Thickness(4, 3),
                        Background = (rowIndex++ % 2 == 0)
                            ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(40, 128, 128, 128))
                            : Brushes.Transparent
                    };
                    list.Children.Add(rowBorder);
                }
                right.Children.Add(new ScrollViewer
                {
                    Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0),
                    AllowAutoHide = false,
                    Content = list,
                    MaxHeight = 180,
                    VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                    HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
                });
            }

            Grid.SetColumn(left, 0);
            Grid.SetColumn(right, 2);
            split.Children.Add(left);
            split.Children.Add(right);

            var shell = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8 };
            shell.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = $"Groups ({allGroups.Count})", FontSize = 16, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left });
            shell.Children.Add(split);
            panel.Children.Add(new Border { Classes = { "card" }, Child = shell, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, Padding = new Thickness(10) });
        }

        private string ResolvePrimaryGroupDn(DirectoryEntry userDe, int primaryGroupId)
        {
            try
            {
                // Build primary group SID from domain SID + RID
                byte[]? objectSid = null;
                try
                {
                    objectSid = userDe.Properties["objectSid"]?.Value as byte[];
                }
                catch { }
                if (objectSid == null || objectSid.Length < 8) return "";

                // Domain SID is objectSid without the last 4 RID bytes
                byte[] domainSid = new byte[objectSid.Length];
                Array.Copy(objectSid, domainSid, objectSid.Length);
                // Replace last 4 bytes with primaryGroupId (little-endian)
                domainSid[domainSid.Length - 4] = (byte)(primaryGroupId & 0xFF);
                domainSid[domainSid.Length - 3] = (byte)((primaryGroupId >> 8) & 0xFF);
                domainSid[domainSid.Length - 2] = (byte)((primaryGroupId >> 16) & 0xFF);
                domainSid[domainSid.Length - 1] = (byte)((primaryGroupId >> 24) & 0xFF);

                string sidStr = SidToString(domainSid);
                using var searcher = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + _domainDn))
                {
                    Filter = $"(objectSid={sidStr})",
                    SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                    PropertiesToLoad = { "distinguishedName" }
                };
                var result = searcher.FindOne();
                return result?.Properties["distinguishedName"]?[0]?.ToString() ?? "";
            }
            catch { return ""; }
        }

        private static string SidToString(byte[] sid)
        {
            // LDAP binary SID filter form: \01\05\00\00...
            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            foreach (byte b in sid)
                sb.Append('\\').Append(b.ToString("X2"));
            return sb.ToString();
        }

        /// <summary>Load all values of a multi-valued attribute using LDAP range retrieval.</summary>
        private static List<string> GetAllMultiValues(DirectoryEntry de, string attribute)
        {
            var results = new List<string>();
            try
            {
                // Fast path: if already populated and small, use it
                if (de.Properties.Contains(attribute) && de.Properties[attribute].Count > 0)
                {
                    foreach (var v in de.Properties[attribute])
                        if (v != null) results.Add(v.ToString()!);
                    // If Count looks complete for typical sizes, return
                    if (results.Count < 1000) return results;
                    results.Clear();
                }
            }
            catch { results.Clear(); }

            // Range retrieval for large sets
            int start = 0;
            const int page = 500;
            try
            {
                while (true)
                {
                    string rangeProp = $"{attribute};range={start}-{start + page - 1}";
                    using var searcher = new DirectorySearcher(de)
                    {
                        Filter = "(objectClass=*)",
                        SearchScope = SearchScope.Base,
                        PropertiesToLoad = { rangeProp, attribute }
                    };
                    var sr = searcher.FindOne();
                    if (sr == null) break;

                    bool foundRange = false;
                    foreach (string propName in sr.Properties.PropertyNames)
                    {
                        if (!propName.StartsWith(attribute, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                        foundRange = true;
                        foreach (var v in sr.Properties[propName])
                            if (v != null) results.Add(v.ToString()!);
                        // Final range ends with "*" e.g. member;range=1500-*
                        if (propName.EndsWith("-*", StringComparison.Ordinal) || propName.EndsWith("-*", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            return results;
                    }
                    if (!foundRange)
                    {
                        // Fallback: plain attribute
                        if (sr.Properties.Contains(attribute))
                        {
                            foreach (var v in sr.Properties[attribute])
                                if (v != null) results.Add(v.ToString()!);
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    start += page;
                    if (start > 100000) break; // safety
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                // last resort: direct property
                try
                {
                    foreach (var v in de.Properties[attribute])
                        if (v != null && !results.Contains(v.ToString()!))
                            results.Add(v.ToString()!);
                }
                catch { }
            }
            return results;
        }

        private static long? ReadLargeInteger(object? raw)
        {
            if (raw == null) return null;
            if (raw is long l) return l;
            if (raw is int i) return i;
            if (raw is uint ui) return ui;
            try
            {
                // System.DirectoryServices returns IADsLargeInteger as a COM object for pwdLastSet / maxPwdAge / lockoutTime
                var t = raw.GetType();
                var hiObj = t.InvokeMember("HighPart", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.GetProperty, null, raw, null);
                var loObj = t.InvokeMember("LowPart", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.GetProperty, null, raw, null);
                long high = Convert.ToInt64(hiObj);
                long low = Convert.ToInt64(loObj) & 0xFFFFFFFFL;
                return (high << 32) | low;
            }
            catch
            {
                try { return Convert.ToInt64(raw); }
                catch { return null; }
            }
        }

        private static string EscapeLdap(string s)
        {
            return s.Replace("\\", "\\5c").Replace("*", "\\2a").Replace("(", "\\28").Replace(")", "\\29").Replace("\0", "\\00");
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // LIVE SEARCH - debounced so fast typing is not dropped mid-word
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    }
}

        private void LoadThemePreference()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!File.Exists(ThemePreferenceFile)) return;
                string raw = (File.ReadAllText(ThemePreferenceFile) ?? "").Trim();
                if (string.Equals(raw, "Light", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    || string.Equals(raw, "Dark", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    _preferredTheme = string.Equals(raw, "Light", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? "Light" : "Dark";
            }
            catch { }
        }

        private void SaveThemePreference()
        {

                File.WriteAllText(ThemePreferenceFile, _preferredTheme ?? "Dark", Encoding.UTF8);
            }
            catch { }
        }

        private void LoadAppSettings()
        {
            try
            {
                // Theme must load even when vault is not unlocked yet
                LoadThemePreference();
                if (!File.Exists(SettingsFile)) return;
                string json = File.ReadAllText(SettingsFile);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(json)) return;
                var s = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<AppSettings>(json);
                if (s == null) return;
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.LoginLogFolder))
                    _loginLogFolder = s.LoginLogFolder.Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.ComputerLogFolder))
                    _computerLogFolder = s.ComputerLogFolder.Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.VncViewerPath))
                    _vncViewerPath = s.VncViewerPath.Trim();
                _horizonBrokers = ParseBrokerList(s.HorizonBrokers ?? "");
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.HorizonDomain))
                    _horizonDomain = s.HorizonDomain.Trim();
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.HorizonUsername))
                    _horizonUsername = s.HorizonUsername.Trim();
                // Plaintext settings.json passwords are intentionally NOT loaded:
                // credentials are only ever restored from the encrypted vault.
                if (s.RdpUsername != null)
                    _rdpUsername = s.RdpUsername.Trim();
                // (A plaintext RdpPassword is likewise ignored - vault only.)
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.ProfileCifsServer))
                    _profileCifsServer = s.ProfileCifsServer.Trim();
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.ProfileSharePath))
                    _profileSharePath = s.ProfileSharePath.Trim().TrimStart('\\');
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.Theme))
                    _preferredTheme = s.Theme.Trim();
                if (s.StaleComputerDays > 0)
                    _staleComputerDays = s.StaleComputerDays;
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.DelProf2Path))
                    _delProf2Path = s.DelProf2Path.Trim();
                if (s.HorizonCertPins != null)
                    _horizonCertPins = new Dictionary<string, string>(s.HorizonCertPins, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            }
            catch { }
        }

            };
        }

        /// <summary>

                _loginLogFolder = s.LoginLogFolder.Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');

                _preferredTheme = s.Theme.Trim();

                _staleComputerDays = s.StaleComputerDays;

        }

        // Outcome of the most recent vault save. SaveAppSettings is void and
        // fire-and-forget for most callers, so first-run setup reads these to
        // know whether the vault really landed (hash-verified) at its destination.
        private bool _lastVaultSaveVerified;
        private string? _lastVaultSaveError;

        private static string ComputeSha256Hex(byte[] data)
        {
            using var sha = SHA256.Create();
            byte[] hash = sha.ComputeHash(data);
            var sb = new StringBuilder(hash.Length * 2);
            foreach (byte b in hash) sb.Append(b.ToString("x2"));
            return sb.ToString();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Write the encrypted vault payload safely:
        /// 1) stage it in a file in the local Windows temp folder (%TEMP%\ADE),
        /// 2) SHA-256 the payload,
        /// 3) copy the staging file to the vault destination,
        /// 4) read the destination back and verify its SHA-256 matches,
        /// 5) clean up: delete the local temp copy.
        /// Returns true only when the destination copy verified byte-for-byte.
        /// On failure the temp staging file is left in place for recovery and
        /// error describes what happened.
        /// </summary>
        private bool TryWriteVaultVerified(string vaultPath, string payload, out string? error)
        {
            error = null;
            string stagingDir = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "ADE");
            string stagingFile = Path.Combine(stagingDir,
                "ade-settings-staging-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N") + ".vault");
            byte[] payloadBytes = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(payload);
            string expectedHash = ComputeSha256Hex(payloadBytes);
            try
            {
                // 1) Stage locally in Windows temp
                Directory.CreateDirectory(stagingDir);
                File.WriteAllBytes(stagingFile, payloadBytes);
                string stagedHash = ComputeSha256Hex(File.ReadAllBytes(stagingFile));
                if (!string.Equals(stagedHash, expectedHash, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    error = "The local temp staging copy failed its hash check; the vault was not copied.";
                    return false;
                }

                // Snapshot the current vault as .bak first, so a failed copy can
                // be rolled back instead of destroying the last good vault.
                try
                {
                    if (File.Exists(vaultPath))
                        File.Copy(vaultPath, vaultPath + ".bak", true);
                }
                catch { }

                // 2) Copy the staged vault to its destination
                string? destDir = Path.GetDirectoryName(vaultPath);
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(destDir))
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(destDir);
                File.Copy(stagingFile, vaultPath, true);

                // 3) Verify the destination copy by hash
                if (!File.Exists(vaultPath))
                {
                    error = "The copy finished but no vault file was found at the destination.";
                    return false;
                }
                string destHash = ComputeSha256Hex(File.ReadAllBytes(vaultPath));
                if (!string.Equals(destHash, expectedHash, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    // Roll back to the pre-copy snapshot so the last good vault survives.
                    try
                    {
                        string bak = vaultPath + ".bak";
                        if (File.Exists(bak)) File.Copy(bak, vaultPath, true);
                    }
                    catch { }
                    error = "The vault at the destination does not match the staged hash "
                          + "(the copy may be incomplete). The previous vault was restored from its .bak snapshot when possible. "
                          + "The staged copy was kept at:\n" + stagingFile;
                    return false;
                }

                // 4) Cleanup: remove the local temp copy
                try { File.Delete(stagingFile); } catch { }
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                error = ex.Message + "\nThe staged copy was kept at:\n" + stagingFile;
                return false;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Cleanup pass for vault staging files left in Windows temp by an
        /// interrupted save (crash/kill between staging and cleanup). Only
        /// files older than a day are removed, so a save in progress in
        /// another ADE instance is never disturbed.
        /// </summary>
        private static void CleanupStaleVaultStagingFiles()
        {
            try
            {
                string stagingDir = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "ADE");
                if (!Directory.Exists(stagingDir)) return;
                foreach (string f in Directory.GetFiles(stagingDir, "ade-settings-staging-*.vault"))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        if (File.GetLastWriteTimeUtc(f) < DateTime.UtcNow.AddDays(-1))
                            File.Delete(f);
                    }
                    catch { }
                }
            }
            catch { }
        }

        private void SaveAppSettings()
        {
            try
            {
                var s = BuildSettingsObject();
                var opts = new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true };
                string json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(s, opts);

                // Always persist theme locally so next launch (before PIN) uses the same mode
                SaveThemePreference();

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_vaultFilePath) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_vaultPin))
                {
                    // Stage in Windows temp -> hash -> copy to the vault path ->
                    // verify the destination hash -> clean up the temp copy.
                    _lastVaultSaveVerified = TryWriteVaultVerified(
                        _vaultFilePath, EncryptStringToVault(json, _vaultPin), out _lastVaultSaveError);
                    if (!_lastVaultSaveVerified)
                        return; // Do not record the location of a vault that did not verify.
                    try
                    {
                        Directory.CreateDirectory(UndoDir);
                        RememberVaultPath(_vaultFilePath);
                    }
                    catch { }
                    return;
                }

                // No vault unlocked: persist only non-credential settings locally.
                // Passwords are NEVER written to plaintext settings.json - without
                // a vault they are kept in memory for this session only.
                s.HorizonPassword = "";
                s.RdpPassword = "";
                Directory.CreateDirectory(UndoDir);
                File.WriteAllText(SettingsFile, JsonSerializer.Serialize(s, opts));
            }
            catch { }
        }

        private static string EncryptStringToVault(string plainText, string pin)
        {
            byte[] salt = RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(16);
            byte[] iv = RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(16);
            byte[] keyMat;
            using (var kdf = new Rfc2898DeriveBytes(pin, salt, 120000, HashAlgorithmName.SHA256))
                keyMat = kdf.GetBytes(64);
            byte[] cipher;
            using (var aes = Aes.Create())
            {
                aes.Key = keyMat.Take(32).ToArray();
                aes.IV = iv;
                aes.Mode = CipherMode.CBC;
                aes.Padding = PaddingMode.PKCS7;
                using var enc = aes.CreateEncryptor();
                byte[] plain = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(plainText);
                cipher = enc.TransformFinalBlock(plain, 0, plain.Length);
            }
            byte[] mac;
            using (var h = new HMACSHA256(keyMat.Skip(32).ToArray()))
            {
                var data = new byte[salt.Length + iv.Length + cipher.Length];
                Buffer.BlockCopy(salt, 0, data, 0, salt.Length);
                Buffer.BlockCopy(iv, 0, data, salt.Length, iv.Length);
                Buffer.BlockCopy(cipher, 0, data, salt.Length + iv.Length, cipher.Length);
                mac = h.ComputeHash(data);
            }
            // ADE2 adds an HMAC (second half of the derived key) so unlock can tell
            // "wrong PIN" apart from a damaged file. ADE1 vaults still load and are
            // upgraded to ADE2 on the next save.
            return "ADE2$"
                + Convert.ToBase64String(salt) + "$"
                + Convert.ToBase64String(iv) + "$"
                + Convert.ToBase64String(mac) + "$"
                + Convert.ToBase64String(cipher);
        }

        private enum VaultDecryptStatus { Ok, WrongPin, Corrupt }

        private static string? DecryptStringFromVault(string payload, string pin, out VaultDecryptStatus status)
        {
            status = VaultDecryptStatus.Corrupt;
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(payload)) return null;
            payload = payload.Trim();
            bool v2 = payload.StartsWith("ADE2$", StringComparison.Ordinal);
            bool v1 = !v2 && payload.StartsWith("ADE1$", StringComparison.Ordinal);
            if (!v1 && !v2) return null;
            string[] parts = payload.Split('$');
            if (parts.Length != (v2 ? 5 : 4)) return null;
            byte[] salt, iv, cipher;
            byte[]? mac = null;
            try
            {
                salt = Convert.FromBase64String(parts[1]);
                iv = Convert.FromBase64String(parts[2]);
                if (v2) { mac = Convert.FromBase64String(parts[3]); cipher = Convert.FromBase64String(parts[4]); }
                else cipher = Convert.FromBase64String(parts[3]);
            }
            catch { return null; }
            if (salt.Length != 16 || iv.Length != 16 || cipher.Length == 0 || (v2 && mac!.Length != 32))
                return null;
            try
            {
                byte[] keyMat;
                using (var kdf = new Rfc2898DeriveBytes(pin, salt, 120000, HashAlgorithmName.SHA256))
                    keyMat = kdf.GetBytes(v2 ? 64 : 32);
                if (v2)
                {
                    // Authenticate BEFORE decrypting: on a structurally valid ADE2
                    // file, an HMAC mismatch means this PIN does not belong to it.
                    byte[] expect;
                    using (var h = new HMACSHA256(keyMat.Skip(32).ToArray()))
                    {
                        var data = new byte[salt.Length + iv.Length + cipher.Length];
                        Buffer.BlockCopy(salt, 0, data, 0, salt.Length);
                        Buffer.BlockCopy(iv, 0, data, salt.Length, iv.Length);
                        Buffer.BlockCopy(cipher, 0, data, salt.Length + iv.Length, cipher.Length);
                        expect = h.ComputeHash(data);
                    }
                    if (!CryptographicOperations.FixedTimeEquals(expect, mac!))
                    {
                        status = VaultDecryptStatus.WrongPin;
                        return null;
                    }
                }
                using var aes = Aes.Create();
                aes.Key = v2 ? keyMat.Take(32).ToArray() : keyMat;
                aes.IV = iv;
                aes.Mode = CipherMode.CBC;
                aes.Padding = PaddingMode.PKCS7;
                using var dec = aes.CreateDecryptor();
                byte[] plain = dec.TransformFinalBlock(cipher, 0, cipher.Length);
                string text = new System.Text.UTF8Encoding(false, true).GetString(plain);
                if (!text.TrimStart().StartsWith("{", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                {
                    // Decrypted to garbage: wrong PIN (the only route for legacy
                    // ADE1; an ADE2 file cannot get here - its HMAC verified).
                    status = VaultDecryptStatus.WrongPin;
                    return null;
                }
                status = VaultDecryptStatus.Ok;
                return text;
            }
            catch
            {
                // Padding / UTF-8 failure on a structurally valid file = wrong PIN.
                status = VaultDecryptStatus.WrongPin;
                return null;
            }
        }

        // Set when an unlock fails because the vault FILE is damaged / not a vault
        // (as opposed to a wrong PIN), so the unlock prompt can say so instead of
        // looping on "Incorrect PIN".
        private string _lastVaultUnlockError = "";

        private bool TryLoadVaultWithPin(string vaultPath, string pin)
        {
            _lastVaultUnlockError = "";
            if (!File.Exists(vaultPath)) return false;
            string payload = File.ReadAllText(vaultPath);
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(payload)) return false;
            string? json = DecryptStringFromVault(payload, pin, out var dstat);
            if (json == null)
            {
                if (dstat == VaultDecryptStatus.Corrupt)
                    _lastVaultUnlockError = "The vault file appears to be damaged, truncated, or is not an ADE vault file:\n"
                        + vaultPath
                        + "\n\nCheck the path, or restore the backup copy (ade-settings.vault.bak) in the same folder. "
                        + "Your PIN was not the problem, so this did not count as a PIN attempt.";
                return false;
            }
            var s = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<AppSettings>(json);
            if (s == null) return false;
            ApplySettingsObject(s);
            _vaultFilePath = vaultPath;
            _vaultPin = pin;
            _vaultUnlocked = true;
            return true;
        }

        private bool TestLdapCredentials(string username, string password, out string message)
        {
            message = "";
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(username) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(password))
            {
                message = "Username and password are required.";
                return false;
            }
            try
            {
                using var root = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://RootDSE", username, password, AuthenticationTypes.Secure);
                object? nc = root.Properties["defaultNamingContext"]?.Value;
                if (nc == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(nc.ToString()))
                {
                    message = "Bind succeeded but domain naming context was empty.";
                    return false;
                }
                message = "OK - authenticated to AD (" + nc + ")";
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                message = "AD authentication failed: " + ex.Message;
                return false;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Resolve typed admin id (UPN, DOMAIN\user, or sAMAccountName) to a real AD userPrincipalName.
        /// </summary>
        private bool TryResolveAdminUsername(string input, out string resolvedUpn, out string message)
        {
            resolvedUpn = "";
            message = "";
            input = (input ?? "").Trim();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input))
            {
                message = "Username is required.";
                return false;
            }

            string lookup = input;
            if (input.Contains('\\'))
                lookup = input.Split('\\').Last().Trim();
            else if (input.Contains('@'))
                lookup = input.Split('@')[0].Trim();

            try
            {
                using var rootDse = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://RootDSE");
                string nc = rootDse.Properties["defaultNamingContext"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(nc))
                {
                    message = "Could not read domain naming context.";
                    return false;
                }

                string escFull = EscapeLdap(input);
                string escSam = EscapeLdap(lookup);
                using var searcher = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nc))
                {
                    Filter = "(&(objectCategory=person)(objectClass=user)(!(objectClass=computer))"
                           + "(|(sAMAccountName=" + escSam + ")(userPrincipalName=" + escFull + ")(userPrincipalName=" + escSam + "@*)))",
                    SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                    SizeLimit = 5,
                    PropertiesToLoad = { "userPrincipalName", "sAMAccountName", "distinguishedName", "userAccountControl" }
                };
                using var results = searcher.FindAll();
                SearchResult? best = null;
                foreach (SearchResult sr in results)
                {
                    best = sr;
                    break;
                }
                if (best == null)
                {
                    message = "No Active Directory user matched \"" + input + "\". Enter a valid admin username.";
                    return false;
                }

                string sam = "";
                string upn = "";
                try
                {
                    if (best.Properties.Contains("sAMAccountName") && best.Properties["sAMAccountName"].Count > 0)
                        sam = best.Properties["sAMAccountName"][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                    if (best.Properties.Contains("userPrincipalName") && best.Properties["userPrincipalName"].Count > 0)
                        upn = best.Properties["userPrincipalName"][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                }
                catch { }

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(upn))
                {
                    string dns = ResolveDnsDomain();
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sam) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dns))
                        upn = sam + "@" + dns;
                    else if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sam))
                        upn = sam;
                    else
                        upn = input;
                }

                try
                {
                    if (best.Properties.Contains("userAccountControl") && best.Properties["userAccountControl"].Count > 0)
                    {
                        int uac = Convert.ToInt32(best.Properties["userAccountControl"][0]);
                        if ((uac & 0x2) != 0)
                        {
                            message = "Account \"" + upn + "\" is disabled in Active Directory.";
                            resolvedUpn = upn;
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch { }

                resolvedUpn = upn.Trim();
                message = "Resolved admin account: " + resolvedUpn
                    + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sam) ? "" : " (sAMAccountName: " + sam + ")");
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                message = "Could not resolve username in AD: " + ex.Message;
                return false;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Test Horizon credentials over the REST API (POST /rest/login on the first
        /// broker). The Horizon engine is pure C# - no PowerCLI module is involved.
        /// </summary>
        private async Task<bool> TestHorizonCredentialsAsync(string brokersRaw, string domain, string user, string pass, Action<string> status)
        {
            var brokers = ParseBrokerList(brokersRaw ?? "");
            if (brokers.Count == 0)
            {
                status("Add at least one Horizon Connection Server hostname (FQDN) first.");
                return false;
            }
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(user) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pass))
            {
                status("Enter Horizon username and password first.");
                return false;
            }

            string prevUser = _horizonUsername, prevDom = _horizonDomain, prevPass = _horizonPassword;
            var prevBrokers = _horizonBrokers;
            _horizonUsername = user.Trim();
            _horizonDomain = (domain ?? "").Trim();
            _horizonPassword = pass;
            _horizonBrokers = brokers;

            // REST login takes seconds; keep a generous overall timeout + live elapsed status
            const int TimeoutSeconds = 120;
            var overallCts = new CancellationTokenSource();
            overallCts.CancelAfter(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(TimeoutSeconds));
            var token = overallCts.Token;
            var sw = System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.StartNew();
            string phase = "Starting";
            void Report(string msg)
            {
                phase = msg;
                try { status(msg); } catch { }
            }
            // Background ticker so the progress window does not look frozen
            var ticker = Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                try
                {
                    while (!token.IsCancellationRequested)
                    {
                        await Task.Delay(5000, token);
                        int sec = (int)sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds;
                        try
                        {
                            status(phase + "  (" + sec + "s elapsed, timeout " + TimeoutSeconds + "s)");
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }
                }
                catch (OperationCanceledException) { }
            }, token);

            try
            {
                string broker = brokers[0];
                Report("Connecting to " + broker + " (Horizon REST API /rest/login)...");
                int remainMs = Math.Max(15000, (TimeoutSeconds * 1000) - (int)sw.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds);
                Task<(List<HorizonSessionHit> Sessions, string Error)> queryTask =
                    QueryHorizonBrokerSessionsAsync(broker, _horizonDomain, _horizonUsername, _horizonPassword, "__ade_test__");
                Task finished = await Task.WhenAny(queryTask, Task.Delay(remainMs, token));
                if (finished != queryTask || token.IsCancellationRequested)
                {
                    Report("Timed out after " + (int)sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds + "s connecting to " + broker + ".\n"
                        + "Check network access to the Connection Server, DNS, and credentials, then try again.");
                    return false;
                }
                var (sessions, err) = await queryTask;

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(err))
                {
                    // Untrusted certificate: offer to pin the exact certificate this
                    // broker presented (after the user confirms its thumbprint) and
                    // retry once - the flow for self-signed / internal-CA brokers.
                    if (err.IndexOf("certificate", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                    {
                        string? thumb = await ProbeBrokerCertThumbprintAsync(broker);
                        bool alreadyPinned = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(thumb)
                            && _horizonCertPins.TryGetValue(broker, out var existingPin)
                            && string.Equals(NormalizeCertThumbprint(existingPin), NormalizeCertThumbprint(thumb), StringComparison.Ordinal);
                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(thumb) && !alreadyPinned)
                        {
                            bool trust = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                                "Trust Horizon certificate?",
                                broker + " presented a certificate that is not trusted by this PC.\n\n"
                                + "Certificate thumbprint (SHA-1):\n" + thumb + "\n\n"
                                + "Trust this exact certificate for " + broker + " only if you expected a self-signed or internal certificate for this Connection Server.\n\n"
                                + "Yes = trust it and retry\nNo = fail the test");
                            if (trust)
                            {
                                _horizonCertPins[broker] = thumb!;
                                try { SaveAppSettings(); } catch { }
                                Report("Certificate pinned for " + broker + " - retrying...");
                                var retry = await QueryHorizonBrokerSessionsAsync(broker, _horizonDomain, _horizonUsername, _horizonPassword, "__ade_test__");
                                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(retry.Error))
                                {
                                    Report("OK - connected to " + broker + " in " + (int)sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds + "s");
                                    return true;
                                }
                                Report("Failed: " + retry.Error);
                                return false;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Report("Failed: " + err);
                    return false;
                }
                Report("OK - connected to " + broker + " in " + (int)sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds + "s");
                return true;
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException)
            {
                Report("Timed out after " + TimeoutSeconds + "s testing Horizon credentials.");
                return false;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Report("Failed: " + ex.Message);
                return false;
            }
            finally
            {
                try { overallCts.Cancel(); } catch { }
                try { overallCts.Dispose(); } catch { }
                _horizonUsername = prevUser;
                _horizonDomain = prevDom;
                _horizonPassword = prevPass;
                _horizonBrokers = prevBrokers;
            }

        }


        private static bool IsDisallowedVaultPath(string path)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path)) return true;
            string p = path.Trim();
            if (p.StartsWith(@"C:\Users\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return true;
            try
            {
                string profile = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.UserProfile) ?? "";
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(profile) && p.StartsWith(profile, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    return true;
            }
            catch { }
            if (!p.StartsWith(@"\\", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                return true;
            bool homeLike = p.IndexOf("UserHomes", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                || p.IndexOf("dfsshares", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                || p.IndexOf(@"\homes\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                || p.IndexOf(@"\homes", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
            return !homeLike;
        }

        /// <summary>Custom-location rule (the setup menu's "Use a custom vault location"
        /// checkbox): any fully qualified path - a UNC share or a local drive - is accepted.</summary>
        private static bool IsAcceptableCustomVaultPath(string path)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path)) return false;
            string p = path.Trim();
            if (p.StartsWith(@"\\", StringComparison.Ordinal)) return true;
            return p.Length >= 3 && char.IsLetter(p[0]) && p[1] == ':'
                && (p[2] == '\\' || p[2] == '/');
        }

        private bool VaultPathAllowed(string path)
            => _useCustomVaultLocation ? IsAcceptableCustomVaultPath(path) : !IsDisallowedVaultPath(path);

        /// <summary>Remember the vault location, keeping the custom-location marker in
        /// sync so a custom path still passes validation on the next start.</summary>
        private void RememberVaultPath(string path)
        {
            try { File.WriteAllText(VaultLocationFile, path, Encoding.UTF8); } catch { }
            try
            {
                if (_useCustomVaultLocation)
                    File.WriteAllText(VaultCustomMarkerFile, "custom", Encoding.UTF8);
                else if (File.Exists(VaultCustomMarkerFile))
                    File.Delete(VaultCustomMarkerFile);
            }
            catch { }
        }


        /// <summary>DNS domain for UPN (e.g. internal.oln-afmc.af.mil). Not hardcoded.</summary>
        private string ResolveDnsDomain()
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_dnsRoot))
                return _dnsRoot.Trim().TrimStart('.');
            // NO RootDSE bind here (user report 2026-10-27): this helper is
            // called while merely building UI text (the Settings window calls
            // it four times), and a synchronous bind on the UI thread freezes
            // the whole program for minutes when no domain controller is
            // reachable. The domain loader already fills _dnsRoot from the
            // real domain entry on any successful load; until then the local
            // fallbacks below (USERDNSDOMAIN / UserDomainName) are enough -
            // they need no network at all.
            try
            {
                // USERDNSDOMAIN is set on domain-joined Windows (e.g. internal.oln-afmc.af.mil)
                string? envDns = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("USERDNSDOMAIN");
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(envDns))
                {
                    _dnsRoot = envDns.Trim().TrimStart('.');
                    return _dnsRoot;
                }
            }
            catch { }
            return Environment.UserDomainName ?? "";
        }


        /// <summary>Strip UPN or DOMAIN\user down to sAMAccountName.</summary>
        private static string ExtractSamAccountName(string input)
        {
            input = (input ?? "").Trim();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input)) return "";
            int slash = input.LastIndexOf('\\');
            if (slash >= 0 && slash + 1 < input.Length)
                return input.Substring(slash + 1).Trim();
            int at = input.IndexOf('@');
            if (at > 0)
                return input.Substring(0, at).Trim();
            return input;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Build admin UPN as sam@{current DNS domain} so the same short name works on any network.
        /// Domain is auto-detected via RootDSE / USERDNSDOMAIN (not hardcoded).
        /// </summary>
        private string BuildAdminUpnFromUsername(string input)
        {
            string sam = ExtractSamAccountName(input);
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sam)) return "";
            string dns = ResolveDnsDomain();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dns))
                return sam;
            return sam + "@" + dns;
        }

        /// <summary>Default short admin name for prompts (no domain suffix).</summary>
        private string GetAdminUsernamePromptDefault()
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_rdpUsername))
                return ExtractSamAccountName(_rdpUsername);
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_horizonUsername))
                return ExtractSamAccountName(_horizonUsername);
            return ExtractSamAccountName(Environment.UserName ?? "");
        }

        /// <summary>Check vault file exists (UNC-safe with timeout).</summary>
        private static async Task<bool> VaultFileExistsAsync(string path, int timeoutMs = 12000)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path)) return false;
            try
            {
                var t = Task.Run(() =>
                {
                    try { return File.Exists(path); }
                    catch { return false; }
                });
                var done = await Task.WhenAny(t, Task.Delay(timeoutMs));
                if (done != t) return false;
                return t.Result;
            }
            catch { return false; }
        }

        private async Task EnsureVaultReadyAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(UndoDir);
                CleanupStaleVaultStagingFiles();
                try { _useCustomVaultLocation = File.Exists(VaultCustomMarkerFile); } catch { }

                // ---------- 1) Silent path: remembered + file exists → unlock only ----------
                string? remembered = ReadRememberedVaultPath();
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(remembered))
                {
                    if (!VaultPathAllowed(remembered))
                    {
                        await ShowMessageAsync(
                            "Invalid vault location",
                            "Saved path is not allowed (local/admin profile):\n" + remembered
                            + "\n\nYou will choose a valid vault or create a new one.");
                        ClearRememberedVaultPath();
                        remembered = null;
                    }
                    else if (await VaultFileExistsAsync(remembered))
                    {
                        _vaultFilePath = remembered;
                        await UnlockExistingVaultAsync();
                        // PIN OK → done. Cancel / failures fall through to Setup Menu.
                        if (_vaultUnlocked) return;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        await ShowMessageAsync(
                            "Vault file not found",
                            "Saved location is missing or unreachable:\n" + remembered
                            + "\n\nLocate an existing vault or create a new one.");
                        ClearRememberedVaultPath();
                        remembered = null;
                    }
                }

                // ---------- 2) Resilient menu until vault is ready or user exits ----------
                while (true)
                {
                    // Already unlocked mid-loop (e.g. create finished)
                    if (_vaultUnlocked && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_vaultFilePath)
                        && await VaultFileExistsAsync(_vaultFilePath))
                        return;

                    int choice = await PromptVaultSetupMenuAsync();
                    // 0 = locate by path, 1 = create, 2 = exit, 3 = locate by username
                    if (choice == 2)
                    {
                        await ShowMessageAsync(
                            "Setup cancelled",
                            "No vault is configured. ADE will continue with empty settings.\n"
                            + "Use Settings or restart ADE to set up a vault later.\n\n"
                            + "Note: without a vault, passwords entered in Settings are kept only for this session and are never saved.");
                        return;
                    }

                    if (choice == 0 || choice == 3)
                    {
                        string? located = choice == 3
                            ? await PromptLocateVaultByUsernameAsync()
                            : await PromptForExistingVaultPathAsync();
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(located))
                        {
                            // Cancel / not found → back to menu (do not force create)
                            continue;
                        }
                        _vaultFilePath = located.Trim();
                        RememberVaultPath(_vaultFilePath);
                        await UnlockExistingVaultAsync();
                        if (_vaultUnlocked) return;
                        // Failed unlock without recreate → allow menu again
                        continue;
                    }

                    // choice == 1 create
                    await RunFirstTimeVaultSetupAsync();
                    if (_vaultUnlocked && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_vaultFilePath)
                        && await VaultFileExistsAsync(_vaultFilePath!))
                        return;
                    // Create cancelled or failed → menu again
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                try { await ShowMessageAsync("Setup error", ex.Message); } catch { }
            }
        }

        private string? ReadRememberedVaultPath()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!File.Exists(VaultLocationFile)) return null;
                string p = (File.ReadAllText(VaultLocationFile) ?? "").Trim();
                return p.Length == 0 ? null : p;
            }
            catch { return null; }
        }

        private void ClearRememberedVaultPath()
        {
            try { if (File.Exists(VaultLocationFile)) File.Delete(VaultLocationFile); } catch { }
