            try { if (File.Exists(VaultCustomMarkerFile)) File.Delete(VaultCustomMarkerFile); } catch { }
            _vaultFilePath = null;
        }

        // Plain-language explanation shown during first-run setup: most users
        // have never heard of a "vault" and should not have to guess what it
        // is or why ADE wants one before being asked to create it.
        private const string VaultExplainer =
            "What is a vault?\n"
            + "ADE remembers your settings - the admin account to use, your Horizon servers, and options "
            + "like them - in one small encrypted file called a vault. A PIN you choose locks it, so the "
            + "passwords inside cannot be read without it. You set this up once: after that, ADE asks for "
            + "the PIN at startup and everything is filled in for you.\n\n"
            + "The vault normally lives in your home folder, so it follows you from PC to PC. If you "
            + "already made one on another PC, choose \"Locate existing vault\" below instead of creating "
            + "a new one. You can also skip for now, but then ADE forgets your settings and passwords "
            + "every time it closes.";

        /// <summary>0 = locate existing (type path), 1 = create new, 2 = exit / skip, 3 = locate existing by username.</summary>
        private async Task<int> PromptVaultSetupMenuAsync()
        {
            int result = 2;
            var dlg = new Window
            {
                Title = "Vault setup",
                Width = 520,
                MinWidth = 440,
                SizeToContent = SizeToContent.Height,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                CanResize = false
            };
            StyleDialogWindow(dlg);
            var root = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(20), Spacing = 12 };
            root.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Vault setup", FontSize = 18, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold });
            root.Children.Add(Tb("No valid vault is configured on this PC.", wrap: true));
            root.Children.Add(Tb(VaultExplainer, size: 12.5, opacity: 0.9, wrap: true));
            root.Children.Add(Tb("Choose how to continue:", wrap: true));

            var customBox = new CheckBox
            {
                Content = "Use a custom vault location",
                IsChecked = _useCustomVaultLocation,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0)
            };
            var customWarn = Tb("Warning: if you are using a virtual machine or an instant clone, "
                + "the vault will not be persistent. The only way it can be persistent is if it is "
                + "saved on a server or on a physical machine's C: drive.",
                size: 12, opacity: 0.85, wrap: true);
            customWarn.IsVisible = _useCustomVaultLocation;
            customBox.IsCheckedChanged += (_, _) =>
            {
                _useCustomVaultLocation = customBox.IsChecked == true;
                customWarn.IsVisible = _useCustomVaultLocation;
            };
            root.Children.Add(customBox);
            root.Children.Add(customWarn);

            var locate = new Button { Content = "Locate existing vault", Classes = { "action" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, MinHeight = 36 };
            var byUser = new Button { Content = "Open existing vault by username", Classes = { "action" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, MinHeight = 36 };
            var create = new Button { Content = "Create new vault (first-time setup)", Classes = { "action" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, MinHeight = 36 };
            var exit = new Button { Content = "Skip for now", Classes = { "action" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, MinHeight = 36 };
            locate.Click += (_, _) => { result = 0; dlg.Close(); };
            byUser.Click += (_, _) => { result = 3; dlg.Close(); };
            create.Click += (_, _) => { result = 1; dlg.Close(); };
            exit.Click += (_, _) => { result = 2; dlg.Close(); };
            dlg.KeyDown += (_, e) =>
            {
                if (e.Key == Key.Escape) { result = 2; dlg.Close(); e.Handled = true; }
            };
            root.Children.Add(locate);
            root.Children.Add(byUser);
            root.Children.Add(create);
            root.Children.Add(exit);
            dlg.Content = root;
            dlg.Opened += (_, _) => locate.Focus();
            try { await dlg.ShowDialog(this); } catch { }
            return result;
        }

        /// <summary>Ask for path to an existing .vault file; validate it exists and is allowed.</summary>
        private async Task<string?> PromptForExistingVaultPathAsync()
        {
            string loginUser = Environment.UserName ?? "";
            string regularHint = loginUser;
            if (loginUser.StartsWith("nipradmin_", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                regularHint = loginUser.Substring("nipradmin_".Length);

            string example = @"\\internal.oln-afmc.af.mil\dfsshares\UserHomes\" + regularHint + @"\ADE\ade-settings.vault";

            for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
            {
                string? chosen = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Locate vault file",
                    "Enter the full path to your existing encrypted vault file (.vault).\n"
                    + (_useCustomVaultLocation
                        ? "Custom location enabled: any server share or local drive path is accepted."
                        : "Must be under DFS UserHomes (or a user home share), not C:\\Users\\..."),
                    example,
                    false,
                    example,
                    widePath: true);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(chosen))
                    return null;
                chosen = chosen.Trim();
                if (!VaultPathAllowed(chosen))
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync(
                        "Invalid location",
                        _useCustomVaultLocation
                            ? "Enter a full path (a UNC share or a local drive), for example:\n" + @"\\server\share\ADE\ade-settings.vault"
                            : "That path is not allowed (local/admin profile).\nUse DFS UserHomes, for example:\n" + example);
                    continue;
                }

                TextBlock st;
                ProgressBar bar;
                var dlg = ShowProgressWindow("Checking vault", "Looking for vault file...", out st, out bar);
                bool exists = false;
                try
                {
                    await Task.Delay(50);
                    exists = await VaultFileExistsAsync(chosen);
                    st.Text = exists ? "Found." : "Not found.";
                    await Task.Delay(120);
                }
                finally
                {
                    try { dlg.Close(); } catch { }
                }

                if (!exists)
                {
                    bool retry = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                        "Vault not found",
                        "No vault file at:\n" + chosen + "\n\nTry another path?\n(No = create a new vault instead)");
                    if (!retry) return null;
                    continue;
                }
                return chosen;
            }
            return null;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Open an already-created vault by username: ask whose vault it is,
        /// query Active Directory for that user's homeDirectory, and look for
        /// the vault in the ADE folder inside the home path
        /// (&lt;home&gt;\ADE\ade-settings.vault). Returns the vault path when found.
        /// </summary>
        private async Task<string?> PromptLocateVaultByUsernameAsync()
        {
            string loginUser = Environment.UserName ?? "";
            string prefill = loginUser;
            if (loginUser.IndexOf("admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                || loginUser.StartsWith("nipradmin_", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                prefill = "";

            for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
            {
                string? asked = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Open existing vault",
                    "Enter the username the vault was created for (your regular, non-admin username).\n\n"
                    + "ADE will look up that user's home folder in Active Directory and open:\n"
                    + "<home folder>\\ADE\\ade-settings.vault",
                    prefill,
                    false,
                    "jdoe");
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(asked))
                    return null;
                string username = asked.Trim();

                string home = "";
                TextBlock st;
                ProgressBar bar;
                var dlg = ShowProgressWindow(
                    "Finding vault",
                    "Looking up the home folder in Active Directory...",
                    out st,
                    out bar);
                try
                {
                    await Task.Delay(50);
                    home = await Task.Run(() => TryGetAdHomeDirectory(username));
                    st.Text = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(home) ? "Not found." : "Found: " + home;
                    await Task.Delay(120);
                }
                finally
                {
                    try { dlg.Close(); } catch { }
                }

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(home))
                {
                    bool retry = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                        "Home folder not found",
                        "No homeDirectory was found in Active Directory for \"" + username + "\".\n\n"
                        + "Try another username?");
                    if (!retry) return null;
                    continue;
                }

                string candidate = home.TrimEnd('\\', '/') + @"\ADE\ade-settings.vault";
                if (!VaultPathAllowed(candidate))
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync(
                        "Invalid location",
                        "The home folder found for \"" + username + "\" is not an allowed vault location:\n"
                        + candidate);
                    return null;
                }
                if (!await VaultFileExistsAsync(candidate))
                {
                    bool retry = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                        "Vault not found",
                        "No vault file was found at:\n" + candidate + "\n\n"
                        + "Try another username?");
                    if (!retry) return null;
                    continue;
                }
                return candidate;
            }
            return null;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Query AD for the user's homeDirectory (remote home folder). Returns UNC or empty.
        /// </summary>
        private string TryGetAdHomeDirectory(string username)
        {
            username = (username ?? "").Trim();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(username)) return "";
            try
            {
                string lookup = username;
                if (username.Contains('\\'))
                    lookup = username.Split('\\').Last().Trim();
                else if (username.Contains('@'))
                    lookup = username.Split('@')[0].Trim();

                using var rootDse = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://RootDSE");
                string nc = rootDse.Properties["defaultNamingContext"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(nc)) return "";

                string escSam = EscapeLdap(lookup);
                string escFull = EscapeLdap(username);
                using var searcher = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nc))
                {
                    Filter = "(&(objectCategory=person)(objectClass=user)(!(objectClass=computer))"
                           + "(|(sAMAccountName=" + escSam + ")(userPrincipalName=" + escFull + ")))",
                    SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                    SizeLimit = 1,
                    PropertiesToLoad = { "homeDirectory", "sAMAccountName" }
                };
                var r = searcher.FindOne();
                if (r == null) return "";
                string home = "";
                try
                {
                    if (r.Properties.Contains("homeDirectory") && r.Properties["homeDirectory"].Count > 0)
                        home = r.Properties["homeDirectory"][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                }
                catch { }
                home = (home ?? "").Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
                return home;
            }
            catch
            {
                return "";
            }
        }

        /// <summary>First-time: choose DFS path, create PIN, onboard, write vault.</summary>
        private async Task RunFirstTimeVaultSetupAsync()
        {
            string loginUser = Environment.UserName ?? "";
            string regularUser = loginUser;
            bool looksAdmin = loginUser.IndexOf("admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                || loginUser.StartsWith("nipradmin_", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                || loginUser.StartsWith("svc_", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            if (looksAdmin)
            {
                for (int uTry = 0; uTry < 5; uTry++)
                {
                    string? asked = await PromptTextAsync(
                        "Regular username",
                        "You are signed in as admin (" + loginUser + ").\n\n"
                        + "Enter your regular (non-admin) username for the DFS home path.\n"
                        + "This is required - do not use your admin account name.",
                        "",
                        false,
                        "jdoe");
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(asked))
                    {
                        bool quit = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                            "Cancel setup?",
                            "A regular username is required for DFS UserHomes.\n\nYes = cancel\nNo = try again");
                        if (quit) return;
                        continue;
                    }
                    asked = asked.Trim();
                    if (asked.IndexOf("admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                        || asked.StartsWith("nipradmin_", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                        || string.Equals(asked, loginUser, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        await ShowMessageAsync(
                            "Use regular username",
                            "\"" + asked + "\" looks like an admin account. Enter your normal user id (e.g. jdoe).");
                        continue;
                    }
                    regularUser = asked;
                    break;
                }
                if (string.Equals(regularUser, loginUser, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Setup cancelled", "Could not determine a regular username for DFS UserHomes.");
                    return;
                }
            }

            // Prefer AD homeDirectory for the username entered (or current user)
            string adHome = await Task.Run(() => TryGetAdHomeDirectory(regularUser));
            string defaultPath;
            string pathExplain;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(adHome))
            {
                defaultPath = adHome.TrimEnd('\\', '/') + @"\ADE\ade-settings.vault";
                pathExplain = "Active Directory home folder for \"" + regularUser + "\":\n  " + adHome
                    + "\n\nVault will be stored at:\n  " + defaultPath;
            }
            else
            {
                defaultPath = @"\\internal.oln-afmc.af.mil\dfsshares\UserHomes\"
                    + regularUser + @"\ADE\ade-settings.vault";
                pathExplain = "No homeDirectory was found in AD for \"" + regularUser + "\".\n"
                    + "Using the standard DFS UserHomes path:\n  " + defaultPath;
            }

            await ShowMessageAsync(
                "Welcome to Active Directory Explorer",
                "First-time setup.\n\n"
                + "Your vault is one small encrypted file that stores your ADE settings and passwords, "
                + "locked with a PIN you choose. ADE asks for the PIN each time it starts, and the file "
                + "normally lives in your home folder so it follows you to any PC.\n\n"
                + pathExplain + "\n\n"
                + (_useCustomVaultLocation
                    ? "Custom location enabled. Warning: if you are using a virtual machine or an instant clone, the vault will not be persistent. The only way it can be persistent is if it is saved on a server or on a physical machine's C: drive."
                    : "Do NOT use C:\\Users\\... or an admin profile folder."));

            string? chosen = null;
            for (int pTry = 0; pTry < 5; pTry++)
            {
                chosen = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "New vault location",
                    "Full UNC path for the new encrypted vault file.\n"
                    + (_useCustomVaultLocation
                        ? "Folder will be created if needed. Custom location: any server share or local drive path."
                        : "Folder will be created if needed. Local admin profiles are blocked."),
                    defaultPath,
                    false,
                    defaultPath,
                    widePath: true);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(chosen))
                {
                    bool quit = await PromptYesNoAsync("Cancel setup?", "No path entered.\n\nYes = cancel\nNo = try again");
                    if (quit) return;
                    continue;
                }
                chosen = chosen.Trim();
                if (!VaultPathAllowed(chosen))
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync(
                        "Invalid location",
                        _useCustomVaultLocation
                            ? "Enter a full path (a UNC share or a local drive).\nExample:\n" + defaultPath
                            : "Local/admin profile paths are not allowed.\nExample:\n" + defaultPath);
                    chosen = null;
                    continue;
                }
                // If a vault already exists at this path, switch to unlock instead of overwrite
                if (await VaultFileExistsAsync(chosen))
                {
                    bool useExisting = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                        "Vault already exists",
                        "A vault file already exists at:\n" + chosen + "\n\n"
                        + "Yes = use it (unlock with PIN)\n"
                        + "No = choose a different path");
                    if (useExisting)
                    {
                        _vaultFilePath = chosen;
                        RememberVaultPath(chosen);
                        await UnlockExistingVaultAsync();
                        return;
                    }
                    chosen = null;
                    continue;
                }
                break;
            }
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(chosen))
            {
                await ShowMessageAsync("Setup cancelled", "No vault path was chosen.");
                return;
            }

            string? dir = Path.GetDirectoryName(chosen);
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dir))
            {
                await ShowMessageAsync("Invalid path", "Could not determine the folder for that path.");
                return;
            }
            if (!Directory.Exists(dir))
            {
                if (!await PromptYesNoAsync("Create folder?", "Folder does not exist:\n" + dir + "\n\nCreate it now?"))
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Setup cancelled", "Vault folder was not created.");
                    return;
                }
                try { Directory.CreateDirectory(dir); }
                catch (Exception dirEx)
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Folder create failed", dirEx.Message);
                    return;
                }
            }

            _vaultFilePath = chosen;
            RememberVaultPath(chosen);

            // Create PIN with retries
            string? chosenPin = null;
            for (int pinTry = 0; pinTry < 10; pinTry++)
            {
                string? pin1 = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Create PIN",
                    "Create a PIN to encrypt your vault. This PIN protects the passwords ADE saves for you.\n"
                    + "You will need this PIN each time ADE starts.\n"
                    + "Minimum 6 characters - longer is better.",
                    "",
                    true,
                    null,
                    compactPin: true);
                if (pin1 == null)
                {
                    bool abandon = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                        "Cancel setup?",
                        "No PIN was entered.\n\nYes = cancel (no vault created)\nNo = try again");
                    if (abandon) return;
                    continue;
                }
                if (pin1.Length < 6)
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("PIN too short", "Use at least 6 characters. This PIN protects the passwords saved in your vault - longer is better. Try again.");
                    continue;
                }
                string? pin2 = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Confirm PIN",
                    "Re-enter the same PIN to confirm.",
                    "",
                    true,
                    null,
                    compactPin: true);
                if (pin2 == null)
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Confirm required", "PIN confirmation was cancelled. Try again.");
                    continue;
                }
                if (!string.Equals(pin1, pin2, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("PIN mismatch", "The two PINs did not match. Enter them again.");
                    continue;
                }
                chosenPin = pin1;
                break;
            }
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(chosenPin))
            {
                await ShowMessageAsync("Setup incomplete", "A matching PIN is required. No vault was created.");
                return;
            }

            _vaultPin = chosenPin;
            _vaultUnlocked = true;

            TextBlock createStatus;
            ProgressBar createBar;
            var createDlg = ShowProgressWindow(
                "Creating vault",
                "Preparing encrypted settings file...",
                out createStatus,
                out createBar);
            try { await Task.Delay(80); createDlg.Close(); } catch { try { createDlg.Close(); } catch { } }

            try { await TryImportPortableSettingsDuringFirstRunAsync(); }
            catch (Exception impEx)
            {
                try
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync(
                        "Import skipped",
                        "Portable settings were not imported:\n" + impEx.Message
                        + "\n\nContinuing with manual setup.");
                }
                catch { }
            }

            try { await RunOnboardingWizardAsync(); }
            catch (Exception onEx)
            {
                await ShowMessageAsync(
                    "Onboarding note",
                    "Some setup steps did not complete:\n" + onEx.Message
                    + "\n\nYour PIN and vault will still be saved. Finish the rest in Settings later.");
            }

            var saveDlg = ShowProgressWindow(
                "Saving vault",
                "Encrypting and writing settings to:\n" + _vaultFilePath,
                out createStatus,
                out createBar);
            bool saved = false;
            try
            {
                await Task.Delay(50);
                await Task.Run(() => SaveAppSettings());
                saved = _lastVaultSaveVerified && await VaultFileExistsAsync(_vaultFilePath!);
                createStatus.Text = saved ? "Done (hash-verified at destination)." : "Write finished but the vault did not verify.";
                await Task.Delay(150);
                saveDlg.Close();
            }
            catch (Exception saveEx)
            {
                try { saveDlg.Close(); } catch { }
                await ShowMessageAsync("Save failed", "Could not write vault:\n" + saveEx.Message);
            }

            if (saved)
            {
                RememberVaultPath(_vaultFilePath!);
                UpdateVaultBadge();
                await ShowMessageAsync("Setup complete", "Encrypted settings saved to:\n" + _vaultFilePath);
            }
            else
            {
                await ShowMessageAsync(
                    "Vault not saved",
                    "The vault file was not created at:\n" + _vaultFilePath
                    + "\n\nCheck network path permissions and try again."
                    + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_lastVaultSaveError) ? "" : "\n\nWrite error: " + _lastVaultSaveError));
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Unlock a vault that is known to exist at _vaultFilePath (3 PIN attempts).</summary>
        private async Task UnlockExistingVaultAsync()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_vaultFilePath))
                return;

            // Final existence check (path already validated by caller when possible)
            if (!await VaultFileExistsAsync(_vaultFilePath))
            {
                await ShowMessageAsync(
                    "Vault missing",
                    "Vault file is no longer reachable:\n" + _vaultFilePath
                    + "\n\nYou can locate another vault or create a new one.");
                ClearRememberedVaultPath();
                // Return to caller; Setup Menu loop will run
                return;
            }

            bool unlocked = false;
            for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++)
            {
                int left = 3 - attempt;
                string? pin = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Unlock vault",
                    "Enter your PIN to unlock:\n" + _vaultFilePath
                    + "\n\nAttempts remaining: " + left,
                    "",
                    true,
                    null,
                    compactPin: true);
                if (pin == null)
                {
                    // Cancel → return to Setup Menu (do not force exit)
                    await ShowMessageAsync(
                        "Unlock cancelled",
                        "Vault was not unlocked.\n"
                        + "You can try again, locate a different vault, create a new one, or skip.");
                    return;
                }

                TextBlock loadStatus;
                ProgressBar loadBar;
                var loadDlg = ShowProgressWindow(
                    "Loading vault",
                    "Decrypting settings file...",
                    out loadStatus,
                    out loadBar);
                try
                {
                    await Task.Delay(80);
                    bool ok = await Task.Run(() => TryLoadVaultWithPin(_vaultFilePath!, pin));
                    if (ok)
                    {
                        loadStatus.Text = "Vault unlocked. Checking credentials...";
                        await Task.Delay(120);
                        try { loadDlg.Close(); } catch { }
                        unlocked = true;
                        UpdateVaultBadge();
                        ApplyPreferredTheme();
                        UpdateThemeButtonIcon();
                        await ValidateLoadedCredentialsAsync();
                        break;
                    }
                    loadStatus.Text = string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastVaultUnlockError) ? "Incorrect PIN." : "Vault file problem.";
                    await Task.Delay(200);
                    try { loadDlg.Close(); } catch { }
                }
                catch
                {
                    try { loadDlg.Close(); } catch { }
                }

                if (!unlocked && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastVaultUnlockError))
                {
                    // Not a PIN problem: the file itself is damaged / not a vault.
                    // Do not burn PIN attempts on it; back to the Setup Menu.
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Vault file problem", _lastVaultUnlockError);
                    return;
                }

                if (attempt < 2)
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync(
                        "Incorrect PIN",
                        "That PIN did not unlock the vault.\n" + (2 - attempt) + " attempt(s) remaining.");
                }
            }

            if (!unlocked)
            {
                bool recreate = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                    "Vault locked",
                    "3 incorrect PIN attempts.\n\n"
                    + "For security the vault will not be loaded.\n\n"
                    + "Yes = forget this location and open Setup Menu\n"
                    + "No = close ADE (restart later with the correct PIN)");
                if (recreate)
                {
                    ClearRememberedVaultPath();
                    _vaultPin = null;
                    _vaultUnlocked = false;
                    // Return to caller; Setup Menu loop continues
                }
                else
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Exit required", "Close Active Directory Explorer and restart when you have the correct PIN.");
                    try { Close(); } catch { }
                }
            }
        }


        /// <summary>
        /// First-time vault: optional import of portable settings JSON (no credentials).
        /// </summary>
        /// <summary>
        /// Canonical software-share path for ade-portable-settings.json on the
        /// primary site (olncifs1). On other domains the file-server hostname differs.
        /// </summary>
        private const string DefaultPortableSettingsUnc =
            @"\\olncifs1\software$\Software\Restricted\Active Directory Explorer\ade-portable-settings.json";

        /// <summary>
        /// Folders / UNC locations to check for ade-portable-settings.json.
        /// Prefer the software$ share first, then exe folder, parent, cwd, Documents\ADE_Exports.
        /// </summary>
        private static List<string> GetPortableSettingsSearchDirs()
        {
            var dirs = new List<string>();
            void AddDir(string? d)
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(d)) return;
                try
                {
                    d = d.Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
                    // Avoid Path.GetFullPath on pure UNC roots that can throw on some hosts
                    if (d.StartsWith(@"\\", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                    {
                        // keep as-is
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        d = Path.GetFullPath(d);
                    }
                }
                catch { return; }
                if (!dirs.Contains(d, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    dirs.Add(d);
            }

            // 1) Primary software$ share folder (highest priority)
            try
            {
                string uncDir = Path.GetDirectoryName(DefaultPortableSettingsUnc) ?? "";
                AddDir(uncDir);
            }
            catch { }

            // 2) Exe folder and its parent (portable / copied copies)
            try
            {
                string? exe = Environment.ProcessPath
                    ?? System.Diagnostics.Process.GetCurrentProcess().MainModule?.FileName;
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(exe))
                {
                    string? exeDir = Path.GetDirectoryName(exe);
                    AddDir(exeDir);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(exeDir))
                        AddDir(Path.GetDirectoryName(exeDir));
                }
            }
            catch { }

            // NOTE: Environment.CurrentDirectory is intentionally NOT searched. A portable
            // settings file planted in an arbitrary launch folder must not be auto-trusted:
            // it can name Horizon brokers and executable (VNC / DelProf2) paths.
            try
            {
                AddDir(Path.Combine(
                    Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments),
                    "ADE_Exports"));
            }
            catch { }

            return dirs;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Candidate full paths for ade-portable-settings.json (software$ first).
        /// </summary>
        private static List<string> GetPortableSettingsCandidatePaths()
        {
            var paths = new List<string>();
            void AddPath(string? p)
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(p)) return;
                p = p.Trim();
                if (!paths.Contains(p, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    paths.Add(p);
            }

            AddPath(DefaultPortableSettingsUnc);

            const string fileName = "ade-portable-settings.json";
            foreach (string dir in GetPortableSettingsSearchDirs())
            {
                try { AddPath(Path.Combine(dir, fileName)); } catch { }
            }
            return paths;
        }

        private static async Task<string?> FindPortableSettingsJsonNearbyAsync()
        {
            foreach (string candidate in GetPortableSettingsCandidatePaths())
            {
                try
                {
                    // UNC File.Exists can hang on unreachable servers - bound the wait
                    if (candidate.StartsWith(@"\\", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                    {
                        var t = Task.Run(() =>
                        {
                            try { return File.Exists(candidate); }
                            catch { return false; }
                        });
                        var finished = await Task.WhenAny(t, Task.Delay(8000));
                        if (finished == t && t.Result)
                            return candidate;
                    }
                    else if (File.Exists(candidate))
                    {
                        return candidate;
                    }
                }
                catch { }
            }
            return null;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Hint text when portable settings are missing: software$ path + domain note.
        /// </summary>
        private static string BuildPortableSettingsDomainHint()
        {
            string dns = "";
            try
            {
                dns = (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("USERDNSDOMAIN") ?? "").Trim();
            }
            catch { }
            string domainNote;
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dns))
            {
                domainNote =
                    "If you are on a different domain or site, the file server name is usually different "
                    + "(not olncifs1). Ask your local admin for the software$ path on this network.";
            }
            else
            {
                domainNote =
                    "Current domain: " + dns + ".\n"
                    + "If this is not the primary site, the file server is usually named differently "
                    + "(not olncifs1). Use your local software$ share or ask an admin for the path.";
            }
            return "Expected primary location:\n  " + DefaultPortableSettingsUnc + "\n\n" + domainNote;
        }

        private async Task TryImportPortableSettingsDuringFirstRunAsync()
        {
            string? foundNearby = await FindPortableSettingsJsonNearbyAsync();
            string searchHint = "";
            try
            {
                var dirs = GetPortableSettingsSearchDirs();
                if (dirs.Count > 0)
                    searchHint = "\n\nChecked:\n  " + string.Join("\n  ", dirs.Take(4));
            }
            catch { }

            string promptMsg;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(foundNearby))
            {
                promptMsg =
                    "A portable settings file was found:\n"
                    + foundNearby + "\n\n"
                    + "Import it for a quick setup?\n"
                    + "(Paths, Horizon brokers, DelProf2, theme, etc. - not admin passwords.)\n\n"
                    + "Yes = restore from this file\n"
                    + "No  = skip or choose a different file";
            }
            else
            {
                promptMsg =
                    "For a quick setup, ADE looks for:\n\n"
                    + "  ade-portable-settings.json\n\n"
                    + "Primary location (software$ share):\n"
                    + "  " + DefaultPortableSettingsUnc + "\n\n"
                    + "It is also checked next to the ADE program and in Documents\\ADE_Exports.\n"
                    + "The file restores paths, Horizon servers, DelProf2, and other non-password settings.\n"
                    + "(Admin usernames and passwords are never in this file.)\n\n"
                    + BuildPortableSettingsDomainHint()
                    + searchHint + "\n\n"
                    + "Yes = I have the file (browse / enter path)\n"
                    + "No  = configure everything manually";
            }

            bool want = await PromptYesNoAsync("Quick setup - portable settings", promptMsg);
            string defaultDir = Path.Combine(
                Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments),
                "ADE_Exports");
            string defaultPath = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(foundNearby)
                ? foundNearby!
                : DefaultPortableSettingsUnc;

            string? chosen = null;
            if (want && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(foundNearby) && File.Exists(foundNearby))
            {
                // User accepted the auto-detected file next to the program
                chosen = foundNearby;
            }
            else if (want)
            {
                // User said yes but no auto-detect (or wants to pick the path)
                chosen = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Portable settings file",
                    "Enter the full path to ade-portable-settings.json.\n"
                    + "Look in the folder where you got ADE (same folder as the program,\n"
                    + "the share you copied it from, or Documents\\ADE_Exports).",
                    defaultPath,
                    false,
                    DefaultPortableSettingsUnc,
                    widePath: true);
            }
            else if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(foundNearby))
            {
                // File was found nearby but user declined that one -- offer another path
                bool other = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                    "Different file?",
                    "Use a different ade-portable-settings.json path instead?\n\n"
                    + "Yes = enter another path\n"
                    + "No  = skip and configure manually");
                if (other)
                {
                    chosen = await PromptTextAsync(
                        "Portable settings file",
                        "Full path to ade-portable-settings.json.",
                        Path.Combine(defaultDir, "ade-portable-settings.json"),
                        false,
                        Path.Combine(defaultDir, "ade-portable-settings.json"),
                        widePath: true);
                }
            }
            else
            {
                // No file found, user said no → manual setup
                return;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(chosen))
            {
                await ShowMessageAsync("Import skipped", "No file path was entered. Continuing with manual setup.");
                return;
            }
            chosen = chosen.Trim();
            if (!File.Exists(chosen))
            {
                bool retry = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                    "File not found",
                    "Could not find:\n" + chosen + "\n\n"
                    + BuildPortableSettingsDomainHint() + "\n\n"
                    + "Yes = try another path\n"
                    + "No  = continue with manual setup");
                if (!retry)
                    return;
                chosen = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Portable settings file",
                    "Full path to ade-portable-settings.json.",
                    defaultPath,
                    false,
                    defaultPath,
                    widePath: true);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(chosen) || !File.Exists(chosen.Trim()))
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Import skipped", "File not found. Continuing with manual setup.");
                    return;
                }
                chosen = chosen.Trim();
            }

            try
            {
                string json = File.ReadAllText(chosen);
                var portable = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<PortableAppSettings>(json);
                if (portable == null)
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Import skipped", "Could not read settings from that file. Continuing with manual setup.");
                    return;
                }
                if (!IsSupportedPortableFormat(portable))
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Import skipped",
                        "That file uses portable settings format '" + (portable.FormatVersion ?? "?")
                        + "', which this ADE build does not support (expected '1'). Continuing with manual setup.");
                    return;
                }
                // The auto-detected file is unauthenticated: if it names brokers or
                // executable paths, list them and make the user confirm explicitly
                // before anything from it is applied.
                bool autoDetected = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(foundNearby)
                    && string.Equals(chosen, foundNearby, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                if (autoDetected)
                {
                    var sensitive = new List<string>();
                    var pb = ParseBrokerList(portable.HorizonBrokers ?? "");
                    if (pb.Count > 0) sensitive.Add("Horizon brokers:\n  " + string.Join("\n  ", pb));
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.VncViewerPath)) sensitive.Add("VNC viewer path:\n  " + portable.VncViewerPath);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.DelProf2Path)) sensitive.Add("DelProf2 path:\n  " + portable.DelProf2Path);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.ProfileCifsServer)) sensitive.Add("Profile file server:\n  " + portable.ProfileCifsServer);
                    if (sensitive.Count > 0)
                    {
                        bool okImport = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                            "Confirm portable settings",
                            "The auto-found file sets these destinations and program paths:\n\n"
                            + string.Join("\n", sensitive)
                            + "\n\nImport them?\n(No = skip the file and configure manually)");
                        if (!okImport) return;
                    }
                }
                ApplyPortableSettingsObject(portable);
                // Remember exactly which values the restored file supplied, so the
                // onboarding wizard that follows does not prompt for them again.
                // A field left empty in the file stays false and is still prompted for.
                _restoredHorizonBrokers = ParseBrokerList(portable.HorizonBrokers ?? "").Count > 0;
                _restoredHorizonDomain = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.HorizonDomain);
                _restoredLoginLogFolder = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.LoginLogFolder);
                _restoredComputerLogFolder = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.ComputerLogFolder);
                ApplyPreferredTheme();
                UpdateThemeButtonIcon();

                var summary = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
                summary.AppendLine("Restored portable settings from:");
                summary.AppendLine(chosen);
                summary.AppendLine();
                if (_horizonBrokers.Count > 0)
                    summary.AppendLine("Horizon brokers: " + _horizonBrokers.Count + " server(s)");
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_loginLogFolder))
                    summary.AppendLine("User login logs: " + _loginLogFolder);
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_computerLogFolder))
                    summary.AppendLine("Computer logs: " + _computerLogFolder);
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_delProf2Path))
                    summary.AppendLine("DelProf2: " + _delProf2Path);
                summary.AppendLine();
                if (_restoredHorizonBrokers || _restoredHorizonDomain
                    || _restoredLoginLogFolder || _restoredComputerLogFolder)
                    summary.AppendLine("Values restored above will not be asked for again in the next steps.");
                summary.AppendLine("Next you will enter admin username (short name) and password.");
                summary.AppendLine("Those are never stored in the portable JSON file.");

                await ShowMessageAsync("Quick setup restored", summary.ToString());
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                await ShowMessageAsync(
                    "Import failed",
                    ex.Message + "\n\nContinuing with manual setup.");
            }
        }

        private async Task RunOnboardingWizardAsync()
        {
            string dns = ResolveDnsDomain();
            string shortDefault = GetAdminUsernamePromptDefault();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(shortDefault))
                shortDefault = "nipradmin_bcs";

            await ShowMessageAsync(
                "Admin credentials",
                "One admin account is used for RDP/shadow and Horizon.\n\n"
                + "Enter only the admin username (for example nipradmin_bcs).\n"
                + "ADE will store it as username@" + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dns) ? "your-dns-domain" : dns) + "\n"
                + "using the domain detected on this network (not hardcoded).");

            string? adminUser = null;
            string? adminPass = null;
            while (true)
            {
                adminUser = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Admin username",
                    "Admin account used for RDP/shadow and Horizon.\n"
                    + "Enter the short username only (sAMAccountName).\n"
                    + "Example: nipradmin_bcs\n\n"
                    + "Domain is detected automatically and appended as @"
                    + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dns) ? "dns-domain" : dns) + ".",
                    shortDefault,
                    false,
                    shortDefault);
                if (adminUser == null)
                    return;

                string candidateUpn = BuildAdminUpnFromUsername(adminUser);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(candidateUpn))
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Invalid username", "Enter a non-empty admin username.");
                    continue;
                }

                if (!TryResolveAdminUsername(candidateUpn, out string resolvedUpn, out string resolveMsg))
                {
                    // Fall back: still try the built UPN if AD lookup failed for other reasons
                    resolvedUpn = candidateUpn;
                    if (!TryResolveAdminUsername(adminUser.Trim(), out string resolved2, out string resolveMsg2))
                    {
                        await ShowMessageAsync(
                            "Invalid username",
                            resolveMsg + "\n\nEnter a valid Active Directory admin username (short name).");
                        continue;
                    }
                    resolvedUpn = resolved2;
                    resolveMsg = resolveMsg2;
                }

                // Prefer domain-auto UPN form for storage (sam@currentDns)
                string storeUpn = BuildAdminUpnFromUsername(resolvedUpn);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(storeUpn))
                    storeUpn = resolvedUpn;

                adminPass = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Admin password",
                    "Password for " + storeUpn + "\n(Same password for RDP and Horizon.)\n\n"
                    + resolveMsg,
                    "",
                    true,
                    null,
                    compactPin: true);
                if (adminPass == null)
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Password required", "A password is required for " + storeUpn + ".");
                    continue;
                }
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(adminPass))
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync("Invalid password", "Password cannot be empty. Enter a valid password.");
                    continue;
                }

                if (TestLdapCredentials(storeUpn, adminPass, out string msg)
                    || TestLdapCredentials(resolvedUpn, adminPass, out msg))
                {
                    _rdpUsername = storeUpn;
                    _rdpPassword = adminPass;
                    _horizonUsername = storeUpn;
                    _horizonPassword = adminPass;
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_horizonDomain))
                        _horizonDomain = Environment.UserDomainName;
                    await ShowMessageAsync(
                        "Credentials OK",
                        "Stored as: " + storeUpn + "\n" + msg
                        + "\n\nThese will be used for both RDP and Horizon.");
                    break;
                }

                await ShowMessageAsync(
                    "Invalid username or password",
                    msg + "\n\nEnter a valid username and password to continue vault setup.");
            }

            // Horizon broker(s): must resolve in DNS and be online before continuing.
            // Skipped entirely when brokers came from a restored settings file: the
            // restored list is used as-is, and cancelling this prompt can no longer
            // clear it via the old Skip Horizon path.
            if (!_restoredHorizonBrokers)
            {
            while (true)
            {
                string? brokers = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Horizon Connection Servers",
                    "Enter at least one Horizon Connection Server hostname (FQDN).\n"
                    + "One per line or comma-separated.\n\n"
                    + "Each name must resolve in DNS and be reachable (TCP 443 or 8443).\n"
                    + "Invalid or offline servers will not be accepted.",
                    string.Join("\n", _horizonBrokers),
                    false,
                    "olnvdi.internal.oln-afmc.af.mil",
                    widePath: true);

                if (brokers == null)
                {
                    bool skip = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                        "Skip Horizon?",
                        "No Horizon Connection Server was entered.\n\n"
                        + "Yes = skip Horizon setup for now (you can add brokers later in Settings)\n"
                        + "No = enter a valid broker hostname");
                    if (skip)
                    {
                        _horizonBrokers = new List<string>();
                        break;
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                var parsed = ParseBrokerList(brokers);
                if (parsed.Count == 0)
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync(
                        "Invalid server name",
                        "Type a valid Horizon Connection Server FQDN (for example olnvdi.internal.oln-afmc.af.mil).");
                    continue;
                }

                TextBlock valStatus;
                ProgressBar valBar;
                var valDlg = ShowProgressWindow(
                    "Checking Horizon brokers",
                    "Validating DNS and connectivity...",
                    out valStatus,
                    out valBar);
                var okHosts = new List<string>();
                var failNotes = new List<string>();
                try
                {
                    foreach (string host in parsed)
                    {
                        valStatus.Text = "Checking " + host + "...";
                        await Task.Delay(30);
                        var (ok, detail) = await ValidateHorizonBrokerHostAsync(host);
                        if (ok)
                        {
                            okHosts.Add(host);
                            valStatus.Text = host + ": " + detail;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            failNotes.Add(host + "  -  " + detail);
                            valStatus.Text = host + ": failed";
                        }
                        await Task.Delay(80);
                    }
                }
                finally
                {
                    try { valDlg.Close(); } catch { }
                }

                if (okHosts.Count == 0)
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync(
                        "Enter a valid Horizon server",
                        "None of the names could be validated.\n\n"
                        + string.Join("\n", failNotes)
                        + "\n\nEnter a hostname that resolves in DNS and is online.");
                    continue;
                }

                if (failNotes.Count > 0)
                {
                    bool useOk = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                        "Some brokers failed",
                        "These could not be validated:\n"
                        + string.Join("\n", failNotes)
                        + "\n\nValid:\n"
                        + string.Join("\n", okHosts)
                        + "\n\nYes = continue with valid servers only\n"
                        + "No = re-enter hostnames");
                    if (!useOk) continue;
                }

                _horizonBrokers = okHosts;
                await ShowMessageAsync(
                    "Horizon server OK",
                    "Accepted:\n" + string.Join("\n", okHosts));
                break;
            }
            }
            else
            {
                // Restored brokers are untrusted input too (the portable file is
                // unauthenticated): validate them exactly like typed brokers and
                // drop any that fail, so a planted file cannot point ADE - and the
                // admin credential test that follows - at a fake broker.
                var restoredOk = new List<string>();
                var restoredFail = new List<string>();
                foreach (string host in _horizonBrokers.ToList())
                {
                    var (ok, detail) = await ValidateHorizonBrokerHostAsync(host);
                    if (ok) restoredOk.Add(host);
                    else restoredFail.Add(host + "  -  " + detail);
                }
                if (restoredFail.Count > 0)
                {
                    await ShowMessageAsync(
                        "Restored brokers checked",
                        "These restored Horizon servers failed validation and were removed:\n"
                        + string.Join("\n", restoredFail)
                        + (restoredOk.Count > 0
                            ? "\n\nContinuing with:\n" + string.Join("\n", restoredOk)
                            : "\n\nNo valid brokers remain - Horizon features stay unavailable until brokers are fixed in Settings."));
                }
                _horizonBrokers = restoredOk;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_horizonDomain))
                _horizonDomain = Environment.UserDomainName;

            // Optional: allow short domain override once (not username/password again).
            // Skipped when the domain came from a restored settings file.
            if (!_restoredHorizonDomain)
            {
                string? hzDomain = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Horizon domain",
                    "Short NetBIOS domain name (not the full DNS suffix).",
                    _horizonDomain,
                    false,
                    Environment.UserDomainName);
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(hzDomain))
                    _horizonDomain = hzDomain.Trim();
            }

            if (_horizonBrokers.Count > 0)
            {
                // No Horizon module step: the embedded Horizon engine talks to the
                // Connection Servers directly over the REST API (pure C#).

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_horizonUsername)
                    && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_horizonPassword))
                {
                    var statusLines = new List<string>();
                    TextBlock hzStatus;
                    ProgressBar hzBar;
                    var hzDlg = ShowProgressWindow(
                        "Testing Horizon credentials",
                        "Connecting to Horizon (REST API)...",
                        out hzStatus,
                        out hzBar);
                    bool ok = false;
                    try
                    {
                        await Task.Delay(50);
                        ok = await TestHorizonCredentialsAsync(
                            string.Join("\n", _horizonBrokers),
                            _horizonDomain,
                            _horizonUsername,
                            _horizonPassword,
                            s =>
                            {
                                statusLines.Add(s);
                                try { hzStatus.Text = s; } catch { }
                            });
                    }
                    finally
                    {
                        try { hzDlg.Close(); } catch { }
                    }
                    if (ok)
                    {
                        string brokerName = _horizonBrokers.Count > 0 ? _horizonBrokers[0] : "Horizon";
                        await ShowMessageAsync(
                            "Horizon credentials OK",
                            "Successfully connected to " + brokerName + ".\n\nCredentials are saved for session tools.");
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        string shortErr = statusLines.LastOrDefault(s =>
                            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s)
                            && (s.IndexOf("fail", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                                || s.IndexOf("error", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                                || s.IndexOf("timeout", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                                || s.IndexOf("denied", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0))
                            ?? statusLines.LastOrDefault()
                            ?? "Could not connect to Horizon.";
                        if (shortErr.Length > 220)
                            shortErr = shortErr.Substring(0, 220) + "...";
                        await ShowMessageAsync(
                            "Horizon test failed",
                            shortErr + "\n\nYou can fix this later in Settings.");
                    }
                }
            }

            // Login history folders: skipped when restored from a settings file.
            if (!_restoredLoginLogFolder)
            {
                string? logs = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Workstation activity folder",
                    "UNC or local path to user activity CSV logs.",
                    _loginLogFolder,
                    false,
                    DefaultLoginLogFolder,
                    widePath: true);
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(logs))
                    _loginLogFolder = logs.Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
            }

            if (!_restoredComputerLogFolder)
            {
                string? clogs = await PromptTextAsync(
                    "Computer logons folder",
                    "UNC or local path to computer activity CSV logs.",
                    _computerLogFolder,
                    false,
                    DefaultComputerLogFolder,
                    widePath: true);
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(clogs))
                    _computerLogFolder = clogs.Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
            }
        }

        private async Task ValidateLoadedCredentialsAsync()
        {
            // When vault is already created and unlocked: verify stored admin password still
            // works against AD. If the password changed, allow updating it in the vault and
            // re-test in a loop before Horizon testing and the rest of the app continues.

            // ----- 1) AD / RDP admin credentials (loop until OK or user skips) -----
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_rdpUsername) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_rdpPassword))
            {
                while (true)
                {
                    if (TestLdapCredentials(_rdpUsername, _rdpPassword, out string msg))
                        break; // password still valid

                    bool update = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                        "Admin credentials failed",
                        msg + "\n\n"
                        + "The password stored in the vault no longer works against Active Directory.\n"
                        + "(Common after a password change.)\n\n"
                        + "Yes = update username/password in the vault and re-test\n"
                        + "No = continue without fixing (RDP/Horizon may fail)");
                    if (!update)
                        break;

                    string? user = await PromptTextAsync(
                        "Admin username",
                        "Admin account used for RDP/shadow and Horizon.\n"
                        + "Enter the short username only (example: nipradmin_bcs).\n"
                        + "Domain is detected automatically on this network.",
                        GetAdminUsernamePromptDefault(),
                        false,
                        GetAdminUsernamePromptDefault());
                    if (user == null)
                    {
                        bool again = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                            "Update cancelled",
                            "No username was entered.\n\nYes = try updating again\nNo = continue without fixing");
                        if (!again) break;
                        continue;
                    }

                    string storeUpn = BuildAdminUpnFromUsername(user);
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(storeUpn))
                    {
                        await ShowMessageAsync("Invalid username", "Enter a non-empty admin username.");
                        continue;
                    }
                    string resolveMsg = "Will use: " + storeUpn;
                    if (TryResolveAdminUsername(storeUpn, out string resolvedUpn, out string resolveMsg2))
                    {
                        storeUpn = BuildAdminUpnFromUsername(resolvedUpn);
                        resolveMsg = resolveMsg2;
                    }

                    string? pass = await PromptTextAsync(
                        "Admin password",
                        "Password for " + storeUpn + "\n"
                        + "(Same password is used for RDP and Horizon.)\n\n"
                        + resolveMsg,
                        "",
                        true,
                        null,
                        compactPin: true);
                    if (pass == null)
                    {
                        bool again = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                            "Update cancelled",
                            "No password was entered.\n\nYes = try updating again\nNo = continue without fixing");
                        if (!again) break;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(pass))
                    {
                        await ShowMessageAsync("Invalid password", "Password cannot be empty. Enter a valid password.");
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (TestLdapCredentials(storeUpn, pass, out string msg2))
                    {
                        _rdpUsername = storeUpn;
                        _rdpPassword = pass;
                        // Keep Horizon in sync - same admin for both
                        _horizonUsername = storeUpn;
                        _horizonPassword = pass;
                        try { SaveAppSettings(); } catch { }
                        await ShowMessageAsync(
                            "Credentials updated",
                            "Stored as: " + storeUpn + "\n" + msg2
                            + "\n\nSaved to vault. Horizon will be tested next with the new password.");
                        break; // success - exit AD loop and proceed to Horizon
                    }

                    await ShowMessageAsync(
                        "Still failing",
                        msg2 + "\n\nCheck the password and try again, or skip.");
                    // loop continues → re-prompt and re-test
                }
            }

            // ----- 2) Horizon credentials (loop until OK or user skips) -----
            if (_horizonBrokers.Count > 0
                && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_horizonUsername)
                && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_horizonPassword))
            {
                while (true)
                {
                    var lines = new List<string>();
                    TextBlock vStatus;
                    ProgressBar vBar;
                    var vDlg = ShowProgressWindow(
                        "Testing Horizon credentials",
                        "Connecting to Horizon (REST API)...",
                        out vStatus,
                        out vBar);
                    bool ok = false;
                    try
                    {
                        await Task.Delay(50);
                        ok = await TestHorizonCredentialsAsync(
                            string.Join("\n", _horizonBrokers),
                            _horizonDomain,
                            _horizonUsername,
                            _horizonPassword,
                            s =>
                            {
                                lines.Add(s);
                                try { vStatus.Text = s; } catch { }
                            });
                    }
                    finally
                    {
                        try { vDlg.Close(); } catch { }
                    }

                    if (ok)
                        break; // Horizon OK  -  continue loading the rest of the program

                    string failDetail = string.Join("\n", lines);
                    if (failDetail.Length > 400)
                        failDetail = failDetail.Substring(0, 400) + "...";

                    bool update = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                        "Horizon credentials failed",
                        failDetail + "\n\n"
                        + "Password may have changed, or the broker is unreachable.\n\n"
                        + "Yes = update Horizon credentials in the vault and re-test\n"
                        + "No = continue without fixing");
                    if (!update)
                        break;

                    string? u = await PromptTextAsync(
                        "Horizon username",
                        "Admin account for Horizon.\n"
                        + "Enter the short username only (example: nipradmin_bcs).\n"
                        + "Domain is detected automatically on this network.",
                        GetAdminUsernamePromptDefault(),
                        false,
                        GetAdminUsernamePromptDefault());
                    if (u == null)
                    {
                        bool again = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                            "Update cancelled",
                            "No username entered.\n\nYes = try again\nNo = continue without fixing");
                        if (!again) break;
                        continue;
                    }

                    string horizonUser = BuildAdminUpnFromUsername(u);
                    string hzResolveMsg = "Will use: " + horizonUser;
                    if (TryResolveAdminUsername(horizonUser, out string resolvedHz, out string hzMsg2))
                    {
                        horizonUser = BuildAdminUpnFromUsername(resolvedHz);
                        hzResolveMsg = hzMsg2;
                    }

                    string? p = await PromptTextAsync(
                        "Horizon password",
                        "Password for " + horizonUser
                        + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(hzResolveMsg) ? "" : "\n\n" + hzResolveMsg),
                        "",
                        true,
                        null,
                        compactPin: true);
                    if (p == null)
                    {
                        bool again = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                            "Update cancelled",
                            "No password entered.\n\nYes = try again\nNo = continue without fixing");
                        if (!again) break;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(p))
                    {
                        await ShowMessageAsync("Invalid password", "Password cannot be empty.");
                        continue;
                    }

                    // Apply candidate credentials, save vault, then re-test on next loop iteration
                    _horizonUsername = horizonUser;
                    _horizonPassword = p;
                    // Sync RDP when the same password also authenticates to AD
                    if (TestLdapCredentials(horizonUser, p, out _))
                    {
                        _rdpUsername = horizonUser;
                        _rdpPassword = p;
                    }
                    try { SaveAppSettings(); } catch { }
                    // loop continues → re-run Horizon test with the new password
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Header badge: VAULT LOADED (green) or VAULT NOT LOADED (yellow/orange).</summary>
        private void UpdateVaultBadge()
        {
            try
            {
                var badge = this.FindControl<Border>("VaultBadge");
                var text = this.FindControl<TextBlock>("VaultBadgeText");
                if (badge == null || text == null) return;

                bool loaded = _vaultUnlocked
                    && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_vaultFilePath)
                    && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_vaultPin);

                badge.Classes.Clear();
                if (loaded)
                {
                    text.Text = "VAULT LOADED";
                    badge.Classes.Add("statusOk");
                    ToolTip.SetTip(badge,
                        "VAULT LOADED - encrypted settings are unlocked for this session.\n\n"
                        + "What this means:\n"
                        + "  - Admin credentials for RDP / shadow sessions are available\n"
                        + "  - Horizon Connection Server login can run without re-entering passwords\n"
                        + "  - Saved paths (login logs, computer logs, profile share) are applied\n"
                        + "  - Settings stay encrypted on disk under your DFS user home\n\n"
                        + "You can change any of these in Settings; changes are written back to the vault "
                        + "when you save (while the vault is unlocked).\n\n"
                        + "Vault file:\n" + (_vaultFilePath ?? "(unknown)"));
                }
                else
                {
                    text.Text = "VAULT NOT LOADED";
                    badge.Classes.Add("statusWarn");
                    string tip =
                        "VAULT NOT LOADED - encrypted settings were not unlocked.\n\n"
                        + "What this means:\n"
                        + "  - RDP and shadow may not work as intended until you enter admin credentials "
                        + "manually in Settings (or unlock/create a vault)\n"
                        + "  - Horizon session lookup / Horizon login tests will fail or prompt until "
                        + "Horizon credentials are set in Settings\n"
                        + "  - Saved paths and other stored config are not available this session\n\n"
                        + "To fix:\n"
                        + "  - Restart ADE and unlock with your PIN, or complete first-time setup\n"
                        + "  - Or open Settings and configure RDP / Horizon / paths manually for this session\n"
                        + "  - Settings > Dev > Erase all and restart clears a bad vault and starts over\n\n";
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_vaultFilePath))
                        tip += "Last known vault path:\n" + _vaultFilePath;
                    else
                        tip += "No vault path is remembered on this PC yet.";
                    ToolTip.SetTip(badge, tip);
                }

            }
            catch { }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Easter egg for Settings > About: an animated Nyan Cat drawn from
        /// plain Border "pixels" (rainbow trail, poptart cat, twinkling
        /// stars). No image assets, so the single-file build stays
        /// self-contained. The timer runs only while the panel is on screen.
        /// </summary>
        private Control BuildNyanPanel()
        {
            var host = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8 };
            var canvas = new Canvas
            {
                Width = 360,
                Height = 150,
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0, 51, 102)),
                ClipToBounds = true,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left
            };
            host.Children.Add(canvas);

            Border Pix(double w, double h, uint argb, double x, double y, Canvas parent)
            {
                var b = new Border { Width = w, Height = h, Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromUInt32(argb)) };
                Canvas.SetLeft(b, x);
                Canvas.SetTop(b, y);
                parent.Children.Add(b);
                return b;
            }

            // Stars (twinkle by fading in and out)
            var stars = new List<Border>();
            var starPos = new[] { (18.0, 18.0), (70.0, 96.0), (120.0, 26.0), (200.0, 110.0), (300.0, 20.0), (330.0, 120.0), (40.0, 128.0), (250.0, 130.0), (160.0, 8.0), (320.0, 66.0) };
            foreach (var (sx, sy) in starPos)
                stars.Add(Pix(5, 5, 0xFFFFFFFF, sx, sy, canvas));

            // Rainbow trail: six stripes that ripple as the cat runs
            uint[] rainbow = { 0xFFFF0000, 0xFFFF8000, 0xFFFFFF00, 0xFF00E000, 0xFF0090FF, 0xFF9000FF };
            var stripes = new List<Border>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
                stripes.Add(Pix(150, 10, rainbow[i], 0, 45 + i * 10, canvas));

            // The cat, on its own canvas so it can bob as one piece
            var cat = new Canvas { Width = 200, Height = 115 };
            Canvas.SetLeft(cat, 145);
            Canvas.SetTop(cat, 22);
            canvas.Children.Add(cat);
            const uint Fur = 0xFF999999;
            const uint Crust = 0xFFF0C08A;
            const uint Frost = 0xFFFF9DE2;
            var tail = Pix(26, 10, Fur, 0, 22, cat);
            var leg1 = Pix(10, 18, Fur, 42, 84, cat);
            var leg2 = Pix(10, 18, Fur, 74, 84, cat);
            var leg3 = Pix(10, 18, Fur, 108, 84, cat);
            var leg4 = Pix(10, 18, Fur, 140, 84, cat);
            Pix(110, 66, Crust, 30, 20, cat);          // poptart crust
            Pix(98, 54, Frost, 36, 26, cat);          // frosting
            uint[] sprinkles = { 0xFFFF5D8F, 0xFFFDE74C, 0xFF6BF178, 0xFF4CC9F0 };
            var sprinklePos = new[] { (46.0, 34.0), (70.0, 52.0), (96.0, 36.0), (118.0, 58.0), (58.0, 66.0), (104.0, 72.0), (82.0, 30.0), (126.0, 42.0) };
            for (int i = 0; i < sprinklePos.Length; i++)
                Pix(7, 4, sprinkles[i % sprinkles.Length], sprinklePos[i].Item1, sprinklePos[i].Item2, cat);
            Pix(46, 44, Fur, 132, 28, cat);            // head
            Pix(10, 14, Fur, 134, 14, cat);           // ear
            Pix(10, 14, Fur, 164, 14, cat);           // ear
            Pix(5, 8, 0xFF1A1A1A, 146, 44, cat);      // eye
            Pix(5, 8, 0xFF1A1A1A, 164, 44, cat);      // eye
            Pix(10, 5, 0xFFFFB3C6, 138, 58, cat);     // cheek
            Pix(10, 5, 0xFFFFB3C6, 168, 58, cat);     // cheek
            Pix(8, 3, 0xFF1A1A1A, 155, 60, cat);      // mouth

            int tick = 0;
            var timer = new DispatcherTimer { Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(130) };
            timer.Tick += (_, _) =>
            {
                tick++;
                Canvas.SetTop(cat, 22 + (tick % 2 == 0 ? 0 : -5));
                for (int i = 0; i < stripes.Count; i++)
                    Canvas.SetTop(stripes[i], 45 + i * 10 + ((tick + i) % 2 == 0 ? 0 : 3));
                double legSwap = tick % 2 == 0 ? 0 : 5;
                Canvas.SetLeft(leg1, 42 + legSwap);
                Canvas.SetLeft(leg2, 74 - legSwap);
                Canvas.SetLeft(leg3, 108 + legSwap);
                Canvas.SetLeft(leg4, 140 - legSwap);
                Canvas.SetTop(tail, 22 + (tick % 2 == 0 ? 0 : 4));
                for (int i = 0; i < stars.Count; i++)
                    stars[i].Opacity = (tick + i) % 3 == 0 ? 0.25 : 1.0;
            };
            host.AttachedToVisualTree += (_, _) => timer.Start();
            host.DetachedFromVisualTree += (_, _) => timer.Stop();

            host.Children.Add(new TextBlock
            {
                Text = "Credits: created by Brandon Schreiber and AI.",
                FontSize = 13,
