                FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap
            });
            host.Children.Add(new TextBlock
            {
                Text = "No domain controllers were harmed in the making of this easter egg.",
                FontSize = 11,
                Opacity = 0.7,
                TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap
            });
            return host;
        }

        private void OnOpenSettings(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var dlg = new Window
            {
                Title = "Settings",
                Width = 780,
                Height = 640,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                CanResize = true,
                MinWidth = 680,
                MinHeight = 560
            };
            StyleDialogWindow(dlg);

            // Shared fields (all categories keep values when switching tabs)
            var pathBox = new TextBox { Text = _loginLogFolder, MinHeight = 32, FontSize = 13, Watermark = DefaultLoginLogFolder };
            var computerPathBox = new TextBox { Text = _computerLogFolder, MinHeight = 32, FontSize = 13, Watermark = DefaultComputerLogFolder };
            var vncBox = new TextBox { Text = _vncViewerPath, MinHeight = 32, FontSize = 13, Watermark = DefaultVncViewerPath };
            var rdpUserBox = new TextBox
            {
                Text = GetAdminUsernamePromptDefault(),
                MinHeight = 32,
                FontSize = 13,
                Watermark = "nipradmin_bcs"
            };
            var rdpPassBox = new TextBox { Text = _rdpPassword, MinHeight = 32, FontSize = 13, Watermark = "Password", PasswordChar = '\u2022' };
            var brokersBox = new TextBox
            {
                Text = string.Join(Environment.NewLine, _horizonBrokers),
                MinHeight = 72,
                AcceptsReturn = true,
                TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap,
                FontSize = 13,
                Watermark = "oln-horizon01.internal.oln-afmc.af.mil"
            };
            var domainBox = new TextBox { Text = _horizonDomain, MinHeight = 32, FontSize = 13, Watermark = "Domain" };
            var userBox = new TextBox
            {
                Text = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_horizonUsername)
                    ? GetAdminUsernamePromptDefault()
                    : ExtractSamAccountName(_horizonUsername),
                MinHeight = 32,
                FontSize = 13,
                Watermark = "nipradmin_bcs"
            };
            var passBox = new TextBox { Text = _horizonPassword, MinHeight = 32, FontSize = 13, Watermark = "Password", PasswordChar = '\u2022' };
            var cifsBox = new TextBox { Text = _profileCifsServer, MinHeight = 32, FontSize = 13, Watermark = DefaultProfileCifsServer };
            var shareBox = new TextBox { Text = _profileSharePath, MinHeight = 32, FontSize = 13, Watermark = DefaultProfileSharePath };
            var delProfBox = new TextBox { Text = _delProf2Path, MinHeight = 32, FontSize = 13, Watermark = DefaultDelProf2Path };

            TextBlock Lbl(string t) => new TextBlock { Text = t, FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, FontSize = 13, Margin = new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 2) };
            TextBlock Hint(string t) => new TextBlock { Text = t, FontSize = 11, Opacity = 0.7, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4) };

            var pageLogs = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };
            pageLogs.Children.Add(Lbl("Workstation activity folder"));
            pageLogs.Children.Add(Hint("CSV files named {username}_ActivityHistory.csv"));
            pageLogs.Children.Add(pathBox);
            pageLogs.Children.Add(Lbl("Computer logons folder"));
            pageLogs.Children.Add(Hint("CSV files named {computer}_ActivityHistory.csv"));
            pageLogs.Children.Add(computerPathBox);
            pageLogs.Children.Add(Lbl("VNC viewer (RealVNC)"));
            pageLogs.Children.Add(Hint("Full path to vncviewer.exe for WT thin clients"));
            pageLogs.Children.Add(vncBox);
            pageLogs.Children.Add(Lbl("Stale threshold (days)"));
            pageLogs.Children.Add(Hint("Users and computers with no logon for this many days count as \"stale\" everywhere in ADE: the Stale users / Stale computers counts, STALE badges in lists, and quick-delete eligibility."));
            var staleDaysBox = new TextBox
            {
                Text = _staleComputerDays.ToString(),
                MinHeight = 32,
                FontSize = 13,
                Watermark = "90",
                Width = 120,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left
            };
            pageLogs.Children.Add(staleDaysBox);

            var pageRdp = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };
            pageRdp.Children.Add(Lbl("RDP admin credentials"));
            pageRdp.Children.Add(Hint(
                "Remote Desktop / shadow (cmdkey TERMSRV). Enter short username only (example: nipradmin_bcs). "
                + "ADE stores it as username@" + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ResolveDnsDomain()) ? "detected-dns-domain" : ResolveDnsDomain()) + "."));
            var rdpGrid = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,10,*") };
            Grid.SetColumn(rdpUserBox, 0);
            Grid.SetColumn(rdpPassBox, 2);
            rdpGrid.Children.Add(rdpUserBox);
            rdpGrid.Children.Add(rdpPassBox);
            pageRdp.Children.Add(rdpGrid);

            var pageHorizon = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };
            pageHorizon.Children.Add(Lbl("Horizon Connection Servers"));
            pageHorizon.Children.Add(Hint("One hostname per line (or comma-separated)"));
            pageHorizon.Children.Add(brokersBox);
            pageHorizon.Children.Add(Lbl("Horizon credentials"));
            pageHorizon.Children.Add(Hint(
                "Domain = short NetBIOS name. Username = short admin name only (example: nipradmin_bcs); "
                + "ADE appends @" + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ResolveDnsDomain()) ? "detected-dns-domain" : ResolveDnsDomain()) + " automatically."));
            var credGrid = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,8,*,8,*") };
            Grid.SetColumn(domainBox, 0);
            Grid.SetColumn(userBox, 2);
            Grid.SetColumn(passBox, 4);
            credGrid.Children.Add(domainBox);
            credGrid.Children.Add(userBox);
            credGrid.Children.Add(passBox);
            pageHorizon.Children.Add(credGrid);
            var hvTestStatus = new TextBlock { Text = "", FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.9, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap, Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0) };
            var hvTestBtn = new Button { Content = "Test Horizon login", Classes = { "action" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, MinWidth = 160 };
            hvTestBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                hvTestBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                hvTestStatus.Text = "Testing...";
                try
                {
                    await TestHorizonCredentialsAsync(
                        brokersBox.Text ?? "",
                        domainBox.Text ?? "",
                        userBox.Text ?? "",
                        passBox.Text ?? "",
                        s => hvTestStatus.Text = s);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    hvTestStatus.Text = "Failed: " + ex.Message;
                }
                finally { hvTestBtn.IsEnabled = true; }
            };
            pageHorizon.Children.Add(hvTestBtn);
            pageHorizon.Children.Add(hvTestStatus);

            var pageProfile = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };
            pageProfile.Children.Add(Lbl("Profile CIFS server"));
            pageProfile.Children.Add(Hint("File server that hosts user profiles (e.g. olncifs1)"));
            pageProfile.Children.Add(cifsBox);
            pageProfile.Children.Add(Lbl("Profile share path"));
            pageProfile.Children.Add(Hint("Share and folder under the server (e.g. pu\\profiles). Full path becomes \\\\server\\share\\username"));
            pageProfile.Children.Add(shareBox);
            var preview = Tb("Example: \\\\" + (_profileCifsServer ?? "server") + "\\" + (_profileSharePath ?? "share") + "\\username", size: 12, opacity: 0.85, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0), fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas"));
            void UpdatePreview()
            {
                string s = (cifsBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string sh = (shareBox.Text ?? "").Trim().TrimStart('\\');
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s)) s = DefaultProfileCifsServer;
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sh)) sh = DefaultProfileSharePath;
                preview.Text = "Example: \\\\" + s + "\\" + sh + "\\username  ->  \\\\" + s + "\\" + sh + "\\username.old";
            }
            cifsBox.TextChanged += (_, _) => UpdatePreview();
            shareBox.TextChanged += (_, _) => UpdatePreview();
            UpdatePreview();
            pageProfile.Children.Add(preview);
            var profileTestStatus = new TextBlock { Text = "", FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.9, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap, Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0) };
            var profileTestBtn = new Button { Content = "Test profile share path", Classes = { "action" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, MinWidth = 180 };
            profileTestBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                profileTestBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                profileTestStatus.Text = "Testing...";
                try
                {
                    string s = (cifsBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                    string sh = (shareBox.Text ?? "").Trim().TrimStart('\\');
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s)) s = DefaultProfileCifsServer;
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sh)) sh = DefaultProfileSharePath;
                    string unc = @"\\" + s + @"\" + sh;
                    if (Directory.Exists(unc))
                        profileTestStatus.Text = "OK - reachable: " + unc;
                    else
                        profileTestStatus.Text = "Not found or no access: " + unc;
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    profileTestStatus.Text = "Failed: " + ex.Message;
                }
                finally { profileTestBtn.IsEnabled = true; }
            };
            pageProfile.Children.Add(profileTestBtn);
            pageProfile.Children.Add(profileTestStatus);

            pageProfile.Children.Add(new Border
            {
                Height = 1,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 14, 0, 10),
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(60, 128, 128, 128)),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            });
            pageProfile.Children.Add(Lbl("DelProf2 location"));
            pageProfile.Children.Add(Hint(
                "Full path to DelProf2.exe (Helge Klein). ADE copies this file to the remote PC when cleaning inactive profiles.\n"
                + "UNC or local path is allowed."));
            pageProfile.Children.Add(delProfBox);
            var delProfTestStatus = new TextBlock { Text = "", FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.9, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap, Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0) };
            var delProfTestBtn = new Button { Content = "Test DelProf2 path", Classes = { "action" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, MinWidth = 180 };
            delProfTestBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                delProfTestBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                delProfTestStatus.Text = "Checking...";
                try
                {
                    string p = (delProfBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(p)) p = DefaultDelProf2Path;
                    if (File.Exists(p))
                        delProfTestStatus.Text = "OK - found: " + p;
                    else
                        delProfTestStatus.Text = "Not found or no access: " + p;
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    delProfTestStatus.Text = "Failed: " + ex.Message;
                }
                finally { delProfTestBtn.IsEnabled = true; }
            };
            pageProfile.Children.Add(delProfTestBtn);
            pageProfile.Children.Add(delProfTestStatus);


            var pageTools = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8 };
            pageTools.Children.Add(Lbl("Export / import portable settings"));
            pageTools.Children.Add(Hint(
                "Exports non-credential settings: log folders, VNC path, Horizon brokers (any number of servers), "
                + "Horizon domain, profile share, DelProf2 path, theme, and stale thresholds.\n"
                + "Admin usernames and passwords (RDP / Horizon) are never written to the export file.\n"
                + "Import only updates non-credential values and will not overwrite stored admin usernames or passwords."));

            var exportStatus = new TextBlock
            {
                Text = "",
                FontSize = 12,
                Opacity = 0.9,
                TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 8),
                MaxHeight = 48,
                TextTrimming = TextTrimming.CharacterEllipsis
            };
            var exportBtn = new Button { Content = "Export portable settings...", Classes = { "action" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, MinWidth = 220 };
            exportBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                exportBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                exportStatus.Text = "";
                try
                {
                    string exportLogin = (pathBox.Text ?? "").Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
                    string exportComputer = (computerPathBox.Text ?? "").Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
                    string exportVnc = (vncBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                    string exportBrokers = brokersBox.Text ?? "";
                    string exportDomain = (domainBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                    string exportCifs = (cifsBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                    string exportShare = (shareBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                    string exportDelProf = (delProfBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                    int exportStale = _staleComputerDays;
                    if (int.TryParse((staleDaysBox.Text ?? "").Trim(), out int sd) && sd > 0)
                        exportStale = sd;

                    string defaultDir = Path.Combine(
                        Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments),
                        "ADE_Exports");
                    try { Directory.CreateDirectory(defaultDir); } catch { }
                    // Prefer writing a personal copy under Documents; shared canonical path is software$
                    string defaultPath = Path.Combine(defaultDir, "ade-portable-settings.json");

                    string? chosen = await PromptTextAsync(
                        "Export portable settings",
                        "Save non-credential settings to a JSON file.\n"
                        + "Admin usernames and passwords are not included.\n\n"
                        + "Shared location on primary site:\n  " + DefaultPortableSettingsUnc + "\n"
                        + "On other domains the file server name (olncifs1) is usually different.",
                        defaultPath,
                        false,
                        DefaultPortableSettingsUnc,
                        widePath: true);
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(chosen))
                    {
                        exportStatus.Text = "Export cancelled.";
                        return;
                    }
                    chosen = chosen.Trim();
                    string? parent = Path.GetDirectoryName(chosen);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(parent))
                        Directory.CreateDirectory(parent);

                    var portable = new PortableAppSettings
                    {
                        FormatVersion = "1",
                        LoginLogFolder = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(exportLogin) ? _loginLogFolder : exportLogin,
                        ComputerLogFolder = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(exportComputer) ? _computerLogFolder : exportComputer,
                        VncViewerPath = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(exportVnc) ? _vncViewerPath : exportVnc,
                        HorizonBrokers = exportBrokers,
                        HorizonDomain = exportDomain,
                        ProfileCifsServer = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(exportCifs) ? _profileCifsServer : exportCifs,
                        ProfileSharePath = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(exportShare) ? _profileSharePath : exportShare,
                        Theme = _preferredTheme,
                        StaleComputerDays = exportStale,
                        DelProf2Path = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(exportDelProf) ? _delProf2Path : exportDelProf
                    };
                    var opts = new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true };
                    File.WriteAllText(chosen, JsonSerializer.Serialize(portable, opts), new UTF8Encoding(encoderShouldEmitUTF8Identifier: true));
                    exportStatus.Text = "Exported to:\n" + chosen;
                    ShowNotification("Portable settings exported (no admin credentials).", "success");
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    exportStatus.Text = "Export failed: " + ex.Message;
                    ShowNotification("Export failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                }
                finally { exportBtn.IsEnabled = true; }
            };
            pageTools.Children.Add(exportBtn);
            pageTools.Children.Add(exportStatus);

            var importStatus = new TextBlock
            {
                Text = "",
                FontSize = 12,
                Opacity = 0.9,
                TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 8),
                MaxHeight = 48,
                TextTrimming = TextTrimming.CharacterEllipsis
            };
            var importBtn = new Button { Content = "Import portable settings...", Classes = { "action" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, MinWidth = 220 };
            importBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                importBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                importStatus.Text = "";
                try
                {
                    string defaultPath = DefaultPortableSettingsUnc;
                    string? chosen = await PromptTextAsync(
                        "Import portable settings",
                        "Load a portable settings JSON file.\n"
                        + "Admin usernames and passwords in this vault will not be changed.\n\n"
                        + "Primary site path:\n  " + DefaultPortableSettingsUnc + "\n"
                        + "On other domains the file server name (olncifs1) is usually different.",
                        defaultPath,
                        false,
                        defaultPath,
                        widePath: true);
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(chosen))
                    {
                        importStatus.Text = "Import cancelled.";
                        return;
                    }
                    chosen = chosen.Trim();
                    if (!File.Exists(chosen))
                    {
                        importStatus.Text = "File not found: " + chosen;
                        ShowNotification("Import file not found.", "error");
                        return;
                    }
                    string json = File.ReadAllText(chosen);
                    var portable = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<PortableAppSettings>(json);
                    if (portable == null)
                    {
                        importStatus.Text = "Could not read settings from file.";
                        ShowNotification("Import failed: invalid file.", "error");
                        return;
                    }
                    if (!IsSupportedPortableFormat(portable))
                    {
                        importStatus.Text = "Unsupported portable settings format: " + (portable.FormatVersion ?? "?");
                        ShowNotification("Import failed: unsupported format version.", "error");
                        return;
                    }

                    ApplyPortableSettingsObject(portable);

                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.LoginLogFolder))
                        pathBox.Text = portable.LoginLogFolder.Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.ComputerLogFolder))
                        computerPathBox.Text = portable.ComputerLogFolder.Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.VncViewerPath))
                        vncBox.Text = portable.VncViewerPath.Trim();
                    if (portable.HorizonBrokers != null)
                        brokersBox.Text = string.Join(Environment.NewLine, ParseBrokerList(portable.HorizonBrokers));
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.HorizonDomain))
                        domainBox.Text = portable.HorizonDomain.Trim();
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.ProfileCifsServer))
                        cifsBox.Text = portable.ProfileCifsServer.Trim();
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.ProfileSharePath))
                        shareBox.Text = portable.ProfileSharePath.Trim();
                    if (portable.StaleComputerDays > 0)
                        staleDaysBox.Text = portable.StaleComputerDays.ToString();
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(portable.DelProf2Path))
                        delProfBox.Text = portable.DelProf2Path.Trim();
                    try { UpdatePreview(); } catch { }

                    SaveAppSettings();
                    ApplyPreferredTheme();
                    UpdateThemeButtonIcon();

                    importStatus.Text = "Imported (admin usernames/passwords unchanged).\n" + chosen;
                    ShowNotification("Portable settings imported. Admin credentials were not overwritten.", "success");
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    importStatus.Text = "Import failed: " + ex.Message;
                    ShowNotification("Import failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                }
                finally { importBtn.IsEnabled = true; }
            };
            pageTools.Children.Add(importBtn);
            pageTools.Children.Add(importStatus);

            var pageDev = new StackPanel { Spacing = 10 };
            pageDev.Children.Add(Lbl("Developer / reset"));
            pageDev.Children.Add(Hint("Destructive actions. Use only when onboarding a new machine or clearing a bad vault."));
            var eraseBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Erase all settings & restart",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 220,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(176, 96, 96)),
                Foreground = Brushes.White,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0)
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(eraseBtn, "Delete vault, PIN, and all saved settings, then restart ADE as a new user");
            var eraseStatus = new TextBlock { Text = "", FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.9, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap, Margin = new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0) };
            pageDev.Children.Add(eraseBtn);
            pageDev.Children.Add(eraseStatus);

            var pageAbout = new StackPanel { Spacing = 10 };
            pageAbout.Children.Add(Lbl("About"));
            pageAbout.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Active Directory Explorer", FontSize = 20, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap });
            // The version looks like plain text until the mouse is over it;
            // then it shows itself as a button. Every press counts down from
            // 7 (Android developer-options style) and the last one reveals
            // the easter egg. No hint anywhere - it is found organically.
            var aboutVersionBorder = new Border
            {
                Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                BorderBrush = Brushes.Transparent,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(4),
                Padding = new Thickness(10, 5),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand)
            };
            aboutVersionBorder.Child = new TextBlock { Text = "Version 4.2.0  -  Build 2026.10  -  \"Schreiber Edition\"", FontSize = 13 };
            pageAbout.Children.Add(aboutVersionBorder);
            pageAbout.Children.Add(Hint("A single-file Active Directory tool for help desk and sysadmins: browse the tree, reset passwords, manage groups, chase Horizon sessions, and clean up stale accounts - with the safety checks left on."));
            var aboutEggStatus = new TextBlock { Text = "", FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.75, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap };
            pageAbout.Children.Add(aboutEggStatus);
            var nyanHost = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8, IsVisible = false };
            pageAbout.Children.Add(nyanHost);

            aboutVersionBorder.PointerEntered += (_, _) =>
            {
                aboutVersionBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(46, 128, 128, 128));
                aboutVersionBorder.BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(130, 128, 128, 128));
            };
            aboutVersionBorder.PointerExited += (_, _) =>
            {
                aboutVersionBorder.Background = Brushes.Transparent;
                aboutVersionBorder.BorderBrush = Brushes.Transparent;
            };

            int aboutClicks = 0;
            aboutVersionBorder.PointerPressed += (_, _) =>
            {
                aboutClicks++;
                if (nyanHost.IsVisible)
                {
                    aboutEggStatus.Text = "Already found. Nyan responsibly.";
                    return;
                }
                int remaining = 7 - aboutClicks;
                if (remaining > 0)
                {
                    aboutEggStatus.Text = remaining + " more to go...";
                    return;
                }
                aboutEggStatus.Text = "";
                nyanHost.Children.Add(BuildNyanPanel());
                nyanHost.IsVisible = true;
                ShowNotification("You found the Nyan Cat.", "info");
            };

            // Layout: left nav + content
            var contentScroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch,
                Content = pageLogs
            };
            var contentHost = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Padding = new Thickness(16),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch,
                Child = contentScroll
            };

            var navBtns = new List<Button>();
            void ShowPage(StackPanel page, Button active)
            {
                contentScroll.Content = page;
                contentScroll.Offset = new Vector(0, 0);
                foreach (var b in navBtns)
                {
                    b.Background = Brushes.Transparent;
                    b.FontWeight = FontWeight.Normal;
                }
                active.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(60, 80, 160, 255));
                active.FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold;
            }
            Button NavBtn(string label, StackPanel page)
            {
                var b = new Button
                {
                    Content = label,
                    Classes = { "action" },
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                    Padding = new Thickness(12, 8),
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4),
                    Background = Brushes.Transparent
                };
                b.Click += (_, _) => ShowPage(page, b);
                navBtns.Add(b);
                return b;
            }

            var nav = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2, Width = 150, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 12, 0) };
            nav.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Category", FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.7, Margin = new Thickness(4, 0, 0, 6) });
            var n1 = NavBtn("Logs & VNC", pageLogs);
            var n2 = NavBtn("Remote Desktop", pageRdp);
            var n3 = NavBtn("Horizon", pageHorizon);
            var n4 = NavBtn("Profile Fix", pageProfile);
            var n5 = NavBtn("Tools", pageTools);
            var n6 = NavBtn("Dev", pageDev);
            var n7 = NavBtn("About", pageAbout);
            nav.Children.Add(n1);
            nav.Children.Add(n2);
            nav.Children.Add(n3);
            nav.Children.Add(n4);
            nav.Children.Add(n5);
            nav.Children.Add(n6);
            nav.Children.Add(n7);
            ShowPage(pageLogs, n1);

            var mid = new Grid
            {
                ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("Auto,*"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            Grid.SetColumn(nav, 0);
            Grid.SetColumn(contentHost, 1);
            mid.Children.Add(nav);
            mid.Children.Add(contentHost);

            var err = new TextBlock { Text = "", Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(229, 115, 115)), FontSize = 12, IsVisible = false };

            var btnRow = new StackPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                Spacing = 10,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 16, 0, 0)
            };
            var resetBtn = new Button { Content = "Reset defaults", Classes = { "action" }, MinWidth = 120 };
            var cancelBtn = new Button { Content = "Cancel", Classes = { "action" }, MinWidth = 100 };
            var saveBtn = new Button { Content = "Save", Classes = { "action" }, MinWidth = 100 };
            resetBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                pathBox.Text = DefaultLoginLogFolder;
                computerPathBox.Text = DefaultComputerLogFolder;
                vncBox.Text = DefaultVncViewerPath;
                cifsBox.Text = DefaultProfileCifsServer;
                shareBox.Text = DefaultProfileSharePath;
                delProfBox.Text = DefaultDelProf2Path;
                staleDaysBox.Text = "90";
                UpdatePreview();
            };
            eraseBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                bool sure = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                    "Erase all settings?",
                    "This permanently deletes:\n"
                    + "  - Encrypted vault file (if path is known)\n"
                    + "  - Saved vault location\n"
                    + "  - Local settings cache\n"
                    + "  - In-memory credentials and paths\n\n"
                    + "ADE will close and reopen as a first-time user.\n\nContinue?");
                if (!sure) return;
                try
                {
                    // Delete vault file
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_vaultFilePath) && File.Exists(_vaultFilePath))
                    {
                        try { File.Delete(_vaultFilePath); } catch { }
                    }
                    // Delete remembered location
                    try
                    {
                        if (File.Exists(VaultLocationFile)) File.Delete(VaultLocationFile);
                        if (File.Exists(VaultCustomMarkerFile)) File.Delete(VaultCustomMarkerFile);
                    }
                    catch { }
                    // Delete legacy plaintext settings
                    try
                    {
                        if (File.Exists(SettingsFile)) File.Delete(SettingsFile);
                    }
                    catch { }
                    // Clear in-memory state
                    _vaultFilePath = null;
                    _vaultPin = null;
                    _vaultUnlocked = false;
                    UpdateVaultBadge();
                    _rdpUsername = "";
                    _rdpPassword = "";
                    _horizonUsername = "";
                    _horizonPassword = "";
                    _horizonDomain = Environment.UserDomainName;
                    _horizonBrokers = new List<string>();
                    _loginLogFolder = DefaultLoginLogFolder;
                    _computerLogFolder = DefaultComputerLogFolder;
                    _vncViewerPath = DefaultVncViewerPath;
                    _profileCifsServer = DefaultProfileCifsServer;
                    _profileSharePath = DefaultProfileSharePath;
                    _delProf2Path = DefaultDelProf2Path;
                    _staleComputerDays = 90;
                }
                catch { }

                try { dlg.Close(); } catch { }

                // Restart process then close this instance
                try
                {
                    string? exe = Environment.ProcessPath
                        ?? System.Diagnostics.Process.GetCurrentProcess().MainModule?.FileName;
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(exe) && File.Exists(exe))
                    {
                        System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                        {
                            FileName = exe,
                            UseShellExecute = true
                        });
                    }
                }
                catch { }
                try { Close(); } catch { }
            };
            cancelBtn.Click += (_, _) => dlg.Close();
            saveBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                string path = (pathBox.Text ?? "").Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
                string vnc = (vncBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string computerPath = (computerPathBox.Text ?? "").Trim().TrimEnd('\\', '/');
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path))
                {
                    err.Text = "Login log folder cannot be empty.";
                    err.IsVisible = true;
                    ShowPage(pageLogs, n1);
                    return;
                }
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(computerPath))
                {
                    err.Text = "Computer logons folder cannot be empty.";
                    err.IsVisible = true;
                    ShowPage(pageLogs, n1);
                    return;
                }
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(vnc))
                {
                    err.Text = "VNC viewer path cannot be empty.";
                    err.IsVisible = true;
                    ShowPage(pageLogs, n1);
                    return;
                }
                string cifs = (cifsBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string share = (shareBox.Text ?? "").Trim().TrimStart('\\');
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cifs))
                {
                    err.Text = "Profile CIFS server cannot be empty.";
                    err.IsVisible = true;
                    ShowPage(pageProfile, n4);
                    return;
                }
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(share))
                {
                    err.Text = "Profile share path cannot be empty.";
                    err.IsVisible = true;
                    ShowPage(pageProfile, n4);
                    return;
                }
                err.IsVisible = false;
                _loginLogFolder = path;
                _computerLogFolder = computerPath;
                _vncViewerPath = vnc;
                if (int.TryParse((staleDaysBox.Text ?? "").Trim(), out int staleDays) && staleDays > 0)
                    _staleComputerDays = staleDays;
                string rdpSam = (rdpUserBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                _rdpUsername = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rdpSam) ? "" : BuildAdminUpnFromUsername(rdpSam);
                _rdpPassword = rdpPassBox.Text ?? "";
                _horizonBrokers = ParseBrokerList(brokersBox.Text ?? "");
                _horizonDomain = (domainBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string hzSam = (userBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                _horizonUsername = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(hzSam) ? "" : BuildAdminUpnFromUsername(hzSam);
                _horizonPassword = passBox.Text ?? "";
                _profileCifsServer = cifs;
                _profileSharePath = share;
                string delProf = (delProfBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                _delProf2Path = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(delProf) ? DefaultDelProf2Path : delProf;
                SaveAppSettings();
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_vaultFilePath) && !_lastVaultSaveVerified)
                {
                    // The vault write failed its hash verification - never claim success.
                    err.Text = "Settings were NOT saved to the vault: " + (_lastVaultSaveError ?? "write verification failed");
                    err.IsVisible = true;
                    ShowNotification("Vault save failed - settings not saved.", "error");
                    return;
                }
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_vaultFilePath))
                    ShowNotification("Settings saved locally. Passwords are kept only for this session - they are saved permanently only inside a vault.", "info");
                else
                    ShowNotification("Settings saved to your vault (hash-verified).", "success");
                dlg.Close();
            };
            btnRow.Children.Add(resetBtn);
            btnRow.Children.Add(cancelBtn);
            btnRow.Children.Add(saveBtn);

            var root = new DockPanel { Margin = new Thickness(16) };
            var title = new TextBlock { Text = "Settings", FontSize = 18, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 10) };
            DockPanel.SetDock(title, Dock.Top);
            root.Children.Add(title);
            DockPanel.SetDock(btnRow, Dock.Bottom);
            root.Children.Add(btnRow);
            DockPanel.SetDock(err, Dock.Bottom);
            root.Children.Add(err);
            root.Children.Add(mid);
            dlg.Content = root;

            try { dlg.ShowDialog(this); }
            catch { dlg.Show(); }
        }

    }
}
'@
$mwHorizonCs = @'
// ADE - MainWindow partial class: Horizon REST engine, adapters and sessions panel.
// Split from MainWindow.axaml.cs for maintainability (2026-10-26):
// same partial class, same namespace - no behavior change.
using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.DirectoryServices;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Net.Security;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Security.Principal;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Avalonia;
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Input;
using Avalonia.Interactivity;
using Avalonia.Layout;
using Avalonia.Markup.Xaml;
using Avalonia.Media;
using Avalonia.Threading;


namespace __APPNAME__
{
    public sealed class HorizonAdUserInfo
    {
        public string Broker { get; set; } = "";
        public string Id { get; set; } = "";
        public string Username { get; set; } = "";
        public string Domain { get; set; } = "";
        public string DisplayName { get; set; } = "";
        public string FirstName { get; set; } = "";
        public string LastName { get; set; } = "";
        public string UserPrincipalName { get; set; } = "";
        public string DistinguishedName { get; set; } = "";
        public string Email { get; set; } = "";
        public string Phone { get; set; } = "";
        public string Sid { get; set; } = "";
        public Dictionary<string, string> AllProperties { get; set; } = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    }

    public sealed class HorizonSessionRecord
    {
        public string Broker { get; set; } = "";
        public string EndpointUsed { get; set; } = "";
        public string SessionId { get; set; } = "";
        public string UserId { get; set; } = "";
        public string ResolvedUserName { get; set; } = "";
        public string ResolvedDisplayName { get; set; } = "";
        public string MachineId { get; set; } = "";
        public string MachineName { get; set; } = "";
        public string MachineDnsName { get; set; } = "";
        public string MachineState { get; set; } = "";
        public string MachineOs { get; set; } = "";
        public string AgentVersion { get; set; } = "";
        public string DesktopPoolId { get; set; } = "";
        public string DesktopPoolName { get; set; } = "";
        public string DesktopPoolDisplayName { get; set; } = "";
        public string SessionState { get; set; } = "";
        public string SessionType { get; set; } = "";
        public string SessionProtocol { get; set; } = "";
        public string ClientName { get; set; } = "";
        public string ClientAddress { get; set; } = "";
        public string ClientVersion { get; set; } = "";
        public string ClientType { get; set; } = "";
        public string BrokerGatewayHost { get; set; } = "";
        public string SecurityGatewayDns { get; set; } = "";
        public string StartTimeText { get; set; } = "";
        public string DisconnectTimeText { get; set; } = "";
        public string LastSessionDurationMinutes { get; set; } = "";
        public string IdleDurationSeconds { get; set; } = "";
        public Dictionary<string, string> RawSessionProperties { get; set; } = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        public Dictionary<string, string> RawMachineProperties { get; set; } = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        public Dictionary<string, string> RawPoolProperties { get; set; } = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    }

    public sealed class BrokerQueryReport
    {
        public string Broker { get; set; } = "";
        public bool Connected { get; set; }
        public string Error { get; set; } = "";
        public string QueryMode { get; set; } = "";
        public int TotalSessionsScanned { get; set; }
        public List<HorizonAdUserInfo> MatchedUsers { get; set; } = new();
        public List<HorizonSessionRecord> MatchedSessions { get; set; } = new();
        public List<JsonElement> RawPayloads { get; set; } = new();
    }

    internal sealed class CachedBrokerSession
    {
        public HttpClient Client { get; set; } = null!;
        public string AccessToken { get; set; } = "";
        public string RefreshToken { get; set; } = "";
        public string AuthKey { get; set; } = "";
        public DateTime ExpiresUtc { get; set; }
    }

    public partial class MainWindow : Window
    {
        // Horizon REST engine state (from Horizon Inspector): persistent HttpClient +
        // Bearer token pool, learned per-broker session endpoint, pool cache, SID cache.
        private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, CachedBrokerSession> BrokerSessionPool = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, string> PreferredSessionEndpointByBroker = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, (DateTime ExpiresUtc, JsonElement Payload)> PoolCache = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, string> WindowsSidCache = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        private sealed class HorizonSessionHit
        {
            public string Broker { get; set; } = "";
            public string UserName { get; set; } = "";
            public string MachineName { get; set; } = "";
            public string DesktopName { get; set; } = "";
            public string ClientName { get; set; } = "";
            public string Protocol { get; set; } = "";
            public string SessionType { get; set; } = "";
            public string State { get; set; } = "";
            public string Error { get; set; } = "";
            /// <summary>Horizon REST session id (used by the /sessions/action endpoints).</summary>
            public string SessionId { get; set; } = "";
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Query configured Horizon Connection Servers for active sessions matching the username.
        /// Uses Horizon REST API: POST /rest/login then GET /rest/inventory/v1/sessions (and v2 fallback).
        /// </summary>
        private void RenderHorizonSessionsPanel(StackPanel panel, string samAccountName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(samAccountName)) return;

            var shell = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            shell.Children.Add(Tb("Horizon sessions", size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));
            shell.Children.Add(Tb("Active desktop sessions for " + samAccountName + " across configured brokers", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));

            var statusLine = Tb(_horizonBrokers.Count == 0
                    ? "No Horizon brokers configured. Open Settings (top-right) to add connection servers."
                    : "Querying " + _horizonBrokers.Count + " broker(s)...", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, margin: new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 2));
            shell.Children.Add(statusLine);

            var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            shell.Children.Add(list);

            var refreshBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Refresh Horizon sessions",
                Classes = { "action" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0)
            };
            shell.Children.Add(refreshBtn);

            panel.Children.Add(new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Child = shell,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Padding = new Thickness(10),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0)
            });

            void RunQuery()
            {
                list.Children.Clear();
                if (_horizonBrokers.Count == 0)
                {
                    statusLine.Text = "No Horizon brokers configured. Open Settings (top-right) to add connection servers.";
                    return;
                }
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_horizonPassword))
                {
                    statusLine.Text = "Horizon password not set. Open Settings and enter credentials used to query the brokers.";
                    return;
                }
                statusLine.Text = "Querying Horizon (REST API)...";
                string sam = samAccountName.Trim();
                var brokers = _horizonBrokers.ToList();
                // Pass username exactly as configured (UPN preferred: user@domain). Domain box is only used if username has no @ or \.
                string domain = (_horizonDomain ?? "").Trim();
                string user = (_horizonUsername ?? "").Trim();
                string pass = _horizonPassword;

                Task.Run(async () =>
                {
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        statusLine.Text = "Querying " + brokers.Count + " broker(s) in parallel (filtered by username)...";
                    });

                    var hits = new List<HorizonSessionHit>();
                    var errors = new List<string>();
                    // Query all brokers in parallel
                    var tasks = brokers.Select(async broker =>
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            return await QueryHorizonBrokerSessionsAsync(broker, domain, user, pass, sam);
                        }
                        catch (Exception ex)
                        {
                            return (Sessions: new List<HorizonSessionHit>(), Error: broker + ": " + ex.Message);
                        }
                    }).ToList();
                    var results = await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
                    bool authFail = false;
                    foreach (var found in results)
                    {
                        hits.AddRange(found.Sessions);
                        string err = found.Error ?? "";
                        string errLower = err.ToLowerInvariant();
                        // Skip pure success diagnostics (shown in status line instead)
                        bool isSuccessDiag = errLower.Contains("match(es) of");
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(err) && !isSuccessDiag)
                            errors.Add(err);
                        if (errLower.Contains("1326") || errLower.Contains("1909")
                            || errLower.Contains("password is incorrect")
                            || errLower.Contains("locked out")
                            || errLower.Contains("logonuser failed")
                            || errLower.Contains("authentication failed"))
                            authFail = true;
                    }
                    // When sessions were found, hide "0 matched" notes from other brokers
                    if (hits.Count > 0)
                        errors.RemoveAll(e => (e ?? "").IndexOf("0 matched", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0);
                    if (authFail)
                        errors.Add("Authentication failed on one or more brokers. Fix credentials in Settings if needed.");

                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        list.Children.Clear();
                        if (hits.Count == 0)
                        {
                            statusLine.Text = "No active Horizon sessions found for " + sam + ".";
                            foreach (string e in errors.Take(8))
                            {
                                list.Children.Add(Tb(e, size: 12, opacity: 0.85, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 0)));
                            }
                            if (errors.Count == 0)
                            {
                                list.Children.Add(Tb("No broker response. Check Settings (brokers + credentials) and click Refresh.", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true));
                            }
                            return;
                        }

                        // Group by machine so each VM is one sub-panel (even if reported by multiple brokers)
                        var groups = hits
                            .GroupBy(x => string.IsNullOrEmpty(x.MachineName) ? "(unknown)" : x.MachineName,
                                StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            .OrderBy(g => g.Key)
                            .ToList();
                        statusLine.Text = groups.Count + " virtual machine(s) for " + sam;

                        string samCap = sam;
                        string domainCap = domain;
                        string userCap = user;
                        string passCap = pass;

                        foreach (var g in groups)
                        {
                            var primary = g.First();
                            // Prefer a CONNECTED report for display state
                            var best = g.FirstOrDefault(x =>
                                (x.State ?? "").IndexOf("CONNECTED", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                                || (x.State ?? "").IndexOf("ACTIVE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                                ?? primary;

                            var vmPanel = new Border
                            {
                                Padding = new Thickness(12, 10),
                                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(6),
                                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                                BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(80, 128, 128, 128)),
                                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(36, 128, 128, 128)),
                                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8)
                            };
                            var body = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };

                            // Header: machine + badges
                            var top = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 10 };
                            top.Children.Add(Tb(g.Key, size: 15, weight: FontWeight.Bold, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas")));
                            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(g.Key) && g.Key != "(unknown)")
                            {
                                var copyM = new Button
                                {
                                    Content = "\U0001F4CB",
                                    Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                                    FontSize = 12,
                                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand)
                                };
                                ToolTip.SetTip(copyM, "Copy machine name");
                                string mCap = g.Key;
                                copyM.Click += async (_, _) =>
                                {
                                    try
                                    {
                                        var topLvl = TopLevel.GetTopLevel(this);
                                        if (topLvl?.Clipboard != null)
                                        {
                                            await topLvl.Clipboard.SetTextAsync(mCap);
                                            ShowNotification("Copied: " + mCap, "success");
                                        }
                                    }
                                    catch { }
                                };
                                top.Children.Add(copyM);
                                if (!g.Key.StartsWith("WT", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                {
                                    var rdp = new Button
                                    {
                                        Content = MakeMdi("mdi-remote-desktop", 16),
                                        Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                                        Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                                        BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                                        FontSize = 14,
                                        Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand)
                                    };
                                    ToolTip.SetTip(rdp, "Shadow RDP to " + g.Key);
                                    string hostCap = g.Key;
                                    rdp.Click += (_, _) => LaunchShadowRdp(hostCap, samCap);
                                    top.Children.Add(rdp);
                                }
                            }
                            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(best.State))
                            {
                                string st = best.State.ToUpperInvariant();
                                bool okState = st.Contains("ACTIVE") || st.Contains("CONNECTED");
                                top.Children.Add(MakeBadge(st, okState ? "statusOk" : "statusWarn"));
                            }
                            body.Children.Add(top);

                            // Consolidated details across brokers (no duplicate lines)
                            string protocol = g.Select(x => x.Protocol).FirstOrDefault(s => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(s)) ?? "";
                            string sessionType = g.Select(x => x.SessionType).FirstOrDefault(s => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(s)) ?? "";
                            string desktop = g.Select(x => x.DesktopName).FirstOrDefault(s => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(s)) ?? "";
                            string client = g.Select(x => x.ClientName).FirstOrDefault(s => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(s)) ?? "";
                            var brokerNames = g.Select(x => x.Broker)
                                .Where(b => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(b))
                                .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                                .OrderBy(b => b)
                                .ToList();

                            var detailRow = new StackPanel
                            {
                                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                                Spacing = 6
                            };
                            var meta = new List<string>();
                            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(desktop)) meta.Add("Pool " + desktop);
                            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(protocol)) meta.Add(protocol);
                            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sessionType)) meta.Add(sessionType);
                            if (meta.Count > 0)
                            {
                                detailRow.Children.Add(Tb(string.Join("  |  ", meta), size: 12, opacity: 0.8, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));
                            }
                            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(client))
                            {
                                detailRow.Children.Add(Tb("Client " + client, size: 12, weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas")));
                                string clientCap = client;
                                var copyClient = new Button
                                {
                                    Content = "\U0001F4CB",
                                    Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                                    FontSize = 12,
                                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand)
                                };
                                ToolTip.SetTip(copyClient, "Copy client name");
                                copyClient.Click += async (_, _) =>
                                {
                                    try
                                    {
                                        var topLvl = TopLevel.GetTopLevel(this);
                                        if (topLvl?.Clipboard != null)
                                        {
                                            await topLvl.Clipboard.SetTextAsync(clientCap);
                                            ShowNotification("Copied: " + clientCap, "success");
                                        }
                                    }
                                    catch { }
                                };
                                detailRow.Children.Add(copyClient);

                                var vncBtn = new Button
                                {
                                    Content = MakeMdi("mdi-monitor", 16),
                                    Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                                    FontSize = 14,
                                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand)
                                };
                                ToolTip.SetTip(vncBtn, "VNC to client " + clientCap);
                                vncBtn.Click += (_, _) => LaunchVnc(clientCap);
                                detailRow.Children.Add(vncBtn);
                            }
                            if (detailRow.Children.Count > 0)
                                body.Children.Add(detailRow);

                            if (brokerNames.Count > 0)
                            {
                                body.Children.Add(Tb("Brokers: " + string.Join("  |  ", brokerNames), size: 11, opacity: 0.65, wrap: true));
                            }

                            // Actions - use first broker that reported this machine
                            var actions = new StackPanel
                            {
                                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                                Spacing = 6,
                                Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0)
                            };
                            string machineCap = g.Key;
                            string brokerCap = best.Broker;
                            string sessionIdCap = best.SessionId ?? "";
                            string poolCap = best.DesktopName ?? "";
                            var brokersForVm = g.Select(x => x.Broker).Where(b => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(b))
                                .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();

                            Button ActBtn(string label, string tip, string action, string? confirm)
                            {
                                var b = new Button
                                {
                                    Content = label,
                                    Classes = { "action" },
                                    Padding = new Thickness(10, 4),
                                    FontSize = 12,
                                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand)
                                };
                                ToolTip.SetTip(b, tip);
                                b.Click += async (_, _) =>
                                {
                                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(confirm))
                                    {
                                        string confirmMsg = confirm.Replace("{machine}", machineCap).Replace("{user}", samCap)
                                            + "\n\nBroker: " + brokerCap
                                            + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(poolCap) ? "" : "\nPool: " + poolCap);
                                        bool yes = await ConfirmDialogAsync(
                                            "Confirm " + label,
                                            confirmMsg);
                                        if (!yes) return;
                                    }
                                    b.IsEnabled = false;
                                    ShowNotification(label + " -> " + machineCap + "...", "info");

                                    // Act on the exact session that was displayed and confirmed
                                    // (its broker + REST session id) - never re-resolve by machine
                                    // name at click time, and never hop brokers silently.
                                    string result;
                                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sessionIdCap))
                                    {
                                        result = await InvokeHorizonSessionActionByIdAsync(brokerCap, sessionIdCap, action);
                                    }
                                    else
                                    {
                                        // Defensive fallback (rendered hits normally always carry a session id)
                                        result = "";
                                        foreach (string br in brokersForVm)
                                        {
                                            result = await InvokeHorizonSessionActionAsync(br, machineCap, samCap, action);
                                            if (result.StartsWith("OK", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                                break;
                                        }
                                    }

                                    if (!result.StartsWith("OK", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                    {
                                        b.IsEnabled = true;
                                        ShowNotification(label + " failed: " + result, "error");
                                        return;
                                    }

                                    // Verify: wait for Horizon to settle, then re-query brokers
                                    ShowNotification("Verifying " + label + " on " + machineCap + "...", "info");
                                    await Task.Delay(6000);
                                    var verifyHits = new List<HorizonSessionHit>();
                                    var verifyBrokers = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sessionIdCap)
                                        ? new List<string> { brokerCap }
                                        : brokersForVm;
                                    foreach (string br in verifyBrokers)
                                    {
                                        try
                                        {
                                            var v = await QueryHorizonBrokerSessionsAsync(br, domainCap, userCap, passCap, samCap);
                                            verifyHits.AddRange(v.Sessions.Where(s =>
                                                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sessionIdCap)
                                                    ? string.Equals(s.SessionId, sessionIdCap, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                                                    : string.Equals(s.MachineName, machineCap, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)));
                                        }
                                        catch { }
                                    }

                                    string verifyMsg;
                                    bool verified;
                                    string actionU = action.ToUpperInvariant();
                                    if (actionU == "LOGOFF" || actionU == "LOGOFFFORCED")
                                    {
                                        // Success if session gone from all brokers
                                        verified = verifyHits.Count == 0;
                                        verifyMsg = verified
                                            ? label + " verified: " + machineCap + " session is gone."
                                            : label + " sent, but session still present on "
                                                + string.Join(", ", verifyHits.Select(v => v.Broker + " (" + v.State + ")").Distinct());
                                    }
                                    else if (actionU == "DISCONNECT")
                                    {
                                        verified = verifyHits.Count == 0 || verifyHits.All(v =>
                                            (v.State ?? "").IndexOf("DISCONNECT", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0);
                                        verifyMsg = verified
                                            ? label + " verified: " + machineCap + " is disconnected (or gone)."
                                            : label + " sent, current state: "
                                                + string.Join(", ", verifyHits.Select(v => v.State).Distinct());
                                    }
                                    else // Reset
                                    {
                                        verified = verifyHits.Count == 0 || verifyHits.All(v =>
                                            (v.State ?? "").IndexOf("DISCONNECT", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                                            || (v.State ?? "").IndexOf("AGENT", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0);
                                        verifyMsg = verified
                                            ? label + " verified: " + machineCap + " is resetting / disconnected."
                                            : label + " sent, current state: "
                                                + (verifyHits.Count == 0 ? "session gone" : string.Join(", ", verifyHits.Select(v => v.State).Distinct()));
                                    }

                                    b.IsEnabled = true;
                                    ShowNotification(verifyMsg, verified ? "success" : "info");
                                    RunQuery();
                                };
                                return b;
                            }

                            actions.Children.Add(ActBtn("Logoff", "Graceful Horizon logoff", "Logoff",
                                "Log off the Horizon session on {machine} for {user}?\n\nThe user will be disconnected."));
                            actions.Children.Add(ActBtn("Force Logoff", "Forced logoff (no wait)", "LogoffForced",
                                "FORCE log off the session on {machine}?\n\nUnsaved work may be lost."));
                            actions.Children.Add(ActBtn("Disconnect", "Disconnect client, leave session running on VM", "Disconnect",
                                "Disconnect the client from {machine}?\n\nThe desktop session stays active on the VM."));
                            actions.Children.Add(ActBtn("Reset VM", "Reset/restart the desktop VM", "Reset",
                                "Reset (restart) the VM {machine}?\n\nThis is like a hard reboot of the desktop."));
                            body.Children.Add(actions);

                            vmPanel.Child = body;
                            list.Children.Add(vmPanel);
                        }
                        foreach (string e in errors.Take(3))
                        {
                            list.Children.Add(Tb(e, size: 11, opacity: 0.7, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0)));
                        }
                    });
                });
            }

            // Do NOT auto-query on open - prevents accidental account lockouts from bad/stale credentials.
            // User clicks Refresh when ready.
            statusLine.Text = _horizonBrokers.Count == 0
                ? "No Horizon brokers configured. Open Settings to add connection servers."
                : string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_horizonPassword)
                    ? "Set Horizon credentials in Settings, then click Refresh."
                    : "Click Refresh to query Horizon (queries run one broker at a time).";
            refreshBtn.Click += (_, _) => RunQuery();
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // HORIZON INSPECTOR ENGINE (embedded 2026-10-26) - pure C# Horizon REST API.
        // Ported from Horizon User Inspector: POST /rest/login with a persistent
        // Bearer-token + HttpClient pool, server-side SID/login_name filters,
        // v1/v2 session endpoints with per-broker learning, parallel machine/pool
        // enrichment, and REST session actions. No PowerCLI / PowerShell anywhere.
        // =====================================================================
        private static void HorizonRestLog(string message)
        {
            try { System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine("[HorizonREST] " + message); } catch { }
        }

        private static (string Domain, string Username) NormalizeRestCredentials(string rawDomain, string rawUser)
        {
            string domain = (rawDomain ?? "").Trim();
            string user = (rawUser ?? "").Trim();

            if (user.Contains('\\'))
            {
                int idx = user.IndexOf('\\');
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(domain))
                    domain = user.Substring(0, idx).Trim();
                user = user.Substring(idx + 1).Trim();
            }
            else if (user.Contains('@'))
            {
                int idx = user.IndexOf('@');
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(domain))
                    domain = user.Substring(idx + 1).Trim();
                user = user.Substring(0, idx).Trim();
            }
            return (domain, user);
        }

        private static string ExtractShortUser(string raw)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw)) return "";
            string s = raw.Trim();
            if (s.Contains('\\')) s = s.Split('\\').Last();
            if (s.Contains('@')) s = s.Split('@')[0];
            return s.Trim();
        }

        private string TryResolveWindowsSidFast(string targetUser, string targetDomain, string fallbackAuthDomain)
        {
            string cacheKey = $"{targetDomain}|{fallbackAuthDomain}|{targetUser}".ToLowerInvariant();
            if (WindowsSidCache.TryGetValue(cacheKey, out var cachedSid))
                return cachedSid;

            string shortUser = ExtractShortUser(targetUser);
            var candidates = new List<string>();
            if (targetUser.Contains('\\') || targetUser.Contains('@'))
                candidates.Add(targetUser.Trim());
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(targetDomain))
                candidates.Add(targetDomain.Trim() + "\\" + shortUser);
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fallbackAuthDomain))
                candidates.Add(fallbackAuthDomain.Trim() + "\\" + shortUser);
            candidates.Add(shortUser);

            foreach (string cand in candidates.Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                try
                {
                    var nt = new NTAccount(cand);
                    var sid = (SecurityIdentifier)nt.Translate(typeof(SecurityIdentifier));
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sid?.Value))
                    {
                        WindowsSidCache[cacheKey] = sid.Value;
                        HorizonRestLog($"Fast LSA SID resolved: {cand} -> {sid.Value}");
                        return sid.Value;
                    }
                }
                catch { }
            }
            return "";
        }

        private static string NormalizeCertThumbprint(string? thumbprint)
            => (thumbprint ?? "").Replace(" ", "").Replace(":", "").Trim().ToUpperInvariant();

        /// <summary>Connect to a broker over TLS accepting any certificate, and return
        /// the SHA-1 thumbprint of the certificate it presented (for user-confirmed pinning).</summary>
        private static async Task<string?> ProbeBrokerCertThumbprintAsync(string broker)
        {
            try
            {
                using var tcp = new TcpClient();
                await tcp.ConnectAsync(broker, 443);
                using var ssl = new SslStream(tcp.GetStream(), false, (_, _, _, _) => true);
                await ssl.AuthenticateAsClientAsync(broker);
                return ssl.RemoteCertificate?.GetCertHashString();
            }
            catch { return null; }
        }

        private HttpClient CreateHorizonHttpClient(string broker, int port, bool ignoreSsl, int timeoutSec)
        {
            var handler = new SocketsHttpHandler
            {
                UseCookies = true,
                CookieContainer = new CookieContainer(),
                AutomaticDecompression = DecompressionMethods.GZip | DecompressionMethods.Deflate,
                PooledConnectionLifetime = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(15),
                PooledConnectionIdleTimeout = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5),
                MaxConnectionsPerServer = 16
            };
            // Full TLS validation by default. A certificate that fails validation is
            // accepted only when it exactly matches the thumbprint the user pinned for
            // this broker (self-signed / internal-CA Horizon servers), or when the
            // caller explicitly requested ignoreSsl.
            handler.SslOptions.RemoteCertificateValidationCallback = (_, cert, _, sslErrors) =>
            {
                if (sslErrors == SslPolicyErrors.None) return true;
                if (ignoreSsl) return true;
                if (cert == null) return false;
                string host = (broker ?? "").Trim();
                if (_horizonCertPins.TryGetValue(host, out var pin) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pin))
                {
                    string thumb = "";
                    try { thumb = cert.GetCertHashString() ?? ""; } catch { }
                    return string.Equals(
                        NormalizeCertThumbprint(thumb),
                        NormalizeCertThumbprint(pin),
                        StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                }
                return false;
            };
            string baseUri = port == 443 ? $"https://{broker}" : $"https://{broker}:{port}";
            var client = new HttpClient(handler, disposeHandler: true)
            {
                BaseAddress = new Uri(baseUri),
                Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(timeoutSec)
            };
            client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Accept.Clear();
            client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Accept.Add(new MediaTypeWithQualityHeaderValue("application/json"));
            return client;
        }

        private async Task<(bool Ok, HttpClient Client, string Error)> GetOrAuthenticateBrokerClientAsync(
            string broker, int port, bool ignoreSsl, int timeoutSec,
            string domain, string username, string password,
            bool forceNewLogin, CancellationToken ct)
        {
            string poolKey = $"{broker}:{port}:{ignoreSsl}";
            string authKey = $"{domain}\\{username}:{password.GetHashCode()}";

            if (!forceNewLogin && BrokerSessionPool.TryGetValue(poolKey, out var existing))
            {
                if (existing.AuthKey == authKey && DateTime.UtcNow < existing.ExpiresUtc)
                {
