                    HorizonRestLog($"Reusing cached Bearer token & TLS connection for {broker}");
                    return (true, existing.Client, "");
                }
            }

            var client = CreateHorizonHttpClient(broker, port, ignoreSsl, timeoutSec);
            try
            {
                var payload = new Dictionary<string, string>
                {
                    ["domain"] = domain,
                    ["username"] = username,
                    ["password"] = password
                };
                string bodyJson = JsonSerializer.Serialize(payload);
                HorizonRestLog($"POST {client.BaseAddress}rest/login (domain={domain}, username={username})");

                using var content = new StringContent(bodyJson, Encoding.UTF8, "application/json");
                using var resp = await client.PostAsync("/rest/login", content, ct);
                string respText = await resp.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(ct);

                if (!resp.IsSuccessStatusCode)
                {
                    return (false, client, $"HTTP {(int)resp.StatusCode} ({resp.ReasonPhrase}): {Truncate(respText, 240)}");
                }

                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(respText);
                var root = doc.RootElement;
                string access = GetJsonString(root, "access_token");
                string refresh = GetJsonString(root, "refresh_token");
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(access))
                    return (false, client, "Login succeeded but no access_token was returned in JSON.");

                client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", access);

                BrokerSessionPool[poolKey] = new CachedBrokerSession
                {
                    Client = client,
                    AccessToken = access,
                    RefreshToken = refresh,
                    AuthKey = authKey,
                    ExpiresUtc = DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(20)
                };

                return (true, client, "");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return (false, client, ex.Message);
            }
        }

        private async Task<(bool Ok, List<JsonElement> Items, string EndpointUsed, HttpStatusCode Status, string Error)> TryGetJsonElementsAsync(
            HttpClient client, string path, CancellationToken ct)
        {
            try
            {
                var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                using var resp = await client.GetAsync(path, ct);
                string text = await resp.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(ct);
                sw.Stop();
                HorizonRestLog($"GET {path} -> {(int)resp.StatusCode} ({sw.ElapsedMilliseconds} ms, {text.Length} bytes)");

                if (!resp.IsSuccessStatusCode)
                {
                    return (false, new List<JsonElement>(), path, resp.StatusCode, $"HTTP {(int)resp.StatusCode}: {Truncate(text, 200)}");
                }

                var list = new List<JsonElement>();
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(text);
                var root = doc.RootElement;
                if (root.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Array)
                {
                    foreach (var el in root.EnumerateArray())
                        list.Add(el.Clone());
                }
                else if (root.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object)
                {
                    if (root.TryGetProperty("items", out var itemsEl) && itemsEl.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Array)
                    {
                        foreach (var el in itemsEl.EnumerateArray())
                            list.Add(el.Clone());
                    }
                    else if (root.TryGetProperty("results", out var resEl) && resEl.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Array)
                    {
                        foreach (var el in resEl.EnumerateArray())
                            list.Add(el.Clone());
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        list.Add(root.Clone());
                    }
                }
                return (true, list, path, resp.StatusCode, "");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return (false, new List<JsonElement>(), path, HttpStatusCode.InternalServerError, ex.Message);
            }
        }

        private async Task<BrokerQueryReport> QueryBrokerForUserAsync(
            string broker, int port, bool ignoreSsl, int apiMode, int timeoutSec,
            string authDom, string authUser, string authPass,
            string targetUser, string targetDom, string fastWindowsSid,
            bool incAd, bool incVm, bool incPool,
            CancellationToken ct)
        {
            var report = new BrokerQueryReport { Broker = broker };

            var auth = await GetOrAuthenticateBrokerClientAsync(
                broker, port, ignoreSsl, timeoutSec, authDom, authUser, authPass, forceNewLogin: false, ct);
            if (!auth.Ok)
            {
                report.Error = auth.Error;
                return report;
            }
            HttpClient client = auth.Client;
            report.Connected = true;

            string shortTarget = ExtractShortUser(targetUser).ToLowerInvariant();
            string fullTarget = targetUser.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
            string normTargetDom = (targetDom ?? "").Trim().ToLowerInvariant();

            var userMapById = new ConcurrentDictionary<string, HorizonAdUserInfo>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            // =================================================================
            // STEP 1: Fast AD User Lookup (Direct by SID if available, else server-side filter)
            // Never falls back to downloading the entire unfiltered AD user table!
            // =================================================================
            Task adLookupTask = Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                if (!incAd) return;

                // 1A: If Windows LSA resolved the user's SID locally, fetch /rest/external/v1/ad-users-or-groups/{SID} in O(1) (~40ms)
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(fastWindowsSid))
                {
                    var directAd = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(
                        client, $"/rest/external/v1/ad-users-or-groups/{Uri.EscapeDataString(fastWindowsSid)}", ct);
                    if (directAd.Ok && directAd.Items.Count > 0)
                    {
                        var u = ParseAdUserElement(broker, directAd.Items[0]);
                        userMapById[u.Id] = u;
                        lock (report)
                        {
                            report.MatchedUsers.Add(u);
                            report.RawPayloads.Add(directAd.Items[0]);
                        }
                        return;
                    }
                }

                // 1B: Server-side Equals/Contains filter on login_name
                string eqFilter = Uri.EscapeDataString(
                    "{\"type\":\"And\",\"filters\":[{\"type\":\"Equals\",\"name\":\"group\",\"value\":false},{\"type\":\"Equals\",\"name\":\"login_name\",\"value\":\"" + shortTarget + "\"}]}");
                var eqRes = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(client, $"/rest/external/v1/ad-users-or-groups?filter={eqFilter}", ct);
                if (!eqRes.Ok || eqRes.Items.Count == 0)
                {
                    string containsFilter = Uri.EscapeDataString(
                        "{\"type\":\"And\",\"filters\":[{\"type\":\"Equals\",\"name\":\"group\",\"value\":false},{\"type\":\"Contains\",\"name\":\"login_name\",\"value\":\"" + shortTarget + "\"}]}");
                    eqRes = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(client, $"/rest/external/v1/ad-users-or-groups?filter={containsFilter}", ct);
                }

                if (eqRes.Ok)
                {
                    foreach (var el in eqRes.Items)
                    {
                        var u = ParseAdUserElement(broker, el);
                        bool matchLogin = string.Equals(ExtractShortUser(u.Username), shortTarget, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            || string.Equals(ExtractShortUser(u.UserPrincipalName), shortTarget, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                        if (matchLogin)
                        {
                            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(u.Id))
                                userMapById[u.Id] = u;
                            lock (report)
                            {
                                report.MatchedUsers.Add(u);
                                report.RawPayloads.Add(el);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }, ct);

            // Determine preferred session endpoint order (remembers whichever version succeeded on this broker)
            string[] baseSessionPaths;
            if (apiMode == 1)
                baseSessionPaths = new[] { "/rest/inventory/v1/sessions" };
            else if (apiMode == 2)
                baseSessionPaths = new[] { "/rest/inventory/v2/sessions", "/rest/inventory/v1/sessions" };
            else if (PreferredSessionEndpointByBroker.TryGetValue(broker, out var learnedEp))
                baseSessionPaths = learnedEp.Contains("/v1/")
                    ? new[] { "/rest/inventory/v1/sessions", "/rest/inventory/v2/sessions" }
                    : new[] { "/rest/inventory/v2/sessions", "/rest/inventory/v1/sessions" };
            else
                baseSessionPaths = new[] { "/rest/inventory/v1/sessions", "/rest/inventory/v2/sessions" };

            // If we don't have a Windows SID yet, wait briefly for AD lookup to see if it found the user's SID
            string targetSid = fastWindowsSid;
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(targetSid))
            {
                await adLookupTask;
                targetSid = userMapById.Keys.FirstOrDefault() ?? "";
            }

            // =================================================================
            // STEP 2: Fast Session Query (Server-side user_id filter first, then full list)
            // =================================================================
            List<JsonElement>? sessionElements = null;
            string sessionEndpointUsed = "";

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(targetSid))
            {
                string sessFilter = Uri.EscapeDataString(
                    "{\"type\":\"Equals\",\"name\":\"user_id\",\"value\":\"" + targetSid + "\"}");
                foreach (string baseEp in baseSessionPaths)
                {
                    var filteredRes = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(client, $"{baseEp}?filter={sessFilter}", ct);
                    if (filteredRes.Status == HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized)
                    {
                        var reAuth = await GetOrAuthenticateBrokerClientAsync(broker, port, ignoreSsl, timeoutSec, authDom, authUser, authPass, forceNewLogin: true, ct);
                        if (reAuth.Ok)
                        {
                            client = reAuth.Client;
                            filteredRes = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(client, $"{baseEp}?filter={sessFilter}", ct);
                        }
                    }
                    if (filteredRes.Ok)
                    {
                        PreferredSessionEndpointByBroker[broker] = baseEp;
                        sessionElements = filteredRes.Items;
                        sessionEndpointUsed = baseEp + " (Server-Filtered by SID)";
                        report.QueryMode = "Server-Side SID Filter";
                        report.TotalSessionsScanned = sessionElements.Count;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            // Fallback: fetch active sessions from the preferred endpoint
            if (sessionElements == null)
            {
                string lastErr = "";
                foreach (string baseEp in baseSessionPaths)
                {
                    var allRes = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(client, baseEp, ct);
                    if (allRes.Status == HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized)
                    {
                        var reAuth = await GetOrAuthenticateBrokerClientAsync(broker, port, ignoreSsl, timeoutSec, authDom, authUser, authPass, forceNewLogin: true, ct);
                        if (reAuth.Ok)
                        {
                            client = reAuth.Client;
                            allRes = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(client, baseEp, ct);
                        }
                    }
                    if (allRes.Ok)
                    {
                        PreferredSessionEndpointByBroker[broker] = baseEp;
                        sessionElements = allRes.Items;
                        sessionEndpointUsed = baseEp;
                        report.QueryMode = "In-Memory Scan";
                        report.TotalSessionsScanned = sessionElements.Count;
                        break;
                    }
                    lastErr = allRes.Error;
                }

                if (sessionElements == null)
                {
                    await adLookupTask;
                    report.Error = "Session query failed: " + lastErr;
                    return report;
                }
            }

            await adLookupTask;

            // =================================================================
            // STEP 3: Filter Sessions Locally (NO N+1 HTTP calls inside loop!)
            // =================================================================
            var matchedRecords = new List<(HorizonSessionRecord Rec, JsonElement Raw)>();

            foreach (var sEl in sessionElements)
            {
                var rec = ParseSessionElement(broker, sessionEndpointUsed, sEl);
                bool isMatch = false;

                // Match 1: Direct SID / UserId match (O(1) — zero HTTP calls)
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.UserId))
                {
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(targetSid) && string.Equals(rec.UserId, targetSid, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        isMatch = true;
                    else if (userMapById.ContainsKey(rec.UserId))
                        isMatch = true;
                }

                // Match 2: Resolved username already present in session payload
                if (!isMatch && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.ResolvedUserName))
                {
                    string shortSessUser = ExtractShortUser(rec.ResolvedUserName).ToLowerInvariant();
                    if (shortSessUser == shortTarget || rec.ResolvedUserName.ToLowerInvariant().Contains(fullTarget))
                        isMatch = true;
                }

                // Match 3: Check any user-related property in the session JSON
                if (!isMatch)
                {
                    foreach (var kv in rec.RawSessionProperties)
                    {
                        if (kv.Key.IndexOf("user", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                        {
                            string shortVal = ExtractShortUser(kv.Value).ToLowerInvariant();
                            if (shortVal == shortTarget || kv.Value.ToLowerInvariant() == fullTarget)
                            {
                                isMatch = true;
                                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.ResolvedUserName))
                                    rec.ResolvedUserName = kv.Value;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (isMatch)
                {
                    matchedRecords.Add((rec, sEl));
                }
            }

            // =================================================================
            // STEP 4: Parallel Enrichment for ONLY Matched Sessions (AD + Machine + Pool)
            // =================================================================
            string apiVerPrefix = (PreferredSessionEndpointByBroker.TryGetValue(broker, out var pref) && pref.Contains("/v2/"))
                ? "/rest/inventory/v2"
                : "/rest/inventory/v1";

            var enrichTasks = matchedRecords.Select(async item =>
            {
                var rec = item.Rec;

                // Run AD User by ID, Machine by ID, and Desktop Pool by ID concurrently!
                Task userTask = Task.CompletedTask;
                if (incAd && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.UserId))
                {
                    if (userMapById.TryGetValue(rec.UserId, out var knownUser))
                    {
                        rec.ResolvedUserName = string.IsNullOrEmpty(knownUser.Domain)
                            ? knownUser.Username
                            : knownUser.Domain + "\\" + knownUser.Username;
                        rec.ResolvedDisplayName = knownUser.DisplayName;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        userTask = Task.Run(async () =>
                        {
                            var singleUser = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(
                                client, $"/rest/external/v1/ad-users-or-groups/{Uri.EscapeDataString(rec.UserId)}", ct);
                            if (singleUser.Ok && singleUser.Items.Count > 0)
                            {
                                var u = ParseAdUserElement(broker, singleUser.Items[0]);
                                userMapById[rec.UserId] = u;
                                rec.ResolvedUserName = string.IsNullOrEmpty(u.Domain) ? u.Username : u.Domain + "\\" + u.Username;
                                rec.ResolvedDisplayName = u.DisplayName;
                                lock (report)
                                {
                                    if (!report.MatchedUsers.Any(x => string.Equals(x.Id, u.Id, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                                    {
                                        report.MatchedUsers.Add(u);
                                        report.RawPayloads.Add(singleUser.Items[0]);
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }, ct);
                    }
                }

                Task vmTask = Task.CompletedTask;
                if (incVm && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.MachineId))
                {
                    vmTask = Task.Run(async () =>
                    {
                        string primaryVmPath = $"{apiVerPrefix}/machines/{Uri.EscapeDataString(rec.MachineId)}";
                        var vmRes = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(client, primaryVmPath, ct);
                        if (!vmRes.Ok)
                        {
                            string fallbackVmPath = apiVerPrefix.EndsWith("v2")
                                ? $"/rest/inventory/v1/machines/{Uri.EscapeDataString(rec.MachineId)}"
                                : $"/rest/inventory/v2/machines/{Uri.EscapeDataString(rec.MachineId)}";
                            vmRes = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(client, fallbackVmPath, ct);
                        }
                        if (vmRes.Ok && vmRes.Items.Count > 0)
                        {
                            var vmEl = vmRes.Items[0];
                            FlattenJsonElement(vmEl, "", rec.RawMachineProperties);
                            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.MachineName))
                                rec.MachineName = GetFirstNonEmpty(vmEl, "name", "machine_name", "dns_name");
                            rec.MachineDnsName = GetFirstNonEmpty(vmEl, "dns_name", "name");
                            rec.MachineState = GetFirstNonEmpty(vmEl, "state", "basic_state");
                            rec.MachineOs = GetFirstNonEmpty(vmEl, "operating_system", "os");
                            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.AgentVersion))
                                rec.AgentVersion = GetFirstNonEmpty(vmEl, "agent_version");
                            lock (report) { report.RawPayloads.Add(vmEl); }
                        }
                    }, ct);
                }

                Task poolTask = Task.CompletedTask;
                if (incPool && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.DesktopPoolId))
                {
                    poolTask = Task.Run(async () =>
                    {
                        string poolCacheKey = $"{broker}:{rec.DesktopPoolId}";
                        JsonElement? cachedPool = null;
                        if (PoolCache.TryGetValue(poolCacheKey, out var pc) && DateTime.UtcNow < pc.ExpiresUtc)
                        {
                            cachedPool = pc.Payload;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            string primaryPoolPath = $"{apiVerPrefix}/desktop-pools/{Uri.EscapeDataString(rec.DesktopPoolId)}";
                            var poolRes = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(client, primaryPoolPath, ct);
                            if (!poolRes.Ok)
                            {
                                string fallbackPoolPath = apiVerPrefix.EndsWith("v2")
                                    ? $"/rest/inventory/v1/desktop-pools/{Uri.EscapeDataString(rec.DesktopPoolId)}"
                                    : $"/rest/inventory/v2/desktop-pools/{Uri.EscapeDataString(rec.DesktopPoolId)}";
                                poolRes = await TryGetJsonElementsAsync(client, fallbackPoolPath, ct);
                            }
                            if (poolRes.Ok && poolRes.Items.Count > 0)
                            {
                                cachedPool = poolRes.Items[0];
                                PoolCache[poolCacheKey] = (DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(10), cachedPool.Value);
                            }
                        }

                        if (cachedPool.HasValue)
                        {
                            var pEl = cachedPool.Value;
                            FlattenJsonElement(pEl, "", rec.RawPoolProperties);
                            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.DesktopPoolName))
                                rec.DesktopPoolName = GetFirstNonEmpty(pEl, "name", "id");
                            rec.DesktopPoolDisplayName = GetFirstNonEmpty(pEl, "display_name", "name");
                            lock (report) { report.RawPayloads.Add(pEl); }
                        }
                    }, ct);
                }

                await Task.WhenAll(userTask, vmTask, poolTask);

                lock (report)
                {
                    report.MatchedSessions.Add(rec);
                    report.RawPayloads.Add(item.Raw);
                }
            });

            await Task.WhenAll(enrichTasks);
            return report;
        }

        private async Task<(bool Ok, string Message)> InvokeHorizonRestSessionActionAsync(
            string broker, string sessionId, string actionType,
            string authDom, string authUser, string authPass)
        {
            var auth = await GetOrAuthenticateBrokerClientAsync(
                broker, 443, false, 60, authDom, authUser, authPass, forceNewLogin: false, CancellationToken.None);
            if (!auth.Ok) return (false, auth.Error);
            HttpClient client = auth.Client;

            try
            {
                string jsonIds = JsonSerializer.Serialize(new[] { sessionId });
                string[] endpoints = actionType switch
                {
                    "LOGOFF" => new[]
                    {
                        "/rest/inventory/v1/sessions/action/logoff",
                        "/rest/inventory/v2/sessions/action/logoff"
                    },
                    "LOGOFF_FORCED" => new[]
                    {
                        "/rest/inventory/v1/sessions/action/logoff?forced=true",
                        "/rest/inventory/v2/sessions/action/logoff?forced=true"
                    },
                    "DISCONNECT" => new[]
                    {
                        "/rest/inventory/v1/sessions/action/disconnect",
                        "/rest/inventory/v2/sessions/action/disconnect"
                    },
                    "RESET" => new[]
                    {
                        "/rest/inventory/v1/sessions/action/reset",
                        "/rest/inventory/v2/sessions/action/reset"
                    },
                    _ => Array.Empty<string>()
                };

                string lastErr = "No action endpoint matched.";
                foreach (string ep in endpoints)
                {
                    HorizonRestLog($"POST {client.BaseAddress}{ep.TrimStart('/')} Body={jsonIds}");
                    using var content = new StringContent(jsonIds, Encoding.UTF8, "application/json");
                    using var resp = await client.PostAsync(ep, content);
                    string body = await resp.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();
                    if (resp.IsSuccessStatusCode)
                    {
                        return (true, $"{actionType} accepted by {broker} ({ep}).");
                    }
                    lastErr = $"HTTP {(int)resp.StatusCode}: {Truncate(body, 200)}";
                }
                return (false, lastErr);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return (false, ex.Message);
            }
        }

        private static HorizonAdUserInfo ParseAdUserElement(string broker, JsonElement el)
        {
            var info = new HorizonAdUserInfo
            {
                Broker = broker,
                Id = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "id"),
                Username = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "login_name", "username", "sam_account_name", "name"),
                Domain = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "domain", "domain_name"),
                DisplayName = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "display_name", "long_display_name", "full_name", "name"),
                FirstName = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "first_name", "given_name"),
                LastName = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "last_name", "sn", "surname"),
                UserPrincipalName = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "user_principal_name", "upn", "login_domain_name"),
                DistinguishedName = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "distinguished_name", "dn"),
                Email = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "email", "mail"),
                Phone = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "phone", "telephone_number"),
                Sid = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "sid", "object_sid", "id")
            };
            FlattenJsonElement(el, "", info.AllProperties);
            return info;
        }

        private static HorizonSessionRecord ParseSessionElement(string broker, string endpoint, JsonElement el)
        {
            var rec = new HorizonSessionRecord
            {
                Broker = broker,
                EndpointUsed = endpoint,
                SessionId = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "id", "session_id"),
                UserId = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "user_id"),
                ResolvedUserName = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "user_name", "username", "login_name"),
                MachineId = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "machine_id", "rds_server_id"),
                MachineName = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "machine_name", "machine_or_rds_server_name", "machine_dns_name"),
                DesktopPoolId = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "desktop_pool_id", "farm_id"),
                DesktopPoolName = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "desktop_pool_name", "pool_name"),
                SessionState = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "session_state", "state"),
                SessionType = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "session_type", "type"),
                SessionProtocol = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "session_protocol", "protocol"),
                ClientName = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "client_name"),
                ClientAddress = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "client_address", "client_ip"),
                ClientVersion = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "client_version"),
                ClientType = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "client_type"),
                AgentVersion = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "agent_version"),
                BrokerGatewayHost = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "broker_gateway_host", "access_point"),
                SecurityGatewayDns = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "security_gateway_dns_name"),
                StartTimeText = FormatEpochMillis(GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "start_time", "connected_time")),
                DisconnectTimeText = FormatEpochMillis(GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "disconnect_time", "disconnected_time")),
                LastSessionDurationMinutes = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "last_session_duration_ms"),
                IdleDurationSeconds = GetFirstNonEmpty(el, "idle_duration")
            };

            if (el.TryGetProperty("names_data", out var nd) && nd.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object)
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.ResolvedUserName))
                    rec.ResolvedUserName = GetFirstNonEmpty(nd, "user_name", "userName");
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.MachineName))
                    rec.MachineName = GetFirstNonEmpty(nd, "machine_or_rds_server_name", "machineOrRDSServerName");
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.DesktopPoolName))
                    rec.DesktopPoolName = GetFirstNonEmpty(nd, "desktop_pool_name", "desktopPoolName");
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.ClientName))
                    rec.ClientName = GetFirstNonEmpty(nd, "client_name", "clientName");
            }
            if (el.TryGetProperty("session_data", out var sd) && sd.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object)
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.SessionState))
                    rec.SessionState = GetFirstNonEmpty(sd, "session_state", "sessionState");
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.SessionProtocol))
                    rec.SessionProtocol = GetFirstNonEmpty(sd, "session_protocol", "sessionProtocol");
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.SessionType))
                    rec.SessionType = GetFirstNonEmpty(sd, "session_type", "sessionType");
            }

            FlattenJsonElement(el, "", rec.RawSessionProperties);
            return rec;
        }

        private static void FlattenJsonElement(JsonElement el, string prefix, Dictionary<string, string> target)
        {
            switch (el.ValueKind)
            {
                case JsonValueKind.Object:
                    foreach (var prop in el.EnumerateObject())
                    {
                        string key = string.IsNullOrEmpty(prefix) ? prop.Name : prefix + "." + prop.Name;
                        FlattenJsonElement(prop.Value, key, target);
                    }
                    break;
                case JsonValueKind.Array:
                    int idx = 0;
                    foreach (var item in el.EnumerateArray())
                    {
                        FlattenJsonElement(item, $"{prefix}[{idx++}]", target);
                    }
                    break;
                case JsonValueKind.String:
                    target[prefix] = el.GetString() ?? "";
                    break;
                case JsonValueKind.Number:
                case JsonValueKind.True:
                case JsonValueKind.False:
                    target[prefix] = el.ToString();
                    break;
            }
        }

        private static string GetJsonString(JsonElement el, string propName)
        {
            if (el.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object && el.TryGetProperty(propName, out var p))
            {
                if (p.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.String) return p.GetString() ?? "";
                if (p.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Number || p.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.True || p.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.False)
                    return p.ToString();
            }
            return "";
        }

        private static string GetFirstNonEmpty(JsonElement el, params string[] names)
        {
            foreach (string n in names)
            {
                string val = GetJsonString(el, n).Trim();
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(val)) return val;
            }
            return "";
        }

        private static string FormatEpochMillis(string raw)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw)) return "";
            if (long.TryParse(raw, out long ms) && ms > 100000000000L)
            {
                try
                {
                    var dt = DateTimeOffset.FromUnixTimeMilliseconds(ms).ToLocalTime();
                    return dt.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
                }
                catch { }
            }
            return raw;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Query one Horizon Connection Server for active sessions matching the username,
        /// using the embedded Horizon Inspector engine (pure C# REST: POST /rest/login,
        /// then GET /rest/inventory/v1/sessions with a server-side user_id filter and
        /// v2 fallback, AD-user resolution, and machine/pool enrichment).
        /// The "__ade_test__" search performs a login-only credential test.
        /// </summary>
        private async Task<(List<HorizonSessionHit> Sessions, string Error)> QueryHorizonBrokerSessionsAsync(
            string broker, string domain, string username, string password, string searchSam)
        {
            var hits = new List<HorizonSessionHit>();
            try
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(broker))
                    return (hits, "Server name is empty");
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(username))
                    return (hits, broker + ": Username is empty");
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(password))
                    return (hits, broker + ": Password is empty");

                var (authDom, authUser) = NormalizeRestCredentials(domain ?? "", username ?? "");
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(authUser))
                    return (hits, broker + ": Username is empty");
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(authDom))
                    return (hits, broker + ": Horizon domain is empty (set the NetBIOS domain in Settings)");

                // Credential test only: authenticate via /rest/login, skip the session query
                if (string.Equals((searchSam ?? "").Trim(), "__ade_test__", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                {
                    var test = await GetOrAuthenticateBrokerClientAsync(
                        broker, 443, false, 60, authDom, authUser, password,
                        forceNewLogin: true, CancellationToken.None);
                    if (!test.Ok)
                        return (hits, broker + ": Horizon REST login failed: " + test.Error);
                    return (hits, "");
                }

                string fastSid = "";
                try { fastSid = TryResolveWindowsSidFast(searchSam ?? "", authDom, authDom); } catch { }

                var report = await QueryBrokerForUserAsync(
                    broker, 443, false, 0, 60,
                    authDom, authUser, password,
                    searchSam ?? "", authDom, fastSid,
                    incAd: true, incVm: true, incPool: true,
                    CancellationToken.None);

                foreach (var rec in report.MatchedSessions)
                {
                    hits.Add(new HorizonSessionHit
                    {
                        Broker = broker,
                        UserName = rec.ResolvedUserName ?? "",
                        MachineName = rec.MachineName ?? "",
                        DesktopName = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.DesktopPoolDisplayName)
                            ? rec.DesktopPoolDisplayName : (rec.DesktopPoolName ?? ""),
                        ClientName = rec.ClientName ?? "",
                        Protocol = rec.SessionProtocol ?? "",
                        SessionType = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.SessionType) ? "DESKTOP" : rec.SessionType,
                        State = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.SessionState) ? "ACTIVE" : rec.SessionState,
                        SessionId = rec.SessionId ?? ""
                    });
                }

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(report.Error))
                    return (hits, broker + ": " + report.Error);
                if (hits.Count == 0)
                    return (hits, broker + ": connected, " + report.TotalSessionsScanned + " session(s) total, 0 matched '" + searchSam + "'");
                return (hits, broker + ": " + hits.Count + " match(es) of " + report.TotalSessionsScanned + " session(s)");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return (hits, broker + ": " + ex.Message);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Onboarding check: hostname must look valid, resolve in DNS, and respond on 443 or 8443 (Horizon HTTPS).
        /// </summary>
        private static async Task<(bool Ok, string Detail)> ValidateHorizonBrokerHostAsync(string host)
        {
            host = (host ?? "").Trim();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host))
                return (false, "Hostname is empty.");

            if (host.StartsWith("https://", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) host = host.Substring(8);
            if (host.StartsWith("http://", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) host = host.Substring(7);
            int slash = host.IndexOf('/');
            if (slash >= 0) host = host.Substring(0, slash);
            host = host.Trim().TrimEnd('.');

            if (host.Length < 3 || host.Contains(' ') || host.Contains('\\'))
                return (false, "Not a valid DNS hostname.");

            if (host.IndexOf('.') < 0)
                return (false, "Use a full DNS name (FQDN), not a short name.");

            System.Net.IPAddress[]? addrs = null;
            try
            {
                addrs = await Task.Run(() =>
                {
                    try { return System.Net.Dns.GetHostAddresses(host); }
                    catch { return Array.Empty<System.Net.IPAddress>(); }
                });
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return (false, "DNS lookup error: " + ex.Message);
            }

            if (addrs == null || addrs.Length == 0)
                return (false, "DNS name does not resolve.");

            string ipList = string.Join(", ", addrs.Take(3).Select(a => a.ToString()));

            bool p443 = await Task.Run(() => TryTcpPort(host, 443, 3500));
            bool p8443 = false;
            if (!p443)
                p8443 = await Task.Run(() => TryTcpPort(host, 8443, 2500));

            if (p443)
                return (true, "DNS OK (" + ipList + "), TCP 443 open");
            if (p8443)
                return (true, "DNS OK (" + ipList + "), TCP 8443 open");

            var ping = await Task.Run(() => TryPingHost(host, 2000));
            if (ping.Ok)
                return (false, "DNS OK (" + ipList + ") and ping works, but TCP 443/8443 are closed. Horizon needs HTTPS open.");

            return (false, "DNS OK (" + ipList + ") but host is offline or filtered (no TCP 443/8443).");
        }

        private static bool TryTcpPort(string host, int port, int timeoutMs = 1500)
        {
            try
            {
                using var client = new System.Net.Sockets.TcpClient();
                var task = client.ConnectAsync(host, port);
                if (!task.Wait(timeoutMs)) return false;
                return client.Connected;
            }
            catch { return false; }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Perform a Horizon session action (Logoff, LogoffForced, Disconnect, Reset)
        /// over the REST API: re-query the broker for the user's sessions (the engine
        /// reuses its cached Bearer token), find the session on the target machine,
        /// and POST the action with its REST session id.
        /// Returns "OK:..." on success, otherwise an error message.
        /// </summary>
        /// <summary>True when a broker query result string is a success diagnostic
        /// (the broker answered), as opposed to an error message.</summary>
        private static bool HorizonBrokerAnswered(string? err)
            => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(err)
               && (err.Contains("match(es)") || err.Contains(": connected,"));

        /// <summary>
        /// Act on one exact Horizon session by its REST session id on one broker.
        /// No re-query, no machine-name matching: the session the operator saw and
        /// confirmed is the session that gets acted on. Returns "OK: ..." on success.
        /// </summary>
        private async Task<string> InvokeHorizonSessionActionByIdAsync(string broker, string sessionId, string action)
        {
            try
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(broker)) return "Broker is empty.";
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sessionId)) return "Session id is empty.";
                string credUser = (_horizonUsername ?? "").Trim();
                string pass = _horizonPassword ?? "";
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(credUser) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pass))
                    return "Horizon credentials not set in Settings.";

                string restAction;
                if (string.Equals(action, "Logoff", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) restAction = "LOGOFF";
                else if (string.Equals(action, "LogoffForced", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) restAction = "LOGOFF_FORCED";
                else if (string.Equals(action, "Disconnect", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) restAction = "DISCONNECT";
                else if (string.Equals(action, "Reset", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) restAction = "RESET";
                else return "Unknown action: " + action;

                var (authDom, authUser) = NormalizeRestCredentials(_horizonDomain ?? "", credUser);
                var (ok, message) = await InvokeHorizonRestSessionActionAsync(
                    broker, sessionId, restAction, authDom, authUser, pass);
                return ok ? "OK: " + action + " sent for session " + sessionId : message;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return ex.Message;
            }
        }

        private async Task<string> InvokeHorizonSessionActionAsync(
            string broker, string machineName, string searchSam, string action)
        {
            try
            {
                string credUser = (_horizonUsername ?? "").Trim();
                string pass = _horizonPassword ?? "";
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(credUser) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pass))
                    return "Horizon credentials not set in Settings.";
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(broker) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(machineName))
                    return "Broker or machine name missing.";

                string restAction;
                if (string.Equals(action, "Logoff", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) restAction = "LOGOFF";
                else if (string.Equals(action, "LogoffForced", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) restAction = "LOGOFF_FORCED";
                else if (string.Equals(action, "Disconnect", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) restAction = "DISCONNECT";
                else if (string.Equals(action, "Reset", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) restAction = "RESET";
                else return "Unknown action: " + action;

                var (sessions, err) = await QueryHorizonBrokerSessionsAsync(
                    broker, _horizonDomain, credUser, pass, searchSam);
                var target = sessions.FirstOrDefault(s =>
                    string.Equals(s.MachineName, machineName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.SessionId));
                if (target == null)
                {
                    if (sessions.Count > 0)
                        return "Session found on " + machineName + " but it has no REST session id to act on.";
                    string msg = "Session not found (user/machine). It may have already ended.";
                    string e = err ?? "";
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(e)
                        && e.IndexOf("0 matched", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0
                        && e.IndexOf("match(es) of", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0)
                        msg += " " + e;
                    return msg;
                }

                var (authDom, authUser) = NormalizeRestCredentials(_horizonDomain ?? "", credUser);
                var (ok, message) = await InvokeHorizonRestSessionActionAsync(
                    broker, target.SessionId, restAction, authDom, authUser, pass);
                return ok ? "OK:" + action + " sent for " + target.MachineName : message;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return ex.Message;
            }
        }

    }
}

            void StyleTab(Button tab, bool selected, bool locked, bool done)
            {
                if (locked)
                {
                    tab.Opacity = 0.45;
                    tab.IsEnabled = false;
                }
                else
                {
                    tab.Opacity = done && !selected ? 0.7 : 1;
                    tab.IsEnabled = true;
                }
                tab.FontWeight = selected ? FontWeight.SemiBold : FontWeight.Normal;
                if (selected)
                    tab.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(55, 128, 128, 128));

                    tab.Background = Brushes.Transparent;
            }

            void ShowTab(int n)
            {
                // Enforce sequential access
                if (n == 2 && !step1Clear) return;
                if (n == 3 && !(step1Clear && step2Clear)) return;
                activeTab = n;
                contentHost.Child = n == 1 ? page1 : (n == 2 ? page2 : page3);
                StyleTab(tab1, n == 1, false, step1Clear);
                StyleTab(tab2, n == 2, !step1Clear, step2Clear);
                StyleTab(tab3, n == 3, !(step1Clear && step2Clear), false);
                renameBtn.IsEnabled = step1Clear && step2Clear;
                if (step1Clear && step2Clear)
                {
                    SetNeutralBadge(step3Badge, "Ready");
                    if (n == 3) SetNextAction(renameBtn);
                }
            }

            void UpdateTabs()
            {
                // If current tab becomes invalid, snap back
                if (activeTab == 2 && !step1Clear) activeTab = 1;
                if (activeTab == 3 && !(step1Clear && step2Clear))
                    activeTab = step1Clear ? 2 : 1;
                ShowTab(activeTab);
            }
