            DockPanel.SetDock(tabRow, Dock.Top);
            root.Children.Add(tabRow);
            root.Children.Add(contentHost);
            dlg.Content = root;

            var currentSessions = new List<HorizonSessionHit>();
            var currentFiles = new List<OpenFileHit>();

            async Task LoadHorizonAsync()
            {
                hvList.Children.Clear();
                hvClose.IsEnabled = false;
                ClearNextActionHighlight();
                SetNeutralBadge(step1Badge, "Checking...");
                ShowProgress(true, indeterminate: true);
                SetFooter("Querying Horizon sessions...");
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_horizonPassword) || _horizonBrokers.Count == 0)
                {
                    ShowProgress(false);
                    SetNeutralBadge(step1Badge, "Not configured");
                    SetFooter("Configure Horizon brokers and credentials in Settings first.");
                    hvList.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Horizon not configured in Settings.", Opacity = 0.7 });
                    Log("Horizon not configured in Settings.");
                    step1Clear = false;
                    UpdateTabs();
                    return;
                }
                try
                {
                    var hits = new List<HorizonSessionHit>();
                    int brokersAnswered = 0;
                    foreach (string broker in _horizonBrokers)
                    {
                        var (sessions, err) = await QueryHorizonBrokerSessionsAsync(
                            broker, _horizonDomain, _horizonUsername, _horizonPassword, userCap);
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(err)) Log(broker + ": " + err);
                        if (HorizonBrokerAnswered(err)) brokersAnswered++;
                        hits.AddRange(sessions);
                    }
                    currentSessions = hits;
                    if (hits.Count == 0 && brokersAnswered == 0)
                    {
                        // Every broker query failed: "no sessions" is NOT proven, and
                        // Step 1 must not clear - renaming a profile folder under a
                        // live session can corrupt the profile.
                        hvList.Children.Add(Tb("Could not verify Horizon sessions - every broker query failed (see log). Step 1 is NOT complete.", size: 13, opacity: 0.9, wrap: true));
                        SetNeutralBadge(step1Badge, "Unverified");
                        SetFooter("Horizon verification failed for " + userCap + " - fix credentials/connectivity, then Refresh.");
                        Log("Horizon verification FAILED for " + userCap + " (no broker answered) - Step 1 NOT clear.");
                        step1Clear = false;
                        hvClose.IsEnabled = false;
                        ClearNextActionHighlight();
                    }
                    else if (hits.Count == 0)
                    {
                        hvList.Children.Add(Tb("No active Horizon sessions. Step 1 is complete - continue to File locks.", size: 13, opacity: 0.9));
                        SetNeutralBadge(step1Badge, "Clear");
                        SetFooter("Step 1 complete for " + userCap + ". Open the File locks tab.");
                        Log("No Horizon sessions for " + userCap + " - Step 1 clear.");
                        step1Clear = true;
                        hvClose.IsEnabled = false;
                        ClearNextActionHighlight();
                        // Auto-advance to step 2
                        activeTab = 2;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        step1Clear = false;
                        var seen = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        foreach (var h in hits)
                        {
                            string key = (h.UserName ?? "") + "|" + (h.MachineName ?? "") + "|" + (h.State ?? "") + "|" + (h.Protocol ?? "");
                            if (!seen.Add(key)) continue;
                            var bits = new List<string>();
                            string displayUser = FormatHorizonUserName(
                                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.UserName) ? userCap : h.UserName);
                            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(displayUser)) displayUser = userCap;
                            bits.Add(displayUser);
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.MachineName)) bits.Add(h.MachineName);
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.DesktopName)) bits.Add(h.DesktopName);
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.State)) bits.Add(h.State);
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.Protocol)) bits.Add(h.Protocol);
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.ClientName)) bits.Add("Client " + h.ClientName);
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.Broker)) bits.Add("via " + h.Broker);
                            hvList.Children.Add(Tb(string.Join("  |  ", bits), size: 13, wrap: true, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas")));
                            Log("  Session: " + displayUser
                                + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.MachineName) ? "" : " on " + h.MachineName)
                                + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.DesktopName) ? "" : " (" + h.DesktopName + ")")
                                + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.State) ? "" : " [" + h.State + "]")
                                + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.Protocol) ? "" : " " + h.Protocol)
                                + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.ClientName) ? "" : " from " + h.ClientName)
                                + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(h.Broker) ? "" : " @ " + h.Broker));
                        }
                        hvClose.IsEnabled = true;
                        SetNeutralBadge(step1Badge, hits.Count + " session(s)");
                        SetFooter(hits.Count + " Horizon session(s) for " + userCap + " - close them to continue.");
                        Log("Found " + hits.Count + " Horizon session(s) for " + userCap + ".");
                        SetNextAction(hvClose);
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    step1Clear = false;
                    SetNeutralBadge(step1Badge, "Error");
                    SetFooter("Horizon query failed.");
                    Log("Horizon error: " + ex.Message);
                    ClearNextActionHighlight();
                }
                ShowProgress(false);
                UpdateTabs();
                if (step1Clear) LoadFiles();
            }

            void LoadFiles()
            {
                if (!step1Clear)
                {
                    fileList.Children.Clear();
                    fileList.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Complete Step 1 (Horizon) first.", Opacity = 0.7 });
                    step2Clear = false;
                    fileClose.IsEnabled = false;
                    fileRefresh.IsEnabled = false;
                    SetNeutralBadge(step2Badge, "LOCKED");
                    UpdateTabs();
                    return;
                }
                fileRefresh.IsEnabled = true;
                fileList.Children.Clear();
                fileClose.IsEnabled = false;
                ClearNextActionHighlight();
                SetNeutralBadge(step2Badge, "Checking...");
                progressBar2.IsVisible = true;
                progressBar2.IsIndeterminate = true;
                try
                {
                    currentFiles = EnumerateOpenFiles(_profileCifsServer, userCap);
                    if (currentFiles.Count == 0)
                    {
                        fileList.Children.Add(Tb("No open file locks. Step 2 is complete - continue to Rename.", size: 13, opacity: 0.9));
                        SetNeutralBadge(step2Badge, "Clear");
                        SetFooter("Step 2 complete for " + userCap + ". Open the Rename tab.");
                        Log("No file locks for " + userCap + " on " + _profileCifsServer + " - Step 2 clear.");
                        step2Clear = true;
                        fileClose.IsEnabled = false;
                        activeTab = 3;
                        SetNextAction(renameBtn);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        step2Clear = false;
                        foreach (var f in currentFiles.Take(50))
                        {
                            fileList.Children.Add(Tb(f.FileId + "  " + f.Path
                                    + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(f.Username) ? "" : "  [" + f.Username + "]"), size: 12, wrap: true, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas")));
                        }
                        if (currentFiles.Count > 50)
                            fileList.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "... and " + (currentFiles.Count - 50) + " more", Opacity = 0.6 });
                        fileClose.IsEnabled = true;
                        SetNeutralBadge(step2Badge, currentFiles.Count + " lock(s)");
                        SetFooter(currentFiles.Count + " file lock(s) for " + userCap + " - close them before renaming.");
                        Log("Found " + currentFiles.Count + " file lock(s) for " + userCap + " on \\\\" + _profileCifsServer + ".");
                        int sample = Math.Min(5, currentFiles.Count);
                        for (int i = 0; i < sample; i++)
                            Log("  Lock " + currentFiles[i].FileId + ": " + currentFiles[i].Path);
                        if (currentFiles.Count > sample)
                            Log("  ... +" + (currentFiles.Count - sample) + " more");
                        SetNextAction(fileClose);
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    step2Clear = false;
                    SetNeutralBadge(step2Badge, "Error");
                    SetFooter("File lock query failed: " + ex.Message);
                    Log("File enum error: " + ex.Message);
                    ClearNextActionHighlight();
                }
                progressBar2.IsVisible = false;
                UpdateTabs();
            }

            hvRefresh.Click += async (_, _) => await LoadHorizonAsync();
            fileRefresh.Click += (_, _) => { if (step1Clear) LoadFiles(); };

            hvClose.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                if (currentSessions.Count == 0) return;
                bool ok = await ConfirmDialogAsync("Confirm Horizon logoff",
                    "Log off " + currentSessions.Count + " Horizon session(s) for " + userCap + "?\n\nThe user will be disconnected from their virtual desktop.");
                if (!ok) return;
                hvClose.IsEnabled = false;
                ClearNextActionHighlight();
                SetNeutralBadge(step1Badge, "Logging off...");
                ShowProgress(true, indeterminate: true);
                SetFooter("Sending Horizon logoff for " + userCap + "...");
                Log("Logging off " + currentSessions.Count + " Horizon session(s) for " + userCap + "...");
                int closed = 0, failed = 0;
                var actedIds = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                foreach (var s in currentSessions)
                {
                    string who = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.UserName) ? userCap : s.UserName;
                    string where = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.MachineName) ? "(unknown machine)" : s.MachineName;
                    bool success;
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.SessionId))
                    {
                        // Log off exactly the session that was shown and confirmed
                        // (by REST session id), once - never re-resolve by machine
                        // name, and never log off sessions that appeared later.
                        if (!actedIds.Add((s.Broker ?? "") + "|" + s.SessionId))
                            continue;
                        string r = await InvokeHorizonSessionActionByIdAsync(s.Broker ?? "", s.SessionId, "Logoff");
                        success = r.StartsWith("OK", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        if (!success) Log("  Logoff by session id failed: " + r);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        success = await LogoffHorizonSessionAsync(s.Broker ?? "", userCap, s.MachineName);
                    }
                    if (success)
                    {
                        closed++;
                        Log("  Logoff sent: " + who + " on " + where
                            + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.DesktopName) ? "" : " / " + s.DesktopName)
                            + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.Broker) ? "" : " via " + s.Broker));
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        failed++;
                        Log("  Logoff failed: " + who + " on " + where
                            + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.Broker) ? "" : " via " + s.Broker));
                    }
                }
                Log("Waiting up to 90s for logoff (checks every 5s, exits early when clear)...");
                const int waitTotal = 90;
                const int pollEvery = 5;
                ShowProgress(true, indeterminate: false, value: waitTotal, maximum: waitTotal);
                bool earlyClear = false;
                for (int i = waitTotal; i > 0; i--)
                {
                    SetFooter("Waiting for Horizon logoff... " + i + "s remaining (checks every " + pollEvery + "s)");
                    SetNeutralBadge(step1Badge, i + "s left");
                    progressBar.Value = i;
                    progressBar.Maximum = waitTotal;
                    await Task.Delay(1000);
                    // Poll Horizon every 5 seconds
                    if (i % pollEvery == 0 || i == 1)
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            var recheck = new List<HorizonSessionHit>();
                            bool anyAnswered = false;
                            foreach (string broker in _horizonBrokers)
                            {
                                var (sessions, err) = await QueryHorizonBrokerSessionsAsync(
                                    broker, _horizonDomain, _horizonUsername, _horizonPassword, userCap);
                                if (!HorizonBrokerAnswered(err)) continue;
                                anyAnswered = true;
                                recheck.AddRange(sessions);
                            }
                            if (anyAnswered && recheck.Count == 0)
                            {
                                earlyClear = true;
                                Log("Horizon clear after " + (waitTotal - i + 1) + "s - continuing early.");
                                break;
                            }
                            Log("  Still " + recheck.Count + " session(s) at " + i + "s remaining...");
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }
                }
                ShowProgress(false);
                if (earlyClear)
                    Log("Early exit - sessions cleared. Closed=" + closed + " Failed=" + failed);
                else
                    Log("Countdown done. Closed=" + closed + " Failed=" + failed);
                await LoadHorizonAsync();
            };

            fileClose.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                if (!step1Clear || currentFiles.Count == 0) return;
                bool ok = await ConfirmDialogAsync("Confirm close file locks",
                    "Release " + currentFiles.Count + " file lock(s) on " + _profileCifsServer + "?\n\nThis does not delete any files.");
                if (!ok) return;
                fileClose.IsEnabled = false;
                SetNeutralBadge(step2Badge, "RELEASING...");
                int closed = 0;
                foreach (var f in currentFiles)
                {
                    if (CloseOpenFile(_profileCifsServer, f.FileId)) closed++;
                }
                Log("Released " + closed + " / " + currentFiles.Count + " lock(s).");
                LoadFiles();
            };

            renameBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                if (!step1Clear || !step2Clear) return;
                bool ok = await ConfirmDialogAsync("Confirm profile rename",
                    "Rename profile folder for " + userCap + "?\n\nFrom:\n  " + pathCap + "\n\nTo:\n  " + pathOldCap + "\n\nA new profile is created on next login.");
                if (!ok) return;
                renameBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                SetNeutralBadge(step3Badge, "RENAMING...");
                try
                {
                    var remaining = EnumerateOpenFiles(_profileCifsServer, userCap);
                    foreach (var f in remaining) CloseOpenFile(_profileCifsServer, f.FileId);

                    if (!Directory.Exists(pathCap))
                    {
                        if (Directory.Exists(pathOldCap))
                        {
                            SetNeutralBadge(step3Badge, "Already done");
                            Log("Already .old: " + pathOldCap);
                            Log("Profile fix complete for " + userCap + ". You can close this window.");
                            ShowNotification("Profile already renamed to .old for " + userCap, "info");
                            renameBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                            MarkCompleteAndPromptClose("Complete - profile already .old. Click Close.");
                            return;
                        }
                        throw new Exception("Profile folder not found: " + pathCap);
                    }
                    if (Directory.Exists(pathOldCap))
                    {
                        bool overwrite = await ConfirmDialogAsync("Existing .old found",
                            "Remove existing .old and continue?\n\n" + pathOldCap);
                        if (!overwrite) { renameBtn.IsEnabled = true; SetNeutralBadge(step3Badge, "Ready"); return; }
                        Directory.Delete(pathOldCap, recursive: true);
                        Log("Removed existing .old");
                    }
                    Directory.Move(pathCap, pathOldCap);
                    SetNeutralBadge(step3Badge, "Done");
                    Log("Renamed: " + pathCap + " -> " + pathOldCap);
                    Log("Profile fix complete for " + userCap + ". A new profile will be created on next login.");
                    Log("You can close this window.");
                    ShowNotification("Profile reset complete for " + userCap, "success");
                    renameBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                    MarkCompleteAndPromptClose("Complete - profile renamed to .old. Click Close.");
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    SetNeutralBadge(step3Badge, "Failed");
                    SetFooter("Rename failed: " + ex.Message);
                    Log("Rename error: " + ex.Message);
                    ShowNotification("Profile rename failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                    renameBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                }
            };

            // Enable rename when step 3 becomes available
            dlg.Opened += async (_, _) =>
            {
                Log("Profile Fix opened for " + userCap);
                SetFooter("Querying Horizon sessions...");
                ShowTab(1);
                await LoadHorizonAsync();
            };

            try { dlg.ShowDialog(this); }
            catch { dlg.Show(); }
        }

        private List<OpenFileHit> EnumerateOpenFiles(string server, string username)
        {
            var list = new List<OpenFileHit>();
            IntPtr buf = IntPtr.Zero;
            try
            {
                int entries = 0, total = 0;
                int result = NetFileEnum(server, null, username, 3, out buf, -1, out entries, out total, IntPtr.Zero);
                if (result != 0)
                {
                    if (result == 5) throw new Exception("Access denied enumerating files on " + server);
                    if (result == 53) throw new Exception("Network path not found: " + server);
                    throw new Exception("NetFileEnum error " + result);
                }
                if (entries <= 0 || buf == IntPtr.Zero) return list;
                int size = Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(FILE_INFO_3));
                IntPtr cur = buf;
                for (int i = 0; i < entries; i++)
                {
                    var info = Marshal.PtrToStructure<FILE_INFO_3>(cur);
                    string path = info.fi3_pathname ?? "";
                    // Skip service paths
                    if (path.IndexOf("srvsvc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                        || !System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(path, @"^[A-Za-z]:\\"))
                    {
                        cur = IntPtr.Add(cur, size);
                        continue;
                    }
                    list.Add(new OpenFileHit
                    {
                        FileId = info.fi3_id,
                        Username = info.fi3_username ?? "",
                        Path = path,
                        Locks = info.fi3_num_locks,
                        Permissions = info.fi3_permissions switch
                        {
                            1 => "Read",
                            2 => "Write",
                            3 => "Read/Write",
                            _ => "Other"
                        }
                    });
                    cur = IntPtr.Add(cur, size);
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                if (buf != IntPtr.Zero) NetApiBufferFree(buf);
            }
            return list.OrderByDescending(f => f.Path).ToList();
        }

        private bool CloseOpenFile(string server, int fileId)
        {
            try
            {
                int r = NetFileClose(server, fileId);
                return r == 0 || r == 2; // 2 = already closed
            }
            catch { return false; }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Log off Horizon session(s) matching username + machine on the given broker
        /// over the REST API (queries the user's sessions with the embedded Horizon
        /// Inspector engine, then POSTs the logoff action for each matching session id).
        /// </summary>
        private async Task<bool> LogoffHorizonSessionAsync(string broker, string targetUser, string machineName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(broker) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(targetUser))
                return false;
            try
            {
                var (sessions, _) = await QueryHorizonBrokerSessionsAsync(
                    broker, _horizonDomain, _horizonUsername, _horizonPassword, targetUser);
                var targets = sessions.Where(s =>
                    !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.SessionId)
                    && (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(machineName)
                        || string.Equals(s.MachineName, machineName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                    .ToList();
                if (targets.Count == 0) return false;

                var (authDom, authUser) = NormalizeRestCredentials(_horizonDomain ?? "", _horizonUsername ?? "");
                bool any = false;
                foreach (var t in targets)
                {
                    var (ok, _) = await InvokeHorizonRestSessionActionAsync(
                        broker, t.SessionId, "LOGOFF", authDom, authUser, _horizonPassword ?? "");
                    if (ok) any = true;
                }
                return any;
            }
            catch { return false; }
        }

        private const string DefaultDelProf2Path =
            @"\\olncifs1\software$\Software\Restricted\Active Directory Explorer\DelProf2.exe";
        private string _delProf2Path = DefaultDelProf2Path;

        /// <summary>Resolved DelProf2.exe path (settings override, else default share path).</summary>
        private string GetDelProf2SourcePath()
        {
            string p = (_delProf2Path ?? "").Trim();
            return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(p) ? DefaultDelProf2Path : p;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// DelProf2 by Helge Klein - deletes inactive Windows user profiles (not currently loaded).
        /// Official: delprof2 [/l] [/u] [/q] [/p] [/r] [/c:[\\]computername] [/d:days [/ntuserini]] [/ed:pattern] [/id:pattern] [/i]
        /// We run: DelProf2.exe /c:COMPUTER /u /i
        /// Runs a local copy staged from the configured DelProf2 source (hash-verified).
        /// A binary found on the target machine is never copied or executed.
        /// </summary>
        private async void RunDelProf2OnHost(string host, AdNode? refreshNode = null)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host))
            {
                ShowNotification("No computer name for DelProf2.", "error");
                return;
            }
            string resolveHost = ResolveRdpHost(host);
            // One click must never start an irreversible mass deletion: confirm
            // first, naming the target and the consequence.
            bool confirmed = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                "Delete inactive profiles?",
                "DelProf2 will permanently delete EVERY inactive (not currently loaded) user profile on:\n"
                + resolveHost + "\n\n"
                + "This cannot be undone. Profiles belonging to staff on leave or between logons are deleted too.\n\n"
                + "Continue?");
            if (!confirmed) return;
            // Official example: Delprof2 /c:computername
            string shortName = host.Trim().TrimEnd('$');
            if (shortName.Contains('.')) shortName = shortName.Split('.')[0];

            bool isLight = Application.Current?.RequestedThemeVariant == Avalonia.Styling.ThemeVariant.Light
                || string.Equals(_preferredTheme, "Light", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            IBrush logFg = isLight
                ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(33, 33, 33))
                : new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(220, 220, 220));
            IBrush headerBg = isLight
                ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(232, 245, 233))
                : new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 45, 52, 58));
            IBrush headerBorder = isLight
                ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(56, 142, 60))
                : new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(200, 76, 175, 80));
            IBrush headerFg = isLight
                ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(33, 33, 33))
                : Brushes.White;
            IBrush noteFg = isLight
                ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(27, 94, 32))
                : new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(129, 199, 132));
            IBrush dlgBg = isLight
                ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(250, 250, 250))
                : new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(30, 30, 30));

            var dlg = new Window
            {
                Title = "DelProf2 - " + shortName,
                Width = 720,
                Height = 480,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                CanResize = true,
                Background = dlgBg
            };
            StyleDialogWindow(dlg);
            var root = new DockPanel { Margin = new Thickness(12) };
            var headerInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };
            headerInner.Children.Add(Tb("Deletes inactive profiles on " + shortName
                    + " (not currently loaded). Command: DelProf2.exe /c:" + resolveHost + " /u /i", size: 13, wrap: true, foreground: headerFg));
            headerInner.Children.Add(Tb("This can take a while on machines with many profiles - wait for the \"Finished\" message at the end of the log.", size: 12, weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, wrap: true, foreground: noteFg));
            headerInner.Children.Add(Tb("You can minimize this window and keep using ADE - it will auto-close when finished and refresh the computer page.", size: 12, wrap: true, foreground: noteFg));
            var header = new Border
            {
                Child = headerInner,
                Padding = new Thickness(12, 10),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 10),
                Background = headerBg,
                BorderBrush = headerBorder,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(6)
            };
            DockPanel.SetDock(header, Dock.Top);
            root.Children.Add(header);

            // Custom close control (not Button) so theme hover styles cannot wash out the text
            IBrush closeBg = isLight
                ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(46, 125, 50))
                : new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(76, 175, 80));
            IBrush closeBgHover = isLight
                ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(27, 94, 32))
                : new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(102, 187, 106));
            var closeLabel = Tb("Close", size: 13, weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Center, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center, foreground: Brushes.White);
            var closeBtn = new Border
            {
                Child = closeLabel,
                Background = closeBg,
                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(4),
                Padding = new Thickness(20, 8),
                MinWidth = 110,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 10, 0, 0),
                Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand)
            };
            closeBtn.PointerEntered += (_, _) => closeBtn.Background = closeBgHover;
            closeBtn.PointerExited += (_, _) => closeBtn.Background = closeBg;
            closeBtn.PointerPressed += (_, e) =>
            {
                if (e.GetCurrentPoint(closeBtn).Properties.IsLeftButtonPressed)
                    dlg.Close();
            };
            DockPanel.SetDock(closeBtn, Dock.Bottom);
            root.Children.Add(closeBtn);

            var logLines = new StackPanel { Spacing = 1 };
            var logScroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Content = logLines,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto
            };
            root.Children.Add(logScroll);
            dlg.Content = root;

            void ScrollLogToEnd()
            {
                try
                {
                    logScroll.InvalidateArrange();
                    double extent = logScroll.Extent.Height;
                    double viewport = logScroll.Viewport.Height;
                    if (extent > viewport && viewport > 0)
                        logScroll.Offset = new Vector(0, extent - viewport);
                    else if (logLines.Children.Count > 0)
                        logScroll.Offset = new Vector(0, double.MaxValue / 4);
                }
                catch { }
            }

            void Append(string line)
            {
                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    logLines.Children.Add(Tb(line, size: 12, wrap: true, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas"), foreground: logFg));
                    ScrollLogToEnd();
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(ScrollLogToEnd, DispatcherPriority.Background);
                });
            }

            try { dlg.Show(this); } catch { dlg.Show(); }

            _ = Task.Run(() =>
            {
                try
                {
                    string remoteSys32 = @"\\" + resolveHost + @"\C$\Windows\System32";
                    string remoteExe = Path.Combine(remoteSys32, "DelProf2.exe");
                    Append("[" + DateTime.Now.ToString("HH:mm:ss") + "] Target: " + resolveHost);
                    Append("[" + DateTime.Now.ToString("HH:mm:ss") + "] Note: DelProf2 can take several minutes when many profiles exist. Please wait for the \"Finished\" message.");
                    Append("[" + DateTime.Now.ToString("HH:mm:ss") + "] Checking " + remoteExe);

                    if (!Directory.Exists(remoteSys32))
                    {
                        Append("ERROR: Cannot access " + remoteSys32 + " (offline or no admin share rights).");
                        return;
                    }

                    // Trusted binary only: the local copy is always staged from the
                    // CONFIGURED source (Settings > Tools - normally the software
                    // share) and hash-checked against it. A DelProf2.exe found on the
                    // target machine is never copied or run: a planted, outdated or
                    // replaced binary on a less-trusted PC must not execute on this
                    // workstation with admin credentials.
                    string delProfSrc = GetDelProf2SourcePath();
                    if (!File.Exists(delProfSrc))
                    {
                        Append("ERROR: Configured DelProf2 source not found: " + delProfSrc);
                        Append("Set the DelProf2 path under Settings > Tools.");
                        return;
                    }
                    string localDir = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "ADE_DelProf2");
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(localDir);
                    string localExe = Path.Combine(localDir, "DelProf2.exe");
                    string srcHash = ComputeSha256Hex(File.ReadAllBytes(delProfSrc));
                    bool stagedOk = false;
                    try
                    {
                        stagedOk = File.Exists(localExe)
                            && string.Equals(ComputeSha256Hex(File.ReadAllBytes(localExe)), srcHash, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                    }
                    catch { stagedOk = false; }
                    if (!stagedOk)
                    {
                        File.Copy(delProfSrc, localExe, overwrite: true);
                        Append("Staged DelProf2 from the configured source (hash-verified): " + delProfSrc);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        Append("Staged DelProf2 already matches the configured source (hash-verified).");
                    }

                    // Official params (Helge Klein Delprof2 /?):
                    //   /c:computername  delete on remote computer
                    //   /u               unattended (no confirmation)
                    //   /i               ignore errors, continue deleting
                    // Omit /q so stdout is captured; omit /p so no per-profile prompts.
                    // Target the same resolved FQDN the pre-checks used - not the
                    // short name, which can resolve to a different machine.
                    string args = "/c:" + resolveHost + " /u /i";
                    Append("[" + DateTime.Now.ToString("HH:mm:ss") + "] Running: DelProf2.exe " + args);
                    Append("[" + DateTime.Now.ToString("HH:mm:ss") + "] Full path: " + localExe);
                    Append("--- output ---");

                    var psi = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = localExe,
                        Arguments = args,
                        UseShellExecute = false,
                        RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                        RedirectStandardError = true,
                        CreateNoWindow = true,
                        WorkingDirectory = localDir
                    };
                    using var p = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psi);
                    if (p == null)
                    {
                        Append("ERROR: Failed to start DelProf2.exe");
                        return;
                    }
                    p.OutputDataReceived += (_, e) => { if (e.Data != null) Append(e.Data); };
                    p.ErrorDataReceived += (_, e) => { if (e.Data != null) Append(e.Data); };
                    p.BeginOutputReadLine();
                    p.BeginErrorReadLine();
                    if (!p.WaitForExit(600000))
                    {
                        // Timed out: the process is still running. Reading ExitCode
                        // here would throw and falsely report failure while deletion
                        // continues - say what is actually true instead.
                        Append("--- still running after 10 minutes ---");
                        Append("DelProf2 keeps running in the background on this PC. Do NOT start it again - check the target machine later.");
                        string stillHost = resolveHost;
                        Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                            ShowNotification("DelProf2 still running on " + stillHost + " after 10 minutes (left running in background).", "info"));
                        return;
                    }
                    Append(p.ExitCode == 0 ? "--- Finished ---" : "--- Finished with errors (exit " + p.ExitCode + ") ---");
                    int exitCode = p.ExitCode;
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(async () =>
                    {
                        if (exitCode == 0)
                            ShowNotification("DelProf2 finished on " + shortName, "success");
                        else
                            ShowNotification("DelProf2 finished with errors on " + shortName + " (exit " + exitCode + ")", "error");
                        // Brief pause so the done line is visible, then close and refresh
                        await Task.Delay(1500);
                        try { dlg.Close(); } catch { }
                        if (refreshNode != null)
                        {
                            try { ShowHelpDeskInfo(refreshNode); } catch { }
                        }
                    });
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Append("ERROR: " + ex.Message);
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(async () =>
                    {
                        ShowNotification("DelProf2 failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                        await Task.Delay(2000);
                        try { dlg.Close(); } catch { }
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        private const string DefaultProfileCifsServer = "olncifs1";
        private const string DefaultProfileSharePath = @"pu\profiles";
        private string _profileCifsServer = DefaultProfileCifsServer;
        private string _profileSharePath = DefaultProfileSharePath;

        // NetApi32 for profile file-lock release (same as Profile Reset Tool)
        [DllImport("netapi32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)]
        private static extern int NetFileEnum(
            string servername, string basepath, string username, int level,
            out IntPtr bufptr, int prefmaxlen, out int entriesread, out int totalentries, IntPtr resume_handle);

        [DllImport("netapi32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)]
        private static extern int NetFileClose(string servername, int fileid);

        [DllImport("netapi32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
        private static extern int NetApiBufferFree(IntPtr Buffer);

        [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
        private struct FILE_INFO_3
        {
            public int fi3_id;
            public int fi3_permissions;
            public int fi3_num_locks;
            public string fi3_pathname;
            public string fi3_username;
        }

    }
}

        private void FocusSearchBox()
        {
            try
            {
                var tb = this.FindControl<TextBox>("SearchBox");
                if (tb == null) return;

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tb.Text))
                    tb.SelectAll();

        }

        private void GoHomeAndFocusSearch()
        {
            try
            {
                var tb = this.FindControl<TextBox>("SearchBox");
                if (tb != null) tb.Text = "";
                _currentFilter = "";
                _searchMatches = new List<AdNode>();
                _searchSelectedIndex = -1;
                _searchResultButtons = new List<Button>();

                    if (tree != null && tree.Items.Count > 0 && tree.Items[0] is TreeViewItem rootItem)
                        tree.SelectedItem = rootItem;
                    // Prefer silent path when called from OnHome (already recorded)

                    _navSilent = true;
                    try { ShowContainerContents(_rootData); }
                    finally { _navSilent = wasSilent; }
                    UpdateBreadcrumbBar(_rootData);
                }
                else
                {
                    UpdateBreadcrumbBar(null);
                }
                SetStatus("");
            }
            catch { }
            // Focus after UI updates
            Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(FocusSearchBox, DispatcherPriority.Input);
        }

        private void OnWindowKeyDown(object? sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
            {
                e.Handled = true;
                GoHomeAndFocusSearch();
            }
        }

        private void ShowNotification(string message, string kind = "info")
        {
            // kind: "success" | "error" | "info"
            // Normalize fancy Unicode that some fonts render as boxes/mojibake in the toast
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(message))
            {
                message = message
                    .Replace("\u2192", "->")   // →
                    .Replace("\u2026", "...")  // ...
                    .Replace("\u2013", "-")    // -
                    .Replace("\u2014", "-")    // -
                    .Replace("\u2018", "'")    // '
                    .Replace("\u2019", "'")    // '
                    .Replace("\u201C", "\"")   // "
                    .Replace("\u201D", "\"");  // "
            }
            Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
            {
                var border = this.FindControl<Border>("ToastBorder");
                var text = this.FindControl<TextBlock>("ToastText");
                var closeBtn = this.FindControl<Button>("ToastCloseBtn");
                if (border == null || text == null) return;

                border.Classes.Clear();
                border.Classes.Add(kind switch
                {
                    "success" => "toastSuccess",
                    "error"   => "toastError",
                    _         => "toastInfo"
                });
                text.Text = message;
                border.IsVisible = true;

                _toastDismiss = () =>
                {
                    border.IsVisible = false;
                    _toastTimer?.Stop();
                };

                if (closeBtn != null)
                {
                    closeBtn.Click -= OnToastCloseClick;
                    closeBtn.Click += OnToastCloseClick;
                }

                _toastTimer?.Stop();
                _toastTimer = new DispatcherTimer { Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10) };
                _toastTimer.Tick += (_, _) => _toastDismiss?.Invoke();
                _toastTimer.Start();
            });
        }

        private void OnToastCloseClick(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => _toastDismiss?.Invoke();

        // ============================================================
        // Material Design icons (Projektanker) - baked into the EXE.
        // Used for AD types AND all toolbar / chrome glyphs.
        // ============================================================

        private static string GetIconKey(string type) => type switch
        {
            "Domain"    => "mdi-earth",
            "OU"        => "mdi-folder",
            "Container" => "mdi-folder-outline",
            "User"      => "mdi-account",
            "Group"     => "mdi-account-group",
            "Computer"  => "mdi-desktop-classic",
            _           => "mdi-circle-small"
        };


        private static Projektanker.Icons.Avalonia.Icon MakeMdi(string mdiKey, double size = 16)
        {
            return new Projektanker.Icons.Avalonia.Icon
            {
                Value = mdiKey,
                FontSize = size,
                Width = size + 2,
                Height = size + 2,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center
            };
        }

        private static Projektanker.Icons.Avalonia.Icon MakeMaterialIcon(string typeOrKey, double size = 16)
        {
            string key = typeOrKey ?? "";
            if (!key.StartsWith("mdi-", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                key = GetIconKey(key);
            return MakeMdi(key, size);
        }

        private static string FormatObjectLine(AdNode n)
        {
            if (n.Type == "User")
            {
                string sam = string.IsNullOrEmpty(n.SamAccountName) ? "" : "    " + n.SamAccountName;
                return n.Name + sam;
            }
            return n.Name;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Compact TextBlock factory: sets only the properties passed, so it is a
        /// drop-in replacement for the multi-line object initializers that used to
        /// dominate the UI code. Named arguments keep call sites self-describing.
        /// </summary>
        private static TextBlock Tb(string text, double? size = null, FontWeight? weight = null,
            double? opacity = null, bool? wrap = null, Thickness? margin = null,
            HorizontalAlignment? halign = null, VerticalAlignment? valign = null,
            FontFamily? fontFamily = null, IBrush? foreground = null, bool? visible = null,
            TextTrimming? trimming = null)
        {
            var tb = new TextBlock { Text = text };
            if (size.HasValue) tb.FontSize = size.Value;
            if (weight.HasValue) tb.FontWeight = weight.Value;
            if (opacity.HasValue) tb.Opacity = opacity.Value;
            if (wrap.HasValue) tb.TextWrapping = wrap.Value ? TextWrapping.Wrap : TextWrapping.NoWrap;
            if (margin.HasValue) tb.Margin = margin.Value;
            if (halign.HasValue) tb.HorizontalAlignment = halign.Value;
            if (valign.HasValue) tb.VerticalAlignment = valign.Value;
            if (fontFamily != null) tb.FontFamily = fontFamily;
            if (foreground != null) tb.Foreground = foreground;
            if (visible.HasValue) tb.IsVisible = visible.Value;
            if (trimming != null) tb.TextTrimming = trimming;
            return tb;
        }

        private static StackPanel MakeIconLine(string typeOrKey, string text, double iconSize = 15, double fontSize = 13, bool bold = false)
        {
            var sp = new StackPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                Spacing = 6,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
            };
            sp.Children.Add(MakeMaterialIcon(typeOrKey, iconSize));
            var tb = Tb(text ?? "", size: fontSize, wrap: false, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center, trimming: TextTrimming.CharacterEllipsis);
            if (bold) tb.FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold;
            sp.Children.Add(tb);
            return sp;
        }


        /// <summary>Assign Material icons to all toolbar / chrome buttons (no system glyphs).</summary>
        private void WireChromeIcons()
        {
            void Set(string name, string mdi, double size = 16)
            {
                var b = this.FindControl<Button>(name);
                if (b != null)
                {
                    b.Content = MakeMdi(mdi, size);
                    b.HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center;
                    b.VerticalContentAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
                }
            }
            Set("HomeBtn", "mdi-home", 16);
            Set("ExpandAllBtn", "mdi-chevron-down", 14);
            Set("CollapseAllBtn", "mdi-chevron-up", 14);
            Set("RefreshBtn", "mdi-refresh", 16);
            Set("ClearSearchBtn", "mdi-close", 14);
            Set("SettingsBtn", "mdi-cog", 16);
            Set("ToastCloseBtn", "mdi-close", 12);
            // App title bar icon (original Material sitemap glyph before title text)
            var titlePanel = this.FindControl<StackPanel>("AppTitlePanel");
            if (titlePanel != null
                && (titlePanel.Children.Count == 0
                    || titlePanel.Children[0] is not Projektanker.Icons.Avalonia.Icon))
            {
                // Remove any leftover Image from previous builds
                while (titlePanel.Children.Count > 0 && titlePanel.Children[0] is Image)
                    titlePanel.Children.RemoveAt(0);
                titlePanel.Children.Insert(0, MakeMdi("mdi-sitemap", 20));
            }

            // Back button: icon + text
            var back = this.FindControl<Button>("BackBtn");
            if (back != null)
            {
                var sp = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 6 };
                sp.Children.Add(MakeMdi("mdi-arrow-left", 16));
                sp.Children.Add(Tb("Back", size: 13, opacity: 0.9, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));
                back.Content = sp;
            }
            UpdateThemeButtonIcon();
        }

        private void SetStatus(string msg)
        {
            var s = this.FindControl<TextBlock>("StatusText");
            if (s != null) s.Text = msg;
        }


        private void SetProgressVisible(bool visible)
        {
            var p = this.FindControl<ProgressBar>("SearchProgress");
            if (p != null) p.IsVisible = visible;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // PROGRESSIVE LOAD: top level first, then deeper levels in background
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        private void BeginProgressiveLoad(bool showProgress = false)
        {
            _loadCts?.Cancel();
            _loadCts = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var token = _loadCts.Token;

            var tree = this.FindControl<TreeView>("AdTree");
            if (tree == null) return;

            tree.Items.Clear();
            _flatNodes.Clear();
            _rootData = null;
            _loadedCount = 0;
            _maxPwdAge = null;

            // Always surface status for domain load (this is the multi-second wait users notice)
            SetStatus("Connecting to Active Directory...");
            SetProgressVisible(true);

            Window? loadOverlay = null;
            TextBlock? loadOverlayStatus = null;
            if (showProgress)
            {
                try
                {
                    loadOverlay = ShowProgressWindow(
                        "Loading Active Directory",
                        "Connecting to domain...",
                        out loadOverlayStatus,
                        out _);
                }
                catch { loadOverlay = null; loadOverlayStatus = null; }
            }

            void UiProgress(string msg)
            {
                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    SetStatus(msg);
                    SetProgressVisible(true);
                    if (loadOverlayStatus != null)
                        loadOverlayStatus.Text = msg;
                });
            }

            void CloseOverlay()
            {
                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    try { loadOverlay?.Close(); } catch { }
                    loadOverlay = null;
                });
            }

            var placeholder = new TreeViewItem { Header = "Connecting..." };
            tree.Items.Add(placeholder);

            // Watchdog (user report 2026-10-27): on a machine with no
            // reachable domain, the RootDSE lookup below can block inside
            // ADSI for minutes while Windows retries DC discovery - the
            // "Loading" overlay then never closes and the program looks
            // frozen. If the domain has not answered within 20 s, cancel the
            // load, close the overlay, and hand the UI back with an honest
            // status. The blocked lookup thread is abandoned, never awaited;
            // its late posts are suppressed by the cancellation token.
            bool loadDone = false;
            var loadWatchdog = new DispatcherTimer { Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20) };
            loadWatchdog.Tick += (_, _) =>
            {
                loadWatchdog.Stop();
                if (loadDone || token.IsCancellationRequested || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_domainDn)) return;
                _loadCts?.Cancel();
                try { loadOverlay?.Close(); } catch { }
                loadOverlay = null;
                SetProgressVisible(false);
                tree.Items.Clear();
                SetStatus("Could not reach a domain controller - ADE is not connected to a domain. Check the network and DNS, then press Refresh to try again.");
            };
            loadWatchdog.Start();

            Task.Run(() =>
            {
                var sw = System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.StartNew();
                try
                {
                    UiProgress("Connecting to domain (RootDSE)...");
                    string dn = "";
                    using (var root = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://RootDSE"))
                        dn = root.Properties["defaultNamingContext"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";

                    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn))
                    {
                        Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                        {
                            loadDone = true;
                            loadWatchdog.Stop();
                            SetProgressVisible(false);
                            SetStatus("Could not determine current domain.");
                            tree.Items.Clear();
                            try { loadOverlay?.Close(); } catch { }
                        });
                        return;
                    }

                    _domainDn = dn;
                    string domainName = dn.Replace("DC=", "").Replace(",", ".");
                    _dnsRoot = domainName; // DC=internal,DC=oln-afmc,... -> internal.oln-afmc.af.mil

                    UiProgress("Reading domain policy and DNS root...");
                    try
                    {
                        using var dom = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + dn);
                        try
                        {
                            string? dns = dom.Properties["dnsRoot"]?.Value?.ToString();
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dns))
                                _dnsRoot = dns.Trim();
                        }
                        catch { }
                        var rawAge = ReadLargeInteger(dom.Properties["maxPwdAge"]?.Value);
                        if (rawAge.HasValue && rawAge.Value != 0 && rawAge.Value != long.MaxValue)
                        {
                            long ticks = rawAge.Value < 0 ? -rawAge.Value : rawAge.Value;
                            _maxPwdAge = TimeSpan.FromTicks(ticks);
                        }
                        else _maxPwdAge = null;
                    }
                    catch { _maxPwdAge = null; }

                    var rootNode = new AdNode
                    {
                        Name = domainName,
                        Type = "Domain",
                        Dn = dn,
                        IsContainer = true
                    };

                    UiProgress("Loading top-level containers...");
                    // Top-level containers only → show tree ASAP
                    LoadContainersOf(rootNode, token);
                    if (token.IsCancellationRequested) { CloseOverlay(); return; }
                    _rootData = rootNode;

                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;
                        tree.Items.Clear();
                        var rootItem = BuildTreeItem(rootNode, true);
                        tree.Items.Add(rootItem);
                        rootItem.IsExpanded = true;
                        tree.SelectedItem = rootItem;
                        if (loadOverlayStatus != null)
                            loadOverlayStatus.Text = "Building dashboard and tree...";
                        SetStatus("Building dashboard...");
                        ShowContainerContents(rootNode);
                        SetStatus($"Top level ready ({rootNode.Children.Count} containers). Loading deeper OUs...");
                        // Allow typing searches as soon as top level is up
                        FocusSearchBox();
                        Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(FocusSearchBox, DispatcherPriority.Input);
                        if (loadOverlayStatus != null)
                            loadOverlayStatus.Text = "Loading OU structure under " + rootNode.Children.Count + " top containers...";
                    });

                    // Parallel deeper containers (same aggressive DOP as full load)
                    var topContainers = rootNode.Children.Where(c => c.IsContainer).ToList();
                    var parallelOpts = new ParallelOptions
                    {
                        MaxDegreeOfParallelism = Math.Max(8, Math.Min(32, Environment.ProcessorCount * 4)),
                        CancellationToken = token
                    };
                    try
                    {
                        Parallel.ForEach(topContainers, parallelOpts, child =>
                        {
                            if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;
                            LoadContainersRecursive(child, token);
                        });
                    }
                    catch (OperationCanceledException) { CloseOverlay(); return; }
                    catch (AggregateException aex) when (aex.InnerExceptions.All(e => e is OperationCanceledException))
                    { CloseOverlay(); return; }

                    if (token.IsCancellationRequested) { CloseOverlay(); return; }

                    sw.Stop();
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        loadDone = true;
                        loadWatchdog.Stop();
                        try { loadOverlay?.Close(); } catch { }
                        SetProgressVisible(false);
                        SetStatus($"Loaded in {sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds:F1}s");
                        var clearTimer = new DispatcherTimer { Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60) };
                        clearTimer.Tick += (_, __) =>
                        {
                            clearTimer.Stop();
                            var st = this.FindControl<TextBlock>("StatusText");
                            if (st != null && st.Text != null && st.Text.StartsWith("Loaded in", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                                st.Text = "";
                        };
                        clearTimer.Start();
                        if (_rootData != null && tree.SelectedItem == null)
                        {
                            if (tree.Items.Count > 0 && tree.Items[0] is TreeViewItem ri)
                            {
                                tree.SelectedItem = ri;
                                ShowContainerContents(_rootData);
                            }
                        }
                        // Ready for quick searches
                        FocusSearchBox();
                        Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(FocusSearchBox, DispatcherPriority.Input);
                    });
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    sw.Stop();
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        loadDone = true;
                        loadWatchdog.Stop();
                        try { loadOverlay?.Close(); } catch { }
                        SetProgressVisible(false);
                        SetStatus("Background load failed: " + ex.Message);
                        ShowNotification("Background load failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                    });
                }
            }, token);
        }

        private void LoadContainersOf(AdNode parent, CancellationToken token)
        {
            if (token.IsCancellationRequested || parent.ChildrenLoaded) return;
            try
            {
                using var de = BindLdap(parent.Dn);

                var containers = new List<AdNode>(32);
                foreach (DirectoryEntry entry in de.Children)
                {
                    if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;
                    using (entry)
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            bool isCont = false;
                            string typ = "Container";
                            try
                            {
                                var ocProp = entry.Properties["objectClass"];
                                if (ocProp != null && ocProp.Count > 0)
                                {
                                    for (int i = ocProp.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                                    {
                                        string c = ocProp[i]?.ToString() ?? "";
                                        if (c.Equals("organizationalUnit", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                        { typ = "OU"; isCont = true; break; }
                                        if (c.Equals("container", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                            c.Equals("builtinDomain", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                            c.Equals("lostAndFound", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                        { typ = "Container"; isCont = true; break; }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            catch { }
                            if (!isCont) continue;

                            string name = entry.Name;
                            if (name.StartsWith("CN=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                name.StartsWith("OU=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                name = name.Substring(3);

                            string edn;
                            try { edn = entry.Properties["distinguishedName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? ""; }
                            catch { edn = ""; }
                            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(edn))
                            {
                                try { edn = entry.Path.StartsWith("LDAP://", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? entry.Path.Substring(7) : entry.Path; }
                                catch { continue; }
                            }
                            if (string.Equals(edn, parent.Dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;

                            containers.Add(new AdNode
                            {
                                Name = name,
                                Type = typ,
                                Dn = edn,
                                IsContainer = true,
                                Parent = parent
                            });
                            Interlocked.Increment(ref _loadedCount);
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }
                }

                foreach (var c in containers
                    .OrderBy(x => x.Type == "OU" ? 0 : 1)
                    .ThenBy(x => x.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    parent.Children.Add(c);
                    lock (_flatNodes) { _flatNodes.Add(c); }
                }
                parent.ChildrenLoaded = true;

                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    if (parent.UiItem == null) return;
                    var toRemove = parent.UiItem.Items.OfType<TreeViewItem>()
                        .Where(i => i.Tag is string s && s == "placeholder").ToList();
                    foreach (var r in toRemove) parent.UiItem.Items.Remove(r);
                    foreach (var c in parent.Children.Where(x => x.IsContainer))
                    {
                        if (parent.UiItem.Items.OfType<TreeViewItem>().Any(i => i.Tag == c)) continue;
                        parent.UiItem.Items.Add(BuildTreeItem(c, false));
                    }
                });
            }
            catch { /* Load failed: leave ChildrenLoaded false so the next expand retries. */ }
        }

        private void LoadContainersRecursive(AdNode parent, CancellationToken token)
        {
            LoadContainersOf(parent, token);
            if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;

            var sub = parent.Children.Where(c => c.IsContainer).ToList();
            if (sub.Count == 0) return;

            var opts = new ParallelOptions
            {
                MaxDegreeOfParallelism = Math.Max(4, Math.Min(32, Environment.ProcessorCount * 4)),
                CancellationToken = token
            };
            try
            {
                Parallel.ForEach(sub, opts, child =>
                {
                    if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;
                    LoadContainersRecursive(child, token);
                });
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) { }
            catch (AggregateException) { }
        }


        private TreeViewItem BuildTreeItem(AdNode node, bool isTopLevel)
        {
            var sp = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 6 };
            sp.Children.Add(MakeMaterialIcon(node.Type, 16));
            sp.Children.Add(Tb(node.Name, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));

            var item = new TreeViewItem { Header = sp, Tag = node };
            node.UiItem = item;

            if (node.ChildrenLoaded)
            {
                foreach (var child in node.Children.Where(c => c.IsContainer))
                    item.Items.Add(BuildTreeItem(child, false));
            }
            else if (node.IsContainer)
            {
                var ph = new TreeViewItem
                {
                    Header = "Loading...",
                    Tag = "placeholder",
                    IsEnabled = false
                };
                item.Items.Add(ph);
            }

            return item;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // EXPAND / COLLAPSE ALL
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        private void OnExpandAll(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var tree = this.FindControl<TreeView>("AdTree");
            if (tree == null) return;
            foreach (var item in tree.Items.OfType<TreeViewItem>())
                SetExpandedRecursive(item, true);
            SetStatus("All nodes expanded.");
        }

        private void OnCollapseAll(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var tree = this.FindControl<TreeView>("AdTree");
            if (tree == null) return;
            foreach (var item in tree.Items.OfType<TreeViewItem>())
            {
                // Never collapse the root domain node
                item.IsExpanded = true;
                foreach (var child in item.Items.OfType<TreeViewItem>())
                    SetExpandedRecursive(child, false);
            }
            SetStatus("All nodes collapsed (root remains expanded).");
        }

        private static void SetExpandedRecursive(TreeViewItem item, bool expanded)
        {
            item.IsExpanded = expanded;
            foreach (var child in item.Items.OfType<TreeViewItem>())
                SetExpandedRecursive(child, expanded);
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SELECTION / DETAILS PANEL
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        private void OnSelectionChanged(object? sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!_isInitialized ||
                sender is not TreeView tv ||
                tv.SelectedItem is not TreeViewItem tvi ||
                tvi.Tag is not AdNode node)
                return;

            // Containers list their children on the right (DSA.msc). Leaves open properties.
            if (node.IsContainer)
                ShowContainerContents(node);
            else
                ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
        }

        private void OnSearchTextChanged(object? sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            ScheduleLiveSearch(immediate: false);
        }

        private void OnSearch(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            // Search button / Enter path: run immediately with current text
            ScheduleLiveSearch(immediate: true);
        }

        private void OnClearSearch(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var tb = this.FindControl<TextBox>("SearchBox");
            if (tb != null) tb.Text = "";
            ScheduleLiveSearch(immediate: true);
        }

        // Keyboard navigation for live search results (Up / Down / Enter)
        private List<AdNode> _searchMatches = new();
        private int _searchSelectedIndex = -1;
        private List<Button> _searchResultButtons = new();

        private void OnSearchKeyDown(object? sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
            {
                e.Handled = true;
                GoHomeAndFocusSearch();
                return;
            }
            if (e.Key == Key.Down)
            {
                e.Handled = true;
                if (_searchMatches.Count == 0)
                {
                    ScheduleLiveSearch(immediate: true);
                    return;
                }
                MoveSearchSelection(1);
                return;
            }
            if (e.Key == Key.Up)
            {
                e.Handled = true;
                if (_searchMatches.Count == 0) return;
                MoveSearchSelection(-1);
                return;
            }
            if (e.Key == Key.Enter)
            {
                e.Handled = true;
                if (_searchMatches.Count > 0 && _searchSelectedIndex >= 0
                    && _searchSelectedIndex < _searchMatches.Count)
                {
                    ShowHelpDeskInfo(_searchMatches[_searchSelectedIndex]);
                    return;
                }
                if (_searchMatches.Count == 1)
                {
                    ShowHelpDeskInfo(_searchMatches[0]);
                    return;
                }
                ScheduleLiveSearch(immediate: true);
            }
        }

        private void MoveSearchSelection(int delta)
        {
            if (_searchMatches.Count == 0) return;
            if (_searchSelectedIndex < 0)
                _searchSelectedIndex = delta > 0 ? 0 : _searchMatches.Count - 1;
            else
                _searchSelectedIndex = (_searchSelectedIndex + delta + _searchMatches.Count) % _searchMatches.Count;
            HighlightSearchSelection();
        }

        private void HighlightSearchSelection()
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < _searchResultButtons.Count; i++)
            {
                var btn = _searchResultButtons[i];
                if (i == _searchSelectedIndex)
                {
                    btn.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(80, 80, 160, 255));
                    btn.BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(80, 160, 255));
                    btn.BorderThickness = new Thickness(1);
                }
                else
                {
                    btn.Background = Brushes.Transparent;
                    btn.BorderThickness = new Thickness(0);
                }
            }
            if (_searchSelectedIndex >= 0 && _searchSelectedIndex < _searchMatches.Count)
            {
                var n = _searchMatches[_searchSelectedIndex];
                SetStatus("Selected: " + n.Type + " \"" + n.Name + "\"  (Enter to open, Up/Down to move)");
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Debounce live search. Fast typing only schedules one query after a short pause.
        /// immediate=true (Search button / Enter / clear) runs now with the current box text.
        /// </summary>
        private void ScheduleLiveSearch(bool immediate)
        {
            if (_searchDebounceTimer == null)
            {
                _searchDebounceTimer = new DispatcherTimer
                {
                    Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(350)
                };
                _searchDebounceTimer.Tick += (_, _) =>
                {
                    _searchDebounceTimer.Stop();
                    ExecuteLiveSearch();
                };
            }

            _searchDebounceTimer.Stop();
            if (immediate)
                ExecuteLiveSearch();
            else
                _searchDebounceTimer.Start();
        }

        private void ExecuteLiveSearch()
        {
            var tb = this.FindControl<TextBox>("SearchBox");
            // Always re-read the box so the final typed text is used
            string raw = tb?.Text?.Trim() ?? "";
            _currentFilter = raw;

            // Cancel any in-flight LDAP search
            try { _searchCts?.Cancel(); } catch { }
            try { _searchCts?.Dispose(); } catch { }
            _searchCts = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var token = _searchCts.Token;
            int seq = ++_searchSeq;

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(raw))
            {
                _isSearching = false;
                SetProgressVisible(false);
                SetStatus($"Showing containers. {_loadedCount:N0} containers loaded.");
                if (_rootData != null) ShowContainerContents(_rootData);
                return;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_domainDn))
            {
                _isSearching = false;
                SetProgressVisible(false);
                SetStatus("Domain not ready yet.");
                return;
            }

            _isSearching = true;
            SetStatus($"Searching for \"{raw}\"...");
            SetProgressVisible(true);
            string esc = EscapeLdap(raw);
            string domainDn = _domainDn;

            Task.Run(() =>
            {
                var matches = new List<AdNode>();
                try
                {
                    if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;

                    using var rootEntry = BindLdap(domainDn);

                    // One combined search (was three separate subtree searches,
                    // one per object type). The type comes from each result's
                    // objectClass; per-type counters keep the same 30-per-type
                    // cap the separate searches had.
                    try
                    {
                        using var s = new DirectorySearcher(rootEntry)
                        {
                            Filter = $"(&(|(&(objectCategory=person)(objectClass=user))(&(objectClass=group))(&(objectClass=computer)))(|(sAMAccountName={esc}*)(cn={esc}*)(name={esc}*)(displayName={esc}*)))",
                            SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                            SizeLimit = 0,
                            PageSize = 100,
                            ClientTimeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3),
                            ServerTimeLimit = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(8),
                            PropertiesToLoad = { "name", "cn", "distinguishedName", "sAMAccountName", "displayName", "objectClass" }
                        };
                        using var r = s.FindAll();
                        int nUser = 0, nGroup = 0, nComputer = 0;
                        foreach (SearchResult sr in r)
                        {
                            if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;
                            if (nUser >= 30 && nGroup >= 30 && nComputer >= 30) break;
                            try
                            {
                                string dn = Prop(sr, "distinguishedName");
                                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn)) continue;
                                bool isComputer = false, isGroup = false;
                                try
                                {
                                    if (sr.Properties.Contains("objectClass"))
                                        foreach (object oc in sr.Properties["objectClass"])
                                        {
                                            string ocs = oc?.ToString() ?? "";
                                            if (string.Equals(ocs, "computer", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) isComputer = true;
                                            else if (string.Equals(ocs, "group", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) isGroup = true;
                                        }
                                }
                                catch { }
                                string type = isComputer ? "Computer" : (isGroup ? "Group" : "User");
                                if (type == "User") { if (nUser >= 30) continue; nUser++; }
                                else if (type == "Group") { if (nGroup >= 30) continue; nGroup++; }
                                else { if (nComputer >= 30) continue; nComputer++; }
                                string name = Prop(sr, "displayName");
                                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) name = Prop(sr, "name");
                                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) name = Prop(sr, "cn");
                                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) name = dn;
                                matches.Add(new AdNode
                                {
                                    Name = name,
                                    Type = type,
                                    Dn = dn,
                                    SamAccountName = Prop(sr, "sAMAccountName"),
                                    IsContainer = false
                                });
                            }
                            catch { }
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }
                }
                catch { }

                if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;

                matches = matches
