                    .GroupBy(n => n.Dn, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    .Select(g => g.First())
                    .OrderBy(n => n.Type == "User" ? 0 : n.Type == "Group" ? 1 : 2)
                    .ThenBy(n => n.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    .Take(25)
                    .ToList();

                string querySnapshot = raw;
                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    // Ignore stale results from an older keystroke
                    if (seq != _searchSeq) return;

                    _isSearching = false;
                    SetProgressVisible(false);

                    // Re-check box in case user typed more after this query started
                    var boxNow = this.FindControl<TextBox>("SearchBox");
                    string now = boxNow?.Text?.Trim() ?? "";
                    if (!string.Equals(now, querySnapshot, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        // Text changed again; schedule another search for the latest value
                        ScheduleLiveSearch(immediate: false);
                        return;
                    }

                    if (matches.Count == 0)
                    {
                        SetStatus($"No users, computers, or groups start with \"{querySnapshot}\"");
                        ShowSearchResults(querySnapshot, matches);
                    }
                    else if (matches.Count == 1)
                    {
                        // Only auto-open when the query is "settled" (debounce already waited)
                        var only = matches[0];
                        SetStatus($"1 match - opening {only.Type} \"{only.Name}\"");
                        ShowHelpDeskInfo(only);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SetStatus($"{matches.Count} match(es) for \"{querySnapshot}\". Click or use Up/Down + Enter.");
                        ShowSearchResults(querySnapshot, matches);
                    }
                });
            }, token);
        }

        private void ShowSearchResults(string raw, List<AdNode> matches)
        {
            RecordNavigation("search", null);
            var panel = this.FindControl<StackPanel>("DetailPanel");
            if (panel == null) return;
            panel.Children.Clear();
            _searchMatches = matches ?? new List<AdNode>();
            _searchSelectedIndex = _searchMatches.Count > 0 ? 0 : -1;
            _searchResultButtons = new List<Button>();

            panel.Children.Add(Tb($"Search: {raw}", size: 20, weight: FontWeight.Bold, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));
            panel.Children.Add(Tb("Use Up / Down to select, Enter to open", size: 12, opacity: 0.7, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));
            if (_searchMatches.Count == 0)
            {
                panel.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "No matches.", Opacity = 0.7, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left });
                return;
            }
            var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };
            foreach (var c in _searchMatches)
            {
                var btn = new Button
                {
                    Content = MakeIconLine(c.Type, FormatObjectLine(c), 14, 14),
                    Classes = { "memberLink" },
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                    Tag = c,
                    Background = Brushes.Transparent
                };
                var captured = c;
                btn.Click += (_, _) => ShowHelpDeskInfo(captured);
                list.Children.Add(btn);
                _searchResultButtons.Add(btn);
            }
            panel.Children.Add(new Border { Classes = { "card" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, Child = list });
            HighlightSearchSelection();
        }

        private void RecordNavigation(string kind, AdNode? node)
        {
            if (_navSilent) return;
            if (_currentNav != null)
            {
                var cur = _currentNav.Value;
                // Avoid stacking identical consecutive views
                if (cur.Kind == kind)
                {
                    if (cur.Node == null && node == null) { _currentNav = (kind, node); return; }
                    if (cur.Node != null && node != null
                        && string.Equals(cur.Node.Dn, node.Dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        _currentNav = (kind, node);
                        return;
                    }
                }
                _navHistory.Push(cur);
                // Cap history depth
                if (_navHistory.Count > 40)
                {
                    var tmp = _navHistory.ToArray().Take(30).Reverse().ToList();
                    _navHistory.Clear();
                    foreach (var x in tmp) _navHistory.Push(x);
                }
            }
            _currentNav = (kind, node);
            UpdateBreadcrumbBar(node);
        }

        private void UpdateBreadcrumbBar(AdNode? node)
        {
            var bar = this.FindControl<StackPanel>("BreadcrumbPanel");
            if (bar == null) return;
            bar.Children.Clear();

            void AddSep()
            {
                bar.Children.Add(Tb(" / ", size: 12, opacity: 0.55, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));
            }

            // Home crumb
            var homeBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Home",
                Classes = { "action" },
                Padding = new Thickness(6, 2),
                FontSize = 12,
                Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand)
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(homeBtn, "Environment overview");
            homeBtn.Click += (_, _) => OnHome(null, new RoutedEventArgs());
            bar.Children.Add(homeBtn);

            if (node == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(node.Dn))
                return;

            // Build path segments from DN (leaf first in string, reverse to root→leaf)
            var rawParts = node.Dn.Split(',')
                .Select(s => s.Trim())
                .Where(s => s.Length > 0)
                .ToList();
            if (rawParts.Count == 0) return;

            // Cumulative DN for each level from root to current
            var levels = new List<(string Label, string Dn)>();
            for (int i = rawParts.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            {
                string dn = string.Join(",", rawParts.Skip(i));
                string part = rawParts[i];
                string label = part;
                int eq = part.IndexOf('=');
                if (eq >= 0 && eq + 1 < part.Length)
                    label = part.Substring(eq + 1);
                // Domain DC components: collapse into one domain label at the root
                if (part.StartsWith("DC=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    // Merge all trailing DCs into one domain crumb at the start
                    continue;
                }
                levels.Add((label, dn));
            }

            // Domain as first AD crumb
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_domainDn))
            {
                string domainLabel = _domainDn.Replace("DC=", "").Replace(",", ".");
                AddSep();
                var domBtn = new Button
                {
                    Content = domainLabel,
                    Classes = { "action" },
                    Padding = new Thickness(6, 2),
                    FontSize = 12,
                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand)
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(domBtn, _domainDn);
                string domainDnCap = _domainDn;
                domBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
                {
                    if (_rootData != null)
                        NavigateFromBreadcrumb(_rootData);
                    else
                        OnHome(null, new RoutedEventArgs());
                };
                bar.Children.Add(domBtn);
            }

            // Intermediate OUs / CN path (exclude leaf if object  -  show parent path + leaf non-click or click)
            for (int i = 0; i < levels.Count; i++)
            {
                var (label, dn) = levels[i];
                bool isLeaf = (i == levels.Count - 1);
                AddSep();
                if (isLeaf)
                {
                    bar.Children.Add(Tb(label, size: 12, weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, opacity: 0.95, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));
                }
                else
                {
                    var b = new Button
                    {
                        Content = label,
                        Classes = { "action" },
                        Padding = new Thickness(6, 2),
                        FontSize = 12,
                        Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                        BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                        Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand)
                    };
                    ToolTip.SetTip(b, dn);
                    string dnCap = dn;
                    string labelCap = label;
                    b.Click += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        var target = FindNodeByDn(dnCap);
                        if (target != null)
                            NavigateFromBreadcrumb(target);
                        else
                        {
                            // Synthetic container node for jump
                            var synth = new AdNode
                            {
                                Name = labelCap,
                                Dn = dnCap,
                                Type = dnCap.StartsWith("OU=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? "OU" : "Container",
                                IsContainer = true
                            };
                            NavigateFromBreadcrumb(synth);
                        }
                    };
                    bar.Children.Add(b);
                }
            }
        }

        private AdNode? FindNodeByDn(string dn)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dn)) return null;
            if (_rootData != null && string.Equals(_rootData.Dn, dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return _rootData;
            lock (_flatNodes)
            {
                foreach (var n in _flatNodes)
                {
                    if (string.Equals(n.Dn, dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        return n;
                }
            }
            // Walk tree
            AdNode? Walk(AdNode n)
            {
                if (string.Equals(n.Dn, dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return n;
                foreach (var c in n.Children)
                {
                    var f = Walk(c);
                    if (f != null) return f;
                }
                return null;
            }
            return _rootData != null ? Walk(_rootData) : null;
        }

        private void NavigateFromBreadcrumb(AdNode node)
        {
            try
            {
                _navSilent = false; // allow recording from current → target
                if (node.IsContainer || node.Type == "Domain" || node.Type == "OU" || node.Type == "Container")
                {
                    // Select in tree when possible
                    try
                    {
                        if (node.UiItem != null)
                        {
                            var tree = this.FindControl<TreeView>("AdTree");
                            if (tree != null) tree.SelectedItem = node.UiItem;
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }
                    ShowContainerContents(node);
                }
                else
                {
                    ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowNotification("Navigate failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
            }
        }

        private void OnHome(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                // Record current before home if needed
                if (!_navSilent && _currentNav != null && _currentNav.Value.Kind != "home")
                    _navHistory.Push(_currentNav.Value);
                _navSilent = true;
                try
                {
                    _currentNav = ("home", _rootData);
                    UpdateBreadcrumbBar(_rootData);
                    GoHomeAndFocusSearch();
                }
                finally { _navSilent = false; }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowNotification("Home failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
            }
        }

        private void OnBack(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                if (_navHistory.Count == 0)
                {
                    OnHome(sender, e);
                    return;
                }
                var prev = _navHistory.Pop();
                _navSilent = true;
                try
                {
                    _currentNav = prev;
                    if (prev.Kind == "home" || prev.Node == null)
                    {
                        UpdateBreadcrumbBar(_rootData);
                        if (_rootData != null)
                            ShowContainerContents(_rootData);
                        else
                            GoHomeAndFocusSearch();
                        FocusSearchBox();
                        return;
                    }
                    UpdateBreadcrumbBar(prev.Node);
                    if (prev.Kind == "container" || prev.Node.IsContainer
                        || prev.Node.Type == "Domain" || prev.Node.Type == "OU" || prev.Node.Type == "Container")
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            if (prev.Node.UiItem != null)
                            {
                                var tree = this.FindControl<TreeView>("AdTree");
                                if (tree != null) tree.SelectedItem = prev.Node.UiItem;
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }
                        ShowContainerContents(prev.Node);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        ShowHelpDeskInfo(prev.Node);
                    }
                }
                finally { _navSilent = false; }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowNotification("Back failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
            }
        }


        private void OnRefresh(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var tb = this.FindControl<TextBox>("SearchBox");
            if (tb != null) tb.Text = "";
            _currentFilter = "";
            _statsLoaded = false;
            InvalidateDomainSnapshot();
            _navHistory.Clear();
            _currentNav = null;
            BeginProgressiveLoad(showProgress: true);
        }

        private void UpdateThemeButtonIcon()
        {
            var btn = this.FindControl<Button>("ThemeBtn");
            if (btn == null) return;
            var app = Application.Current;
            bool isLight = app != null && app.RequestedThemeVariant == Avalonia.Styling.ThemeVariant.Light;
            // Moon = switch to dark, Sun = switch to light
            btn.Content = MakeMdi(isLight ? "mdi-weather-night" : "mdi-weather-sunny", 16);
            btn.HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center;
            btn.VerticalContentAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
            ToolTip.SetTip(btn, isLight
                ? "Switch to Midnight Navy (dark)"
                : "Switch to Sky Ice (light)");
        }

        private void ApplyPreferredTheme()
        {
            var app = Application.Current;
            if (app == null) return;
            bool light = string.Equals(_preferredTheme, "Light", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            app.RequestedThemeVariant = light
                ? Avalonia.Styling.ThemeVariant.Light
                : Avalonia.Styling.ThemeVariant.Dark;
            UpdateThemeButtonIcon();
        }

        private void OnToggleTheme(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var app = Application.Current;
            if (app == null) return;
            bool goingLight = app.RequestedThemeVariant != Avalonia.Styling.ThemeVariant.Light;
            app.RequestedThemeVariant = goingLight
                ? Avalonia.Styling.ThemeVariant.Light
                : Avalonia.Styling.ThemeVariant.Dark;
            _preferredTheme = goingLight ? "Light" : "Dark";
            SaveThemePreference();
            SaveAppSettings();
            UpdateThemeButtonIcon();
            ShowNotification(
                goingLight
                    ? "Light theme: Sky Ice"
                    : "Dark theme: Midnight Navy",
                "info");
        }

    }
}
'@
$mwDashboardCs = @'
// ADE - MainWindow partial class: domain statistics, overview panels, stale objects and OS groupings.
// Split from MainWindow.axaml.cs for maintainability (2026-10-26):
// same partial class, same namespace - no behavior change.
using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.DirectoryServices;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Net.Security;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Security.Principal;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Avalonia;
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Controls.Primitives;
using Avalonia.Input;
using Avalonia.Interactivity;
using Avalonia.Layout;
using Avalonia.Markup.Xaml;
using Avalonia.Media;
using Avalonia.Threading;

namespace __APPNAME__
{
    public partial class MainWindow : Window
    {
        private bool _statsLoaded;
        private DateTime? _statsLastRefresh;
        private int _statUsers, _statDisabled, _statLocked, _statExpired, _statMustChange, _statPwdNever, _statStaleUsers, _statOrgBoxes, _statDoNotDelete;
        private int _statComputers, _statServers, _statWorkstations, _statDisabledComputers, _statStaleComputers;
        private int _statGroups, _statSecGroups, _statDistGroups, _statEmptyGroups;

        // ---- Shared domain snapshot -------------------------------------
        // One enumeration of users / computers / groups, shared by the
        // overview counts and most drill-down panels (they used to rescan
        // the whole domain separately - a dozen full scans per session).
        // Cached 60 seconds, keyed by domain; a snapshot with a failed
        // section is handed to its caller but never cached, so the next
        // open retries. Deletes and the overview Refresh invalidate it
        // immediately (same call sites as the server-computer cache).
        private sealed class DomainSnapshot
        {
            public List<(string Name, string Cn, string Display, string Sam, string Dn, int Uac, long LockoutTime, long? PwdLastSet, DateTime? LastLogon, DateTime WhenChanged, string Description, string Mail, byte[]? Sid, object? Crit)> Users = new();
            public List<(string Name, string Cn, string Display, string Sam, string Dn, int Uac, string Os, byte[]? Sid, object? Crit)> Computers = new();
            public List<(string Name, string Cn, string Display, string Sam, string Dn, int GroupType, byte[]? Sid, object? Crit)> Groups = new();
            public (int Users, int Disabled, int Locked, int Expired, int MustChange, int PwdNever, int StaleUsers, int OrgBoxes, int DoNotDelete) UserCounts;
            public (int Computers, int Servers, int Workstations, int DisabledComputers, int StaleComputers) ComputerCounts;
            public (int Groups, int SecGroups, int DistGroups, int EmptyGroups) GroupCounts;
            public bool UsersOk, ComputersOk, GroupsOk;
            public bool AllOk => UsersOk && ComputersOk && GroupsOk;
        }

        private DomainSnapshot? _domainSnapshot;
        private DateTime _domainSnapshotUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
        private string _domainSnapshotDomain = "";
        private Task<DomainSnapshot>? _domainSnapshotTask;
        private readonly object _domainSnapshotLock = new();

        private void InvalidateDomainSnapshot()
        {
            lock (_domainSnapshotLock) { _domainSnapshot = null; }
        }

        private Task<DomainSnapshot> GetDomainSnapshotAsync()
        {
            lock (_domainSnapshotLock)
            {
                if (_domainSnapshot != null
                    && string.Equals(_domainSnapshotDomain, _domainDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    && (DateTime.UtcNow - _domainSnapshotUtc) < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60))
                    return Task.FromResult(_domainSnapshot);
                if (_domainSnapshotTask != null && !_domainSnapshotTask.IsCompleted)
                    return _domainSnapshotTask;
                string dn = _domainDn;
                TimeSpan? maxPwd = _maxPwdAge;
                int staleDays = Math.Max(1, _staleComputerDays);
                _domainSnapshotTask = Task.Run(() => BuildDomainSnapshot(dn, maxPwd, staleDays));
                return _domainSnapshotTask;
            }
        }

        private DomainSnapshot BuildDomainSnapshot(string domainDn, TimeSpan? maxPwd, int staleDays)
        {
            var snap = new DomainSnapshot();
            DateTime staleCutoff = DateTime.Now.AddDays(-staleDays);

            DirectoryEntry OpenRoot() => BindLdap(domainDn);

            DirectorySearcher MakeSearcher(DirectoryEntry root, string filter, params string[] props)
            {
                var s = new DirectorySearcher(root)
                {
                    Filter = filter,
                    SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                    PageSize = 1000,
                    SizeLimit = 0,
                    CacheResults = false,
                    ReferralChasing = ReferralChasingOption.None,
                    ClientTimeout = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5),
                    ServerTimeLimit = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5)
                };
                foreach (string p in props)
                    s.PropertiesToLoad.Add(p);
                return s;
            }

            string Str(SearchResult sr, string p)
            {
                try
                {
                    if (sr.Properties.Contains(p) && sr.Properties[p].Count > 0)
                        return sr.Properties[p][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                }
                catch { }
                return "";
            }

            byte[]? SidOf(SearchResult sr)
            {
                try
                {
                    if (sr.Properties.Contains("objectSid") && sr.Properties["objectSid"].Count > 0)
                        return sr.Properties["objectSid"][0] as byte[];
                }
                catch { }
                return null;
            }

            object? CritOf(SearchResult sr)
            {
                try
                {
                    if (sr.Properties.Contains("isCriticalSystemObject") && sr.Properties["isCriticalSystemObject"].Count > 0)
                        return sr.Properties["isCriticalSystemObject"][0];
                }
                catch { }
                return null;
            }

            DateTime WhenChangedOf(SearchResult sr)
            {
                try
                {
                    if (sr.Properties.Contains("whenChanged") && sr.Properties["whenChanged"].Count > 0)
                    {
                        var raw = sr.Properties["whenChanged"][0];
                        if (raw is DateTime dt) return dt;
                        if (DateTime.TryParse(raw?.ToString(), out var parsed)) return parsed;
                    }
                }
                catch { }
                return DateTime.MinValue;
            }

            var usersTask = Task.Run(() =>
            {
                int users = 0, disabled = 0, locked = 0, expired = 0, mustChange = 0, pwdNever = 0, staleUsers = 0, orgBoxes = 0, doNotDelete = 0;
                var list = new List<(string Name, string Cn, string Display, string Sam, string Dn, int Uac, long LockoutTime, long? PwdLastSet, DateTime? LastLogon, DateTime WhenChanged, string Description, string Mail, byte[]? Sid, object? Crit)>();
                bool ok = true;
                try
                {
                    using var rootEntry = OpenRoot();
                    using var s = MakeSearcher(rootEntry,
                        "(&(objectCategory=person)(objectClass=user))",
                        "userAccountControl", "lockoutTime", "pwdLastSet", "lastLogonTimestamp",
                        "sAMAccountName", "displayName", "cn", "name", "description", "mail",
                        "distinguishedName", "objectSid", "isCriticalSystemObject", "whenChanged");
                    using var r = s.FindAll();
                    foreach (SearchResult sr in r)
                    {
                        users++;
                        int uac = 0;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("userAccountControl") && sr.Properties["userAccountControl"].Count > 0)
                                uac = Convert.ToInt32(sr.Properties["userAccountControl"][0]);
                            if ((uac & 0x2) != 0) disabled++;
                            if ((uac & 0x10000) != 0) pwdNever++;
                        }
                        catch { }
                        long lt = 0;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("lockoutTime") && sr.Properties["lockoutTime"].Count > 0)
                            {
                                lt = ReadLargeInteger(sr.Properties["lockoutTime"][0]) ?? 0;
                                if (lt != 0) locked++;
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }
                        long? pls = null;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("pwdLastSet") && sr.Properties["pwdLastSet"].Count > 0)
                                pls = ReadLargeInteger(sr.Properties["pwdLastSet"][0]);
                            if (pls.HasValue && pls.Value == 0) mustChange++;
                            bool never = (uac & 0x10000) != 0;
                            if (!never && maxPwd.HasValue && maxPwd.Value.TotalDays > 0
                                && pls.HasValue && pls.Value > 0)
                            {
                                var setOn = DateTime.FromFileTime(pls.Value);
                                if (setOn + maxPwd.Value < DateTime.Now)
                                    expired++;
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }
                        DateTime? last = null;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("lastLogonTimestamp") && sr.Properties["lastLogonTimestamp"].Count > 0)
                            {
                                long? ts = ReadLargeInteger(sr.Properties["lastLogonTimestamp"][0]);
                                if (ts.HasValue && ts.Value > 0)
                                    last = DateTime.FromFileTimeUtc(ts.Value).ToLocalTime();
                            }
                            if ((uac & 0x2) == 0 && (!last.HasValue || last.Value < staleCutoff))
                                staleUsers++;
                        }
                        catch { }
                        string sam = Str(sr, "sAMAccountName");
                        string display = Str(sr, "displayName");
                        string cn = Str(sr, "cn");
                        string name = Str(sr, "name");
                        string desc = Str(sr, "description");
                        string mail = Str(sr, "mail");
                        try
                        {
                            if (IsOrgBoxAccount(sam, display, cn, name, desc, mail)) orgBoxes++;
                            if (DescriptionSaysDoNotDelete(desc)) doNotDelete++;
                        }
                        catch { }
                        string dn2 = Str(sr, "distinguishedName");
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn2))
                            list.Add((name, cn, display, sam, dn2, uac, lt, pls, last, WhenChangedOf(sr), desc, mail, SidOf(sr), CritOf(sr)));
                    }
                }
                catch { ok = false; }
                return (Counts: (users, disabled, locked, expired, mustChange, pwdNever, staleUsers, orgBoxes, doNotDelete), List: list, Ok: ok);
            });

            var computersTask = Task.Run(() =>
            {
                int computers = 0, servers = 0, workstations = 0, disabledComputers = 0, staleComputers = 0;
                var list = new List<(string Name, string Cn, string Display, string Sam, string Dn, int Uac, string Os, byte[]? Sid, object? Crit)>();
                bool ok = true;
                try
                {
                    using var rootEntry = OpenRoot();
                    using var s = MakeSearcher(rootEntry,
                        "(objectClass=computer)",
                        "userAccountControl", "operatingSystem", "lastLogonTimestamp", "distinguishedName",
                        "name", "cn", "displayName", "sAMAccountName", "objectSid", "isCriticalSystemObject");
                    using var r = s.FindAll();
                    foreach (SearchResult sr in r)
                    {
                        computers++;
                        int uac = 0;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("userAccountControl") && sr.Properties["userAccountControl"].Count > 0)
                                uac = Convert.ToInt32(sr.Properties["userAccountControl"][0]);
                            if ((uac & 0x2) != 0) disabledComputers++;
                        }
                        catch { }
                        string os = Str(sr, "operatingSystem");
                        string dn2 = Str(sr, "distinguishedName");
                        bool isServer = IsServerComputer(os, dn2);
                        if (isServer) servers++;
                        else workstations++;
                        DateTime? last = null;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("lastLogonTimestamp") && sr.Properties["lastLogonTimestamp"].Count > 0)
                            {
                                long? ts = ReadLargeInteger(sr.Properties["lastLogonTimestamp"][0]);
                                if (ts.HasValue && ts.Value > 0)
                                    last = DateTime.FromFileTimeUtc(ts.Value).ToLocalTime();
                            }
                            if (!last.HasValue || last.Value < staleCutoff)
                                staleComputers++;
                        }
                        catch { }
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn2))
                            list.Add((Str(sr, "name"), Str(sr, "cn"), Str(sr, "displayName"), Str(sr, "sAMAccountName"), dn2, uac, os, SidOf(sr), CritOf(sr)));
                    }
                }
                catch { ok = false; }
                return (Counts: (computers, servers, workstations, disabledComputers, staleComputers), List: list, Ok: ok);
            });

            var groupsTask = Task.Run(() =>
            {
                int groups = 0, secGroups = 0, distGroups = 0, emptyGroups = 0;
                var list = new List<(string Name, string Cn, string Display, string Sam, string Dn, int GroupType, byte[]? Sid, object? Crit)>();
                bool ok = true;
                try
                {
                    using var rootEntry = OpenRoot();
                    using var s = MakeSearcher(rootEntry,
                        "(objectClass=group)",
                        "groupType", "member", "distinguishedName", "sAMAccountName", "displayName", "name", "cn", "objectSid", "isCriticalSystemObject");
                    using var r = s.FindAll();
                    foreach (SearchResult sr in r)
                    {
                        groups++;
                        int gt = 0;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("groupType") && sr.Properties["groupType"].Count > 0)
                                gt = Convert.ToInt32(sr.Properties["groupType"][0]);
                            // ADS_GROUP_TYPE_SECURITY_ENABLED = 0x80000000
                            if ((gt & unchecked((int)0x80000000)) != 0) secGroups++;
                            else distGroups++;
                        }
                        catch { secGroups++; gt = unchecked((int)0x80000000); }
                        try
                        {
                            // member is multi-valued; any returned value means not empty.
                            // (Primary-group-only members e.g. Domain Users are not in member - same as ADUC.)
                            if (!sr.Properties.Contains("member") || sr.Properties["member"].Count == 0)
                                emptyGroups++;
                        }
                        catch { }
                        string dn2 = Str(sr, "distinguishedName");
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn2))
                            list.Add((Str(sr, "name"), Str(sr, "cn"), Str(sr, "displayName"), Str(sr, "sAMAccountName"), dn2, gt, SidOf(sr), CritOf(sr)));
                    }
                }
                catch
                {
                    ok = false;
                    list.Clear();
                    // Fallback: separate count queries (still domain-wide Subtree).
                    // Counts restart from zero - the original code kept counting
                    // on top of the partial enumeration, double-counting groups
                    // when a scan failed partway through.
                    try
                    {
                        groups = 0; secGroups = 0; distGroups = 0; emptyGroups = 0;
                        using var rootEntry = OpenRoot();
                        using (var s = MakeSearcher(rootEntry, "(objectClass=group)", "cn"))
                        using (var r = s.FindAll())
                            foreach (SearchResult _ in r) groups++;
                        using (var s = MakeSearcher(rootEntry, "(&(objectClass=group)(!(member=*)))", "cn"))
                        using (var r = s.FindAll())
                            foreach (SearchResult _ in r) emptyGroups++;
                        secGroups = groups; // unknown split on fallback
                    }
                    catch { }
                }
                return (Counts: (groups, secGroups, distGroups, emptyGroups), List: list, Ok: ok);
            });

            try { Task.WaitAll(usersTask, computersTask, groupsTask); } catch { }
            var u = usersTask.Result;
            var c = computersTask.Result;
            var g = groupsTask.Result;
            snap.Users = u.List; snap.UserCounts = u.Counts; snap.UsersOk = u.Ok;
            snap.Computers = c.List; snap.ComputerCounts = c.Counts; snap.ComputersOk = c.Ok;
            snap.Groups = g.List; snap.GroupCounts = g.Counts; snap.GroupsOk = g.Ok;
            if (snap.AllOk)
            {
                lock (_domainSnapshotLock)
                {
                    _domainSnapshot = snap;
                    _domainSnapshotUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
                    _domainSnapshotDomain = domainDn;
                }
            }
            return snap;
        }


        private void LoadDomainStats(StackPanel panel)
        {
            var statsCard = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Padding = new Thickness(12),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8)
            };
            var statsBody = new StackPanel { Spacing = 12 };
            var overviewHeader = new Grid
            {
                ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            {
                var ovTitle = MakeIconLine("mdi-view-dashboard", "Environment overview", 18, 16, bold: true);
                ovTitle.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
                ovTitle.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
                overviewHeader.Children.Add(ovTitle);
            }
            var refreshCluster = new StackPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                Spacing = 8,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right
            };
            var lastRefreshTb = Tb(_statsLastRefresh.HasValue
                    ? FormatStatsRefreshAge(_statsLastRefresh.Value)
                    : "Not refreshed yet", size: 11, opacity: 0.7, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
            var statsRefreshBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = MakeMdi("mdi-refresh", 16),
                FontSize = 16,
                Width = 32,
                Height = 28,
                Padding = new Thickness(0),
                Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center,
                VerticalContentAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand)
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(statsRefreshBtn, "Refresh environment overview counts");
            statsRefreshBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                _statsLoaded = false;
                InvalidateDomainSnapshot();
                if (_rootData != null)
                    ShowContainerContents(_rootData);
            };
            refreshCluster.Children.Add(lastRefreshTb);
            refreshCluster.Children.Add(statsRefreshBtn);
            Grid.SetColumn(refreshCluster, 1);
            overviewHeader.Children.Add(refreshCluster);
            statsBody.Children.Add(overviewHeader);
            statsBody.Children.Add(Tb("Click any card to see the accounts, computers, or groups behind the number.", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true));

            Button MkStatBtn(string label, out TextBlock valueTb, out ProgressBar busy)
            {
                valueTb = Tb("", size: 22, weight: FontWeight.Bold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left, visible: false);
                busy = new ProgressBar
                {
                    IsIndeterminate = true,
                    Width = 48,
                    Height = 6,
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 8)
                };
                var sp = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };
                sp.Children.Add(valueTb);
                sp.Children.Add(busy);
                sp.Children.Add(Tb(label, size: 12, opacity: 0.75, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));
                var btn = new Button
                {
                    Content = sp,
                    Classes = { "memberLink" },
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                    Padding = new Thickness(8, 6),
                    Margin = new Thickness(2)
                };
                return btn;
            }

            Border MkSection(string title, params Control[] cells)
            {
                var outer = new Border
                {
                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                    BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(70, 128, 128, 128)),
                    Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(28, 128, 128, 128)),
                    CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(6),
                    Padding = new Thickness(10, 8),
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                };
                var body = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };
                string sectionKey = title switch
                {
                    "Users" => "User",
                    "Computers" => "Computer",
                    "Groups" => "Group",
                    _ => "Domain"
                };
                var sectionTitle = MakeIconLine(sectionKey, title, 15, 13, bold: true);
                sectionTitle.Opacity = 0.95;
                body.Children.Add(sectionTitle);
                int cols = Math.Min(3, Math.Max(1, cells.Length));
                int rows = (cells.Length + cols - 1) / cols;
                var grid = new Grid();
                for (int c = 0; c < cols; c++)
                    grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition(GridLength.Star));
                for (int r = 0; r < rows; r++)
                    grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition(GridLength.Auto));
                for (int i = 0; i < cells.Length; i++)
                {
                    Grid.SetColumn(cells[i], i % cols);
                    Grid.SetRow(cells[i], i / cols);
                    grid.Children.Add(cells[i]);
                }
                body.Children.Add(grid);
                outer.Child = body;
                return outer;
            }

            // --- Users ---
            TextBlock vUsers, vEnabled, vDisabled, vLocked, vExpired, vMustChange, vPwdNever, vStaleUsers, vOrgBoxes, vDoNotDelete;
            ProgressBar bUsers, bEnabled, bDisabled, bLocked, bExpired, bMustChange, bPwdNever, bStaleUsers, bOrgBoxes, bDoNotDelete;
            var cUsers = MkStatBtn("Users", out vUsers, out bUsers);
            var cEnabled = MkStatBtn("Enabled", out vEnabled, out bEnabled);
            var cDisabled = MkStatBtn("Disabled", out vDisabled, out bDisabled);
            var cLocked = MkStatBtn("Locked", out vLocked, out bLocked);
            var cExpired = MkStatBtn("Password expired", out vExpired, out bExpired);
            var cMustChange = MkStatBtn("Must change password", out vMustChange, out bMustChange);
            var cPwdNever = MkStatBtn("Password never expires", out vPwdNever, out bPwdNever);
            var cStaleUsers = MkStatBtn("Stale users (" + Math.Max(1, _staleComputerDays) + "d)", out vStaleUsers, out bStaleUsers);
            var cOrgBoxes = MkStatBtn("Org boxes", out vOrgBoxes, out bOrgBoxes);
            var cDoNotDelete = MkStatBtn("Do not delete", out vDoNotDelete, out bDoNotDelete);

            // --- Computers ---
            TextBlock vComputers, vWorkstations, vServers, vDisabledComputers, vStaleComputers;
            ProgressBar bComputers, bWorkstations, bServers, bDisabledComputers, bStaleComputers;
            var cComputers = MkStatBtn("Computers", out vComputers, out bComputers);
            var cWorkstations = MkStatBtn("Workstations", out vWorkstations, out bWorkstations);
            var cServers = MkStatBtn("Servers", out vServers, out bServers);
            var cDisabledComputers = MkStatBtn("Disabled computers", out vDisabledComputers, out bDisabledComputers);
            var cStaleComputers = MkStatBtn("Stale computers (" + Math.Max(1, _staleComputerDays) + "d)", out vStaleComputers, out bStaleComputers);

            // --- Groups ---
            TextBlock vGroups, vSecGroups, vDistGroups, vEmptyGroups;
            ProgressBar bGroups, bSecGroups, bDistGroups, bEmptyGroups;
            var cGroups = MkStatBtn("Groups", out vGroups, out bGroups);
            var cSecGroups = MkStatBtn("Security groups", out vSecGroups, out bSecGroups);
            var cDistGroups = MkStatBtn("Distribution groups", out vDistGroups, out bDistGroups);
            var cEmptyGroups = MkStatBtn("Empty groups (no members)", out vEmptyGroups, out bEmptyGroups);

            // Plain-language tooltips: several card names are insider shorthand,
            // so each card says what it counts before a new user has to click it.
            int staleDaysTip = Math.Max(1, _staleComputerDays);
            ToolTip.SetTip(cUsers, "Every user account in the domain. Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cEnabled, "User accounts that are not disabled. Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cDisabled, "User accounts that have been disabled. Click to see the list - you can re-enable an account from its page.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cLocked, "Accounts locked out right now, usually after too many wrong passwords. Click to see the list and unlock them.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cExpired, "Accounts whose password is older than the domain's maximum password age. Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cMustChange, "Accounts flagged \"must change password at next logon\". Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cPwdNever, "Accounts whose password never expires. Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cStaleUsers, "Enabled users with no logon in the last " + staleDaysTip + " days (or never logged on). \"Stale\" usually means the person left or the account was abandoned. The day count is the stale threshold in Settings. Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cOrgBoxes, "Accounts that look like shared or org mailboxes: the name, description, or email mentions org box, mailbox, or similar. Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cDoNotDelete, "User accounts whose description says DO NOT DELETE. ADE will not delete these. Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cComputers, "Every computer account in the domain. Click to browse them by operating system.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cWorkstations, "Computers running a Windows client system (7, 8, 10, 11). Click to browse by version.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cServers, "Computers running Windows Server, or sitting in a Server / Servers folder. Click to browse by version.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cDisabledComputers, "Computer accounts that have been disabled. Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cStaleComputers, "Computers with no logon in the last " + staleDaysTip + " days (or never) - usually machines that were retired but never cleaned up. Click to see the list; the clearly dead ones can be deleted from there.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cGroups, "Every group in the domain. Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cSecGroups, "Security groups - used to grant permissions. Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cDistGroups, "Distribution groups - used for email lists; they grant no permissions. Click to see the list.");
            ToolTip.SetTip(cEmptyGroups, "Groups with no members at all. Click to see the list - unused ones can be exported or deleted from there.");

            cUsers.Click += (_, _) => ShowSnapStatList("Users", "(&(objectCategory=person)(objectClass=user))", "User",
                s => s.UsersOk,
                s => s.Users.Select(u => (SnapTitle(u.Display, u.Name, u.Cn, u.Dn), u.Sam, u.Dn, IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(u.Dn, u.Sid, u.Crit, u.Sam))).ToList());
            cEnabled.Click += (_, _) => ShowSnapStatList("Enabled users",
                "(&(objectCategory=person)(objectClass=user)(!(userAccountControl:1.2.840.113556.1.4.803:=2)))", "User",
                s => s.UsersOk,
                s => s.Users.Where(u => (u.Uac & 0x2) == 0).Select(u => (SnapTitle(u.Display, u.Name, u.Cn, u.Dn), u.Sam, u.Dn, IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(u.Dn, u.Sid, u.Crit, u.Sam))).ToList());
            cDisabled.Click += (_, _) => ShowDisabledUsersPanel();
            cLocked.Click += (_, _) => ShowLockedUsersPanel();
            cExpired.Click += (_, _) => ShowPasswordExpiredPanel();
            cMustChange.Click += (_, _) => ShowSnapStatList("Must change password",
                "(&(objectCategory=person)(objectClass=user)(pwdLastSet=0))", "User",
                s => s.UsersOk,
                s => s.Users.Where(u => u.PwdLastSet.HasValue && u.PwdLastSet.Value == 0).Select(u => (SnapTitle(u.Display, u.Name, u.Cn, u.Dn), u.Sam, u.Dn, IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(u.Dn, u.Sid, u.Crit, u.Sam))).ToList());
            cPwdNever.Click += (_, _) => ShowSnapStatList("Password never expires",
                "(&(objectCategory=person)(objectClass=user)(userAccountControl:1.2.840.113556.1.4.803:=65536))", "User",
                s => s.UsersOk,
                s => s.Users.Where(u => (u.Uac & 0x10000) != 0).Select(u => (SnapTitle(u.Display, u.Name, u.Cn, u.Dn), u.Sam, u.Dn, IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(u.Dn, u.Sid, u.Crit, u.Sam))).ToList());
            cStaleUsers.Click += (_, _) => ShowStaleObjectsPanel("User");
            cOrgBoxes.Click += (_, _) => ShowOrgBoxesPanel();
            cDoNotDelete.Click += (_, _) => ShowDoNotDeletePanel();

            cComputers.Click += (_, _) => ShowComputersByOsPanel(workstationsOnly: false);
            cWorkstations.Click += (_, _) => ShowComputersByOsPanel(workstationsOnly: true);
            cServers.Click += (_, _) => ShowServersByOsPanel();
            cDisabledComputers.Click += (_, _) => ShowSnapStatList("Disabled computers",
                "(&(objectClass=computer)(userAccountControl:1.2.840.113556.1.4.803:=2))", "Computer",
                s => s.ComputersOk,
                s => s.Computers.Where(c => (c.Uac & 0x2) != 0).Select(c => (SnapTitle(c.Display, c.Name, c.Cn, c.Dn), c.Sam, c.Dn, IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(c.Dn, c.Sid, c.Crit, c.Sam))).ToList());
            cStaleComputers.Click += (_, _) => ShowStaleObjectsPanel("Computer");

            cGroups.Click += (_, _) => ShowSnapStatList("Groups", "(objectClass=group)", "Group",
                s => s.GroupsOk,
                s => s.Groups.Select(g => (SnapTitle(g.Display, g.Name, g.Cn, g.Dn), g.Sam, g.Dn, IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(g.Dn, g.Sid, g.Crit, g.Sam))).ToList());
            cSecGroups.Click += (_, _) => ShowSnapStatList("Security groups",
                "(&(objectClass=group)(groupType:1.2.840.113556.1.4.803:=2147483648))", "Group",
                s => s.GroupsOk,
                s => s.Groups.Where(g => (g.GroupType & unchecked((int)0x80000000)) != 0).Select(g => (SnapTitle(g.Display, g.Name, g.Cn, g.Dn), g.Sam, g.Dn, IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(g.Dn, g.Sid, g.Crit, g.Sam))).ToList());
            cDistGroups.Click += (_, _) => ShowSnapStatList("Distribution groups",
                "(&(objectClass=group)(!(groupType:1.2.840.113556.1.4.803:=2147483648)))", "Group",
                s => s.GroupsOk,
                s => s.Groups.Where(g => (g.GroupType & unchecked((int)0x80000000)) == 0).Select(g => (SnapTitle(g.Display, g.Name, g.Cn, g.Dn), g.Sam, g.Dn, IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(g.Dn, g.Sid, g.Crit, g.Sam))).ToList());
            cEmptyGroups.Click += (_, _) => ShowStatList("Empty groups (no members)",
                "(&(objectClass=group)(!(member=*)))", "Group", false, withQuickDelete: true);

            statsBody.Children.Add(MkSection("Users",
                cUsers, cEnabled, cDisabled, cLocked, cExpired, cMustChange, cPwdNever, cStaleUsers, cOrgBoxes, cDoNotDelete));
            statsBody.Children.Add(MkSection("Computers",
                cComputers, cWorkstations, cServers, cDisabledComputers, cStaleComputers));
            statsBody.Children.Add(MkSection("Groups",
                cGroups, cSecGroups, cDistGroups, cEmptyGroups));

            statsBody.Children.Add(Tb("Click a stat to browse and take action", size: 11, opacity: 0.55, margin: new Thickness(4, 2, 0, 0)));
            statsCard.Child = statsBody;
            panel.Children.Add(statsCard);

            void SetVal(TextBlock tb, ProgressBar bar, int n)
            {
                bar.IsVisible = false;
                bar.IsIndeterminate = false;
                tb.Text = n.ToString("N0");
                tb.IsVisible = true;
            }


            void ApplyCached()
            {
                SetVal(vUsers, bUsers, _statUsers);
                SetVal(vEnabled, bEnabled, Math.Max(0, _statUsers - _statDisabled));
                SetVal(vDisabled, bDisabled, _statDisabled);
                SetVal(vLocked, bLocked, _statLocked);
                SetVal(vExpired, bExpired, _statExpired);
                SetVal(vMustChange, bMustChange, _statMustChange);
                SetVal(vPwdNever, bPwdNever, _statPwdNever);
                SetVal(vStaleUsers, bStaleUsers, _statStaleUsers);
                SetVal(vOrgBoxes, bOrgBoxes, _statOrgBoxes);
                SetVal(vDoNotDelete, bDoNotDelete, _statDoNotDelete);
                SetVal(vComputers, bComputers, _statComputers);
                SetVal(vWorkstations, bWorkstations, _statWorkstations);
                SetVal(vServers, bServers, _statServers);
                SetVal(vDisabledComputers, bDisabledComputers, _statDisabledComputers);
                SetVal(vStaleComputers, bStaleComputers, _statStaleComputers);
                SetVal(vGroups, bGroups, _statGroups);
                SetVal(vSecGroups, bSecGroups, _statSecGroups);
                SetVal(vDistGroups, bDistGroups, _statDistGroups);
                SetVal(vEmptyGroups, bEmptyGroups, _statEmptyGroups);
            }

            if (_statsLoaded)
            {
                ApplyCached();
                if (_statsLastRefresh.HasValue)
                    lastRefreshTb.Text = FormatStatsRefreshAge(_statsLastRefresh.Value);
                return;
            }

            // Counts now come from the ONE shared enumeration behind
            // GetDomainSnapshotAsync - the same snapshot the drill-down
            // panels read, instead of three more scans of the domain.
            // (The overview Refresh button invalidates the snapshot first,
            // so reaching this loader always rebuilds or joins a build.)
            Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                var snap = await GetDomainSnapshotAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
                int statsFailures = (snap.UsersOk ? 0 : 1) + (snap.ComputersOk ? 0 : 1) + (snap.GroupsOk ? 0 : 1);

                var uc = snap.UserCounts;
                _statUsers = uc.Users; _statDisabled = uc.Disabled; _statLocked = uc.Locked;
                _statExpired = uc.Expired; _statMustChange = uc.MustChange; _statPwdNever = uc.PwdNever;
                _statStaleUsers = uc.StaleUsers; _statOrgBoxes = uc.OrgBoxes; _statDoNotDelete = uc.DoNotDelete;
                var cc = snap.ComputerCounts;
                _statComputers = cc.Computers; _statServers = cc.Servers; _statWorkstations = cc.Workstations;
                _statDisabledComputers = cc.DisabledComputers; _statStaleComputers = cc.StaleComputers;
                var gc = snap.GroupCounts;
                _statGroups = gc.Groups; _statSecGroups = gc.SecGroups; _statDistGroups = gc.DistGroups;
                _statEmptyGroups = gc.EmptyGroups;

                _statsLoaded = statsFailures == 0;
                _statsLastRefresh = DateTime.Now;
                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    // Final pass ensures any late paint is consistent
                    ApplyCached();
                    lastRefreshTb.Text = FormatStatsRefreshAge(_statsLastRefresh.Value)
                        + (statsFailures > 0 ? " (incomplete - will retry)" : "");
                });
            });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// True when the AD description contains "do not delete" in any capitalization
        /// or common variants (do-not-delete, donotdelete, do_not_delete).
        /// </summary>
        private static bool DescriptionSaysDoNotDelete(string? description)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(description)) return false;
            string d = description.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
            // Normalize separators so "DO-NOT-DELETE" / "do_not_delete" still match
            string spaced = d.Replace('_', ' ').Replace('-', ' ').Replace('.', ' ');
            while (spaced.Contains("  "))
                spaced = spaced.Replace("  ", " ");
            string compact = spaced.Replace(" ", "");
            if (spaced.Contains("do not delete")) return true;
            if (compact.Contains("donotdelete")) return true;
            return false;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// List users whose description says do not delete (any case / common variants). Domain-wide.
        /// </summary>
        private void ShowDoNotDeletePanel()
        {
            var exportRows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Detail)>();
            var shell = BeginListPanel("Do not delete", "Scanning domain for \"do not delete\" descriptions...",
                "User accounts whose Description contains \"do not delete\" (any capitalization) "
                    + "or variants like do-not-delete / donotdelete. Searched domain-wide across all OUs.",
                "Filter by name, username, or description...",
                MkListExportButton("do_not_delete", () => exportRows, "Description"));
            if (shell == null) return;
            var (filterBox, statusLine, list) = shell.Value;
            RecordNavigation("donotdelete", null);

            var allRows = new List<(AdNode Node, string Blob, string Desc)>();
            Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                var found = new List<(AdNode Node, string Blob, string Desc)>();
                var snap = await GetDomainSnapshotAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
                if (!snap.UsersOk)
                {
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() => { statusLine.Text = "Scan failed: the domain could not be read. Press Refresh on the overview and try again."; });
                    return;
                }
                foreach (var u in snap.Users)
                {
                    if (!DescriptionSaysDoNotDelete(u.Description)) continue;
                    string title = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(u.Display) ? u.Display
                        : (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(u.Cn) ? u.Cn : (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(u.Name) ? u.Name : u.Sam));
                    var node = new AdNode
                    {
                        Name = title,
                        Type = "User",
                        Dn = u.Dn,
                        SamAccountName = u.Sam,
                        IsContainer = false
                    };
                    string blob = string.Join(" ", title, u.Sam, u.Description);
                    found.Add((node, blob, u.Description));
                }

                found = found.OrderBy(x => x.Node.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();

                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    allRows.Clear();
                    allRows.AddRange(found);
                    exportRows.Clear();
                    exportRows.AddRange(found.Select(f => (f.Node.Name, f.Node.SamAccountName ?? "", f.Node.Dn, f.Desc)));
                    void ApplyFilter()
                    {
                        string q = (filterBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                        list.Children.Clear();
                        int shown = 0;
                        foreach (var (node, blob, desc) in allRows)
                        {
                            if (q.Length > 0 && blob.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0)
                                continue;
                            list.Children.Add(MkListRow("User", node.Name, node.SamAccountName ?? "", node.Dn,
                                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(desc) ? null : desc));
                            shown++;
                        }
                        if (q.Length > 0)
                            statusLine.Text = shown.ToString("N0") + " of " + allRows.Count.ToString("N0") + " match \"" + q + "\"";
                        else
                            statusLine.Text = allRows.Count.ToString("N0") + " account(s) with \"do not delete\" in description";
                    }
                    filterBox.TextChanged += (_, _) => ApplyFilter();
                    ApplyFilter();
                });
            });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Detect org-box / shared-mailbox style user accounts by name fields (case-insensitive).
        /// Matches: "org box" and variants (orgbox, org-box, org_box), "mailbox", "email", "org", "box".
        /// </summary>
        private static bool IsOrgBoxAccount(string? sam, string? displayName, string? cn, string? name, string? description, string? mail)
        {
            // Build a single searchable blob from common naming attributes
            var parts = new[] { sam, displayName, cn, name, description, mail };
            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            foreach (var p in parts)
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(p)) continue;
                if (sb.Length > 0) sb.Append(' ');
                sb.Append(p.Trim());
            }
            if (sb.Length == 0) return false;

            string blob = sb.ToString().ToLowerInvariant();
            // Normalize separators so "Org-Box", "org_box", "Org.Box" still match
            string spaced = blob.Replace('_', ' ').Replace('-', ' ').Replace('.', ' ');
            while (spaced.Contains("  "))
                spaced = spaced.Replace("  ", " ");
            string compact = spaced.Replace(" ", "");

            // Exclusions: debriefed accounts and CFP accounts are never org boxes
            if (spaced.Contains("debriefed") || compact.Contains("debriefed"))
                return false;
            // CFP as its own token (not part of a longer unrelated word)
            if (spaced == "cfp" || spaced.StartsWith("cfp ") || spaced.EndsWith(" cfp")
                || spaced.Contains(" cfp ") || compact == "cfp"
                || spaced.StartsWith("cfp/") || spaced.Contains("/cfp")
                || spaced.Contains("cfp ") || spaced.Contains(" cfp"))
                return false;

            // Org-box patterns only (no bare "org" / "email" / "box" - those caused blank-description false positives)
            if (spaced.Contains("org box") || compact.Contains("orgbox"))
                return true;
            if (spaced.Contains("mailbox") || spaced.Contains("mail box") || compact.Contains("mailbox"))
                return true;
            if (spaced.Contains("email box") || compact.Contains("emailbox"))
                return true;
            if (spaced.Contains("org mailbox") || compact.Contains("orgmailbox"))
                return true;
            if (spaced.Contains("shared mailbox") || compact.Contains("sharedmailbox"))
                return true;

            return false;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// List user accounts that look like org boxes / shared mailboxes (name-based match).
        /// Domain-wide Subtree  -  every OU.
        /// </summary>
        private void ShowOrgBoxesPanel()
        {
            var exportRows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Detail)>();
            var shell = BeginListPanel("Org boxes", "Scanning domain for org box accounts...",
                "User accounts whose name, display name, description, or email looks like an org box / shared mailbox "
                    + "(matches: org box, orgbox, mailbox, email box - any case). Excludes debriefed and CFP. Domain-wide, all OUs.",
                "Filter by name or username...",
                MkListExportButton("org_boxes", () => exportRows, ""));
            if (shell == null) return;
            var (filterBox, statusLine, list) = shell.Value;
            RecordNavigation("orgboxes", null);

            var allRows = new List<(AdNode Node, string Blob)>();
            Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                var found = new List<(AdNode Node, string Blob)>();
                var snap = await GetDomainSnapshotAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
                if (!snap.UsersOk)
                {
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        statusLine.Text = "Scan failed: the domain could not be read. Press Refresh on the overview and try again.";
                    });
                    return;
                }
                foreach (var u in snap.Users)
                {
                    if (!IsOrgBoxAccount(u.Sam, u.Display, u.Cn, u.Name, u.Description, u.Mail))
                        continue;
                    string title = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(u.Display) ? u.Display
                        : (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(u.Cn) ? u.Cn : (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(u.Name) ? u.Name : u.Sam));
                    var node = new AdNode
                    {
                        Name = title,
                        Type = "User",
                        Dn = u.Dn,
                        SamAccountName = u.Sam,
                        IsContainer = false
                    };
                    string blob = string.Join(" ", title, u.Sam, u.Mail, u.Description);
                    found.Add((node, blob));
                }

                found = found
                    .OrderBy(x => x.Node.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    .ToList();


                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    allRows.Clear();
                    allRows.AddRange(found);
                    exportRows.Clear();
                    exportRows.AddRange(found.Select(f => (f.Node.Name, f.Node.SamAccountName ?? "", f.Node.Dn, "")));
                    statusLine.Text = found.Count.ToString("N0") + " org box account(s) found domain-wide";
                    void ApplyFilter()
                    {
                        string q = (filterBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                        list.Children.Clear();
                        int shown = 0;
                        foreach (var (node, blob) in allRows)
                        {
                            if (q.Length > 0 && blob.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0)
                                continue;
                            list.Children.Add(MkListRow("User", node.Name, node.SamAccountName ?? "", node.Dn, null));
                            shown++;
                        }
                        if (q.Length > 0)
                            statusLine.Text = shown.ToString("N0") + " of " + allRows.Count.ToString("N0") + " match \"" + q + "\"";
                        else
                            statusLine.Text = allRows.Count.ToString("N0") + " org box account(s) found domain-wide";
                    }
                    filterBox.TextChanged += (_, _) => ApplyFilter();
                    ApplyFilter();
                });
            });
        }

        private static string FormatStatsRefreshAge(DateTime when)
        {
            // Always show the actual last refresh date and time (as requested)
            return "Last refreshed: " + when.ToString("g");
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// All server computer accounts in the domain (IsServerComputer: OS name or
        /// Server/Servers OU), with the fields the server panels need.
        /// </summary>
        // The server enumeration below is a full domain scan; the Servers-by-OS
        // panel uses it, so a result is reused for a minute instead of
        // rescanning on every panel open.
        // Failed scans are never cached. Deletes clear the cache immediately.
        private List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Os, string OsVer, string DnsHost, DateTime? LastLogon, bool Disabled)>? _serverComputersCache;
        private DateTime _serverComputersCacheUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
        private string _serverComputersCacheDomain = "";
        private readonly object _serverComputersCacheLock = new();

        private void InvalidateServerComputerCache()
        {
            lock (_serverComputersCacheLock) { _serverComputersCache = null; }
        }

        private List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Os, string OsVer, string DnsHost, DateTime? LastLogon, bool Disabled)> LoadServerComputers(string domainDn)
        {
            lock (_serverComputersCacheLock)
            {
                if (_serverComputersCache != null
                    && string.Equals(_serverComputersCacheDomain, domainDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    && (DateTime.UtcNow - _serverComputersCacheUtc) < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60))
                    return _serverComputersCache;
            }
            var list = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Os, string OsVer, string DnsHost, DateTime? LastLogon, bool Disabled)>();
            bool scanOk = false;
            try
            {
                using var rootEntry = BindLdap(domainDn);
                using var s = new DirectorySearcher(rootEntry)
                {
                    Filter = "(objectClass=computer)",
                    SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                    PageSize = 1000,
                    SizeLimit = 0,
                    PropertiesToLoad = { "name", "cn", "sAMAccountName", "distinguishedName", "operatingSystem", "operatingSystemVersion", "dNSHostName", "lastLogonTimestamp", "userAccountControl" }
                };
                using var r = s.FindAll();
                foreach (SearchResult sr in r)
                {
                    string Sp(string prop)
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains(prop) && sr.Properties[prop].Count > 0)
                                return sr.Properties[prop][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                        }
                        catch { }
                        return "";
                    }
                    string dn = Sp("distinguishedName");
                    string os = Sp("operatingSystem");
                    if (!IsServerComputer(os, dn)) continue;
                    string name = Sp("name");
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name)) name = Sp("cn");
                    int uac = 0;
                    try
                    {
                        if (sr.Properties.Contains("userAccountControl") && sr.Properties["userAccountControl"].Count > 0)
                            uac = Convert.ToInt32(sr.Properties["userAccountControl"][0]);
                    }
                    catch { }
                    DateTime? last = null;
                    try
                    {
                        if (sr.Properties.Contains("lastLogonTimestamp") && sr.Properties["lastLogonTimestamp"].Count > 0)
                        {
                            long? ts = ReadLargeInteger(sr.Properties["lastLogonTimestamp"][0]);
                            if (ts.HasValue && ts.Value > 0)
                                last = DateTime.FromFileTimeUtc(ts.Value).ToLocalTime();
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }
                    list.Add((name, Sp("sAMAccountName"), dn, os, Sp("operatingSystemVersion"), Sp("dNSHostName"), last, (uac & 0x2) != 0));
                }
                scanOk = true;
            }
            catch { }
            if (scanOk)
            {
                lock (_serverComputersCacheLock)
                {
                    _serverComputersCache = list;
                    _serverComputersCacheDomain = domainDn;
                    _serverComputersCacheUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
                }
            }
            return list;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Lightweight built-in check for a user account from search-result values
        /// (no extra directory bind): critical-system flag, Builtin container,
        /// systemFlags DISALLOW_DELETE, well-known RIDs, and built-in names.
        /// ShowQuickDeleteDialog still re-checks with IsProtectedAdObject at
        /// delete time (fail closed).
        /// </summary>
        private static bool IsBuiltInUserAccount(string? dn, byte[]? objectSid, object? isCriticalSystemObject, object? systemFlags, string? samAccountName, string? displayName)
        {
            if (IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(dn, objectSid, isCriticalSystemObject, samAccountName))
                return true;

            try
            {
                if (systemFlags != null)
                {
                    long sf = Convert.ToInt64(systemFlags);
                    if ((sf & 0x80000000L) != 0) // systemFlags DISALLOW_DELETE
                        return true;
                }
            }
            catch { }

            if (objectSid != null && objectSid.Length >= 8)
            {
                int rid = objectSid[objectSid.Length - 4]
                        | (objectSid[objectSid.Length - 3] << 8)
                        | (objectSid[objectSid.Length - 2] << 16)
                        | (objectSid[objectSid.Length - 1] << 24);
                switch (rid)
                {
                    case 500: // Administrator
                    case 501: // Guest
                    case 502: // krbtgt
                    case 503: // DefaultAccount
                        return true;
                }
            }

            foreach (string candidate in new[] { samAccountName ?? "", displayName ?? "" })
            {
                string n = candidate.Trim();
                foreach (string builtIn in new[] { "Administrator", "Guest", "krbtgt", "DefaultAccount", "WDAGUtilityAccount" })
                {
                    if (string.Equals(n, builtIn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// True when a user looks Exchange-related or like a system/service/role
        /// account that must never be quick-deleted (user request 2026-10-26):
        /// sAMAccountName, name, mail, or DN contains HealthMailbox, Mail.Recovery,
        /// Mail-Recovery, Postmaster, SCCM, or a classic Exchange system-mailbox
        /// pattern, or the object lives in the Microsoft Exchange System Objects
        /// container. matchedPattern receives the pattern that hit ("" if none).
        /// </summary>
        private static bool IsExchangeOrServiceAccount(string? name, string? samAccountName, string? dn, string? mail, out string matchedPattern)
        {
            matchedPattern = "";
            string[] patterns = new[]
            {
                "HealthMailbox", "Mail.Recovery", "Mail-Recovery", "Postmaster", "SCCM",
                "SystemMailbox", "FederatedEmail", "MigrationMailbox", "DiscoverySearchMailbox",
                "Microsoft Exchange", "MSExch"
            };
            string[] fields = new[] { samAccountName ?? "", name ?? "", mail ?? "", dn ?? "" };
            foreach (string pattern in patterns)
            {
                foreach (string field in fields)
                {
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(field)
                        && field.IndexOf(pattern, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                    {
                        matchedPattern = pattern;
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn)
                && dn.IndexOf("CN=Microsoft Exchange System Objects,", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
            {
                matchedPattern = "Microsoft Exchange System Objects";
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Quick delete for stale-list rows: one simple confirm (no type-the-name
        /// step - that stays on the detail page), a protection re-check right
        /// before deleting, then DeleteTree and row removal via onDeleted.
        /// </summary>
        private void ShowQuickDeleteDialog(string dn, string name, string sam, string typeLabel, string evidence, Action onDeleted)
        {
            var dlg = new Window
            {
                Title = "Quick delete " + typeLabel.ToLower(),
                Width = 440,
                SizeToContent = SizeToContent.Height,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                CanResize = false
            };
            StyleDialogWindow(dlg);
            var root = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(20), Spacing = 12 };
            root.Children.Add(Tb("Delete stale " + typeLabel.ToLower() + "?", size: 18, weight: FontWeight.Bold));
            root.Children.Add(Tb(name + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sam) ? "" : " (" + sam + ")"), weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, wrap: true));
            root.Children.Add(Tb(evidence, opacity: 0.85, wrap: true));
            root.Children.Add(Tb("This permanently deletes the account from Active Directory and cannot be undone.", opacity: 0.85, wrap: true));
            var err = new TextBlock
            {
                Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(229, 115, 115)),
                FontSize = 12,
                TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap,
                IsVisible = false
            };
            root.Children.Add(err);
            var btns = new StackPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                Spacing = 10,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right
            };
            var cancelBtn = new Button { Content = "Cancel", MinWidth = 100, Classes = { "action" } };
            var deleteBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Delete",
                MinWidth = 100,
                Classes = { "action" },
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(176, 96, 96)),
                Foreground = Brushes.White
            };
            cancelBtn.Click += (_, _) => dlg.Close();
            deleteBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                try
                {
                    // Final safety re-check at delete time (fail closed), same guard
                    // as the detail-page delete: built-in / protected objects, domain
                    // controllers, and Exchange system objects can never be deleted.
                    using var check = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + dn);
                    if (IsProtectedAdObject(check))
                    {
