                        err.Text = "This is a built-in or protected Active Directory object and cannot be deleted from ADE.";
                        err.IsVisible = true;
                        return;
                    }
                    // Users: Exchange / service accounts stay blocked even if
                    // the account changed after the scan (same rule as the
                    // detail-page delete).
                    if (string.Equals(typeLabel, "User", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        try { check.RefreshCache(new[] { "name", "sAMAccountName", "mail" }); } catch { }
                        string? uName = null, uSam = null, uMail = null;
                        try { uName = check.Properties["name"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                        try { uSam = check.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                        try { uMail = check.Properties["mail"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                        if (IsExchangeOrServiceAccount(uName, uSam, dn, uMail, out string exchPattern))
                        {
                            err.Text = "This looks like an Exchange or service account (matched \"" + exchPattern + "\") and cannot be deleted from ADE.";
                            err.IsVisible = true;
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                    // Computers: re-verify live that the machine is still
                    // offline - it may have come back since the scan ran.
                    if (string.Equals(typeLabel, "Computer", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        deleteBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                        string block = await Task.Run(() => ComputerDeleteBlockReason(dn));
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(block))
                        {
                            err.Text = block;
                            err.IsVisible = true;
                            deleteBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                    using var entry = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + dn);
                    entry.DeleteTree();
                    dlg.Close();
                    ShowNotification(typeLabel + " deleted: " + name, "success");
                    _statsLoaded = false;
                    InvalidateServerComputerCache();
                    InvalidateDomainSnapshot();
                    onDeleted();
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    err.Text = "Delete failed: " + ex.Message;
                    err.IsVisible = true;
                }
            };
            btns.Children.Add(cancelBtn);
            btns.Children.Add(deleteBtn);
            root.Children.Add(btns);
            dlg.Content = root;
            // Keyboard flow (user request 2026-10-27): Delete is the default
            // button and starts focused, so Enter alone confirms the delete.
            deleteBtn.IsDefault = true;
            dlg.Opened += (_, _) => deleteBtn.Focus();
            try { dlg.ShowDialog(this); } catch { dlg.Show(); }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// List users or computers whose lastLogonTimestamp is missing or older than StaleComputerDays.
        /// Users: enabled only. Computers: all.
        /// </summary>
        private void ShowStaleObjectsPanel(string type)
        {
            int days = Math.Max(1, _staleComputerDays);
            bool isUser = string.Equals(type, "User", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            string title = isUser
                ? "Stale users (enabled, no logon in " + days + " days)"
                : "Stale computers (no logon in " + days + " days)";

            var exportRows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Detail)>();
            var shell = BeginListPanel(title, "Scanning...",
                "Based on lastLogonTimestamp (replicated). Threshold: " + days + " days (Settings). "
                + (isUser
                    ? "Quick delete: built-in accounts and Exchange / service accounts are excluded (HealthMailbox, Mail.Recovery, Postmaster, SCCM, and similar never get a Delete button)."
                    : "Quick delete: only for stale computers with no IP in DNS and no ping reply. Both are checked for every computer during the scan."),
                isUser ? "Filter by name or username..." : "Filter by computer name...",
                MkListExportButton(isUser ? "stale_users" : "stale_computers", () => exportRows, "Last logon / detail"));
            if (shell == null) return;
            var (filterBox, statusLine, list) = shell.Value;

            var rows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, Border Row)>();
            string domainDn = _domainDn;
            DateTime cutoff = DateTime.Now.AddDays(-days);

            Task.Run(() =>
            {
                var items = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Age, string DnsHost, string Detail, bool Eligible, bool Protected)>();
                try
                {
                    using var rootEntry = BindLdap(domainDn);
                    // Let the DC do the age filtering: no lastLogonTimestamp at
                    // all, or one at/older than the cutoff (sent as a FILETIME).
                    // The client-side checks below still run and stay the exact
                    // authority - this only cuts what gets transferred.
                    long cutoffFt = cutoff.ToFileTimeUtc();
                    string staleClause = "(|(!(lastLogonTimestamp=*))(lastLogonTimestamp<=" + cutoffFt + "))";
                    string filter = isUser
                        ? "(&(objectCategory=person)(objectClass=user)" + staleClause + ")"
                        : "(&(objectClass=computer)" + staleClause + ")";
                    using var s = new DirectorySearcher(rootEntry)
                    {
                        Filter = filter,
                        SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                        PageSize = 1000,
                        SizeLimit = 0,
                        PropertiesToLoad = { "name", "cn", "displayName", "sAMAccountName", "distinguishedName", "userAccountControl", "lastLogonTimestamp", "dNSHostName", "mail", "objectSid", "isCriticalSystemObject", "systemFlags" }
                    };
                    using var r = s.FindAll();
                    foreach (SearchResult sr in r)
                    {
                        int uac = 0;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("userAccountControl") && sr.Properties["userAccountControl"].Count > 0)
                                uac = Convert.ToInt32(sr.Properties["userAccountControl"][0]);
                        }
                        catch { }
                        if (isUser && (uac & 0x2) != 0) continue; // enabled users only

                        DateTime? last = null;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("lastLogonTimestamp") && sr.Properties["lastLogonTimestamp"].Count > 0)
                            {
                                long? ts = ReadLargeInteger(sr.Properties["lastLogonTimestamp"][0]);
                                if (ts.HasValue && ts.Value > 0)
                                    last = DateTime.FromFileTimeUtc(ts.Value).ToLocalTime();
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }
                        if (last.HasValue && last.Value >= cutoff) continue;

                        string name = "";
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("displayName") && sr.Properties["displayName"].Count > 0)
                                name = sr.Properties["displayName"][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name) && sr.Properties.Contains("cn") && sr.Properties["cn"].Count > 0)
                                name = sr.Properties["cn"][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name) && sr.Properties.Contains("name") && sr.Properties["name"].Count > 0)
                                name = sr.Properties["name"][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                        }
                        catch { }
                        string sam = "";
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("sAMAccountName") && sr.Properties["sAMAccountName"].Count > 0)
                                sam = sr.Properties["sAMAccountName"][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                        }
                        catch { }
                        string dn = "";
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("distinguishedName") && sr.Properties["distinguishedName"].Count > 0)
                                dn = sr.Properties["distinguishedName"][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                        }
                        catch { }
                        string dnsHost = "";
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("dNSHostName") && sr.Properties["dNSHostName"].Count > 0)
                                dnsHost = sr.Properties["dNSHostName"][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                        }
                        catch { }
                        string mail = "";
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("mail") && sr.Properties["mail"].Count > 0)
                                mail = sr.Properties["mail"][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                        }
                        catch { }
                        byte[]? sid = null;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("objectSid") && sr.Properties["objectSid"].Count > 0)
                                sid = sr.Properties["objectSid"][0] as byte[];
                        }
                        catch { }
                        object? crit = null;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("isCriticalSystemObject") && sr.Properties["isCriticalSystemObject"].Count > 0)
                                crit = sr.Properties["isCriticalSystemObject"][0];
                        }
                        catch { }
                        object? sysFlags = null;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("systemFlags") && sr.Properties["systemFlags"].Count > 0)
                                sysFlags = sr.Properties["systemFlags"][0];
                        }
                        catch { }

                        string age = !last.HasValue
                            ? "Never / no data"
                            : ((int)(DateTime.Now - last.Value).TotalDays) + "d ago (" + last.Value.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd") + ")";

                        string detail;
                        bool eligible;
                        if (isUser)
                        {
                            bool builtIn = IsBuiltInUserAccount(dn, sid, crit, sysFlags, sam, name);
                            bool exch = IsExchangeOrServiceAccount(name, sam, dn, mail, out string exchPattern);
                            eligible = !builtIn && !exch;
                            detail = eligible
                                ? "Quick delete available - not built-in or Exchange/service"
                                : builtIn
                                    ? "Quick delete blocked - built-in / protected account"
                                    : "Quick delete blocked - Exchange/service account (" + exchPattern + ")";
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            // Filled in after the scan by the DNS + ping check below.
                            detail = "Checking IP / ping...";
                            eligible = false;
                        }
                        bool protectedFlag = !isUser
                            && ((uac & 0x2000) != 0 || IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(dn, sid, crit, sam));
                        items.Add((name, sam, dn, age, dnsHost, detail, eligible, protectedFlag));
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() => statusLine.Text = "Scan failed: " + ex.Message);
                    return;
                }

                if (!isUser && items.Count > 0)
                {
                    // Quick-delete eligibility for stale computers (user request
                    // 2026-10-26): no IP in DNS *and* no ping reply. Both checks run
                    // for every stale computer, in parallel. A computer that still
                    // resolves to an IP is never eligible, even when ping fails -
                    // it may simply be powered off or blocking ICMP.
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                        statusLine.Text = "Checking IP / ping for " + items.Count.ToString("N0") + " stale computer(s)...");
                    var arr = items.ToArray();
                    System.Threading.Tasks.Parallel.For(0, arr.Length, i =>
                    {
                        var it = arr[i];
                        if (it.Protected)
                        {
                            arr[i] = (it.Name, it.Sam, it.Dn, it.Age, it.DnsHost,
                                "Protected computer account (e.g. domain controller) - not eligible", false, true);
                            return;
                        }
                        string host = ResolveRdpHost(
                            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(it.DnsHost) ? it.DnsHost
                            : !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(it.Sam) ? it.Sam
                            : it.Name);
                        System.Net.IPAddress[] addrs = Array.Empty<System.Net.IPAddress>();
                        try { addrs = System.Net.Dns.GetHostAddresses(host); } catch { }
                        bool noIp = addrs.Length == 0;
                        var ping = TryPingHost(host, 700);
                        bool eligibleNow = noIp && !ping.Ok;
                        string detailNow;
                        if (eligibleNow)
                            detailNow = "No IP in DNS - No ping reply - " + host;
                        else if (!noIp && ping.Ok)
                            detailNow = "IP " + addrs[0] + " - Ping OK " + ping.Ms + " ms - not eligible";
                        else if (!noIp)
                            detailNow = "IP " + addrs[0] + " - No ping reply (has an IP - not eligible)";
                        else
                            detailNow = "No IP in DNS - Ping replied - not eligible";
                        arr[i] = (it.Name, it.Sam, it.Dn, it.Age, it.DnsHost, detailNow, eligibleNow, it.Protected);
                    });
                    items = arr.ToList();
                }

                items = items
                    .OrderByDescending(x => x.Age.StartsWith("Never") ? int.MaxValue : 0)
                    .ThenBy(x => x.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    .ToList();

                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    list.Children.Clear();
                    rows.Clear();
                    statusLine.Text = items.Count.ToString("N0") + " stale " + (isUser ? "user(s)" : "computer(s)")
                        + " - " + items.Count(x => x.Eligible).ToString("N0") + " eligible for quick delete";
                    exportRows.Clear();
                    exportRows.AddRange(items.Select(x => (x.Name, x.Sam, x.Dn, x.Age + " - " + x.Detail)));
                    var delButtons = new List<(string Dn, Button Btn)>();
                    foreach (var it in items)
                    {
                        string delDn = it.Dn;
                        string delName = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(it.Name) ? it.Sam : it.Name;
                        string delSam = it.Sam;
                        string delType = isUser ? "User" : "Computer";
                        string delEvidence = "Last logon: " + it.Age + "\n" + it.Detail;
                        var row = MkListRow(delType, delName, it.Sam, it.Dn,
                            it.Age + " - " + it.Detail,
                            it.Eligible ? rowB =>
                            {
                                var delBtn = new Button
                                {
                                    Content = "Delete",
                                    Classes = { "action" },
                                    Width = 84,
                                    Height = 28,
                                    Margin = new Thickness(8, 0, 0, 0),
                                    Padding = new Thickness(8, 2),
                                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                                    Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(176, 96, 96)),
                                    Foreground = Brushes.White
                                };
                                delBtn.PointerEntered += (_, _) => rowB.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(70, 176, 96, 96));
                                delBtn.PointerExited += (_, _) => rowB.Background = Brushes.Transparent;
                                delBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
                                {
                                    ShowQuickDeleteDialog(delDn, delName, delSam, delType, delEvidence, () =>
                                    {
                                        // Keyboard flow (user request 2026-10-27):
                                        // after a delete, focus moves to the next
                                        // row's Delete button, so Enter opens the
                                        // next confirm (whose Delete button is
                                        // focused too) - a whole list can be
                                        // deleted without touching the mouse.
                                        int idx = delButtons.FindIndex(b => string.Equals(b.Dn, delDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                                        list.Children.Remove(rowB);
                                        rows.RemoveAll(r => string.Equals(r.Dn, delDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                                        if (idx >= 0)
                                        {
                                            delButtons.RemoveAt(idx);
                                            Button? nextBtn = null;
                                            if (idx < delButtons.Count) nextBtn = delButtons[idx].Btn;
                                            else if (delButtons.Count > 0) nextBtn = delButtons[delButtons.Count - 1].Btn;
                                            if (nextBtn != null)
                                                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() => nextBtn.Focus());
                                        }
                                        statusLine.Text = rows.Count.ToString("N0") + " stale " + (isUser ? "user(s)" : "computer(s)") + " remaining";
                                    });
                                };
                                delButtons.Add((delDn, delBtn));
                                return delBtn;
                            } : null);
                        list.Children.Add(row);
                        rows.Add((it.Name, it.Sam, it.Dn, row));
                    }

                    void ApplyFilter()
                    {
                        string q = (filterBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                        int shown = 0;
                        foreach (var r in rows)
                        {
                            bool match = string.IsNullOrEmpty(q)
                                || (r.Name?.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? -1) >= 0
                                || (r.Sam?.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? -1) >= 0;
                            r.Row.IsVisible = match;
                            if (match) shown++;
                        }
                        statusLine.Text = shown.ToString("N0") + " of " + rows.Count.ToString("N0") + " shown";
                    }
                    filterBox.TextChanged += (_, _) => ApplyFilter();
                });
            });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if this computer should be treated as a server:
        /// - operatingSystem contains "Server", or
        /// - it lives under an OU named Server / Servers (or whose name contains "server").
        /// </summary>
        private static bool IsServerComputer(string? operatingSystem, string? distinguishedName)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(operatingSystem)
                && operatingSystem.IndexOf("server", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                return true;

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(distinguishedName))
                return false;

            // DN components: CN=name,OU=Servers,OU=...,DC=...
            foreach (string part in distinguishedName.Split(','))
            {
                string p = part.Trim();
                if (p.Length < 4) continue;
                if (!p.StartsWith("OU=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    continue;
                string ouName = p.Substring(3).Trim();
                if (ouName.Length == 0) continue;
                // Exact or contains "server" / "servers" (any case)
                if (ouName.Equals("Server", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    || ouName.Equals("Servers", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    || ouName.IndexOf("server", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                    return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Map Windows Server OS string to a version bucket (2008..2025 or Undetermined).
        /// </summary>
        private static string ClassifyServerOsVersion(string? operatingSystem, string? operatingSystemVersion = null)
        {
            string os = (operatingSystem ?? "").Trim();
            string ver = (operatingSystemVersion ?? "").Trim();
            string blob = (os + " " + ver).ToLowerInvariant();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(blob))
                return "Undetermined";

            // Prefer explicit year in product name (most reliable)
            if (blob.Contains("2025")) return "2025";
            if (blob.Contains("2022")) return "2022";
            if (blob.Contains("2019")) return "2019";
            if (blob.Contains("2016")) return "2016";
            if (blob.Contains("2012")) return "2012";
            if (blob.Contains("2008")) return "2008";
            if (blob.Contains("2003")) return "Undetermined";

            // Fallback: known build numbers from operatingSystemVersion (e.g. 10.0 (20348))
            // 2025 ~ 26100+, 2022 ~ 20348, 2019 ~ 17763, 2016 ~ 14393, 2012 R2 ~ 9600, 2012 ~ 9200, 2008 R2 ~ 7601
            try
            {
                var digits = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
                foreach (char c in ver)
                {
                    if (char.IsDigit(c)) digits.Append(c);
                    else if (digits.Length > 0) break;
                }
                // also scan parenthetical build
                int paren = ver.IndexOf('(');
                if (paren >= 0)
                {
                    digits.Clear();
                    for (int i = paren + 1; i < ver.Length && char.IsDigit(ver[i]); i++)
                        digits.Append(ver[i]);
                }
                if (digits.Length > 0 && int.TryParse(digits.ToString(), out int build))
                {
                    if (build >= 26000) return "2025";
                    if (build >= 20300) return "2022";
                    if (build >= 17700) return "2019";
                    if (build >= 14300) return "2016";
                    if (build >= 9200) return "2012";
                    if (build >= 6000) return "2008";
                }
            }
            catch { }

            return "Undetermined";
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Servers list with a right-hand panel grouping by OS version
        /// (2008, 2012, 2016, 2019, 2022, 2025, Undetermined).
        /// </summary>
        /// <summary>
        /// Client OS family + display sub-version (e.g. Windows 10 / 22H2, Windows 11 / 24H2).
        /// </summary>
        private static (string Family, string Sub) ClassifyClientOsVersion(string? operatingSystem, string? operatingSystemVersion)
        {
            string os = (operatingSystem ?? "").Trim();
            string ver = (operatingSystemVersion ?? "").Trim();
            string blob = (os + " " + ver).ToLowerInvariant();

            string family = "Other";
            if (blob.Contains("windows 11") || blob.Contains("windows11"))
                family = "Windows 11";
            else if (blob.Contains("windows 10") || blob.Contains("windows10"))
                family = "Windows 10";
            else if (blob.Contains("windows 8") || blob.Contains("windows8"))
                family = "Windows 8";
            else if (blob.Contains("windows 7") || blob.Contains("windows7"))
                family = "Windows 7";
            else if (blob.Contains("windows server") || blob.Contains("server"))
                family = "Windows Server";
            else if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(os) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ver))
                family = "Other";

            // Non Win10/11: no marketing sub-version
            if (family != "Windows 10" && family != "Windows 11")
                return (family, "");

            bool isLtsc = blob.Contains("ltsc") || blob.Contains("ltsb");

            // Prefer H# / year labels already in the OS string
            string? fromName = null;
            foreach (string tag in new[] {
                "26h2", "25h2", "24h2", "23h2", "22h2", "21h2", "21h1", "20h2",
                "2004", "1909", "1903", "1809", "1803", "1709", "1703", "1607", "1511", "1507"
            })
            {
                if (blob.Contains(tag))
                {
                    fromName = tag.ToUpperInvariant().Replace("H", "H"); // 22H2 style
                    // Fix: 22h2 -> 22H2
                    if (fromName.Length >= 3 && char.IsDigit(fromName[0]))
                    {
                        // 22H2, 21H1, etc.
                        if (fromName.Contains("H"))
                            fromName = fromName.Substring(0, 2) + "H" + fromName.Substring(fromName.IndexOf('H') + 1);
                        else
                            fromName = fromName; // 1809, 2004
                    }
                    break;
                }
            }
            // Normalize known tags from name more cleanly
            if (blob.Contains("26h2")) fromName = "26H2";
            else if (blob.Contains("25h2")) fromName = "25H2";
            else if (blob.Contains("24h2")) fromName = "24H2";
            else if (blob.Contains("23h2")) fromName = "23H2";
            else if (blob.Contains("22h2")) fromName = "22H2";
            else if (blob.Contains("21h2")) fromName = "21H2";
            else if (blob.Contains("21h1")) fromName = "21H1";
            else if (blob.Contains("20h2")) fromName = "20H2";
            else if (blob.Contains("2004")) fromName = "2004";
            else if (blob.Contains("1909")) fromName = "1909";
            else if (blob.Contains("1903")) fromName = "1903";
            else if (blob.Contains("1809")) fromName = "1809";
            else if (blob.Contains("1803")) fromName = "1803";
            else if (blob.Contains("1709")) fromName = "1709";
            else if (blob.Contains("1703")) fromName = "1703";
            else if (blob.Contains("1607")) fromName = "1607";
            else if (blob.Contains("1511")) fromName = "1511";
            else if (blob.Contains("1507")) fromName = "1507";

            int build = 0;
            try
            {
                // operatingSystemVersion examples: "10.0 (19045)", "10.0.19045"
                int paren = ver.IndexOf('(');
                if (paren >= 0)
                {
                    var digits = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
                    for (int i = paren + 1; i < ver.Length && char.IsDigit(ver[i]); i++)
                        digits.Append(ver[i]);
                    if (digits.Length > 0)
                        int.TryParse(digits.ToString(), out build);
                }
                if (build == 0)
                {
                    // last dotted segment if large enough to be a build
                    string[] parts = ver.Split(new[] { '.', ' ' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
                    foreach (string part in parts)
                    {
                        string d = new string(part.Where(char.IsDigit).ToArray());
                        if (d.Length >= 4 && int.TryParse(d, out int b) && b >= 1000)
                            build = b;
                    }
                }
            }
            catch { }

            string fromBuild = "";
            if (family == "Windows 11")
            {
                // Official release names by build
                if (build >= 28000) fromBuild = "26H2+";
                else if (build >= 26200) fromBuild = "25H2";
                else if (build >= 26100) fromBuild = "24H2";
                else if (build >= 22631) fromBuild = "23H2";
                else if (build >= 22621) fromBuild = "22H2";
                else if (build >= 22000) fromBuild = "21H2";
                else if (build > 0) fromBuild = "Build " + build;
            }
            else // Windows 10
            {
                if (build >= 19045) fromBuild = "22H2";
                else if (build >= 19044) fromBuild = "21H2";
                else if (build >= 19043) fromBuild = "21H1";
                else if (build >= 19042) fromBuild = "20H2";
                else if (build >= 19041) fromBuild = "2004";
                else if (build >= 18363) fromBuild = "1909";
                else if (build >= 18362) fromBuild = "1903";
                else if (build >= 17763) fromBuild = "1809";
                else if (build >= 17134) fromBuild = "1803";
                else if (build >= 16299) fromBuild = "1709";
                else if (build >= 15063) fromBuild = "1703";
                else if (build >= 14393) fromBuild = "1607";
                else if (build >= 10586) fromBuild = "1511";
                else if (build >= 10240) fromBuild = "1507";
                else if (build > 0) fromBuild = "Build " + build;
            }

            string sub = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(fromName) ? fromName
                : (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(fromBuild) ? fromBuild : "Undetermined");
            if (isLtsc)
            {
                if (!sub.EndsWith("LTSC", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    && !sub.Contains("LTSC", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    sub = sub + " LTSC";
            }
            return (family, sub);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// All computers with right-hand OS family panel (Win 7/8/10/11 + Win10/11 sub-versions).
        /// </summary>
        private void ShowComputersByOsPanel(bool workstationsOnly = false)
        {
            var panel = this.FindControl<StackPanel>("DetailPanel");
            if (panel == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(_domainDn)) return;
            panel.Children.Clear();
            RecordNavigation(workstationsOnly ? "workstations-os" : "computers-os", null);

            var allComputers = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Os, string OsVer, string Family, string Sub, string ServerBucket, string Blob)>();
            string domainDn = _domainDn;
            // activeKey: "All" | "client:Windows 10" | "client:Windows 10|22H2" | "server:2022" | "server:All"
            string activeKey = "All";

            var content = new StackPanel
            {
                MaxWidth = 1200,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Spacing = 0,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 16)
            };
            {
                var titleRow = new Grid
                {
                    ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto"),
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4)
                };
                titleRow.Children.Add(Tb(workstationsOnly ? "Workstations" : "Computers", size: 22, weight: FontWeight.Bold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));
                var exportBtn = MkListExportButton(workstationsOnly ? "workstations" : "computers",
                    () => allComputers.Select(c => (c.Name, c.Sam, c.Dn, c.Os)), "Operating system");
                Grid.SetColumn(exportBtn, 1);
                titleRow.Children.Add(exportBtn);
                content.Children.Add(titleRow);
            }
            content.Children.Add(Tb(workstationsOnly
                    ? "Windows client computers only (Windows 7 / 8 / 10 / 11). Excludes servers by OS name and by Server/Servers OU. Use the panel on the right to filter by version."
                    : "All computer objects domain-wide, including Windows clients and Windows Server. Client and server OS breakdowns are in separate panels on the right.", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 10)));

            // List | (one or two breakdown panels)
            var columns = new Grid
            {
                ColumnDefinitions = workstationsOnly
                    ? new ColumnDefinitions("*,12,*")
                    : new ColumnDefinitions("*,12,*,12,*"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };

            var leftInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };
            var filterBox = new TextBox
            {
                Watermark = "Filter by computer name...",
                MinHeight = 34,
                FontSize = 14,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            leftInner.Children.Add(filterBox);
            var statusLine = new TextBlock { Text = "Loading...", FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.75 };
            leftInner.Children.Add(statusLine);
            var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };
            var scroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 14, 0),
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                Content = list,
                MaxHeight = 560,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
            };
            leftInner.Children.Add(scroll);
            var leftCard = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Padding = new Thickness(10),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Child = leftInner
            };
            Grid.SetColumn(leftCard, 0);
            columns.Children.Add(leftCard);

            var bucketButtons = new Dictionary<string, Button>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            void HighlightBucketButtons()
            {
                foreach (var kv in bucketButtons)
                {
                    bool on = string.Equals(kv.Key, activeKey, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                    kv.Value.FontWeight = on ? FontWeight.Bold : FontWeight.Normal;
                    try
                    {
                        kv.Value.Background = on
                            ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(60, 79, 195, 247))
                            : Brushes.Transparent;
                    }
                    catch { }
                }
            }

            void ApplyComputerFilter()
            {
                string q = (filterBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                list.Children.Clear();
                int shown = 0;
                foreach (var c in allComputers)
                {
                    if (!string.Equals(activeKey, "All", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        if (activeKey.StartsWith("client:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        {
                            string rest = activeKey.Substring("client:".Length);
                            if (rest.Contains('|'))
                            {
                                var parts = rest.Split('|');
                                if (!string.Equals(c.Family, parts[0], StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                                    || !string.Equals(c.Sub, parts[1], StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                    continue;
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                if (!string.Equals(c.Family, rest, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                    continue;
                            }
                        }
                        else if (activeKey.StartsWith("server:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        {
                            string rest = activeKey.Substring("server:".Length);
                            bool isSrv = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(c.ServerBucket);
                            if (!isSrv) continue;
                            if (!string.Equals(rest, "All", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                                && !string.Equals(c.ServerBucket, rest, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                continue;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            // legacy family key (workstations single panel)
                            if (activeKey.Contains('|'))
                            {
                                var parts = activeKey.Split('|');
                                if (!string.Equals(c.Family, parts[0], StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                                    || !string.Equals(c.Sub, parts[1], StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                    continue;
                            }
                            else if (!string.Equals(c.Family, activeKey, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                continue;
                        }
                    }
                    if (q.Length > 0 && c.Blob.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0)
                        continue;

                    string line = c.Name;
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(line))
                        line = (c.Sam ?? "").TrimEnd('$');
                    string samDisplay = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(c.Name) ? "" : (c.Sam ?? "");

                    list.Children.Add(MkListRow("Computer", line, samDisplay, c.Dn, null));
                    shown++;
                }

                string label = activeKey.StartsWith("client:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    ? activeKey.Substring(7).Replace("|", " / ")
                    : (activeKey.StartsWith("server:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                        ? ("Server " + activeKey.Substring(7))
                        : activeKey.Replace("|", " / "));
                if (string.Equals(activeKey, "All", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    label = "all";
                string unit = workstationsOnly ? "workstation(s)" : "computer(s)";
                statusLine.Text = shown.ToString("N0") + " of " + allComputers.Count.ToString("N0")
                    + " " + unit + " shown (" + label + ")"
                    + (q.Length > 0 ? " matching \"" + q + "\"" : "");
            }

            filterBox.TextChanged += (_, _) => ApplyComputerFilter();

            Button MakeBucketBtn(string key, string label, int count, bool indent)
            {
                var countTb = Tb(count.ToString("N0"), size: indent ? 13 : 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
                var labelTb = Tb(label, size: indent ? 12 : 13, opacity: indent ? 0.85 : 0.95, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
                var row = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto") };
                row.Children.Add(labelTb);
                Grid.SetColumn(countTb, 1);
                row.Children.Add(countTb);
                var btn = new Button
                {
                    Content = row,
                    Classes = { "action" },
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    Padding = new Thickness(indent ? 22 : 12, indent ? 5 : 8),
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 2)
                };
                string keyCap = key;
                btn.Click += (_, _) =>
                {
                    activeKey = keyCap;
                    ApplyComputerFilter();
                    HighlightBucketButtons();
                };
                bucketButtons[key] = btn;
                return btn;
            }

            StackPanel BuildClientBucketHost()
            {
                var host = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };
                return host;
            }

            var clientHost = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };
            var serverHost = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };

            Border MakeSideCard(string title, string hint, StackPanel host)
            {
                var inner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4 };
                inner.Children.Add(Tb(title, size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold));
                inner.Children.Add(Tb(hint, size: 11, opacity: 0.7, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4)));
                inner.Children.Add(host);
                return new Border
                {
                    Classes = { "card" },
                    Padding = new Thickness(10),
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
                    Child = inner
                };
            }

            if (workstationsOnly)
            {
                var clientCard = MakeSideCard(
                    "By operating system",
                    "Windows client versions. Click a version to filter the list.",
                    clientHost);
                Grid.SetColumn(clientCard, 2);
                columns.Children.Add(clientCard);
            }
            else
            {
                var clientCard = MakeSideCard(
                    "Windows clients",
                    "Windows 7 / 8 / 10 / 11. Sub-versions (22H2, 24H2, LTSC, ...) under 10 and 11.",
                    clientHost);
                var serverCard = MakeSideCard(
                    "Windows Server",
                    "Server OS years (2008-2025) and Undetermined. Same rules as the Servers overview.",
                    serverHost);
                Grid.SetColumn(clientCard, 2);
                Grid.SetColumn(serverCard, 4);
                columns.Children.Add(clientCard);
                columns.Children.Add(serverCard);
            }

            content.Children.Add(columns);
            panel.Children.Add(content);

            void RebuildBucketPanels()
            {
                clientHost.Children.Clear();
                serverHost.Children.Clear();
                bucketButtons.Clear();

                int total = allComputers.Count;
                // Shared All on client panel for workstations; on computers put All only once on client panel
                clientHost.Children.Add(MakeBucketBtn("All", workstationsOnly ? "All workstations" : "All computers", total, false));

                var familyOrder = new[] { "Windows 11", "Windows 10", "Windows 8", "Windows 7" };
                var familyCounts = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                var subCounts = new Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, int>>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                foreach (string f in familyOrder)
                {
                    familyCounts[f] = 0;
                    subCounts[f] = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                }

                var serverBuckets = new[] { "2025", "2022", "2019", "2016", "2012", "2008", "Undetermined" };
                var serverCounts = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                foreach (string b in serverBuckets) serverCounts[b] = 0;
                int serverTotal = 0;

                foreach (var c in allComputers)
                {
                    if (familyCounts.ContainsKey(c.Family))
                    {
                        familyCounts[c.Family]++;
                        if (c.Family == "Windows 10" || c.Family == "Windows 11")
                        {
                            string sub = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(c.Sub) ? "Undetermined" : c.Sub;
                            if (!subCounts[c.Family].ContainsKey(sub))
                                subCounts[c.Family][sub] = 0;
                            subCounts[c.Family][sub]++;
                        }
                    }
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(c.ServerBucket))
                    {
                        serverTotal++;
                        if (serverCounts.ContainsKey(c.ServerBucket))
                            serverCounts[c.ServerBucket]++;
                        else
                            serverCounts["Undetermined"]++;
                    }
                }

                foreach (string fam in familyOrder)
                {
                    int fc = familyCounts[fam];
                    if (fc == 0 && (fam == "Windows 7" || fam == "Windows 8"))
                        continue;
                    string keyPrefix = workstationsOnly ? "" : "client:";
                    string famKey = workstationsOnly ? fam : ("client:" + fam);
                    clientHost.Children.Add(MakeBucketBtn(famKey, fam, fc, false));

                    if ((fam == "Windows 10" || fam == "Windows 11") && subCounts[fam].Count > 0)
                    {
                        var subs = subCounts[fam].Keys.OrderBy(s =>
                        {
                            if (s.Equals("Undetermined", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return 9000;
                            if (s.StartsWith("Build ", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return 8000;
                            return 0;
                        }).ThenByDescending(s => s, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();

                        foreach (string sub in subs)
                        {
                            int sc = subCounts[fam][sub];
                            string key = workstationsOnly ? (fam + "|" + sub) : ("client:" + fam + "|" + sub);
                            clientHost.Children.Add(MakeBucketBtn(key, sub, sc, true));
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Other clients (non-server, non-7/8/10/11) on workstations / client panel
                int otherClients = allComputers.Count(c =>
                    string.IsNullOrEmpty(c.ServerBucket)
                    && !familyOrder.Contains(c.Family, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                if (otherClients > 0)
                {
                    string otherKey = workstationsOnly ? "Other" : "client:Other";
                    clientHost.Children.Add(MakeBucketBtn(otherKey, "Other clients", otherClients, false));
                }

                if (!workstationsOnly)
                {
                    serverHost.Children.Add(MakeBucketBtn("server:All", "All servers", serverTotal, false));
                    foreach (string b in serverBuckets)
                    {
                        string label = b == "Undetermined" ? "Undetermined" : ("Server " + b);
                        serverHost.Children.Add(MakeBucketBtn("server:" + b, label, serverCounts[b], false));
                    }
                }

                HighlightBucketButtons();
            }

            Task.Run(() =>
            {
                var found = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Os, string OsVer, string Family, string Sub, string ServerBucket, string Blob)>();
                try
                {
                    using var rootEntry = BindLdap(domainDn);
                    using var s = new DirectorySearcher(rootEntry)
                    {
                        Filter = "(objectClass=computer)",
                        SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                        PageSize = 1000,
                        SizeLimit = 0,
                        CacheResults = false,
                        PropertiesToLoad =
                        {
                            "name", "cn", "sAMAccountName", "distinguishedName",
                            "operatingSystem", "operatingSystemVersion"
                        }
                    };
                    using var r = s.FindAll();
                    foreach (SearchResult sr in r)
                    {
                        string dn = Prop(sr, "distinguishedName");
                        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn)) continue;
                        string name = Prop(sr, "name");
                        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) name = Prop(sr, "cn");
                        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) name = Prop(sr, "sAMAccountName").TrimEnd('$');
                        string sam = Prop(sr, "sAMAccountName");
                        string os = Prop(sr, "operatingSystem");
                        string osVer = Prop(sr, "operatingSystemVersion");

                        bool isServer = IsServerComputer(os, dn);
                        if (workstationsOnly && isServer)
                            continue;

                        var (family, sub) = ClassifyClientOsVersion(os, osVer);
                        if (workstationsOnly && family == "Windows Server")
                            continue;

                        string serverBucket = "";
                        if (isServer)
                            serverBucket = ClassifyServerOsVersion(os, osVer);

                        // For dual-panel computers view: client family only meaningful for non-servers
                        if (!workstationsOnly && isServer)
                        {
                            family = "Windows Server";
                            sub = "";
                        }

                        string blob = string.Join(" ", name, sam, os, osVer, family, sub, serverBucket);
                        found.Add((name, sam, dn, os, osVer, family, sub, serverBucket, blob));
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() => { statusLine.Text = "Load failed: " + ex.Message; });
                    return;
                }

                found = found.OrderBy(x => x.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();
                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    allComputers.Clear();
                    allComputers.AddRange(found);
                    RebuildBucketPanels();
                    ApplyComputerFilter();
                });
            });
        }

        private void ShowServersByOsPanel()
        {
            var panel = this.FindControl<StackPanel>("DetailPanel");
            if (panel == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(_domainDn)) return;
            panel.Children.Clear();
            RecordNavigation("servers-os", null);

            // Declared up front so local functions / lambdas never see an unassigned capture
            var allServers = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Os, string OsVer, string Bucket, string Blob)>();
            string domainDn = _domainDn;
            string? activeBucket = "All";

            // Outer content: title on top, then two equal-width panels side by side
            var content = new StackPanel
            {
                MaxWidth = 1200,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Spacing = 0,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 16)
            };
            {
                var titleRow = new Grid
                {
                    ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto"),
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4)
                };
                titleRow.Children.Add(Tb("Servers", size: 22, weight: FontWeight.Bold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));
                var exportBtn = MkListExportButton("servers",
                    () => allServers.Select(s2 => (s2.Name, s2.Sam, s2.Dn, s2.Os)), "Operating system");
                Grid.SetColumn(exportBtn, 1);
                titleRow.Children.Add(exportBtn);
                content.Children.Add(titleRow);
            }
            content.Children.Add(Tb("Servers by OS name (Windows Server) or by location under a Server/Servers OU. Use the panel on the right to filter by version.", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 10)));

            var columns = new Grid
            {
                ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,12,*"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };

            // ---- LEFT: server list (name only) ----
            var leftInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            var filterBox = new TextBox
            {
                Watermark = "Filter by computer name...",
                MinHeight = 34,
                FontSize = 14,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            leftInner.Children.Add(filterBox);
            var statusLine = Tb("Loading servers...", size: 12, opacity: 0.75);
            leftInner.Children.Add(statusLine);
            var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };
            var scroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 14, 0),
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                Content = list,
                MaxHeight = 560,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
            };
            leftInner.Children.Add(scroll);
            var leftCard = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Padding = new Thickness(10),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch,
                Child = leftInner
            };
            Grid.SetColumn(leftCard, 0);
            columns.Children.Add(leftCard);

            // ---- RIGHT: OS version groupings (same width as left) ----
            var rightInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            rightInner.Children.Add(Tb("By operating system", size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold));
            rightInner.Children.Add(Tb("Click a version to filter the list. Click All servers to clear.", size: 11, opacity: 0.7, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4)));

            string[] buckets = { "All", "2025", "2022", "2019", "2016", "2012", "2008", "Undetermined" };
            var bucketButtons = new Dictionary<string, Button>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            var bucketCountTb = new Dictionary<string, TextBlock>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            foreach (string b in buckets)
            {
                var countTb = Tb(b == "All" ? "..." : "0", size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
                var labelTb = Tb(b == "All" ? "All servers" : (b == "Undetermined" ? "Undetermined" : "Server " + b), size: 13, opacity: 0.9, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
                var row = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto") };
                row.Children.Add(labelTb);
                Grid.SetColumn(countTb, 1);
                row.Children.Add(countTb);
                var btn = new Button
                {
                    Content = row,
                    Classes = { "action" },
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    Padding = new Thickness(12, 8),
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 2)
                };
                string bucketCap = b;
                btn.Click += (_, _) =>
                {
                    activeBucket = bucketCap;
                    ApplyServerFilter();
                    HighlightBucketButtons();
                };
                rightInner.Children.Add(btn);
                bucketButtons[b] = btn;
                bucketCountTb[b] = countTb;
            }

            void HighlightBucketButtons()
            {
                foreach (var kv in bucketButtons)
                {
                    bool on = string.Equals(kv.Key, activeBucket, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                    kv.Value.FontWeight = on ? FontWeight.Bold : FontWeight.Normal;
                    try
                    {
                        kv.Value.Background = on
                            ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(60, 79, 195, 247))
                            : Brushes.Transparent;
                    }
                    catch { }
                }
            }
            HighlightBucketButtons();

            var rightCard = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Padding = new Thickness(10),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
                Child = rightInner
            };
            Grid.SetColumn(rightCard, 2);
            columns.Children.Add(rightCard);

            content.Children.Add(columns);
            panel.Children.Add(content);

            void ApplyServerFilter()
            {
                string q = (filterBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                list.Children.Clear();
                int shown = 0;
                foreach (var s in allServers)
                {
                    if (activeBucket != null
                        && !string.Equals(activeBucket, "All", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                        && !string.Equals(s.Bucket, activeBucket, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        continue;
                    if (q.Length > 0 && s.Blob.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0)
                        continue;

                    // Name only (no OS version beside the name)
                    string line = s.Name;
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(line))
                        line = (s.Sam ?? "").TrimEnd('$');
                    string samDisplay = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.Name) ? "" : (s.Sam ?? "");

                    list.Children.Add(MkListRow("Computer", line, samDisplay, s.Dn, null));
                    shown++;
                }

                string bucketLabel = string.Equals(activeBucket, "All", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    ? "all versions"
                    : (string.Equals(activeBucket, "Undetermined", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                        ? "Undetermined"
                        : ("Server " + activeBucket));
                statusLine.Text = shown.ToString("N0") + " of " + allServers.Count.ToString("N0")
                    + " server(s) shown (" + bucketLabel + ")"
                    + (q.Length > 0 ? " matching \"" + q + "\"" : "");
            }

            filterBox.TextChanged += (_, _) => ApplyServerFilter();

            Task.Run(() =>
            {
                var found = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Os, string OsVer, string Bucket, string Blob)>();
                try
                {
                    // Shared server enumeration (briefly cached - see
                    // LoadServerComputers): it already keeps only servers
                    // (Windows Server OS or a Server/Servers OU).
                    foreach (var x in LoadServerComputers(domainDn))
                    {
                        string bucket = ClassifyServerOsVersion(x.Os, x.OsVer);
                        // Blob still includes OS for search; display is name-only
                        string blob = string.Join(" ", x.Name, x.Sam, x.Os, x.OsVer, bucket);
                        found.Add((x.Name, x.Sam, x.Dn, x.Os, x.OsVer, bucket, blob));
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        statusLine.Text = "Load failed: " + ex.Message;
                    });
                    return;
                }

                found = found.OrderBy(x => x.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();

                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    allServers.Clear();
                    allServers.AddRange(found);

                    var counts = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    {
                        ["All"] = found.Count,
                        ["2025"] = 0,
                        ["2022"] = 0,
                        ["2019"] = 0,
                        ["2016"] = 0,
                        ["2012"] = 0,
                        ["2008"] = 0,
                        ["Undetermined"] = 0
                    };
                    foreach (var s in found)
                    {
                        if (counts.ContainsKey(s.Bucket))
                            counts[s.Bucket]++;
                        else
                            counts["Undetermined"]++;
                    }
                    foreach (var kv in counts)
                    {
                        if (bucketCountTb.TryGetValue(kv.Key, out var tb))
                            tb.Text = kv.Value.ToString("N0");
                    }

                    ApplyServerFilter();
                });
            });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Renders a simple drill-down list (no per-row actions) from items
        /// already in memory - mirrors ShowStatList's non-quick-delete
        /// rendering, shared with the snapshot path below.
        /// </summary>
        private void ShowStatListItems(string title, string type,
            List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, bool Protected)> items)
        {
            string filterHint = type switch
            {
                "Computer" => "Filter by computer name...",
                "Group" => "Filter by group name...",
                "User" => "Filter by name or username...",
                _ => "Filter by name..."
            };
            var rows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, Border Row)>();
            var exportItems = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, bool Protected)>();
            var exportBtn = MkListExportButton(title,
                () => exportItems.Select(x => (x.Name, x.Sam, x.Dn, "")),
                "");
            var shell = BeginListPanel(title, "Loading...", null, filterHint, exportBtn);
            if (shell == null) return;
            var (filterBox, statusLine, list) = shell.Value;

            items = items.OrderBy(x => x.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();
            exportItems.AddRange(items);
            statusLine.Text = $"{items.Count:N0} object(s). Click a row to open properties.";
            foreach (var item in items)
            {
                var border = MkListRow(type, item.Name, item.Sam, item.Dn, null);
                list.Children.Add(border);
                rows.Add((item.Name, item.Sam, item.Dn, border));
            }

            filterBox.TextChanged += (_, _) =>
            {
                string q = (filterBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                int shown = 0;
                foreach (var row in rows)
                {
                    bool match = q.Length == 0
                        || row.Name.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                        || row.Sam.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
                    row.Row.IsVisible = match;
                    if (match) shown++;
                }
                statusLine.Text = q.Length == 0
                    ? $"{rows.Count:N0} object(s). Click a row to open properties."
                    : $"{shown:N0} of {rows.Count:N0} shown.";
            };
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Drill-down lists whose LDAP filter maps exactly onto snapshot
        /// fields: served from the shared snapshot when that section loaded
        /// cleanly; otherwise the original per-panel LDAP search runs
        /// (ShowStatList) unchanged.
        /// </summary>
        private async void ShowSnapStatList(string title, string filter, string type,
            Func<DomainSnapshot, bool> sectionOk,
            Func<DomainSnapshot, List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, bool Protected)>> select)
        {
            DomainSnapshot snap;
            try { snap = await GetDomainSnapshotAsync(); }
            catch { ShowStatList(title, filter, type, false); return; }
            if (!sectionOk(snap))
            {
                ShowStatList(title, filter, type, false);
                return;
            }
            ShowStatListItems(title, type, select(snap));
        }

        // The row-title fallback every simple list uses:
        // displayName, then name, then cn, then the DN itself.
        private static string SnapTitle(string display, string name, string cn, string dn)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(display)) return display;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) return name;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cn)) return cn;
            return dn;
        }


        private void ShowStatList(string title, string filter, string type, bool withUnlock, bool withQuickDelete = false)
        {
            string filterHint = type switch
            {
                "Computer" => "Filter by computer name...",
                "Group" => "Filter by group name...",
                "User" => "Filter by name or username...",
                _ => "Filter by name..."
            };
            var rows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, Border Row)>();
            // Full result set for export (Name, Sam, Dn, Protected)
            var exportItems = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, bool Protected)>();
            string domainDn = _domainDn;

            Button? exportEmptyBtn = null;
            if (withQuickDelete)
            {
                exportEmptyBtn = new Button
                {
                    Content = "Export",
                    Classes = { "action" },
                    MinWidth = 100,
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(exportEmptyBtn, "Export empty groups to CSV (excludes built-in / system groups)");
            }
            else
            {
                exportEmptyBtn = MkListExportButton(title,
                    () => exportItems.Select(x => (x.Name, x.Sam, x.Dn, "")),
                    "");
            }
            var shell = BeginListPanel(title, "Loading...", null, filterHint, exportEmptyBtn);
            if (shell == null) return;
            var (filterBox, statusLine, list) = shell.Value;

            Task.Run(() =>
            {
                var items = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, bool Protected)>();
                try
                {
                    using var rootEntry = BindLdap(domainDn);
                    using var s = new DirectorySearcher(rootEntry)
                    {
                        Filter = filter,
                        SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                        PageSize = 1000,
                        SizeLimit = 0,
                        PropertiesToLoad = { "name", "cn", "displayName", "sAMAccountName", "distinguishedName", "objectSid", "isCriticalSystemObject" }
                    };
                    using var r = s.FindAll();
                    foreach (SearchResult sr in r)
                    {
                        string Sp(string p)
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                if (sr.Properties.Contains(p) && sr.Properties[p].Count > 0)
                                    return sr.Properties[p][0]?.ToString() ?? "";
                            }
                            catch { }
                            return "";
                        }
                        string dn = Sp("distinguishedName");
                        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn)) continue;
                        string name = Sp("displayName");
                        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) name = Sp("name");
                        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) name = Sp("cn");
                        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) name = dn;
                        string sam = Sp("sAMAccountName");
                        byte[]? sid = null;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("objectSid") && sr.Properties["objectSid"].Count > 0)
                                sid = sr.Properties["objectSid"][0] as byte[];
                        }
                        catch { }
                        object? crit = null;
                        try
                        {
                            if (sr.Properties.Contains("isCriticalSystemObject") && sr.Properties["isCriticalSystemObject"].Count > 0)
                                crit = sr.Properties["isCriticalSystemObject"][0];
                        }
                        catch { }
                        bool prot = IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(dn, sid, crit, sam);
                        items.Add((name, sam, dn, prot));
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() => statusLine.Text = "Error: " + ex.Message);
                    return;
                }

                items = items.OrderBy(x => x.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();

                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    exportItems.Clear();
                    exportItems.AddRange(items);
                    int deletable = items.Count(x => !x.Protected);
                    statusLine.Text = withQuickDelete
                        ? $"{items.Count:N0} empty group(s) ({deletable:N0} not built-in). Open, export, or delete from a row."
                        : $"{items.Count:N0} object(s). Click a row to open properties.";
                    if (withQuickDelete && exportEmptyBtn != null)
                    {
                        exportEmptyBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
                        {
                            var toExport = exportItems.Where(x => !x.Protected).ToList();
                            if (toExport.Count == 0)
                            {
                                ShowNotification("No non-built-in empty groups to export.", "info");
                                return;
                            }
                            ShowEmptyGroupsExportDialog(toExport);
                        };
                    }
                    foreach (var item in items)
                    {
                        bool showDelete = withQuickDelete
                            && string.Equals(type, "Group", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            && !item.Protected;
                        string capDn = item.Dn;
                        string capName = item.Name;
                        string capSam = item.Sam;
                        var border = MkListRow(type, item.Name, item.Sam, item.Dn, null,
                            showDelete ? rowBorder =>
                            {
                                var delBtn = new Button
                                {
                                    Content = "Delete",
                                    Classes = { "action" },
                                    Width = 84,
                                    Height = 28,
                                    Margin = new Thickness(4, 0, 4, 0),
                                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center,
                                    Padding = new Thickness(8, 2),
                                    Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(176, 96, 96)),
                                    Foreground = Brushes.White
                                };
                                delBtn.PointerEntered += (_, _) => rowBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(70, 176, 96, 96));
                                delBtn.PointerExited += (_, _) => rowBorder.Background = Brushes.Transparent;
                                delBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
                                {
                                    var qdlg = new Window
                                    {
                                        Title = "Delete empty group",
                                        Width = 420,
                                        SizeToContent = SizeToContent.Height,
                                        WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                                        CanResize = false
                                    };
                                    StyleDialogWindow(qdlg);
                                    var qroot = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(20), Spacing = 12 };
                                    qroot.Children.Add(Tb("Delete empty group?", size: 18, weight: FontWeight.Bold));
                                    qroot.Children.Add(Tb(capName + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(capSam) ? "" : " (" + capSam + ")"), weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, wrap: true));
                                    qroot.Children.Add(Tb("This group has no members. Deletion cannot be undone.", opacity: 0.85, wrap: true));
                                    var qerr = new TextBlock
                                    {
                                        Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(229, 115, 115)),
                                        FontSize = 12,
                                        TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap,
                                        IsVisible = false
                                    };
                                    qroot.Children.Add(qerr);
                                    var qbtns = new StackPanel
                                    {
                                        Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                                        Spacing = 10,
                                        HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right
                                    };
                                    var qCancel = new Button { Content = "Cancel", MinWidth = 100, Classes = { "action" } };
                                    var qYes = new Button
                                    {
                                        Content = "Delete",
                                        MinWidth = 100,
                                        Classes = { "action" },
                                        Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(176, 96, 96)),
                                        Foreground = Brushes.White
                                    };
                                    qCancel.Click += (_, _) => qdlg.Close();
                                    qYes.Click += (_, _) =>
                                    {
                                        try
                                        {
                                            using var entry = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + capDn);
                                            // Re-verify at delete time - the list this row came
                                            // from may be hours old. Fail closed: any doubt
                                            // blocks the delete instead of deleting.
                                            if (IsProtectedAdObject(entry))
                                            {
                                                qerr.Text = "Delete blocked: this group is protected.";
                                                qerr.IsVisible = true;
                                                return;
                                            }
                                            int memberCount;
                                            try { memberCount = entry.Properties["member"]?.Count ?? 0; }
                                            catch { memberCount = -1; }
                                            if (memberCount != 0)
                                            {
                                                qerr.Text = memberCount > 0
                                                    ? "Delete blocked: the group now has " + memberCount + " member(s). The list is stale - refresh it."
                                                    : "Delete blocked: could not verify the group is still empty.";
                                                qerr.IsVisible = true;
                                                return;
                                            }
                                            // Primary-group members never appear in 'member'.
                                            if (GroupHasPrimaryMembers(entry))
                                            {
                                                qerr.Text = "Delete blocked: this group is the primary group for one or more users.";
                                                qerr.IsVisible = true;
                                                return;
                                            }
                                            entry.DeleteTree();
                                            qdlg.Close();
                                            ShowNotification("Group deleted: " + capName, "success");
