                                            _statsLoaded = false;
                                            InvalidateDomainSnapshot();
                                            list.Children.Remove(rowBorder);
                                            rows.RemoveAll(r => string.Equals(r.Dn, capDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                                            statusLine.Text = rows.Count.ToString("N0") + " empty group(s) remaining.";
                                        }
                                        catch (Exception ex)
                                        {
                                            qerr.Text = "Delete failed: " + ex.Message;
                                            qerr.IsVisible = true;
                                        }
                                    };
                                    qbtns.Children.Add(qCancel);
                                    qbtns.Children.Add(qYes);
                                    qroot.Children.Add(qbtns);
                                    qdlg.Content = qroot;
                                    try { qdlg.ShowDialog(this); } catch { qdlg.Show(); }
                                };
                                return delBtn;
                            } : null);
                        list.Children.Add(border);
                        rows.Add((item.Name, item.Sam, item.Dn, border));
                    }

                    filterBox.TextChanged += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        string q = (filterBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                        int shown = 0;
                        foreach (var row in rows)
                        {
                            bool match = q.Length == 0
                                || row.Name.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                                || row.Sam.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
                            row.Row.IsVisible = match;
                            if (match) shown++;
                        }
                        statusLine.Text = q.Length == 0
                            ? $"{rows.Count:N0} object(s). Click a row to open properties."
                            : $"{shown:N0} of {rows.Count:N0} shown.";
                    };
                });
            });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Shared CSV export for the dashboard lists - the same convention as the
        /// empty-groups export: UTF-8 CSV in Documents\ADE_Exports, a notification,
        /// and the file opened. Columns are Name / SamAccountName /
        /// DistinguishedName plus one optional detail column.
        /// </summary>
        private void ExportListCsv(string baseName,
            IEnumerable<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Detail)> rows, string detailHeader)
        {
            var list = rows.ToList();
            if (list.Count == 0)
            {
                ShowNotification("Nothing to export yet - let the list finish loading.", "info");
                return;
            }
            try
            {
                string CsvEsc(string? v) => "\"" + (v ?? "").Replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\"";
                string slug = new string(baseName.ToLowerInvariant()
                    .Select(ch => char.IsLetterOrDigit(ch) ? ch : '_').ToArray()).Trim('_');
                if (slug.Length == 0) slug = "ade_list";
                string dir = Path.Combine(
                    Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments),
                    "ADE_Exports");
                Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
                string file = Path.Combine(dir, slug + "_" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss") + ".csv");
                var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
                var headers = new List<string> { "Name", "SamAccountName", "DistinguishedName" };
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(detailHeader)) headers.Add(detailHeader);
                sb.AppendLine(string.Join(",", headers.Select(h => CsvEsc(h))));
                foreach (var r in list)
                {
                    var cells = new List<string> { r.Name, r.Sam, r.Dn };
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(detailHeader)) cells.Add(r.Detail);
                    sb.AppendLine(string.Join(",", cells.Select(c => CsvEsc(c))));
                }
                File.WriteAllText(file, sb.ToString(), new System.Text.UTF8Encoding(encoderShouldEmitUTF8Identifier: true));
                ShowNotification($"Exported {list.Count} row(s) to Documents\\ADE_Exports\\{Path.GetFileName(file)}", "success");
                try
                {
                    System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = file,
                        UseShellExecute = true
                    });
                }
                catch { }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowNotification("Export failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
            }
        }

        private Button MkListExportButton(string baseName,
            Func<IEnumerable<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Detail)>> getRows, string detailHeader)
        {
            var btn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Export",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 100,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(btn, "Export this list to CSV (Documents\\ADE_Exports)");
            btn.Click += (_, _) => ExportListCsv(baseName, getRows(), detailHeader);
            return btn;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// THE shell for every dashboard drill-down list: clears the detail panel
        /// and builds the title (with an optional action at its right), an optional
        /// explanatory note, the filter box, the status line and the scrolling card
        /// list - identical in every panel, so no drill-down looks different from
        /// another. Pairs with MkListRow, the one standard row. Returns null -
        /// after the same guard the panels each applied - when the panel or
        /// domain is unavailable.
        /// </summary>
        private (TextBox Filter, TextBlock Status, StackPanel List)? BeginListPanel(
            string title, string loadingText, string? note = null,
            string filterHint = "Filter by name or username...", Control? titleAction = null)
        {
            var panel = this.FindControl<StackPanel>("DetailPanel");
            if (panel == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(_domainDn)) return null;
            panel.Children.Clear();

            var content = new StackPanel
            {
                MaxWidth = 1200,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Spacing = 0,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 16)
            };
            var titleTb = Tb(title, size: 22, weight: FontWeight.Bold,
                halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
            if (titleAction == null)
            {
                titleTb.Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4);
                content.Children.Add(titleTb);
            }
            else
            {
                var titleRow = new Grid
                {
                    ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto"),
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4),
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                };
                titleRow.Children.Add(titleTb);
                Grid.SetColumn(titleAction, 1);
                titleRow.Children.Add(titleAction);
                content.Children.Add(titleRow);
            }
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(note))
                content.Children.Add(Tb(note, size: 12, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8)));

            var filterBox = new TextBox
            {
                Watermark = filterHint,
                MinHeight = 34,
                FontSize = 14,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8)
            };
            content.Children.Add(filterBox);

            var statusLine = Tb(loadingText, size: 12, opacity: 0.75, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 6));
            content.Children.Add(statusLine);

            var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };
            content.Children.Add(new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Child = new ScrollViewer
                {
                    Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0),
                    AllowAutoHide = false,
                    Content = list,
                    MaxHeight = 640,
                    VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                    HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
                },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            });
            panel.Children.Add(content);
            return (filterBox, statusLine, list);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// THE row for every dashboard drill-down list (see BeginListPanel):
        /// type icon + "Name  (sam)" on the first line, an optional dimmed
        /// subtitle under it, and an optional action control in the right
        /// column. Clicking the row opens the object's page. The action is
        /// built by a factory that receives the finished row border, so its
        /// handlers can tint or remove the row itself.
        /// </summary>
        private Border MkListRow(string type, string name, string sam, string dn, string? subtitle,
            Func<Border, Control>? actionFactory = null, string nameSuffix = "")
        {
            var text = new StackPanel { Spacing = 1 };
            text.Children.Add(MakeIconLine(type,
                name + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sam) ? "" : "  (" + sam + ")") + nameSuffix, 13, 13, bold: false));
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(subtitle))
                text.Children.Add(Tb(subtitle, size: 11, opacity: 0.7, wrap: true));
            var openBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = text,
                Classes = { "memberLink" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                VerticalContentAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
            };
            openBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
                ShowHelpDeskInfo(new AdNode { Name = name, Dn = dn, Type = type, SamAccountName = sam });
            var row = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto"), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            Grid.SetColumn(openBtn, 0);
            row.Children.Add(openBtn);
            var border = new Border { Child = row, Padding = new Thickness(2, 2), CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(4), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            if (actionFactory != null)
            {
                var action = actionFactory(border);
                Grid.SetColumn(action, 1);
                row.Children.Add(action);
            }
            return border;
        }

        private void ShowLockedUsersPanel()
        {
            var exportRows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Detail)>();
            var shell = BeginListPanel("Locked users", "Loading locked accounts...",
                titleAction: MkListExportButton("locked_users", () => exportRows, ""));
            if (shell == null) return;
            var (filterBox, statusLine, list) = shell.Value;


            var rows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, Border Row)>();
            Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                var items = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, long LockTime)>();
                var snap = await GetDomainSnapshotAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
                if (!snap.UsersOk)
                {
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() => statusLine.Text = "Scan failed: the domain could not be read. Press Refresh on the overview and try again.");
                    return;
                }
                foreach (var u in snap.Users)
                {
                    if (u.LockoutTime == 0) continue;
                    items.Add((SnapTitle(u.Display, u.Name, u.Cn, u.Dn), u.Sam, u.Dn, u.LockoutTime));
                }

                items = items.OrderByDescending(x => x.LockTime).ToList();

                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    statusLine.Text = $"{items.Count:N0} locked account(s). Most recently locked first. Use Unlock or open properties.";
                    exportRows.Clear();
                    exportRows.AddRange(items.Select(x => (x.Name, x.Sam, x.Dn, "")));
                    foreach (var item in items)
                    {
                        string capDn = item.Dn;
                        string capName = item.Name;
                        var border = MkListRow("User", item.Name, item.Sam, item.Dn,
                            item.LockTime > 0 ? ("Locked " + DateTime.FromFileTime(item.LockTime).ToString("g")) : "Locked",
                            row =>
                            {
                                var unlockBtn = new Button
                                {
                                    Content = "Unlock",
                                    Classes = { "action" },
                                    FontSize = 12,
                                    Padding = new Thickness(10, 4),
                                    Margin = new Thickness(4, 2),
                                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                                };
                                unlockBtn.PointerEntered += (_, _) =>
                                {
                                    row.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(60, 33, 150, 243));
                                };
                                unlockBtn.PointerExited += (_, _) =>
                                {
                                    row.Background = Brushes.Transparent;
                                };
                                unlockBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
                                {
                                    try
                                    {
                                        using var fresh = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + capDn);
                                        fresh.Properties["lockoutTime"].Value = 0;
                                        fresh.CommitChanges();
                                        ShowNotification("Unlocked: " + capName, "success");
                                        unlockBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                                        unlockBtn.Content = "Unlocked";
                                        row.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(50, 27, 94, 32));
                                        if (_statLocked > 0) { _statLocked--; }
                                    }
                                    catch (Exception ex)
                                    {
                                        ShowNotification("Unlock failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                                    }
                                };
                                return unlockBtn;
                            });
                        list.Children.Add(border);
                        rows.Add((item.Name, item.Sam, item.Dn, border));
                    }

                    filterBox.TextChanged += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        string q = (filterBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                        int shown = 0;
                        foreach (var row in rows)
                        {
                            bool match = q.Length == 0
                                || row.Name.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                                || row.Sam.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
                            row.Row.IsVisible = match;
                            if (match) shown++;
                        }
                        statusLine.Text = q.Length == 0
                            ? $"{rows.Count:N0} locked account(s)."
                            : $"{shown:N0} of {rows.Count:N0} shown.";
                    };
                });
            });
        }

        private void ShowPasswordExpiredPanel()
        {
            var exportRows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Detail)>();
            var shell = BeginListPanel("Password expired", "Scanning password ages...",
                titleAction: MkListExportButton("password_expired", () => exportRows, ""));
            if (shell == null) return;
            var (filterBox, statusLine, list) = shell.Value;


            var rows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, Border Row)>();
            string domainDn = _domainDn;
            TimeSpan? maxPwd = _maxPwdAge;

            Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                var items = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Reason, DateTime ExpiredAt)>();
                var snap = await GetDomainSnapshotAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
                if (!snap.UsersOk)
                {
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() => statusLine.Text = "Scan failed: the domain could not be read. Press Refresh on the overview and try again.");
                    return;
                }
                foreach (var u in snap.Users)
                {
                    if ((u.Uac & 0x10000) != 0) continue;
                    // Only age-based expiry (skip "must change at next logon" / pwdLastSet==0)
                    if (!u.PwdLastSet.HasValue || u.PwdLastSet.Value <= 0) continue;
                    if (!maxPwd.HasValue || maxPwd.Value.TotalDays <= 0) continue;
                    DateTime expOn;
                    try { expOn = DateTime.FromFileTime(u.PwdLastSet.Value) + maxPwd.Value; }
                    catch { continue; }
                    if (expOn >= DateTime.Now) continue;
                    double days = (DateTime.Now - expOn).TotalDays;
                    string reason = "Expired " + expOn.ToString("g") + " (" + days.ToString("F0") + "d ago)";
                    items.Add((SnapTitle(u.Display, u.Name, u.Cn, u.Dn), u.Sam, u.Dn, reason, expOn));
                }

                items = items.OrderByDescending(x => x.ExpiredAt).ToList();

                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    statusLine.Text = $"{items.Count:N0} account(s). Most recently expired first. Click to open and set a new password.";
                    exportRows.Clear();
                    exportRows.AddRange(items.Select(x => (x.Name, x.Sam, x.Dn, "")));
                    foreach (var item in items)
                    {
                        var border = MkListRow("User", item.Name, item.Sam, item.Dn, item.Reason);
                        list.Children.Add(border);
                        rows.Add((item.Name, item.Sam, item.Dn, border));
                    }

                    filterBox.TextChanged += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        string q = (filterBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                        int shown = 0;
                        foreach (var row in rows)
                        {
                            bool match = q.Length == 0
                                || row.Name.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                                || row.Sam.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
                            row.Row.IsVisible = match;
                            if (match) shown++;
                        }
                        statusLine.Text = q.Length == 0
                            ? $"{rows.Count:N0} account(s)."
                            : $"{shown:N0} of {rows.Count:N0} shown.";
                    };
                });
            });
        }


        private void ShowDisabledUsersPanel()
        {
            var exportRows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, string Detail)>();
            var shell = BeginListPanel("Disabled users", "Loading disabled accounts...",
                titleAction: MkListExportButton("disabled_users", () => exportRows, ""));
            if (shell == null) return;
            var (filterBox, statusLine, list) = shell.Value;


            var rows = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, Border Row)>();
            Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                var items = new List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, DateTime WhenDisabled)>();
                var snap = await GetDomainSnapshotAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
                if (!snap.UsersOk)
                {
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() => statusLine.Text = "Scan failed: the domain could not be read. Press Refresh on the overview and try again.");
                    return;
                }
                foreach (var u in snap.Users)
                {
                    if ((u.Uac & 0x2) == 0) continue;
                    items.Add((SnapTitle(u.Display, u.Name, u.Cn, u.Dn), u.Sam, u.Dn, u.WhenChanged));
                }

                items = items.OrderByDescending(x => x.WhenDisabled).ToList();

                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    statusLine.Text = $"{items.Count:N0} disabled account(s). Most recently changed first.";
                    exportRows.Clear();
                    exportRows.AddRange(items.Select(x => (x.Name, x.Sam, x.Dn, "")));
                    foreach (var item in items)
                    {
                        string capDn = item.Dn;
                        string capName = item.Name;
                        var border = MkListRow("User", item.Name, item.Sam, item.Dn,
                            item.WhenDisabled > DateTime.MinValue
                                ? ("Disabled / changed " + item.WhenDisabled.ToLocalTime().ToString("g"))
                                : "Disabled",
                            row =>
                            {
                                var enableBtn = new Button
                                {
                                    Content = "Enable",
                                    Classes = { "action" },
                                    FontSize = 12,
                                    Padding = new Thickness(10, 4),
                                    Margin = new Thickness(4, 2),
                                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                                };
                                enableBtn.PointerEntered += (_, _) =>
                                {
                                    row.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(60, 33, 150, 243));
                                };
                                enableBtn.PointerExited += (_, _) =>
                                {
                                    row.Background = Brushes.Transparent;
                                };
                                enableBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
                                {
                                    try
                                    {
                                        using var fresh = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + capDn);
                                        int cur = (int)(fresh.Properties["userAccountControl"].Value ?? 0);
                                        fresh.Properties["userAccountControl"].Value = cur & ~0x2;
                                        fresh.CommitChanges();
                                        ShowNotification("Enabled: " + capName, "success");
                                        enableBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                                        enableBtn.Content = "Enabled";
                                        row.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(50, 27, 94, 32));
                                        if (_statDisabled > 0) _statDisabled--;
                                    }
                                    catch (Exception ex)
                                    {
                                        ShowNotification("Enable failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                                    }
                                };
                                return enableBtn;
                            });
                        list.Children.Add(border);
                        rows.Add((item.Name, item.Sam, item.Dn, border));
                    }

                    filterBox.TextChanged += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        string q = (filterBox.Text ?? "").Trim();
                        int shown = 0;
                        foreach (var row in rows)
                        {
                            bool match = q.Length == 0
                                || row.Name.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                                || row.Sam.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
                            row.Row.IsVisible = match;
                            if (match) shown++;
                        }
                        statusLine.Text = q.Length == 0
                            ? $"{rows.Count:N0} disabled account(s)."
                            : $"{shown:N0} of {rows.Count:N0} shown.";
                    };
                });
            });
        }

    }
}
'@
$mwDetailCs = @'
// ADE - MainWindow partial class: user/computer/group detail pages, protection and delete, login history, OU move.
// Split from MainWindow.axaml.cs for maintainability (2026-10-26):
// same partial class, same namespace - no behavior change.
using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.DirectoryServices;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Net.Security;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Security.Principal;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Avalonia;

using Avalonia.Controls.Primitives;

namespace __APPNAME__
{
    public partial class MainWindow : Window
    {
        private void ShowContainerContents(AdNode node)
        {
            RecordNavigation("container", node);
            var panel = this.FindControl<StackPanel>("DetailPanel");
            if (panel == null) return;
            panel.Children.Clear();

            panel.Children.Add(MakeIconLine(node.Type, node.Name, 22, 22, bold: true));
            panel.Children.Add(Tb(node.Dn, size: 12, opacity: 0.7, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));

            if (node.Type == "Domain")
                LoadDomainStats(panel);

            var statusLine = Tb("Loading contents...", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left);
            panel.Children.Add(statusLine);

            var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            var listBorder = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Child = list
            };
            panel.Children.Add(listBorder);

            string nodeDn = node.Dn;
            Task.Run(() =>
            {
                var children = new List<AdNode>();
                try
                {
                    using var de = BindLdap(nodeDn);
                    foreach (DirectoryEntry entry in de.Children)
                    {
                        using (entry)
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                string name = entry.Name;
                                if (name.StartsWith("CN=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                    name.StartsWith("OU=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                    name = name.Substring(3);

                                string dn;
                                try { dn = entry.Properties["distinguishedName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? ""; }
                                catch { dn = ""; }
                                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn))
                                {
                                    try { dn = entry.Path.StartsWith("LDAP://", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? entry.Path.Substring(7) : entry.Path; }
                                    catch { continue; }
                                }

                                string type = "Unknown";
                                bool isContainer = false;
                                try
                                {
                                    var ocProp = entry.Properties["objectClass"];
                                    if (ocProp != null && ocProp.Count > 0)
                                    {
                                        for (int i = ocProp.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                                        {
                                            string c = ocProp[i]?.ToString() ?? "";
                                            if (c.Equals("organizationalUnit", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                            { type = "OU"; isContainer = true; break; }
                                            if (c.Equals("container", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                                c.Equals("builtinDomain", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                            { type = "Container"; isContainer = true; break; }
                                            if (c.Equals("computer", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                            { type = "Computer"; break; }
                                            if (c.Equals("group", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                            { type = "Group"; break; }
                                            if (c.Equals("user", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                            { type = "User"; break; }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                catch { }

                                string sam = "";
                                if (!isContainer)
                                {
                                    try { sam = entry.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? ""; } catch { }
                                }

                                children.Add(new AdNode
                                {
                                    Name = name,
                                    Type = type,
                                    Dn = dn,
                                    SamAccountName = sam,
                                    IsContainer = isContainer,
                                    Parent = node
                                });
                            }
                            catch { }
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch { }

                children = children
                    .OrderBy(x => x.IsContainer ? 0 : 1)
                    .ThenBy(x => x.Type switch
                    {
                        "OU" => 0,
                        "Container" => 1,
                        "User" => 2,
                        "Group" => 3,
                        "Computer" => 4,
                        _ => 5
                    })
                    .ThenBy(x => x.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    .ToList();

                Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                {
                    statusLine.Text = children.Count == 0
                        ? "No child objects."
                        : $"{children.Count} object(s). Click a user, computer, or group to open properties.";
                    list.Children.Clear();
                    foreach (var c in children)
                    {
                        var btn = new Button
                        {
                            Content = MakeIconLine(c.Type, FormatObjectLine(c), 14, 14),
                            Classes = { "memberLink" },
                            HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                            HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                            Tag = c
                        };
                        var captured = c;
                        btn.Click += (_, _) => OpenFromList(captured);
                        list.Children.Add(btn);
                    }
                });
            });
        }

        private void OpenFromList(AdNode node)
        {
            if (node.IsContainer)
            {
                if (node.UiItem != null)
                {
                    var tree = this.FindControl<TreeView>("AdTree");
                    if (tree != null)
                    {
                        node.UiItem.IsExpanded = true;
                        tree.SelectedItem = node.UiItem;
                    }
                }
                ShowContainerContents(node);
                return;
            }
            ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
        }

        private void ShowHelpDeskInfo(AdNode node)
        {

            RecordNavigation("object", node);            var panel = this.FindControl<StackPanel>("DetailPanel");
            if (panel == null) return;
            panel.Children.Clear();

            // ---- Title row: icon + name + status badges + refresh ----
            int uacTop = 0;
            bool disabledTop = false, lockedTop = false, pwdNeverTop = false;
            string emailTop = "", samTop = node.SamAccountName;
            try
            {
                using var dePeek = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + node.Dn);
                try { uacTop = (int)(dePeek.Properties["userAccountControl"]?.Value ?? 0); } catch { }
                disabledTop = (uacTop & 0x2) != 0;
                pwdNeverTop = (uacTop & 0x10000) != 0;
                try
                {
                    var lt = ReadLargeInteger(dePeek.Properties["lockoutTime"]?.Value);
                    lockedTop = lt.HasValue && lt.Value != 0;
                }
                catch { }
                try { emailTop = dePeek.Properties["mail"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? ""; } catch { }
                try
                {
                    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(samTop))
                        samTop = dePeek.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                }
                catch { }

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(node.Type)
                    || node.Type.Equals("Unknown", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    || node.Type.Equals("Object", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    string schema = "";
                    try { schema = dePeek.SchemaClassName ?? ""; } catch { }
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(schema))
                    {
                        string detected = schema switch
                        {
                            "user" => "User",
                            "group" => "Group",
                            "computer" => "Computer",
                            "organizationalUnit" => "OU",
                            "container" => "Container",
                            _ => node.Type
                        };
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(detected)) node.Type = detected;
                    }
                }
            }
            catch { }

            var titleRow = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto") };
            var titleLeft = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 10, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center };
            titleLeft.Children.Add(MakeIconLine(node.Type, node.Name, 22, 22, bold: true));
            if (node.Type == "User" || node.Type == "Computer")
            {
                titleLeft.Children.Add(MakeBadge(disabledTop ? "DISABLED" : "ENABLED", disabledTop ? "statusBad" : "statusOk"));
                if (node.Type == "User")
                    titleLeft.Children.Add(MakeBadge(lockedTop ? "LOCKED" : "UNLOCKED", lockedTop ? "statusBad" : "statusOk"));
                if (pwdNeverTop)
                    titleLeft.Children.Add(MakeBadge("PWD NEVER EXPIRES", "statusWarn"));
            }
            if (node.Type == "Computer")
            {
                // Fast online/offline badge  -  fully off UI thread (short timeouts, parallel ports)
                var onlineBadge = MakeBadge("CHECKING...", "statusWarn");
                titleLeft.Children.Add(onlineBadge);
                string pingHost = node.Name.TrimEnd('$');
                try
                {
                    using var dePing = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + node.Dn);
                    dePing.RefreshCache(new[] { "dNSHostName", "sAMAccountName" });
                    string? dns = dePing.Properties["dNSHostName"]?.Value?.ToString();
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dns)) pingHost = dns.Trim();
                    else
                    {
                        string? sam = dePing.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString();
                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sam)) pingHost = sam.Trim().TrimEnd('$');
                    }
                }
                catch { }
                pingHost = ResolveRdpHost(pingHost);
                string pingHostCap = pingHost;
                _ = Task.Run(() =>
                {
                    string state = "OFFLINE";
                    string detail = "No response";
                    string cls = "statusBad";
                    try
                    {
                        var ping = TryPingHost(pingHostCap, 400);
                        if (ping.Ok)
                        {
                            state = "ONLINE";
                            detail = "Ping " + ping.Ms + " ms";
                            cls = "statusOk";
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            int[] ports = new[] { 445, 135, 3389, 5985, 443 };
                            int openPort = -1;
                            System.Threading.Tasks.Parallel.ForEach(ports, (port, loop) =>
                            {
                                if (TryTcpPort(pingHostCap, port, 400))
                                {
                                    System.Threading.Interlocked.CompareExchange(ref openPort, port, -1);
                                    loop.Stop();
                                }
                            });
                            if (openPort > 0)
                            {
                                state = "ONLINE";
                                detail = "TCP " + openPort + " open (ICMP blocked)";
                                cls = "statusOk";
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                bool hasIp = false;
                                try
                                {
                                    var addrs = System.Net.Dns.GetHostAddresses(pingHostCap);
                                    hasIp = addrs != null && addrs.Length > 0;
                                }
                                catch { }
                                if (hasIp)
                                {
                                    state = "NO RESPONSE";
                                    detail = "DNS/IP ok, but ping and common ports failed";
                                    cls = "statusWarn";
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        state = "OFFLINE";
                        detail = ex.Message;
                        cls = "statusBad";
                    }
                    string stateCap = state, detailCap = detail, clsCap = cls;
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            if (onlineBadge.Child is TextBlock tb)
                                tb.Text = stateCap;
                            onlineBadge.Classes.Clear();
                            onlineBadge.Classes.Add(clsCap);
                            ToolTip.SetTip(onlineBadge, detailCap + "\nHost: " + pingHostCap);
                        }
                        catch { }
                    });
                });

                try
                {
                    using var deStale = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + node.Dn);
                    deStale.RefreshCache(new[] { "lastLogonTimestamp", "lastLogon" });
                    DateTime? last = null;
                    var ts = ReadLargeInteger(deStale.Properties["lastLogonTimestamp"]?.Value);
                    if (ts.HasValue && ts.Value > 0)
                        last = DateTime.FromFileTimeUtc(ts.Value).ToLocalTime();
                    if (!last.HasValue)
                    {
                        var ll = ReadLargeInteger(deStale.Properties["lastLogon"]?.Value);
                        if (ll.HasValue && ll.Value > 0)
                            last = DateTime.FromFileTimeUtc(ll.Value).ToLocalTime();
                    }
                    int days = Math.Max(1, _staleComputerDays);
                    if (!last.HasValue)
                        titleLeft.Children.Add(MakeBadge("NO LOGON DATA", "statusWarn"));
                    else if (last.Value < DateTime.Now.AddDays(-days))
                    {
                        int age = (int)(DateTime.Now - last.Value).TotalDays;
                        titleLeft.Children.Add(MakeBadge("STALE " + age + "d", "statusBad"));
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        int age = Math.Max(0, (int)(DateTime.Now - last.Value).TotalDays);
                        titleLeft.Children.Add(MakeBadge("LAST LOGON " + age + "d AGO", "statusOk"));
                    }
                }
                catch { }
            }
            Grid.SetColumn(titleLeft, 0);
            titleRow.Children.Add(titleLeft);

            var titleBtns = new StackPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                Spacing = 8,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
            };
            if (node.Type == "Group")
            {
                var exportBtn = new Button { Content = "Export", Classes = { "action" }, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center };
                ToolTip.SetTip(exportBtn, "Export group membership to CSV");
                string exportDn = node.Dn;
                string exportName = node.Name;
                exportBtn.Click += (_, _) => ShowGroupExportDialog(exportDn, exportName);
                titleBtns.Children.Add(exportBtn);
            }
            var refreshBtn = new Button { Content = "Refresh", Classes = { "action" }, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center };
            var capturedNode = node;
            refreshBtn.Click += (_, _) => ShowHelpDeskInfo(capturedNode);
            titleBtns.Children.Add(refreshBtn);
            Grid.SetColumn(titleBtns, 1);
            titleRow.Children.Add(titleBtns);
            panel.Children.Add(titleRow);

            panel.Children.Add(Tb(node.Type + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(samTop) ? "" : "  |  " + samTop), size: 13, opacity: 0.75, margin: new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 4), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));


            try
            {
                using var de = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + node.Dn);

                // Common attributes with copy buttons for username & email
                // Copy icon sits immediately after the value (not far-right aligned).
                // Text MaxWidth is updated on layout so long values still wrap inside the card.
                var grid = new Grid
                {
                    ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("150,*"),
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                };
                var valueTextBlocks = new List<TextBlock>();
                int row = 0;
                void AddRow(string label, string? value, bool copyable = false)
                {
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value) && !copyable) return;
                    grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });
                    var lbl = Tb(label, weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, margin: new Thickness(0, 5), valign: VerticalAlignment.Top);
                    var valRow = new StackPanel
                    {
                        Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                        Spacing = 6,
                        Margin = new Thickness(0, 5),
                        HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                        VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                    };
                    var valTb = Tb(value ?? "", wrap: true, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
                    valueTextBlocks.Add(valTb);
                    valRow.Children.Add(valTb);
                    if (copyable && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
                    {
                        var copyBtn = new Button
                        {
                            Content = "\U0001F4CB",
                            Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                            Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                            BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                            Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand),
                            VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top
                        };
                        ToolTip.SetTip(copyBtn, "Copy");
                        string capturedVal = value!;
                        copyBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                var top = TopLevel.GetTopLevel(this);
                                if (top?.Clipboard != null)
                                {
                                    await top.Clipboard.SetTextAsync(capturedVal);
                                    ShowNotification("Copied to clipboard", "success");
                                }
                            }
                            catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Copy failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
                        };
                        valRow.Children.Add(copyBtn);
                    }
                    Grid.SetRow(lbl, row); Grid.SetColumn(lbl, 0);
                    Grid.SetRow(valRow, row); Grid.SetColumn(valRow, 1);
                    grid.Children.Add(lbl);
                    grid.Children.Add(valRow);
                    row++;
                }
                grid.SizeChanged += (_, e) =>
                {
                    // Leave room for label column (150) + padding + optional copy icon (~28)
                    double max = Math.Max(120, e.NewSize.Width - 150 - 40);
                    foreach (var tb in valueTextBlocks)
                        tb.MaxWidth = max;
                };

                string displayName = de.Properties["displayName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? de.Properties["name"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                string logonName = de.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                string upn = de.Properties["userPrincipalName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                string description = de.Properties["description"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                string email = de.Properties["mail"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                string phone = de.Properties["telephoneNumber"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                string department = de.Properties["department"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                string title = de.Properties["title"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                string profilePath = de.Properties["profilePath"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                string homeDirectory = de.Properties["homeDirectory"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                string homeDrive = de.Properties["homeDrive"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";

                // Helper: When Created / Last Logon strings (shared)
                string BuildWhenCreated()
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var cv = de.Properties["whenCreated"]?.Value;
                        DateTime createdDt;
                        if (cv is DateTime cdt) createdDt = cdt;
                        else if (cv != null && DateTime.TryParse(cv.ToString(), out createdDt)) { }
                        else return cv?.ToString() ?? "";
                        if (createdDt <= DateTime.MinValue) return "";
                        var span = DateTime.Now - createdDt;
                        int totalDays = Math.Max(0, (int)span.TotalDays);
                        int years = totalDays / 365;
                        int days = totalDays % 365;
                        string age = years > 0
                            ? years + (years == 1 ? " year " : " years ") + days + (days == 1 ? " day" : " days")
                            : totalDays + (totalDays == 1 ? " day" : " days");
                        return createdDt.ToString("g") + " ( " + age + " )";
                    }
                    catch { return ""; }
                }
                string BuildLastLogon()
                {
                    DateTime? best = null;
                    try
                    {
                        var ts = ReadLargeInteger(de.Properties["lastLogonTimestamp"]?.Value);
                        if (ts.HasValue && ts.Value > 0 && ts.Value < 0x7FFFFFFFFFFFFFFF)
                            best = DateTime.FromFileTime(ts.Value);
                    }
                    catch { }
                    try
                    {
                        var ll = ReadLargeInteger(de.Properties["lastLogon"]?.Value);
                        if (ll.HasValue && ll.Value > 0 && ll.Value < 0x7FFFFFFFFFFFFFFF)
                        {
                            var dt = DateTime.FromFileTime(ll.Value);
                            if (!best.HasValue || dt > best.Value) best = dt;
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }
                    if (!best.HasValue) return "Never / not recorded";
                    var span = DateTime.Now - best.Value;
                    string ago;
                    if (span.TotalMinutes < 60)
                        ago = Math.Max(0, (int)span.TotalMinutes) + " min ago";
                    else if (span.TotalHours < 24)
                        ago = (int)span.TotalHours + " hr ago";
                    else if (span.TotalDays < 365)
                        ago = (int)span.TotalDays + " day" + ((int)span.TotalDays == 1 ? "" : "s") + " ago";
                    else
                    {
                        int y = (int)(span.TotalDays / 365);
                        ago = y + " year" + (y == 1 ? "" : "s") + " ago";
                    }
                    return best.Value.ToString("g") + "  (" + ago + ")";
                }

                if (node.Type == "Computer")
                {
                    // Computer/server details - no Logon Name (duplicate of title), no user fields
                    string dnsHost = "";
                    try { dnsHost = de.Properties["dNSHostName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? ""; } catch { }
                    string os = "";
                    try { os = de.Properties["operatingSystem"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? ""; } catch { }
                    string osVer = "";
                    try { osVer = de.Properties["operatingSystemVersion"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? ""; } catch { }
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(osVer) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(os))
                        os = os + "  (" + osVer + ")";
                    else if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(os) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(osVer))
                        os = osVer;

                    string shortName = logonName.Trim().TrimEnd('$');
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(shortName)) shortName = node.Name;
                    string hostDisplay = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dnsHost) ? dnsHost : shortName;

                    string ouPath = "";
                    try
                    {
                        string dn = node.Dn ?? "";
                        int comma = dn.IndexOf(',');
                        if (comma >= 0 && comma + 1 < dn.Length)
                            ouPath = dn.Substring(comma + 1);
                    }
                    catch { }

                    AddRow("Display Name", displayName, copyable: true);
                    AddRow("Host", hostDisplay, copyable: true);

                    // IP directly under Host
                    string resolveTarget = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dnsHost) ? dnsHost : shortName;
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(resolveTarget) && !resolveTarget.Contains('.')
                        && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_dnsRoot))
                        resolveTarget = resolveTarget + "." + _dnsRoot.Trim().TrimStart('.');

                    grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });
                    var ipLbl = Tb("IP Address", weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, margin: new Thickness(0, 5), valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
                    var ipValRow = new StackPanel
                    {
                        Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                        Spacing = 6,
                        Margin = new Thickness(0, 5),
                        VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                    };
                    var ipText = Tb("Resolving...", opacity: 0.7, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
                    ipValRow.Children.Add(ipText);
                    var ipCopy = new Button
                    {
                        Content = "\U0001F4CB",
                        Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                        Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                        BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                        Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand),
                        VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                        IsVisible = false
                    };
                    ipValRow.Children.Add(ipCopy);
                    Grid.SetRow(ipLbl, row); Grid.SetColumn(ipLbl, 0);
                    Grid.SetRow(ipValRow, row); Grid.SetColumn(ipValRow, 1);
                    grid.Children.Add(ipLbl);
                    grid.Children.Add(ipValRow);
                    row++;

                    string resolveCap = resolveTarget;
                    Task.Run(() =>
                    {
                        string ip = "";
                        try
                        {
                            var addrs = System.Net.Dns.GetHostAddresses(resolveCap);
                            ip = addrs.FirstOrDefault(a => a.AddressFamily == System.Net.Sockets.AddressFamily.InterNetwork)?.ToString()
                                ?? addrs.FirstOrDefault()?.ToString()
                                ?? "";
                        }
                        catch { }
                        Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                        {
                            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ip))
                            {
                                ipText.Text = "Unavailable";
                                ipText.Opacity = 0.5;
                                return;
                            }
                            ipText.Text = ip;
                            ipText.Opacity = 1;
                            ipCopy.IsVisible = true;
                            string ipCap = ip;
                            ToolTip.SetTip(ipCopy, "Copy IP address");
                            ipCopy.Click += async (_, _) =>
                            {
                                try
                                {
                                    var top = TopLevel.GetTopLevel(this);
                                    if (top?.Clipboard != null)
                                    {
                                        await top.Clipboard.SetTextAsync(ipCap);
                                        ShowNotification("Copied to clipboard", "success");
                                    }
                                }
                                catch { }
                            };
                        });
                    });

                    // Uptime (WMI last boot)  -  async like IP
                    grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });
                    var upLbl = Tb("Uptime", weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, margin: new Thickness(0, 5), valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
                    var upText = Tb("Checking...", opacity: 0.7, wrap: true, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
                    Grid.SetRow(upLbl, row); Grid.SetColumn(upLbl, 0);
                    Grid.SetRow(upText, row); Grid.SetColumn(upText, 1);
                    grid.Children.Add(upLbl);
                    grid.Children.Add(upText);
                    row++;

                    string uptimeHost = resolveTarget;
                    Task.Run(() =>
                    {
                        string up = "";
                        try { up = QueryRemoteUptimeText(uptimeHost); } catch { }
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(up))
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                string shortH = shortName;
                                if (!string.Equals(shortH, uptimeHost, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                    up = QueryRemoteUptimeText(shortH);
                            }
                            catch { }
                        }
                        Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                        {
                            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(up))
                            {
                                upText.Text = "Unavailable (WMI / admin rights required)";
                                upText.Opacity = 0.5;
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                upText.Text = up;
                                upText.Opacity = 1;
                            }
                        });
                    });

                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(os))
                        AddRow("Operating System", os);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ouPath))
                        AddRow("OU / Location", ouPath, copyable: true);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(description))
                        AddRow("Description", description);
                    AddRow("When Created", BuildWhenCreated());
                    AddRow("Last Logon", BuildLastLogon());
                }
                else
                {
                    // User / Group / other details
                    AddRow("Display Name", displayName, copyable: true);
                    AddRow("Logon Name", logonName, copyable: true);
                    if (node.Type != "Group")
                    {
                        AddRow("User Principal Name", upn, copyable: true);
                        AddRow("Description", description);
                        AddRow("Email", email, copyable: true);
                        AddRow("Phone", phone);
                        AddRow("Department", department);
                        AddRow("Title", title);
                        AddRow("Profile path", profilePath);
                        AddRow("Home folder", homeDirectory);
                        AddRow("Home drive", homeDrive);
                    }
                    // DN location at top of details (user + group)
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(node.Dn))
                        AddRow("Location (DN)", node.Dn, copyable: true);
                    AddRow("When Created", BuildWhenCreated());
                    if (node.Type != "Group")
                        AddRow("Last Logon", BuildLastLogon());
                }

                // ===== Efficient full-width layout =====
                // Row 1: Attributes | Edit Details  (side by side, fill width)
                // Row 2: Account Actions | Password
                // Row 3: Groups (full width)
                // Row 4: Move to OU (full width)

                if (node.Type == "User")
                {
                    string nodeDnEdit = node.Dn;
                    // Two equal-width columns, panels stacked tight (no blank gaps)
                    var cols = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,8,*"), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 2) };
                    var leftCol = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
                    var rightCol = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };

                    // LEFT: Details
                    var detailsInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8 };
                    detailsInner.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Details", FontSize = 16, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left });
                    detailsInner.Children.Add(grid);
                    leftCol.Children.Add(new Border
                    {
                        Classes = { "card" },
                        Child = detailsInner,
                        HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                        Padding = new Thickness(10),
                        ClipToBounds = true
                    });

                    // LEFT: Account Actions + Groups (tight)
                    RenderUserActions(leftCol, de, node);
                    RenderGroupPanel(leftCol, de, node);

                    // RIGHT: Edit Details (tabbed)
                    var tabs = new TabControl { HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, MinHeight = 300 };
                    var basicEditGrid = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("110,*") }; int ber = 0;
                    TextBox MakeBasicEdit(string label, string initial) { basicEditGrid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto }); var el = new TextBlock { Text = label, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, FontSize = 14, Margin = new Thickness(0, 4), VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center }; var tb = new TextBox { Text = initial, FontSize = 14, FontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, Margin = new Thickness(0, 2), MinHeight = 34, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch }; Grid.SetRow(el, ber); Grid.SetColumn(el, 0); Grid.SetRow(tb, ber); Grid.SetColumn(tb, 1); basicEditGrid.Children.Add(el); basicEditGrid.Children.Add(tb); ber++; return tb; }
                    var tbDesc = MakeBasicEdit("Description", description); var tbMail = MakeBasicEdit("Email", email); var tbPhone = MakeBasicEdit("Phone", phone); var tbDept = MakeBasicEdit("Department", department); var tbTitle = MakeBasicEdit("Title", title); var tbProfile = MakeBasicEdit("Profile path", profilePath); var tbHomeDir = MakeBasicEdit("Home folder", homeDirectory); var tbHomeDrive = MakeBasicEdit("Home drive", homeDrive);
                    var basicSave = new Button { Content = "Save Basic Changes", Classes = { "action" }, Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left };
                    basicSave.Click += (_, _) => { try { using var fresh = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDnEdit); void SP(string p, string v) { if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v)) { if (fresh.Properties.Contains(p) && fresh.Properties[p].Count > 0) fresh.Properties[p].Clear(); } else fresh.Properties[p].Value = v; } SP("description", tbDesc.Text ?? ""); SP("mail", tbMail.Text ?? ""); SP("telephoneNumber", tbPhone.Text ?? ""); SP("department", tbDept.Text ?? ""); SP("title", tbTitle.Text ?? ""); SP("profilePath", tbProfile.Text ?? ""); SP("homeDirectory", tbHomeDir.Text ?? ""); SP("homeDrive", tbHomeDrive.Text ?? ""); fresh.CommitChanges(); ShowNotification("Basic details saved for " + node.Name, "success"); ShowHelpDeskInfo(node); } catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Save failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); } };
                    var basicInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2, Margin = new Thickness(4) }; basicInner.Children.Add(basicEditGrid); basicInner.Children.Add(basicSave);
                    tabs.Items.Add(new TabItem { Header = "Basic Details", Content = basicInner });

                    // Advanced: schema attributes + live search, readable fixed-height rows
                    var advFields = new Dictionary<string, TextBox>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                    var advRows = new List<(string Name, Grid Row)>();
                    var readOnlyAttrs = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase) { "objectSid","objectGUID","whenCreated","whenChanged","uSNCreated","uSNChanged","distinguishedName","cn","name","objectClass","objectCategory","memberOf","primaryGroupID","userAccountControl","pwdLastSet","lastLogon","lastLogonTimestamp","lockoutTime","badPwdCount","badPasswordTime","logonCount","accountExpires","dSCorePropagationData","instanceType","nTSecurityDescriptor","sAMAccountType","canonicalName","createTimeStamp","modifyTimeStamp","tokenGroups","isCriticalSystemObject","adminCount","lastLogoff","logonHours","userParameters","msDS-User-Account-Control-Computed","msDS-ResultantPSO","msDS-ReplAttributeMetaData","msDS-ReplValueMetaData","directReports","managedObjects","systemFlags","showInAdvancedViewOnly" };
                    const double RowH = 42;
                    const double AttrFont = 14;
                    var advList = new StackPanel { Spacing = 0, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 0), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
                    var hdr = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("260,*"), Height = RowH, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0), Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(90, 100, 110, 140)) };
                    hdr.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Attribute", FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, FontSize = AttrFont, Margin = new Thickness(8, 0), VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center });
                    var hdrVal = new TextBlock { Text = "Value", FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, FontSize = AttrFont, Margin = new Thickness(8, 0), VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center }; Grid.SetColumn(hdrVal, 1); hdr.Children.Add(hdrVal);
                    advList.Children.Add(hdr);
                    try
                    {
                        var valueMap = new Dictionary<string, List<string>>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        var binarySet = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        SearchResult? full = null;
                        try
                        {
                            using var srch = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDnEdit)) { Filter = "(objectClass=*)", SearchScope = SearchScope.Base };
                            srch.PropertiesToLoad.Add("*");
                            srch.PropertiesToLoad.Add("canonicalName"); srch.PropertiesToLoad.Add("createTimeStamp"); srch.PropertiesToLoad.Add("modifyTimeStamp");
                            full = srch.FindOne();
                        }
                        catch { }
                        void AddVal(string pn, object? raw)
                        {
                            if (raw == null) return;
                            if (!valueMap.TryGetValue(pn, out var list)) { list = new List<string>(); valueMap[pn] = list; }
                            if (raw is byte[] bytes)
                            {
                                binarySet.Add(pn);
                                if (pn.Equals("objectSid", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { try { list.Add(new System.Security.Principal.SecurityIdentifier(bytes, 0).ToString()); } catch { list.Add(BitConverter.ToString(bytes)); } }
                                else if (pn.Equals("objectGUID", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && bytes.Length == 16) list.Add(new Guid(bytes).ToString());
                                else if (bytes.Length <= 128) list.Add(BitConverter.ToString(bytes));
                                else list.Add("(" + bytes.Length + " bytes)");
                            }
                            else if (raw is DateTime dt) list.Add(dt.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"));
                            else
                            {
                                try
                                {
                                    var t = raw.GetType();
                                    if (t.Name.Contains("LargeInteger") || t.GetProperty("HighPart") != null)
                                    {
                                        long hi = Convert.ToInt64(t.InvokeMember("HighPart", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.GetProperty, null, raw, null));
                                        long lo = Convert.ToInt64(t.InvokeMember("LowPart", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.GetProperty, null, raw, null)) & 0xFFFFFFFFL;
                                        long ft = (hi << 32) | lo;
                                        if (ft > 0 && ft < 0x7FFFFFFFFFFFFFFF) { try { list.Add(DateTime.FromFileTime(ft).ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")); return; } catch { } }
                                        list.Add(ft.ToString()); return;
                                    }
                                }
                                catch { }
                                string s = raw.ToString() ?? "";
                                if (s.Length > 0) list.Add(s);
                            }
                        }
                        if (full != null)
                        {
                            foreach (string pn in full.Properties.PropertyNames)
                            {
                                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(pn) || pn.Equals("adspath", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                                foreach (object ov in full.Properties[pn]) AddVal(pn, ov);
                            }
                        }
                        foreach (PropertyValueCollection pvc in de.Properties)
                        {
                            if (pvc == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(pvc.PropertyName)) continue;
                            if (valueMap.ContainsKey(pvc.PropertyName)) continue;
                            foreach (object ov2 in pvc) AddVal(pvc.PropertyName, ov2);
                        }
                        var allAttrs = new SortedSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        foreach (var k in valueMap.Keys) allAttrs.Add(k);
                        try
                        {
                            string schemaNc = "";
                            using (var root = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://RootDSE"))
                                schemaNc = root.Properties["schemaNamingContext"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(schemaNc))
                            {
                                var classes = new List<string>();
                                if (full != null && full.Properties.Contains("objectClass"))
                                    foreach (object oc1 in full.Properties["objectClass"]) if (oc1 != null) classes.Add(oc1.ToString()!);
                                else if (de.Properties.Contains("objectClass"))
                                    foreach (object oc2 in de.Properties["objectClass"]) if (oc2 != null) classes.Add(oc2.ToString()!);
                                if (classes.Count == 0) classes.AddRange(new[] { "top", "person", "organizationalPerson", "user" });
                                foreach (string cls in classes)
                                {
                                    try
                                    {
                                        using var csSch = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + schemaNc))
                                        {
                                            Filter = $"(&(objectClass=classSchema)(lDAPDisplayName={EscapeLdap(cls)}))",
                                            SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                                            SizeLimit = 1
                                        };
                                        csSch.PropertiesToLoad.Add("mustContain"); csSch.PropertiesToLoad.Add("mayContain");
                                        csSch.PropertiesToLoad.Add("systemMustContain"); csSch.PropertiesToLoad.Add("systemMayContain");
                                        var cr = csSch.FindOne();
                                        if (cr == null) continue;
                                        foreach (string prop in new[] { "mustContain", "mayContain", "systemMustContain", "systemMayContain" })
                                        {
                                            if (!cr.Properties.Contains(prop)) continue;
                                            foreach (object oa in cr.Properties[prop])
                                            {
                                                string attr = oa?.ToString() ?? "";
                                                if (attr.Length > 0) allAttrs.Add(attr);
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                    catch { }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }
                        int rowIdx = 0;
                        foreach (string pn in allAttrs)
                        {
                            valueMap.TryGetValue(pn, out var vals);
                            vals ??= new List<string>();
                            string display = vals.Count == 0 ? "" : string.Join("; ", vals);
                            bool ro = readOnlyAttrs.Contains(pn) || binarySet.Contains(pn) || pn.StartsWith("msDS-", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                            var rowG = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("260,*"), Height = RowH, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0), Background = (rowIdx++ % 2 == 0) ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(32, 128, 128, 128)) : Brushes.Transparent };
                            rowG.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = pn, FontSize = AttrFont, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, Margin = new Thickness(8, 0), VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center, TextTrimming = TextTrimming.CharacterEllipsis });
                            if (ro)
                            {
                                var valTb = new TextBlock { Text = display, FontSize = AttrFont, Margin = new Thickness(8, 0, 6, 0), VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center, TextTrimming = TextTrimming.CharacterEllipsis, Opacity = 0.9 };
                                Grid.SetColumn(valTb, 1); rowG.Children.Add(valTb);
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                var tb = new TextBox { Text = display, FontSize = AttrFont, Height = RowH - 6, Margin = new Thickness(2, 3, 6, 3), Padding = new Thickness(6, 0), VerticalContentAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
                                Grid.SetColumn(tb, 1); rowG.Children.Add(tb); advFields[pn] = tb;
                            }
                            advList.Children.Add(rowG);
                            advRows.Add((pn, rowG));
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }
                    var advSave = new Button { Content = "Save Advanced Changes", Classes = { "action" }, Margin = new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left };
                    advSave.Click += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            using var fresh = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDnEdit);
                            int changed = 0;
                            foreach (var kv in advFields)
                            {
                                string prop = kv.Key; string v = kv.Value.Text ?? "";
                                string cur = "";
                                try { if (fresh.Properties.Contains(prop) && fresh.Properties[prop].Count > 0) cur = fresh.Properties[prop].Count == 1 ? (fresh.Properties[prop].Value?.ToString() ?? "") : string.Join("; ", fresh.Properties[prop].Cast<object>().Select(x => x?.ToString() ?? "")); } catch { }
                                if (string.Equals(cur, v, StringComparison.Ordinal)) continue;
                                try { if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v)) { if (fresh.Properties.Contains(prop) && fresh.Properties[prop].Count > 0) { fresh.Properties[prop].Clear(); changed++; } } else if (v.Contains(";")) { fresh.Properties[prop].Clear(); foreach (var part in v.Split(new[] { ';' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)) { string t = part.Trim(); if (t.Length > 0) fresh.Properties[prop].Add(t); } changed++; } else { fresh.Properties[prop].Value = v; changed++; } } catch { }
                            }
                            if (changed > 0) { fresh.CommitChanges(); ShowNotification("Advanced details saved (" + changed + " attribute(s)) for " + node.Name, "success"); ShowHelpDeskInfo(node); }
                            else ShowNotification("No changes to save.", "info");
                        }
                        catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Advanced save failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
                    };
                    var advSearch = new TextBox { Watermark = "Filter attributes (live search)...", FontSize = 14, FontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, MinHeight = 36, Padding = new Thickness(10, 6) };
