                    var advCount = new TextBlock { Text = "", FontSize = 11, Opacity = 0.7, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 2) };
                    void ApplyAdvFilter()
                    {
                        string q = (advSearch.Text ?? "").Trim();
                        int shown = 0;
                        foreach (var (name, row) in advRows)
                        {
                            bool match = q.Length == 0 || name.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
                            if (!match && advFields.TryGetValue(name, out var box))
                                match = (box.Text ?? "").IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
                            row.IsVisible = match;
                            if (match) shown++;
                        }
                        advCount.Text = q.Length == 0 ? $"{advRows.Count} attributes" : $"{shown} of {advRows.Count} attributes";
                    }
                    advSearch.TextChanged += (_, _) => ApplyAdvFilter();
                    ApplyAdvFilter();
                    var advScroll = new ScrollViewer { Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0), AllowAutoHide = false, Content = advList, Height = 420, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch, VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Visible, HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled };
                    var advInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4, Margin = new Thickness(4, 4, 4, 4), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
                    advInner.Children.Add(advSearch);
                    advInner.Children.Add(advCount);
                    advInner.Children.Add(advScroll);
                    advInner.Children.Add(advSave);
                    tabs.Items.Add(new TabItem { Header = "Advanced Details", Content = advInner });
                    var editShell = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8 };
                    editShell.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Edit Details", FontSize = 16, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left });
                    editShell.Children.Add(tabs);
                    rightCol.Children.Add(new Border { Classes = { "card" }, Child = editShell, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, Padding = new Thickness(10) });

                    // RIGHT: Password
                    RenderPasswordPanel(rightCol, de, node);

                    // LEFT: Move to OU (single panel from RenderMoveToOu - no outer duplicate card)
                    RenderMoveToOu(leftCol, de, node);

                    Grid.SetColumn(leftCol, 0); Grid.SetColumn(rightCol, 2);
                    cols.Children.Add(leftCol); cols.Children.Add(rightCol);
                    panel.Children.Add(cols);

                    // Full-width: Horizon active sessions + workstation login history
                    RenderHorizonSessionsPanel(panel, logonName);
                    RenderLoginHistoryPanel(panel, logonName);

                    panel.Children.Add(new Border { Height = 80, Background = Brushes.Transparent });
                }
                else if (node.Type == "Group")
                {
                    panel.Children.Add(new Border { Classes = { "card" }, Child = grid, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch });
                    RenderGroupActions(panel, de, node);
                    RenderGroupMemberManagement(panel, de, node);
                }
                else if (node.Type == "Computer")
                {
                    panel.Children.Add(new Border { Classes = { "card" }, Child = grid, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch });

                    // Resolve host for session queries
                    string sessionHost = node.Name;
                    try
                    {
                        string? dns = de.Properties["dNSHostName"]?.Value?.ToString();
                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dns)) sessionHost = dns.Trim();
                        else
                        {
                            string? sam = de.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString();
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sam)) sessionHost = sam.Trim().TrimEnd('$');
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }

                    // Equal-height half panels: Actions | Logged-in users
                    var split = new Grid
                    {
                        ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,12,*"),
                        RowDefinitions = new RowDefinitions("Auto"),
                        Margin = new Thickness(0, 12, 0, 8),
                        HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                    };

                    var leftCard = new Border
                    {
                        Classes = { "card" },
                        HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                        VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch,
                        Padding = new Thickness(12),
                        MinHeight = 220,
                        ClipToBounds = true
                    };
                    var leftBody = new StackPanel { Spacing = 10, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
                    RenderComputerActions(leftBody, de, node);
                    leftCard.Child = leftBody;
                    Grid.SetColumn(leftCard, 0);
                    Grid.SetRow(leftCard, 0);
                    split.Children.Add(leftCard);

                    var rightCard = new Border
                    {
                        Classes = { "card" },
                        HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                        VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch,
                        Padding = new Thickness(12),
                        MinHeight = 220
                    };
                    var rightBody = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
                    RenderComputerActiveSessionsPanel(rightBody, sessionHost);
                    rightCard.Child = rightBody;
                    Grid.SetColumn(rightCard, 2);
                    Grid.SetRow(rightCard, 0);
                    split.Children.Add(rightCard);

                    panel.Children.Add(split);

                    // Local profile activity via C$\Users (most recently modified profile folder)
                    string profileHost = sessionHost;
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(profileHost))
                    {
                        try { profileHost = de.Properties["dNSHostName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? node.Name; }
                        catch { profileHost = node.Name; }
                    }
                    RenderLocalProfileActivity(panel, profileHost);

                    // Computer logon history (same log format as user login history)
                    string compLogName = node.SamAccountName;
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(compLogName))
                    {
                        try { compLogName = de.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? node.Name; }
                        catch { compLogName = node.Name; }
                    }
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(compLogName)) compLogName = node.Name;
                    compLogName = (compLogName ?? "").Trim().TrimEnd('$');
                    RenderComputerLoginHistoryPanel(panel, compLogName);

                    panel.Children.Add(new Border { Height = 80, Background = Brushes.Transparent });
                }
                else
                {
                    panel.Children.Add(new Border { Classes = { "card" }, Child = grid, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch });
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                panel.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Error reading object: " + ex.Message, Foreground = Brushes.OrangeRed });
            }
        }


        private void RenderPasswordPanel(StackPanel panel, DirectoryEntry de, AdNode node)
        {
            int uac = 0;
            try { uac = (int)(de.Properties["userAccountControl"]?.Value ?? 0); } catch { }
            bool pwdNeverExp = (uac & 0x10000) != 0;
            DateTime? pwdLastSet = null;
            try
            {
                var ft = ReadLargeInteger(de.Properties["pwdLastSet"]?.Value);
                if (ft.HasValue && ft.Value > 0 && ft.Value < 0x7FFFFFFFFFFFFFFF)
                    pwdLastSet = DateTime.FromFileTime(ft.Value);
            }
            catch { }
            string nodeDn = node.Dn;
            string nodeName = node.Name;

            // Equal half split: left = status/set password, right = domain rules + examples (centered)
            var split = new Grid
            {
                ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,16,*"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };

            // ----- LEFT: status + set password -----
            var left = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top };
            var statusGrid = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("100,*") };
            int r = 0;
            void SAdd(string l, string v)
            {
                statusGrid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });
                var lbl = new TextBlock { Text = l, FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, FontSize = 13, Margin = new Thickness(0, 3) };
                var val = new TextBlock { Text = v, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap, FontSize = 13, Margin = new Thickness(0, 3) };
                Grid.SetRow(lbl, r); Grid.SetColumn(lbl, 0);
                Grid.SetRow(val, r); Grid.SetColumn(val, 1);
                statusGrid.Children.Add(lbl); statusGrid.Children.Add(val); r++;
            }
            if (pwdLastSet.HasValue)
            {
                var daysAgo = (DateTime.Now - pwdLastSet.Value).TotalDays;
                SAdd("Last set", $"{pwdLastSet.Value:MM/dd/yyyy}  ({daysAgo:F0} days ago)");
                if (pwdNeverExp)
                    SAdd("Expires", "Never (flag on account)");
                else if (_maxPwdAge.HasValue && _maxPwdAge.Value.TotalDays > 0)
                {
                    var expiresOn = pwdLastSet.Value + _maxPwdAge.Value;
                    var remaining = (expiresOn - DateTime.Now).TotalDays;
                    if (remaining < 0)
                        SAdd("Expires", $"{expiresOn:MM/dd/yyyy}  (EXPIRED {Math.Abs(remaining):F0} days ago)");
                    else
                        SAdd("Expires", $"{expiresOn:MM/dd/yyyy}  (in {remaining:F0} days)");
                }
                else
                    SAdd("Expires", "No domain max age policy");
            }
            else
            {
                SAdd("Last set", "Never / must change at next logon");
                SAdd("Expires", "Immediate");
            }
            left.Children.Add(statusGrid);
            left.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Set new password", FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, FontSize = 14, Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 2), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left });
                        var passBox = new TextBox { HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, Watermark = "New password...", MinHeight = 34, FontSize = 14, Padding = new Thickness(8, 6, 36, 6) };
            passBox.PasswordChar = (char)0x2022;
            var confirmBox = new TextBox { HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, Watermark = "Confirm password...", MinHeight = 34, FontSize = 14, Padding = new Thickness(8, 6, 36, 6) };
            confirmBox.PasswordChar = (char)0x2022;
            bool passVisible = false;
                        // B&W geometric eye glyphs (open / closed)
            string eyeOpen = "\u25CE";  // ◎
            string eyeShut = "\u2298";  // ⊘
            Button MakeInnerEye(TextBox target)
            {
                var eye = new Button
                {
                    Content = eyeShut,
                    Width = 28, Height = 28,
                    Padding = new Thickness(0),
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 4, 0),
                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                    FontSize = 14,
                    FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    Foreground = Brushes.Gray,
                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand),
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(eye, "Show / hide password");
                eye.Click += (_, _) =>
                {
                    passVisible = !passVisible;
                    char ch = passVisible ? '\0' : (char)0x2022;
                    passBox.PasswordChar = ch;
                    confirmBox.PasswordChar = ch;
                    string icon = passVisible ? eyeOpen : eyeShut;
                    eye.Content = icon;
                    // sync sibling eyes via Tag list
                    if (eye.Tag is System.Collections.Generic.List<Button> peers)
                        foreach (var p in peers) p.Content = icon;
                };
                return eye;
            }
            var eye1 = MakeInnerEye(passBox);
            var eye2 = MakeInnerEye(confirmBox);
            var peerList = new System.Collections.Generic.List<Button> { eye1, eye2 };
            eye1.Tag = peerList; eye2.Tag = peerList;
            var passRow = new Grid { HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            passRow.Children.Add(passBox);
            passRow.Children.Add(eye1);
            var confirmRow = new Grid { HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            confirmRow.Children.Add(confirmBox);
            confirmRow.Children.Add(eye2);
            var setBtn = new Button { Content = "Set Password", Classes = { "action" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, MinWidth = 140, FontSize = 14 };
            setBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                string p1 = passBox.Text ?? "";
                string p2 = confirmBox.Text ?? "";
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(p1)) { ShowNotification("Password cannot be empty.", "error"); return; }
                if (p1 != p2) { ShowNotification("Passwords do not match.", "error"); return; }
                try
                {
                    using var fresh = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDn);
                    fresh.Invoke("SetPassword", new object[] { p1 });
                    try { fresh.Properties["lockoutTime"].Value = 0; } catch { }
                    // Force a change at next logon - the standard AD mechanism
                    // (pwdLastSet = 0 means "must change password at next logon"),
                    // so a helpdesk-chosen password never stays valid indefinitely.
                    try { fresh.Properties["pwdLastSet"].Value = 0; } catch { }
                    fresh.CommitChanges();
                    // Verify the must-change flag actually landed before claiming it.
                    bool mustChange = false;
                    try
                    {
                        using var checkEntry = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDn);
                        checkEntry.RefreshCache(new[] { "pwdLastSet" });
                        long? pls = ReadLargeInteger(checkEntry.Properties["pwdLastSet"]?.Value);
                        mustChange = pls.HasValue && pls.Value == 0;
                    }
                    catch { }
                    ShowNotification(mustChange
                        ? $"Password set for {nodeName} (account unlocked; must change at next logon - verified)."
                        : $"Password set for {nodeName} (account unlocked). WARNING: the must-change flag could not be verified - check the account in AD.",
                        mustChange ? "success" : "error");
                    passBox.Text = ""; confirmBox.Text = "";
                    ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                }
                catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("SetPassword failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
            };
            left.Children.Add(passRow); left.Children.Add(confirmRow); left.Children.Add(setBtn);
            left.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Requires Reset Password rights.", FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.6, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap });

            // ----- RIGHT: domain rules (centered in half) + memorable passphrase examples -----
            int minLen = 14; int history = 24; bool complexity = true; string minAge = "1 days"; string maxAge = "60 days";
            try
            {
                using var dom = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + _domainDn);
                try { minLen = Convert.ToInt32(dom.Properties["minPwdLength"]?.Value ?? 14); } catch { }
                try { history = Convert.ToInt32(dom.Properties["pwdHistoryLength"]?.Value ?? 24); } catch { }
                int props = 0;
                try { props = Convert.ToInt32(dom.Properties["pwdProperties"]?.Value ?? 1); } catch { }
                complexity = (props & 1) != 0;
                try
                {
                    var ma = ReadLargeInteger(dom.Properties["minPwdAge"]?.Value);
                    if (ma.HasValue && ma.Value != 0)
                    {
                        long ticks = ma.Value < 0 ? -ma.Value : ma.Value;
                        minAge = TimeSpan.FromTicks(ticks).TotalDays.ToString("F0") + " days";
                    }
                }
                catch { }
                maxAge = _maxPwdAge.HasValue ? _maxPwdAge.Value.TotalDays.ToString("F0") + " days" : "not set";
            }
            catch { }

            var right = new StackPanel
            {
                Spacing = 10,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
            };
            right.Children.Add(Tb("Domain password rules", size: 15, weight: FontWeight.Bold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));
            var rulesBlock = new TextBlock
            {
                Text =
                    "Minimum length: " + minLen + " characters\n" +
                    "Password history: " + history + " remembered\n" +
                    "Complexity required: " + (complexity ? "Yes" : "No") + "\n" +
                    (complexity ? "(upper + lower + digit + symbol)\n" : "") +
                    "Minimum age: " + minAge + "\n" +
                    "Maximum age: " + maxAge,
                FontSize = 13,
                TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap,
                Opacity = 0.95,
                LineHeight = 22,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Left
            };
            right.Children.Add(rulesBlock);

            right.Children.Add(new Border { Height = 1, Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(60, 128, 128, 128)), Margin = new Thickness(20, 4) });

            right.Children.Add(Tb("Quick password copy / paste", size: 14, weight: FontWeight.Bold, margin: new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 0), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));
            right.Children.Add(Tb("Quickly change temporary password", size: 12, opacity: 0.8, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));

            string[] examples = new[] { "Password123456" };
            var exList = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left };
            foreach (string ex in examples)
            {
                var row = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 8, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left };
                var copyEx = new Button
                {
                    Content = "\U0001F4CB",
                    Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand),
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                    FontSize = 13
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(copyEx, "Copy into password fields");
                string cap = ex;
                copyEx.Click += async (_, _) =>
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var top = TopLevel.GetTopLevel(this);
                        if (top?.Clipboard != null)
                        {
                            await top.Clipboard.SetTextAsync(cap);
                            passBox.Text = cap;
                            confirmBox.Text = cap;
                            ShowNotification("Password copied into fields", "success");
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }
                };
                var exTb = Tb(ex, size: 13, weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas"));
                row.Children.Add(copyEx);
                row.Children.Add(exTb);
                exList.Children.Add(row);
            }
            right.Children.Add(exList);
            // (memorable passphrase tip removed - keep UI minimal)

            Grid.SetColumn(left, 0);
            Grid.SetColumn(right, 2);
            split.Children.Add(left);
            split.Children.Add(right);

            var pwdShell = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8 };
            pwdShell.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Password", FontSize = 16, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left });
            pwdShell.Children.Add(split);
            panel.Children.Add(new Border { Classes = { "card" }, Child = pwdShell, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, Padding = new Thickness(10) });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Built-in / system AD groups must never be deleted from ADE
        /// (isCriticalSystemObject, Builtin container, or well-known RID).
        /// </summary>
        private static bool IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(string? dn, byte[]? objectSid, object? isCriticalSystemObject, string? samAccountName = null)
        {
            try
            {
                if (isCriticalSystemObject is bool b && b) return true;
                if (isCriticalSystemObject != null)
                {
                    try { if (Convert.ToBoolean(isCriticalSystemObject)) return true; } catch { }
                    string s = isCriticalSystemObject.ToString() ?? "";
                    if (string.Equals(s, "TRUE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || s == "1") return true;
                }
            }
            catch { }

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn) &&
                dn.IndexOf("CN=Builtin,", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                return true;

            // Well-known group RIDs (domain + Builtin)
            if (objectSid != null && objectSid.Length >= 8)
            {
                int rid = objectSid[objectSid.Length - 4]
                        | (objectSid[objectSid.Length - 3] << 8)
                        | (objectSid[objectSid.Length - 2] << 16)
                        | (objectSid[objectSid.Length - 1] << 24);
                switch (rid)
                {
                    // Domain default groups
                    case 512: // Domain Admins
                    case 513: // Domain Users
                    case 514: // Domain Guests
                    case 515: // Domain Computers
                    case 516: // Domain Controllers
                    case 517: // Cert Publishers
                    case 518: // Schema Admins
                    case 519: // Enterprise Admins
                    case 520: // Group Policy Creator Owners
                    case 521: // Read-only Domain Controllers
                    case 522: // Cloneable Domain Controllers
                    case 525: // Protected Users
                    case 526: // Key Admins
                    case 527: // Enterprise Key Admins
                    case 498: // Enterprise Read-only Domain Controllers
                    case 553: // RAS and IAS Servers
                    // Builtin container groups
                    case 544: // Administrators
                    case 545: // Users
                    case 546: // Guests
                    case 547: // Power Users
                    case 548: // Account Operators
                    case 549: // Server Operators
                    case 550: // Print Operators
                    case 551: // Backup Operators
                    case 552: // Replicators
                    case 554: // Pre-Windows 2000 Compatible Access
                    case 555: // Remote Desktop Users
                    case 556: // Network Configuration Operators
                    case 557: // Incoming Forest Trust Builders
                    case 558: // Performance Monitor Users
                    case 559: // Performance Log Users
                    case 560: // Windows Authorization Access Group
                    case 561: // Terminal Server License Servers
                    case 562: // Distributed COM Users
                    case 568: // IIS_IUSRS
                    case 569: // Cryptographic Operators
                    case 573: // Event Log Readers
                    case 574: // Certificate Service DCOM Access
                    case 575: // RDS Remote Access Servers
                    case 576: // RDS Endpoint Servers
                    case 577: // RDS Management Servers
                    case 578: // Hyper-V Administrators
                    case 579: // Access Control Assistance Operators
                    case 580: // Remote Management Users
                    case 571: // Allowed RODC Password Replication Group
                    case 572: // Denied RODC Password Replication Group
                        return true;
                }
            }

            // Fallback name list for common built-ins if SID/critical missing
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(samAccountName))
            {
                string sam = samAccountName.Trim();
                string[] names = new[]
                {
                    "Domain Admins","Domain Users","Domain Guests","Domain Computers","Domain Controllers",
                    "Enterprise Admins","Schema Admins","Administrators","Users","Guests","Account Operators",
                    "Server Operators","Print Operators","Backup Operators","Replicator",
                    "Allowed RODC Password Replication Group","Denied RODC Password Replication Group",
                    "Cloneable Domain Controllers","Protected Users","Cert Publishers",
                    "Group Policy Creator Owners","Read-only Domain Controllers",
                    "Enterprise Read-only Domain Controllers","Certificate Service DCOM Access",
                    "Access Control Assistance Operators","Remote Desktop Users","Remote Management Users"
                };
                foreach (var n in names)
                    if (string.Equals(n, sam, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        private static bool IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(DirectoryEntry de)
        {
            return IsProtectedAdObject(de);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// True for any AD object that must never be deleted from ADE:
        /// critical system flag, Builtin container, well-known RIDs (users/groups/computers),
        /// systemFlags DISALLOW_DELETE, domain controllers, krbtgt, etc.
        /// </summary>
        /// <summary>
        /// True when any user has this group as its primary group (primary members
        /// never appear in the group's member attribute, so an "empty" group can
        /// still be in use). Fails closed (true) when the check cannot be made.
        /// </summary>
        private static bool GroupHasPrimaryMembers(DirectoryEntry groupEntry)
        {
            try
            {
                byte[]? sid = null;
                try { sid = groupEntry.Properties["objectSid"]?.Value as byte[]; } catch { }
                if (sid == null || sid.Length < 8) return true;
                int rid = sid[sid.Length - 4]
                        | (sid[sid.Length - 3] << 8)
                        | (sid[sid.Length - 2] << 16)
                        | (sid[sid.Length - 1] << 24);
                string? dn = null;
                try { dn = groupEntry.Properties["distinguishedName"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn)) return true;
                string nc = string.Join(",", dn.Split(',')
                    .Where(part => part.TrimStart().StartsWith("DC=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)));
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(nc)) return true;
                using var searcher = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nc))
                {
                    Filter = "(primaryGroupID=" + rid + ")",
                    SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                    SizeLimit = 1
                };
                return searcher.FindOne() != null;
            }
            catch { return true; }
        }

        private static bool IsProtectedAdObject(DirectoryEntry de)
        {
            try
            {
                try { de.RefreshCache(new[] {
                    "distinguishedName", "objectSid", "isCriticalSystemObject", "sAMAccountName",
                    "objectClass", "systemFlags", "userAccountControl", "objectCategory", "cn", "name", "description"
                }); } catch { }

                string? dn = null;
                try { dn = de.Properties["distinguishedName"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        string path = de.Path ?? "";
                        if (path.StartsWith("LDAP://", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            dn = path.Substring(7);
                    }
                    catch { }
                }

                object? crit = null;
                try { if (de.Properties.Contains("isCriticalSystemObject")) crit = de.Properties["isCriticalSystemObject"]?.Value; } catch { }
                byte[]? sid = null;
                try { sid = de.Properties["objectSid"]?.Value as byte[]; } catch { }
                string? sam = null;
                try { sam = de.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                string? objDesc = null;
                try { objDesc = de.Properties["description"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                // The organization's own protection marker: a description saying
                // DO NOT DELETE protects the object on every delete path.
                if (DescriptionSaysDoNotDelete(objDesc))
                    return true;

                if (IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(dn, sid, crit, sam))
                    return true;

                try
                {
                    if (de.Properties.Contains("systemFlags") && de.Properties["systemFlags"].Count > 0)
                    {
                        int sf = Convert.ToInt32(de.Properties["systemFlags"][0]);
                        if ((sf & unchecked((int)0x80000000)) != 0)
                            return true;
                    }
                }
                catch { }

                if (sid != null && sid.Length >= 8)
                {
                    int rid = sid[sid.Length - 4]
                            | (sid[sid.Length - 3] << 8)
                            | (sid[sid.Length - 2] << 16)
                            | (sid[sid.Length - 1] << 24);
                    switch (rid)
                    {
                        case 500: // Administrator
                        case 501: // Guest
                        case 502: // krbtgt
                        case 503: // DefaultAccount
                            return true;
                    }
                }

                try
                {
                    if (de.Properties.Contains("userAccountControl") && de.Properties["userAccountControl"].Count > 0)
                    {
                        int uac = Convert.ToInt32(de.Properties["userAccountControl"][0]);
                        if ((uac & 0x2000) != 0) // SERVER_TRUST_ACCOUNT = Domain Controller
                            return true;
                    }
                }
                catch { }

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn))
                {
                    if (dn.IndexOf("CN=Builtin,", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0) return true;
                    if (dn.IndexOf("CN=Microsoft Exchange System Objects,", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0) return true;
                    if (dn.StartsWith("CN=System,", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return true;
                    if (dn.StartsWith("CN=Infrastructure,", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return true;
                    if (dn.StartsWith("CN=LostAndFound,", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return true;
                    if (dn.StartsWith("CN=Deleted Objects,", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return true;
                    if (dn.StartsWith("CN=RID Manager$", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return true;
                }

                string name = sam ?? "";
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name))
                {
                    try { name = de.Properties["cn"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? ""; } catch { }
                }
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
                {
                    string n = name.Trim().TrimEnd('$');
                    foreach (string r in new[] { "Administrator", "Guest", "krbtgt", "DefaultAccount", "WDAGUtilityAccount" })
                    {
                        if (string.Equals(n, r, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            return true;
                    }
                }

                return false;
            }
            catch
            {
                return true; // fail closed
            }
        }


        /// <summary>
        /// Live delete guard for computers (user request 2026-10-27): a computer
        /// may be deleted only when it is verifiably offline - no address in DNS
        /// and no ping reply, the same rule the stale list uses for eligibility.
        /// Returns "" when the delete may proceed; otherwise the reason it is
        /// blocked. Any doubt (host unknown, check throws) blocks - fail closed.
        /// </summary>
        private string ComputerDeleteBlockReason(string dn)
        {
            try
            {
                string host = "";
                using (var de = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + dn))
                {
                    try { de.RefreshCache(new[] { "dNSHostName", "sAMAccountName" }); } catch { }
                    try { host = de.Properties["dNSHostName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? ""; } catch { }
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host))
                    {
                        try { host = de.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? ""; } catch { }
                    }
                }
                host = ResolveRdpHost(host);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host))
                    return "Could not determine the computer's host name, so it cannot be verified offline - delete blocked for safety.";
                System.Net.IPAddress[] addrs = Array.Empty<System.Net.IPAddress>();
                try { addrs = System.Net.Dns.GetHostAddresses(host); } catch { }
                if (addrs.Length > 0)
                    return "This computer still has a DNS address (" + addrs[0] + " for " + host + ") - it cannot be deleted. If it is really retired, remove its DNS record first.";
                var ping = TryPingHost(host, 700);
                if (ping.Ok)
                    return "This computer answered a ping (" + host + ", " + ping.Ms + " ms) - it is online and cannot be deleted.";
                return "";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return "Could not verify the computer is offline - delete blocked for safety. " + ex.Message;
            }
        }

        private void ConfirmAndDeleteObject(string dn, string name, string typeLabel, AdNode node, Action? onDeleted = null)
        {
            var dlg = new Window
            {
                Title = "Confirm Delete",
                Width = 460,
                SizeToContent = SizeToContent.Height,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                CanResize = false
            };
            StyleDialogWindow(dlg);

            var root = new StackPanel
            {
                Margin = new Thickness(20),
                Spacing = 12
            };
            root.Children.Add(Tb("Delete " + typeLabel, size: 18, weight: FontWeight.Bold));
            root.Children.Add(Tb("This permanently deletes the " + typeLabel.ToLower() + " from Active Directory and cannot be undone.", opacity: 0.85, wrap: true));
            root.Children.Add(Tb("Type the name exactly to confirm:", weight: FontWeight.SemiBold));
            root.Children.Add(Tb(name, size: 14, weight: FontWeight.Bold));

            var input = new TextBox
            {
                Watermark = "Type name here...",
                MinHeight = 36,
                FontSize = 14
            };
            root.Children.Add(input);

            var err = Tb("", size: 12, foreground: new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(229, 115, 115)), visible: false);
            root.Children.Add(err);

            var btnRow = new StackPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                Spacing = 10,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right
            };
            var cancelBtn = new Button { Content = "Cancel", MinWidth = 100, Classes = { "action" } };
            var deleteBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Delete",
                MinWidth = 100,
                Classes = { "action" },
                IsEnabled = false,
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(176, 96, 96)),
                Foreground = Brushes.White
            };

            void SyncEnable()
            {
                string t = (input.Text ?? "").Trim();
                deleteBtn.IsEnabled = string.Equals(t, name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                err.IsVisible = false;
            }
            input.TextChanged += (_, _) => SyncEnable();

            cancelBtn.Click += (_, _) => dlg.Close();
            deleteBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                string t = (input.Text ?? "").Trim();
                if (!string.Equals(t, name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    err.Text = "Name does not match.";
                    err.IsVisible = true;
                    return;
                }
                try
                {
                    // Block deletion of built-in / critical system groups
                    try
                    {
                        using var check = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + dn);
                        if (IsProtectedAdObject(check))
                        {
                            err.Text = "This is a built-in or protected Active Directory object and cannot be deleted from ADE.";
                            err.TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap;
                            err.IsVisible = true;
                            return;
                        }
                        // Users: Exchange / service accounts are blocked on every
                        // delete path (user request 2026-10-27) - the same rule
                        // the stale list's quick delete already enforced.
                        if (string.Equals(typeLabel, "User", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        {
                            try { check.RefreshCache(new[] { "name", "sAMAccountName", "mail" }); } catch { }
                            string? uName = null, uSam = null, uMail = null;
                            try { uName = check.Properties["name"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                            try { uSam = check.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                            try { uMail = check.Properties["mail"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                            if (IsExchangeOrServiceAccount(uName, uSam, dn, uMail, out string exchPattern))
                            {
                                err.Text = "This looks like an Exchange or service account (matched \"" + exchPattern + "\") and cannot be deleted from ADE.";
                                err.TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap;
                                err.IsVisible = true;
                                return;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    catch (Exception pex)
                    {
                        err.Text = "Could not verify object protection  -  delete blocked for safety. " + pex.Message;
                        err.TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap;
                        err.IsVisible = true;
                        return;
                    }
                    // Computers get one more fail-closed gate (user request
                    // 2026-10-27): a machine that still resolves in DNS or
                    // answers a ping can never be deleted.
                    if (string.Equals(typeLabel, "Computer", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        deleteBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                        string block = await Task.Run(() => ComputerDeleteBlockReason(dn));
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(block))
                        {
                            err.Text = block;
                            err.TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap;
                            err.IsVisible = true;
                            SyncEnable();
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                    // DeleteTree removes the object and any child objects (e.g. BitLocker
                    // recovery keys under computer accounts). Children.Remove fails with
                    // "only on a leaf object" when children exist.
                    using var entry = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + dn);
                    entry.DeleteTree();
                    dlg.Close();
                    ShowNotification(typeLabel + " deleted: " + name, "success");
                    _statsLoaded = false; // refresh dashboard counts next time
                    InvalidateServerComputerCache();
                    InvalidateDomainSnapshot();
                    if (onDeleted != null)
                    {
                        onDeleted();
                    }
                    else if (_rootData != null)
                        ShowContainerContents(_rootData);
                    else
                    {
                        var panel = this.FindControl<StackPanel>("DetailPanel");
                        if (panel != null)
                        {
                            panel.Children.Clear();
                            panel.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = typeLabel + " deleted.", Opacity = 0.8 });
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    string msg = ex.Message ?? "Unknown error";
                    if (ex.InnerException != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ex.InnerException.Message))
                        msg += " (" + ex.InnerException.Message + ")";
                    // Common AD case when rights are missing or object is protected
                    if (msg.IndexOf("leaf object", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                        msg = "Object has child entries and could not be fully removed. " + msg;
                    err.Text = "Delete failed: " + msg;
                    err.TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap;
                    err.IsVisible = true;
                }
            };

            btnRow.Children.Add(cancelBtn);
            btnRow.Children.Add(deleteBtn);
            root.Children.Add(btnRow);
            dlg.Content = root;

            try { dlg.ShowDialog(this); }
            catch { dlg.Show(); }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Login / lock / unlock history from configurable folder\{sam}.txt
        /// CSV: DateTime,Action,User,ClientName,ClientIP,ComputerName,ComputerIP,Source
        /// Initial view: last 14 days only. Older events load in pages of 50.
        /// </summary>
        private void RenderLoginHistoryPanel(StackPanel panel, string samAccountName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(samAccountName)) return;

            var shell = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            var headerRow = new Grid
            {
                ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            headerRow.Children.Add(Tb("Workstation activity", size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));
            var refreshCol = new StackPanel
            {
                Spacing = 2,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
            };
            var refreshBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Refresh",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 90,
                Padding = new Thickness(12, 4),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(refreshBtn, "Reload login history from the log file");
            var lastRefreshText = Tb("Last refresh:  - ", size: 11, opacity: 0.7, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Right);
            refreshCol.Children.Add(refreshBtn);
            refreshCol.Children.Add(lastRefreshText);
            Grid.SetColumn(refreshCol, 1);
            headerRow.Children.Add(refreshCol);
            shell.Children.Add(headerRow);
            shell.Children.Add(Tb("Workstation lock / unlock events for " + samAccountName, size: 12, opacity: 0.75, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));

            var statusLine = Tb("Loading last 2 weeks...", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, margin: new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 2));
            shell.Children.Add(statusLine);

            var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            var scroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 12, 0),
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                Content = list,
                MaxHeight = 420,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
            };
            shell.Children.Add(scroll);

            var loadMoreBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Load older events",
                Classes = { "action" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0),
                IsVisible = false
            };
            shell.Children.Add(loadMoreBtn);

            panel.Children.Add(new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Child = shell,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Padding = new Thickness(10),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0)
            });

            string sam = samAccountName.Trim();
            // Strip domain prefix if present (OLN\user -> user)
            if (sam.Contains('\\')) sam = sam.Split('\\').Last();
            if (sam.Contains('@')) sam = sam.Split('@')[0];
            string folder = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_loginLogFolder) ? DefaultLoginLogFolder : _loginLogFolder;
            // New format: {username}_ActivityHistory.csv
            string logPath = Path.Combine(folder, sam + "_ActivityHistory.csv");

            // WT* = thin client / zero client - not a Windows host for RDP shadow
            static bool IsWtMachine(string name) =>
                !string.IsNullOrEmpty(name) && name.StartsWith("WT", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            static bool IsNa(string? s) =>
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s) || s.Equals("N/A", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            Button MakeCopyIcon(string value)
            {
                var btn = new Button
                {
                    Content = "\U0001F4CB",
                    Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                    Margin = new Thickness(4, 0, 0, 0),
                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                    FontSize = 12,
                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand),
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(btn, "Copy " + value);
                string cap = value;
                btn.Click += async (_, _) =>
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var top = TopLevel.GetTopLevel(this);
                        if (top?.Clipboard != null)
                        {
                            await top.Clipboard.SetTextAsync(cap);
                            ShowNotification("Copied: " + cap, "success");
                        }
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Copy failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
                };
                return btn;
            }

            Button MakeRdpIcon(string host)
            {
                // RDP
                var btn = new Button
                {
                    Content = MakeMdi("mdi-remote-desktop", 16),
                    Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                    Margin = new Thickness(4, 0, 0, 0),
                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                    FontSize = 14,
                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand),
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(btn, "Shadow RDP into " + host + " (admin, no consent prompt)");
                string target = host;
                string userSam = sam; // outer scope: login history user
                btn.Click += (_, _) => LaunchShadowRdp(target, userSam);
                return btn;
            }

            Button MakeVncIcon(string host)
            {
                // VNC
                var btn = new Button
                {
                    Content = MakeMdi("mdi-monitor", 16),
                    Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                    Margin = new Thickness(4, 0, 0, 0),
                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                    FontSize = 14,
                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand),
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(btn, "VNC into " + host);
                string target = host;
                btn.Click += (_, _) => LaunchVnc(target);
                return btn;
            }

            StackPanel MakeNameBlock(string label, string name)
            {
                var sp = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 2 };
                sp.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = label, FontSize = 11, Opacity = 0.6, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 4, 0) });
                sp.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = name, FontSize = 13, FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, FontFamily = new FontFamily("Consolas"), VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center });
                sp.Children.Add(MakeCopyIcon(name));
                if (IsWtMachine(name))
                    sp.Children.Add(MakeVncIcon(name)); // WT thin clients -> VNC
                else
                    sp.Children.Add(MakeRdpIcon(name)); // IC / Windows hosts -> shadow RDP
                return sp;
            }

            Control BuildEntryRow((DateTime When, string Action, string Client, string ClientIp, string ClientMac, string Computer, string ComputerIp, string ClientType) e, int rowIdx)
            {
                bool isUnlock = e.Action.Equals("UNLOCK", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                bool isLock = e.Action.Equals("LOCK", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                var row = new Border
                {
                    Padding = new Thickness(10, 8),
                    CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(4),
                    Background = (rowIdx % 2 == 0)
                        ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(28, 128, 128, 128))
                        : Brushes.Transparent,
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 2)
                };

                var body = new StackPanel { Spacing = 3, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };

                // Line 1: time | badge | client type
                var topRow = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 10 };
                topRow.Children.Add(new TextBlock
                {
                    Text = e.When.ToString("MM/dd/yyyy h:mm tt"),
                    FontSize = 13,
                    FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                    MinWidth = 150
                });
                string badgeText = string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Action) ? "?" : e.Action.ToUpperInvariant();
                string badgeClass = isUnlock ? "statusOk" : (isLock ? "statusWarn" : "statusBad");
                if (e.Action.Equals("LOGON", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) badgeClass = "statusOk";
                else if (e.Action.Equals("LOGOFF", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) badgeClass = "statusWarn";
                var badge = MakeBadge(badgeText, badgeClass);
                badge.Padding = new Thickness(8, 3);
                topRow.Children.Add(badge);
                if (!IsNa(e.ClientType))
                {
                    topRow.Children.Add(Tb(e.ClientType, size: 12, opacity: 0.75, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));
                }
                body.Children.Add(topRow);

                // Line 2: Client + Computer with copy icons (+ RDP/VNC)
                var nameRow = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 16 };
                if (!IsNa(e.Client))
                    nameRow.Children.Add(MakeNameBlock("Client", e.Client));
                if (!IsNa(e.Computer))
                    nameRow.Children.Add(MakeNameBlock("Computer", e.Computer));
                if (nameRow.Children.Count > 0)
                    body.Children.Add(nameRow);

                // Line 3: IPs + MAC
                var ipBits = new List<string>();
                if (!IsNa(e.ClientIp)) ipBits.Add("Client IP " + e.ClientIp);
                if (!IsNa(e.ClientMac)) ipBits.Add("MAC " + e.ClientMac);
                if (!IsNa(e.ComputerIp)) ipBits.Add("Host IP " + e.ComputerIp);
                if (ipBits.Count > 0)
                {
                    body.Children.Add(Tb(string.Join("  |  ", ipBits), size: 11, opacity: 0.65, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas")));
                }

                row.Child = body;
                return row;
            }

            const int pageSize = 50;

            // Mutable paging state so Refresh can reload without stacking Click handlers
            var older = new List<(DateTime When, string Action, string Client, string ClientIp, string ClientMac, string Computer, string ComputerIp, string ClientType)>();
            int olderIdx = 0;
            int shown = 0;
            int total = 0;
            int loadSeq = 0;

            void UpdateStatus()
            {
                int remaining = older.Count - olderIdx;
                statusLine.Text = shown + " of " + total.ToString("N0") + " events shown (last 14 days first)"
                    + (remaining > 0 ? "  |  " + remaining.ToString("N0") + " older available" : "");
                if (remaining > 0)
                {
                    int next = Math.Min(pageSize, remaining);
                    loadMoreBtn.Content = "Load older (" + next + " of " + remaining + ")";
                    loadMoreBtn.IsVisible = true;
                    loadMoreBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                }
                else
                {
                    loadMoreBtn.IsVisible = false;
                }
            }

            loadMoreBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                int take = Math.Min(pageSize, older.Count - olderIdx);
                for (int i = 0; i < take; i++)
                {
                    list.Children.Add(BuildEntryRow(older[olderIdx], shown));
                    olderIdx++;
                    shown++;
                }
                UpdateStatus();
            };

            void StartLoad()
            {
                int seq = ++loadSeq;
                // Keep existing rows visible while loading so the page does not jump
                loadMoreBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                refreshBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                statusLine.Text = list.Children.Count > 0 ? "Refreshing..." : "Loading last 2 weeks...";
                DateTime cutoff = DateTime.Now.Date.AddDays(-14);
                string pathCap = logPath;

                // Capture scroll positions so we can restore after content swap
                double innerOff = 0;
                try { innerOff = scroll.Offset.Y; } catch { }
                ScrollViewer? outerSv = null;
                double outerOff = 0;
                try
                {
                    Avalonia.Controls.Control? walk = shell;
                    while (walk != null)
                    {
                        if (walk is ScrollViewer sv && !ReferenceEquals(sv, scroll))
                        {
                            outerSv = sv;
                            outerOff = sv.Offset.Y;
                            break;
                        }
                        walk = walk.Parent as Avalonia.Controls.Control;
                    }
                }
                catch { }

                Task.Run(() =>
                {
                    var recent = new List<(DateTime When, string Action, string Client, string ClientIp, string ClientMac, string Computer, string ComputerIp, string ClientType)>();
                    var olderLocal = new List<(DateTime When, string Action, string Client, string ClientIp, string ClientMac, string Computer, string ComputerIp, string ClientType)>();
                    string error = "";
                    int totalLocal = 0;
                    try
                    {
                        if (!File.Exists(pathCap))
                        {
                            error = "No login log found at: " + pathCap;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            // CSV: Timestamp,Activity,UserID,ClientName,ClientIP,ClientMAC,LocalName,LocalIP,ClientType
                            using var fs = new FileStream(pathCap, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite);
                            using var sr = new StreamReader(fs);
                            string? raw;
                            bool headerSkipped = false;
                            while ((raw = sr.ReadLine()) != null)
                            {
                                string line = raw.Trim();
                                if (line.Length == 0) continue;
                                var parts = ParseActivityCsvLine(line);
                                if (parts.Count < 2) continue;
                                // Skip header row
                                if (!headerSkipped)
                                {
                                    headerSkipped = true;
                                    if (parts[0].Equals("Timestamp", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                                        || parts[0].Contains("Timestamp", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                        continue;
                                }
                                if (!DateTime.TryParse(parts[0], out var when)) continue;
                                totalLocal++;
                                string action = parts.Count > 1 ? parts[1] : "";
                                string client = parts.Count > 3 ? parts[3] : "";
                                string clientIp = parts.Count > 4 ? parts[4] : "";
                                string clientMac = parts.Count > 5 ? parts[5] : "";
                                string computer = parts.Count > 6 ? parts[6] : "";
                                string computerIp = parts.Count > 7 ? parts[7] : "";
                                string clientType = parts.Count > 8 ? parts[8] : "";
                                var entry = (when, action, client, clientIp, clientMac, computer, computerIp, clientType);
                                if (when >= cutoff)
                                    recent.Add(entry);
                                else
                                    olderLocal.Add(entry);
                            }
                            recent = recent.OrderByDescending(e => e.When).ToList();
                            olderLocal = olderLocal.OrderByDescending(e => e.When).ToList();
                        }
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        error = "Log load failed: " + ex.Message;
                    }

                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        // Ignore stale results if user hit Refresh again
                        if (seq != loadSeq) return;
                        refreshBtn.IsEnabled = true;

                        void RestoreScroll()
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                if (outerSv != null)
                                    outerSv.Offset = new Vector(outerSv.Offset.X, outerOff);
                            }
                            catch { }
                            try { scroll.Offset = new Vector(scroll.Offset.X, innerOff); } catch { }
                        }

                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(error))
                        {
                            list.Children.Clear();
                            older.Clear();
                            olderIdx = 0;
                            shown = 0;
                            total = 0;
                            statusLine.Text = error;
                            loadMoreBtn.IsVisible = false;
                            lastRefreshText.Text = "Last refresh: failed";
                            Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(RestoreScroll, DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
                            return;
                        }
                        if (totalLocal == 0)
                        {
                            list.Children.Clear();
                            older.Clear();
                            olderIdx = 0;
                            shown = 0;
                            total = 0;
                            statusLine.Text = "Log file is empty.";
                            loadMoreBtn.IsVisible = false;
                            DateTime emptyAt = DateTime.Now;
                            lastRefreshText.Text = "Last refresh: " + emptyAt.ToString("h:mm:ss tt");
                            ToolTip.SetTip(lastRefreshText, emptyAt.ToString("dddd, MMM d, yyyy h:mm:ss tt"));
                            Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(RestoreScroll, DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
                            return;
                        }

                        // Swap content only after data is ready (avoids page jump)
                        list.Children.Clear();
                        total = totalLocal;
                        older.Clear();
                        older.AddRange(olderLocal);
                        olderIdx = 0;
                        shown = 0;
                        foreach (var e in recent)
                        {
                            list.Children.Add(BuildEntryRow(e, shown));
                            shown++;
                        }
                        UpdateStatus();
                        DateTime refreshedAt = DateTime.Now;
                        lastRefreshText.Text = "Last refresh: " + refreshedAt.ToString("h:mm:ss tt");
                        ToolTip.SetTip(lastRefreshText, refreshedAt.ToString("dddd, MMM d, yyyy h:mm:ss tt"));
                        Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(RestoreScroll, DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
                    });
                });
            }

            refreshBtn.Click += (_, _) => StartLoad();
            StartLoad();
        }


        /// <summary>
        /// Parse a single CSV line with optional double-quoted fields.
        /// </summary>
        private static List<string> ParseActivityCsvLine(string line)
        {
            var fields = new List<string>();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(line)) return fields;
            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            bool inQuotes = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < line.Length; i++)
            {
                char c = line[i];
                if (c == '"')
                {
                    if (inQuotes && i + 1 < line.Length && line[i + 1] == '"')
                    {
                        sb.Append('"');
                        i++;
                    }
                    else
                        inQuotes = !inQuotes;
                }
                else if (c == ',' && !inQuotes)
                {
                    fields.Add(sb.ToString().Trim());
                    sb.Clear();
                }
                else
                    sb.Append(c);
            }
            fields.Add(sb.ToString().Trim());
            return fields;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Computer-side logon history from \\...\UserComputerLogin\computer\{computer}_ActivityHistory.csv
        /// CSV columns: Timestamp,Activity,UserID,ClientName,ClientIP,ClientMAC,LocalName,LocalIP,ClientType
        /// </summary>
        private void RenderComputerLoginHistoryPanel(StackPanel panel, string computerName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(computerName)) return;
            computerName = computerName.Trim().TrimEnd('$');

            var shell = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            var headerRow = new Grid
            {
                ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            headerRow.Children.Add(Tb("Logons to this computer", size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));
            var refreshCol = new StackPanel
            {
                Spacing = 2,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
            };
            var refreshBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Refresh",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 90,
                Padding = new Thickness(12, 4),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(refreshBtn, "Reload logon history from the log file");
            var lastRefreshText = Tb("Last refresh:  - ", size: 11, opacity: 0.7, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Right);
            refreshCol.Children.Add(refreshBtn);
            refreshCol.Children.Add(lastRefreshText);
            Grid.SetColumn(refreshCol, 1);
            headerRow.Children.Add(refreshCol);
            shell.Children.Add(headerRow);
            shell.Children.Add(Tb("Lock / unlock / logon events on " + computerName, size: 12, opacity: 0.75, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));

            var statusLine = Tb("Loading last 2 weeks...", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, margin: new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 2));
            shell.Children.Add(statusLine);

            var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            var scroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 12, 0),
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                Content = list,
                MaxHeight = 420,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
            };
            shell.Children.Add(scroll);

            var loadMoreBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Load older events",
                Classes = { "action" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0),
                IsVisible = false
            };
            shell.Children.Add(loadMoreBtn);

            panel.Children.Add(new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Child = shell,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Padding = new Thickness(10),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0)
            });

            string folder = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_computerLogFolder) ? DefaultComputerLogFolder : _computerLogFolder;
            // New format: {computername}_ActivityHistory.csv
            string logPath = Path.Combine(folder, computerName + "_ActivityHistory.csv");
            string compCap = computerName;

            static bool IsWtMachine(string name) =>
                !string.IsNullOrEmpty(name) && name.StartsWith("WT", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            static bool IsNa(string? s) =>
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s) || s.Equals("N/A", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            Button MakeCopyIcon(string value)
            {
                var btn = new Button
                {
                    Content = "\U0001F4CB",
                    Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                    Margin = new Thickness(4, 0, 0, 0),
                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                    FontSize = 12,
                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand),
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(btn, "Copy " + value);
                string cap = value;
                btn.Click += async (_, _) =>
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var top = TopLevel.GetTopLevel(this);
                        if (top?.Clipboard != null)
                        {
                            await top.Clipboard.SetTextAsync(cap);
                            ShowNotification("Copied: " + cap, "success");
                        }
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Copy failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
                };
                return btn;
            }

            Button MakeRdpIcon(string host)
            {
                var btn = new Button
                {
                    Content = MakeMdi("mdi-remote-desktop", 16),
                    Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                    Margin = new Thickness(4, 0, 0, 0),
                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                    FontSize = 14,
                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand),
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(btn, "Shadow RDP into " + host);
                string target = host;
                btn.Click += (_, _) => LaunchShadowRdp(target, null);
                return btn;
            }

            Button MakeVncIcon(string host)
            {
                var btn = new Button
                {
                    Content = MakeMdi("mdi-monitor", 16),
                    Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                    Margin = new Thickness(4, 0, 0, 0),
                    Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                    BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                    FontSize = 14,
                    Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand),
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(btn, "VNC to " + host);
                string target = host;
                btn.Click += (_, _) => LaunchVnc(target);
                return btn;
            }

            Control BuildEntryRow((DateTime When, string Action, string User, string Client, string ClientIp, string ClientMac, string Computer, string ComputerIp, string ClientType) e, int rowIdx)
            {
                bool isUnlock = e.Action.Equals("UNLOCK", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                bool isLock = e.Action.Equals("LOCK", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                var row = new Border
                {
                    Padding = new Thickness(10, 8),
                    CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(4),
                    Background = (rowIdx % 2 == 0)
                        ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(28, 128, 128, 128))
                        : Brushes.Transparent,
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 2)
                };

                var body = new StackPanel { Spacing = 3, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };

                var topRow = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 10 };
                topRow.Children.Add(new TextBlock
                {
                    Text = e.When.ToString("MM/dd/yyyy h:mm tt"),
                    FontSize = 13,
                    FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                    VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                    MinWidth = 150
                });
                string badgeText = string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Action) ? "?" : e.Action.ToUpperInvariant();
                string badgeClass = isUnlock ? "statusOk" : (isLock ? "statusWarn" : "statusBad");
                if (e.Action.Equals("LOGON", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) badgeClass = "statusOk";
                else if (e.Action.Equals("LOGOFF", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) badgeClass = "statusWarn";
                var badge = MakeBadge(badgeText, badgeClass);
                badge.Padding = new Thickness(8, 3);
                topRow.Children.Add(badge);
                if (!IsNa(e.ClientType))
                {
                    topRow.Children.Add(Tb(e.ClientType, size: 12, opacity: 0.75, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));
                }
                body.Children.Add(topRow);

                // User who performed the action on this computer
                var nameRow = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 16 };
                if (!IsNa(e.User))
                {
                    string userDisplay = e.User;
                    if (userDisplay.Contains('\\')) userDisplay = userDisplay.Split('\\').Last();
                    var userSp = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 2 };
                    userSp.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "User", FontSize = 11, Opacity = 0.6, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 4, 0) });
                    userSp.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = userDisplay, FontSize = 13, FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, FontFamily = new FontFamily("Consolas"), VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center });
                    userSp.Children.Add(MakeCopyIcon(userDisplay));
                    nameRow.Children.Add(userSp);
                }
                if (!IsNa(e.Client))
                {
                    var clientSp = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 2 };
                    clientSp.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Client", FontSize = 11, Opacity = 0.6, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 4, 0) });
                    clientSp.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = e.Client, FontSize = 13, FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, FontFamily = new FontFamily("Consolas"), VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center });
                    clientSp.Children.Add(MakeCopyIcon(e.Client));
                    if (IsWtMachine(e.Client))
                        clientSp.Children.Add(MakeVncIcon(e.Client));
                    else
                        clientSp.Children.Add(MakeRdpIcon(e.Client));
                    nameRow.Children.Add(clientSp);
                }
                if (nameRow.Children.Count > 0)
                    body.Children.Add(nameRow);

                var ipBits = new List<string>();
                if (!IsNa(e.ClientIp)) ipBits.Add("Client IP " + e.ClientIp);
                if (!IsNa(e.ClientMac)) ipBits.Add("MAC " + e.ClientMac);
                if (!IsNa(e.ComputerIp)) ipBits.Add("Host IP " + e.ComputerIp);
                if (ipBits.Count > 0)
                {
                    body.Children.Add(Tb(string.Join("  |  ", ipBits), size: 11, opacity: 0.65, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas")));
                }

                row.Child = body;
                return row;
            }

            const int pageSize = 50;

            // Mutable paging state so Refresh can reload without stacking Click handlers
            var older = new List<(DateTime When, string Action, string User, string Client, string ClientIp, string ClientMac, string Computer, string ComputerIp, string ClientType)>();
            int olderIdx = 0;
            int shown = 0;
            int total = 0;
            int loadSeq = 0;
