            void UpdateStatus()
            {
                int remaining = older.Count - olderIdx;
                statusLine.Text = shown + " of " + total.ToString("N0") + " events shown (last 14 days first)"
                    + (remaining > 0 ? "  |  " + remaining.ToString("N0") + " older available" : "");
                if (remaining > 0)
                {
                    int next = Math.Min(pageSize, remaining);
                    loadMoreBtn.Content = "Load older (" + next + " of " + remaining + ")";
                    loadMoreBtn.IsVisible = true;
                    loadMoreBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                }
                else
                {
                    loadMoreBtn.IsVisible = false;
                }
            }

            loadMoreBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                int take = Math.Min(pageSize, older.Count - olderIdx);
                for (int i = 0; i < take; i++)
                {
                    list.Children.Add(BuildEntryRow(older[olderIdx], shown));
                    olderIdx++;
                    shown++;
                }
                UpdateStatus();
            };

            void StartLoad()
            {
                int seq = ++loadSeq;
                // Keep existing rows visible while loading so the page does not jump
                loadMoreBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                refreshBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                statusLine.Text = list.Children.Count > 0 ? "Refreshing..." : "Loading last 2 weeks...";
                DateTime cutoff = DateTime.Now.Date.AddDays(-14);
                string pathCap = logPath;

                // Capture scroll positions so we can restore after content swap
                double innerOff = 0;
                try { innerOff = scroll.Offset.Y; } catch { }
                ScrollViewer? outerSv = null;
                double outerOff = 0;
                try
                {
                    Avalonia.Controls.Control? walk = shell;
                    while (walk != null)
                    {
                        if (walk is ScrollViewer sv && !ReferenceEquals(sv, scroll))
                        {
                            outerSv = sv;
                            outerOff = sv.Offset.Y;
                            break;
                        }
                        walk = walk.Parent as Avalonia.Controls.Control;
                    }
                }
                catch { }

                Task.Run(() =>
                {
                    var recent = new List<(DateTime When, string Action, string User, string Client, string ClientIp, string ClientMac, string Computer, string ComputerIp, string ClientType)>();
                    var olderLocal = new List<(DateTime When, string Action, string User, string Client, string ClientIp, string ClientMac, string Computer, string ComputerIp, string ClientType)>();
                    string error = "";
                    int totalLocal = 0;
                    try
                    {
                        if (!File.Exists(pathCap))
                        {
                            error = "No computer log found at: " + pathCap;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            // CSV: Timestamp,Activity,UserID,ClientName,ClientIP,ClientMAC,LocalName,LocalIP,ClientType
                            using var fs = new FileStream(pathCap, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite);
                            using var sr = new StreamReader(fs);
                            string? raw;
                            bool headerSkipped = false;
                            while ((raw = sr.ReadLine()) != null)
                            {
                                string line = raw.Trim();
                                if (line.Length == 0) continue;
                                var parts = ParseActivityCsvLine(line);
                                if (parts.Count < 2) continue;
                                if (!headerSkipped)
                                {
                                    headerSkipped = true;
                                    if (parts[0].Equals("Timestamp", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                                        || parts[0].Contains("Timestamp", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                        continue;
                                }
                                if (!DateTime.TryParse(parts[0], out var when)) continue;
                                totalLocal++;
                                string action = parts.Count > 1 ? parts[1] : "";
                                string user = parts.Count > 2 ? parts[2] : "";
                                string client = parts.Count > 3 ? parts[3] : "";
                                string clientIp = parts.Count > 4 ? parts[4] : "";
                                string clientMac = parts.Count > 5 ? parts[5] : "";
                                string computer = parts.Count > 6 ? parts[6] : "";
                                string computerIp = parts.Count > 7 ? parts[7] : "";
                                string clientType = parts.Count > 8 ? parts[8] : "";
                                var entry = (when, action, user, client, clientIp, clientMac, computer, computerIp, clientType);
                                if (when >= cutoff)
                                    recent.Add(entry);
                                else
                                    olderLocal.Add(entry);
                            }
                            recent = recent.OrderByDescending(e => e.When).ToList();
                            olderLocal = olderLocal.OrderByDescending(e => e.When).ToList();
                        }
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        error = "Log load failed: " + ex.Message;
                    }

                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        // Ignore stale results if user hit Refresh again
                        if (seq != loadSeq) return;
                        refreshBtn.IsEnabled = true;

                        void RestoreScroll()
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                if (outerSv != null)
                                    outerSv.Offset = new Vector(outerSv.Offset.X, outerOff);
                            }
                            catch { }
                            try { scroll.Offset = new Vector(scroll.Offset.X, innerOff); } catch { }
                        }

                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(error))
                        {
                            list.Children.Clear();
                            older.Clear();
                            olderIdx = 0;
                            shown = 0;
                            total = 0;
                            statusLine.Text = error;
                            loadMoreBtn.IsVisible = false;
                            lastRefreshText.Text = "Last refresh: failed";
                            Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(RestoreScroll, DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
                            return;
                        }
                        if (totalLocal == 0)
                        {
                            list.Children.Clear();
                            older.Clear();
                            olderIdx = 0;
                            shown = 0;
                            total = 0;
                            statusLine.Text = "Log file is empty.";
                            loadMoreBtn.IsVisible = false;
                            DateTime emptyAt = DateTime.Now;
                            lastRefreshText.Text = "Last refresh: " + emptyAt.ToString("h:mm:ss tt");
                            ToolTip.SetTip(lastRefreshText, emptyAt.ToString("dddd, MMM d, yyyy h:mm:ss tt"));
                            Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(RestoreScroll, DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
                            return;
                        }

                        // Swap content only after data is ready (avoids page jump)
                        list.Children.Clear();
                        total = totalLocal;
                        older.Clear();
                        older.AddRange(olderLocal);
                        olderIdx = 0;
                        shown = 0;
                        foreach (var e in recent)
                        {
                            list.Children.Add(BuildEntryRow(e, shown));
                            shown++;
                        }
                        UpdateStatus();
                        DateTime refreshedAt = DateTime.Now;
                        lastRefreshText.Text = "Last refresh: " + refreshedAt.ToString("h:mm:ss tt");
                        ToolTip.SetTip(lastRefreshText, refreshedAt.ToString("dddd, MMM d, yyyy h:mm:ss tt"));
                        Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(RestoreScroll, DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
                    });
                });
            }

            refreshBtn.Click += (_, _) => StartLoad();
            StartLoad();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// ADUC-style Rename User dialog: full name, first/last, display name, UPN, sAMAccountName.
        /// Uses theme-friendly controls (works in light and dark mode).
        /// </summary>
        private void ShowRenameUserDialog(AdNode node)
        {
            if (node == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(node.Dn)) return;

            // Built-in / protected accounts can never be renamed (user
            // request 2026-10-27). Checked live at open; IsProtectedAdObject
            // fails closed, and the button is hidden for them as well.
            try
            {
                using var guard = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + node.Dn);
                if (IsProtectedAdObject(guard))
                {
                    ShowNotification("Rename blocked: \"" + node.Name + "\" is a built-in or protected account and cannot be renamed.", "error");
                    return;
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                ShowNotification("Rename blocked: could not verify whether \"" + node.Name + "\" is protected.", "error");
                return;
            }

            string fullName = "", givenName = "", surname = "", displayName = "";
            string sam = "", upn = "", upnPrefix = "", upnSuffix = "";
            string netBios = "";
            try
            {
                using var de = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + node.Dn);
                de.RefreshCache(new[] {
                    "cn", "name", "givenName", "sn", "displayName",
                    "sAMAccountName", "userPrincipalName"
                });
                fullName = de.Properties["cn"]?.Value?.ToString()
                        ?? de.Properties["name"]?.Value?.ToString()
                        ?? node.Name
                        ?? "";
                givenName = de.Properties["givenName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                surname = de.Properties["sn"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                displayName = de.Properties["displayName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? fullName;
                sam = de.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? node.SamAccountName ?? "";
                upn = de.Properties["userPrincipalName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowNotification("Could not load user for rename: " + ex.Message, "error");
                return;
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(upn) && upn.Contains('@'))
            {
                int at = upn.IndexOf('@');
                upnPrefix = upn.Substring(0, at);
                upnSuffix = upn.Substring(at + 1);
            }
            else
            {
                upnPrefix = sam;
                // DNS domain from domain DN: DC=oln,DC=afmc,DC=af,DC=mil -> oln.afmc.af.mil
                try
                {
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_domainDn))
                    {
                        var dcs = _domainDn.Split(',')
                            .Select(p => p.Trim())
                            .Where(p => p.StartsWith("DC=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            .Select(p => p.Substring(3));
                        upnSuffix = string.Join(".", dcs);
                    }
                }
                catch { }
            }

            // NetBIOS domain label (pre-Windows 2000): the first DC=
            // component of the domain DN, uppercased. This used to bind
            // RootDSE twice and the domain root once more to compute it -
            // but every value those binds produced (configuration NC, the
            // root's name) was never used, and a synchronous bind on the UI
            // thread is exactly the no-DC freeze fixed on 2026-10-27. The
            // label is derivable from _domainDn alone, so the binds are gone.
            // (Bonus: when a DC was unreachable the old code fell back to
            // "DOMAIN" before ever reaching this derivation; now the real
            // label shows.)
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_domainDn))
            {
                var first = _domainDn.Split(',')
                    .Select(p => p.Trim())
                    .FirstOrDefault(p => p.StartsWith("DC=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(first))
                    netBios = first.Substring(3).ToUpperInvariant();
            }
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(netBios))
                netBios = "DOMAIN";

            TextBox MkField(string initial)
            {
                return new TextBox
                {
                    Text = initial ?? "",
                    MinHeight = 32,
                    FontSize = 13,
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                };
            }

            var tbFull = MkField(fullName);
            var tbGiven = MkField(givenName);
            var tbSur = MkField(surname);
            var tbDisplay = MkField(displayName);
            var tbUpnPrefix = MkField(upnPrefix);
            var tbSam = MkField(sam);

            // Auto-suggest display/full from first+last when those change (only if user has not customized)
            void SyncNamesFromGivenSn()
            {
                string g = (tbGiven.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string s = (tbSur.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string combined = (g + " " + s).Trim();
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(combined)) return;
                // Only auto-fill if full/display still match old pattern or empty-ish
            }
            // Keep UPN prefix in sync with SAM when SAM edits (optional ADUC-like convenience)
            tbSam.TextChanged += (_, _) =>
            {
                // do not force UPN = SAM; user may want them different
            };

            Control LabeledRow(string label, Control field, string? prefix = null, string? suffix = null)
            {
                var grid = new Grid
                {
                    ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("160,*"),
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8),
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                };
                grid.Children.Add(Tb(label, size: 13, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 10, 0), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Right, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));
                Control right = field;
                if (prefix != null || suffix != null)
                {
                    var sp = new Grid
                    {
                        ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions(prefix != null && suffix != null ? "Auto,*,Auto" : (prefix != null ? "Auto,*" : "*,Auto")),
                        HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                    };
                    int col = 0;
                    if (prefix != null)
                    {
                        var pre = Tb(prefix, size: 13, opacity: 0.85, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 4, 0), valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
                        Grid.SetColumn(pre, col++);
                        sp.Children.Add(pre);
                    }
                    Grid.SetColumn(field, col++);
                    sp.Children.Add(field);
                    if (suffix != null)
                    {
                        var suf = Tb(suffix, size: 13, opacity: 0.85, margin: new Thickness(4, 0, 0, 0), valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
                        Grid.SetColumn(suf, col);
                        sp.Children.Add(suf);
                    }
                    right = sp;
                }
                Grid.SetColumn(right, 1);
                grid.Children.Add(right);
                return grid;
            }

            var body = new StackPanel
            {
                Spacing = 0,
                Margin = new Thickness(16),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            body.Children.Add(LabeledRow("Full name:", tbFull));
            body.Children.Add(LabeledRow("First name:", tbGiven));
            body.Children.Add(LabeledRow("Last name:", tbSur));
            body.Children.Add(LabeledRow("Display name:", tbDisplay));
            body.Children.Add(new Border
            {
                Height = 1,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 10),
                Opacity = 0.25,
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(128, 128, 128))
            });
            body.Children.Add(LabeledRow("User logon name:", tbUpnPrefix, null, string.IsNullOrEmpty(upnSuffix) ? null : ("@" + upnSuffix)));
            body.Children.Add(LabeledRow("User logon name\n(pre-Windows 2000):", tbSam, netBios + "\\", null));

            var errTb = Tb("", size: 12, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0), foreground: new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(200, 80, 80)), visible: false);
            body.Children.Add(errTb);

            var dlg = new Window
            {
                Title = "Rename User",
                Width = 460,
                MinWidth = 420,
                SizeToContent = SizeToContent.Height,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                CanResize = false,
                ShowInTaskbar = false
            };
            StyleDialogWindow(dlg);

            var okBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "OK",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 88,
                IsDefault = true
            };
            var cancelBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Cancel",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 88,
                IsCancel = true
            };
            cancelBtn.Click += (_, _) => dlg.Close();

            string originalDn = node.Dn;
            string originalCn = fullName;
            string originalSam = sam;
            string upnSuffixCap = upnSuffix;
            string nodeNameCap = node.Name;

            okBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                errTb.IsVisible = false;
                string newFull = (tbFull.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string newGiven = (tbGiven.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string newSur = (tbSur.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string newDisplay = (tbDisplay.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string newUpnPrefix = (tbUpnPrefix.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string newSam = (tbSam.Text ?? "").Trim();

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(newFull))
                {
                    errTb.Text = "Full name is required.";
                    errTb.IsVisible = true;
                    return;
                }
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(newSam))
                {
                    errTb.Text = "Pre-Windows 2000 logon name (sAMAccountName) is required.";
                    errTb.IsVisible = true;
                    return;
                }
                if (newSam.Length > 20)
                {
                    errTb.Text = "Pre-Windows 2000 logon name cannot exceed 20 characters.";
                    errTb.IsVisible = true;
                    return;
                }

                try
                {
                    using var fresh = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + originalDn);
                    fresh.RefreshCache(new[] {
                        "cn", "givenName", "sn", "displayName", "sAMAccountName", "userPrincipalName"
                    });

                    void SetOrClear(string prop, string value)
                    {
                        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
                        {
                            if (fresh.Properties.Contains(prop) && fresh.Properties[prop].Count > 0)
                                fresh.Properties[prop].Clear();
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            fresh.Properties[prop].Value = value;
                        }
                    }

                    SetOrClear("givenName", newGiven);
                    SetOrClear("sn", newSur);
                    SetOrClear("displayName", newDisplay);

                    if (!string.Equals(newSam, originalSam, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        fresh.Properties["sAMAccountName"].Value = newSam;

                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(upnSuffixCap) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(newUpnPrefix))
                        fresh.Properties["userPrincipalName"].Value = newUpnPrefix + "@" + upnSuffixCap;
                    else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(newUpnPrefix) && newUpnPrefix.Contains('@'))
                        fresh.Properties["userPrincipalName"].Value = newUpnPrefix;
                    else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(newUpnPrefix))
                    {
                        if (fresh.Properties.Contains("userPrincipalName") && fresh.Properties["userPrincipalName"].Count > 0)
                            fresh.Properties["userPrincipalName"].Clear();
                    }

                    fresh.CommitChanges();

                    // Rename CN (RDN) if full name changed - must be after other attribute commits
                    if (!string.Equals(newFull, originalCn, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                    {
                        // Escape special LDAP RDN characters in CN
                        string EscapeRdn(string cn)
                        {
                            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(cn)) return cn;
                            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
                            foreach (char ch in cn)
                            {
                                if (ch == ',' || ch == '+' || ch == '"' || ch == '\\' || ch == '<' || ch == '>' || ch == ';' || ch == '=' || ch == '\0')
                                    sb.Append('\\');
                                sb.Append(ch);
                            }
                            return sb.ToString();
                        }
                        string newRdn = "CN=" + EscapeRdn(newFull);
                        fresh.Rename(newRdn);
                        try { fresh.CommitChanges(); } catch { }

                        // Update node DN for refresh
                        try
                        {
                            string parentDn = originalDn.IndexOf(',') >= 0
                                ? originalDn.Substring(originalDn.IndexOf(',') + 1)
                                : originalDn;
                            node.Dn = newRdn + "," + parentDn;
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }

                    node.Name = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(newDisplay) ? newDisplay : newFull;
                    node.SamAccountName = newSam;

                    ShowNotification("User renamed: " + node.Name, "success");
                    dlg.Close();
                    ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    errTb.Text = "Rename failed: " + ex.Message;
                    errTb.IsVisible = true;
                }
            };

            var btnRow = new StackPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                Spacing = 10,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
                Margin = new Thickness(16, 8, 16, 16)
            };
            btnRow.Children.Add(okBtn);
            btnRow.Children.Add(cancelBtn);

            var root = new DockPanel { LastChildFill = true };
            DockPanel.SetDock(btnRow, Dock.Bottom);
            root.Children.Add(btnRow);
            root.Children.Add(body);
            dlg.Content = root;

            try { dlg.ShowDialog(this); }
            catch { try { dlg.Show(); } catch { } }
        }

        private void RenderUserActions(StackPanel panel, DirectoryEntry de, AdNode node)
        {
            int uac = 0;
            try { uac = (int)(de.Properties["userAccountControl"]?.Value ?? 0); } catch { }
            bool disabled = (uac & 0x2) != 0;
            bool locked = false;
            try
            {
                var lt = ReadLargeInteger(de.Properties["lockoutTime"]?.Value);
                if (lt.HasValue && lt.Value != 0) locked = true;
            }
            catch { }

            string nodeDn = node.Dn;
            string nodeName = node.Name;
            bool wasDisabled = disabled;

            var actionCol = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            actionCol.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Account Actions", FontSize = 16, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4) });
            // Wrap so Unlock / Disable / Profile Fix / Rename / Delete all stay inside the card
            var btnRow = new WrapPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left
            };
            // Spacing between wrapped action buttons
            if (locked && disabled)
            {
                // Combined action when both locked and disabled
                var unlockEnableBtn = new Button
                {
                    Content = "Unlock + Enable",
                    Classes = { "action" },
                    MinWidth = 150,
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 10, 8)
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(unlockEnableBtn, "Clear lockout and enable the account in one step");
                unlockEnableBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
                {
                    try
                    {
                        using var fresh = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDn);
                        fresh.Properties["lockoutTime"].Value = 0;
                        int cur = (int)(fresh.Properties["userAccountControl"].Value ?? 0);
                        fresh.Properties["userAccountControl"].Value = cur & ~0x2;
                        fresh.CommitChanges();
                        ShowNotification($"Account unlocked and enabled: {nodeName}", "success");
                        ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Unlock + Enable failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
                };
                btnRow.Children.Add(unlockEnableBtn);
            }
            else if (locked)
            {
                var unlockBtn = new Button { Content = "Unlock Account", Classes = { "action" }, MinWidth = 140, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 10, 8) };
                unlockBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
                {
                    try
                    {
                        using var fresh = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDn);
                        fresh.Properties["lockoutTime"].Value = 0;
                        fresh.CommitChanges();
                        ShowNotification($"Account unlocked: {nodeName}", "success");
                        ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Unlock failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
                };
                btnRow.Children.Add(unlockBtn);
            }
            var toggleBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = disabled ? "Enable Account" : "Disable Account",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 140,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 10, 8)
            };
            toggleBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                try
                {
                    using var fresh = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDn);
                    int cur = (int)(fresh.Properties["userAccountControl"].Value ?? 0);
                    if (wasDisabled)
                        fresh.Properties["userAccountControl"].Value = cur & ~0x2;
                    else
                        fresh.Properties["userAccountControl"].Value = cur | 0x2;
                    fresh.CommitChanges();
                    ShowNotification(wasDisabled ? $"Account enabled: {nodeName}" : $"Account disabled: {nodeName}", "success");
                    ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                }
                catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Toggle failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
            };
            // Still show Enable when locked+disabled so they can enable-only if needed; Unlock+Enable is primary
            btnRow.Children.Add(toggleBtn);

            string profileSam = node.SamAccountName;
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(profileSam))
            {
                try { profileSam = de.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? nodeName; }
                catch { profileSam = nodeName; }
            }
            var profileFixBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Profile Fix",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 140,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 10, 8)
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(profileFixBtn, "Close Horizon sessions, release profile file locks, rename profile to .old");
            string profileSamCap = (profileSam ?? "").Trim();
            profileFixBtn.Click += (_, _) => ShowProfileFixDialog(profileSamCap);
            btnRow.Children.Add(profileFixBtn);

            // No Rename button for built-in / protected accounts (same
            // treatment as Delete below); the dialog re-checks at open.
            bool userRenameBlocked = false;
            try { userRenameBlocked = IsProtectedAdObject(de); } catch { }
            if (!userRenameBlocked)
            {
                var renameUserBtn = new Button
                {
                    Content = "Rename",
                    Classes = { "action" },
                    MinWidth = 140,
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 10, 8)
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(renameUserBtn, "Rename user (full name, logon name, display name, ...)");
                renameUserBtn.Click += (_, _) => ShowRenameUserDialog(node);
                btnRow.Children.Add(renameUserBtn);
            }

            var deleteUserBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Delete User",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 140,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 10, 8),
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(176, 96, 96)),
                Foreground = Brushes.White
            };
            deleteUserBtn.Click += (_, _) => ConfirmAndDeleteObject(nodeDn, nodeName, "User", node);
            // Delete is never offered for protected accounts or Exchange /
            // service accounts (user request 2026-10-27) - the same treatment
            // the computer page gives protected machines and DCs. The delete
            // dialog re-checks both at click time, fail closed.
            string userDeleteBlock = "";
            try
            {
                if (IsProtectedAdObject(de))
                    userDeleteBlock = "Delete is unavailable: this is a built-in or protected account.";
                else
                {
                    try { de.RefreshCache(new[] { "name", "sAMAccountName", "mail" }); } catch { }
                    string? uName = null, uSam = null, uMail = null;
                    try { uName = de.Properties["name"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                    try { uSam = de.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                    try { uMail = de.Properties["mail"]?.Value?.ToString(); } catch { }
                    if (IsExchangeOrServiceAccount(uName, uSam, nodeDn, uMail, out string exchP))
                        userDeleteBlock = "Delete is unavailable: this looks like an Exchange or service account (matched \"" + exchP + "\").";
                }
            }
            catch { userDeleteBlock = ""; }
            if (userDeleteBlock.Length == 0)
                btnRow.Children.Add(deleteUserBtn);
            actionCol.Children.Add(btnRow);
            if (userDeleteBlock.Length > 0)
                actionCol.Children.Add(Tb(userDeleteBlock, size: 11, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 0)));

            panel.Children.Add(new Border { Classes = { "card" }, Child = actionCol, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Move an object to another OU: protected objects are blocked, the
        /// operator confirms (object + destination named), and afterwards the
        /// move is VERIFIED - the object is re-read at its expected new DN and
        /// its objectGUID compared - so "moved" is proven, not assumed.
        /// Returns true only when the move verified.
        /// </summary>
        private async Task<bool> ConfirmAndMoveToOuAsync(AdNode node, string destDn, string destName)
        {
            string nodeDn = node.Dn;
            string nodeName = node.Name;

            // Protected objects (DCs, critical / built-in, DO NOT DELETE) are never moved.
            try
            {
                using var probe = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDn);
                if (IsProtectedAdObject(probe))
                {
                    ShowNotification("Move blocked: " + nodeName + " is a protected object.", "error");
                    return false;
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                ShowNotification("Move blocked: could not verify protection for " + nodeName + ".", "error");
                return false;
            }

            bool yes = await ConfirmDialogAsync(
                "Move to OU?",
                "Move " + nodeName + " to:\n" + destName + "\n" + destDn + "\n\n"
                + "This changes the Group Policy and management settings that apply to it.");
            if (!yes) return false;

            try
            {
                string guidBefore;
                string rdn;
                using (var obj = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDn))
                {
                    guidBefore = obj.Guid.ToString();
                    string dnNow = obj.Properties["distinguishedName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? nodeDn;
                    int cut = dnNow.IndexOf(',');
                    rdn = cut > 0 ? dnNow.Substring(0, cut) : dnNow;
                    using var dest = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + destDn);
                    obj.MoveTo(dest);
                }

                // Verify: the same object (by GUID) must now live under the destination.
                string expectedDn = rdn + "," + destDn;
                bool verified = false;
                try
                {
                    using var moved = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + expectedDn);
                    moved.RefreshCache(new[] { "distinguishedName" });
                    verified = string.Equals(moved.Guid.ToString(), guidBefore, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                        && string.Equals(moved.Properties["distinguishedName"]?.Value?.ToString(),
                            expectedDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                }
                catch { verified = false; }

                if (verified)
                {
                    node.Dn = expectedDn;
                    ShowNotification("Moved " + nodeName + " to \"" + destName + "\" (verified).", "success");
                    return true;
                }
                ShowNotification("Move sent for " + nodeName
                    + " but could not be verified at the new location yet (replication may lag). Refresh before acting on it.", "error");
                return false;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowNotification("Move failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                return false;
            }
        }

        private void RenderMoveToOu(StackPanel panel, DirectoryEntry de, AdNode node)
        {
            string nodeDn = node.Dn;
            string nodeName = node.Name;

            var ouBox = new TextBox
            {
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Watermark = "Search OU name..."
            };
            var ouSuggest = new StackPanel { Spacing = 3 };
            var ouScroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0),
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                Content = ouSuggest,
                MaxHeight = 260,
                Height = 260,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                IsVisible = false,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0)
            };
            ouBox.TextChanged += async (_, _) =>
            {
                string raw = ouBox.Text?.Trim() ?? "";
                ouSuggest.Children.Clear();
                if (raw.Length < 1)
                {
                    ouScroll.IsVisible = false;
                    return;
                }
                try
                {
                    // Fill/refresh the OU cache off the UI thread; drop the result
                    // if the text has moved on while the search was running.
                    var cache = await Task.Run(() => EnsureOuCache());
                    if (!string.Equals(ouBox.Text?.Trim() ?? "", raw, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                        return;
                    string q = raw.ToLowerInvariant();
                    int n = 0;
                    foreach (var (ouName, ouDn) in cache)
                    {
                        if (ouName.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0
                            && ouDn.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0)
                            continue;
                        if (n++ >= 15) break;
                        var content = new StackPanel { Spacing = 1 };
                        content.Children.Add(MakeIconLine("OU", ouName, 13, 13, bold: false));
                        content.Children.Add(Tb(ouDn, size: 11, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true));
                        var b = new Button
                        {
                            Content = content,
                            Classes = { "suggestItem" },
                            HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                            HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left
                        };
                        string capDn = ouDn;
                        string capName = ouName;
                        b.Click += async (_, _) =>
                        {
                            bool moved = await ConfirmAndMoveToOuAsync(node, capDn, capName);
                            if (!moved) return;
                            ouBox.Text = "";
                            ouSuggest.Children.Clear();
                            ouScroll.IsVisible = false;
                            ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                        };
                        ouSuggest.Children.Add(b);
                    }
                    ouScroll.IsVisible = ouSuggest.Children.Count > 0;
                    // Keep focus/scroll on the search box - do not jump the page to the bottom
                }
                catch { ouScroll.IsVisible = false; }
            };

            var wrap = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            wrap.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Move to OU", FontSize = 16, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4) });
            wrap.Children.Add(ouBox);
            wrap.Children.Add(ouScroll);
            panel.Children.Add(new Border { Classes = { "card" }, Child = wrap, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Read \\host\C$\Users, sort profile folders by LastWriteTime, show most recent local users.
        /// Requires admin share access (C$).
        /// </summary>
        private void RenderLocalProfileActivity(StackPanel panel, string host)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host)) return;
            string resolveHost = ResolveRdpHost(host);
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(resolveHost)) resolveHost = host.Trim().TrimEnd('$');

            var body = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };
            body.Children.Add(Tb("Local profiles (C$\\Users)", size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold));
            body.Children.Add(Tb("Most recently modified profile folders on \\\\" + resolveHost + "\\C$\\Users (proxy for last local activity).", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true));
            var status = new TextBlock { Text = "Reading profile folders...", FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.85 };
            body.Children.Add(status);
            var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 3 };
            body.Children.Add(list);
            panel.Children.Add(new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Padding = new Thickness(12),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0),
                Child = body
            });

            string hostCap = resolveHost;
            // Run off UI thread-ish via Post so UI paints first
            Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
            {
                try
                {
                    string unc = @"\\" + hostCap + @"\C$\Users";
                    if (!Directory.Exists(unc))
                    {
                        status.Text = "Cannot access " + unc + " (offline, blocked, or no rights).";
                        return;
                    }
                    var skip = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    {
                        "Public", "Default", "Default User", "All Users", "DefaultAppPool",
                        "LocalService", "NetworkService", "systemprofile", "administrator.adatum"
                    };
                    var profiles = new List<(string Name, DateTime Modified)>();
                    foreach (string dir in Directory.GetDirectories(unc))
                    {
                        string name = Path.GetFileName(dir);
                        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name) || name.StartsWith(".")) continue;
                        if (skip.Contains(name)) continue;
                        // Skip well-known service-style folders
                        if (name.EndsWith(".NET", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                        DateTime mod = Directory.GetLastWriteTime(dir);
                        profiles.Add((name, mod));
                    }
                    profiles = profiles.OrderByDescending(p => p.Modified).ToList();
                    if (profiles.Count == 0)
                    {
                        status.Text = "No user profile folders found.";
                        return;
                    }
                    status.Text = "Top profiles by last folder change (most recent first):";
                    int show = Math.Min(8, profiles.Count);
                    for (int i = 0; i < show; i++)
                    {
                        var p = profiles[i];
                        string when = p.Modified.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm");
                        int days = Math.Max(0, (int)(DateTime.Now - p.Modified).TotalDays);
                        string userName = p.Name;
                        var row = new StackPanel
                        {
                            Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                            Spacing = 6,
                            VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
                        };
                        // Order: user icon | clipboard | username | date
                        row.Children.Add(MakeMaterialIcon("User", 16));
                        var copyUser = new Button
                        {
                            Content = "\U0001F4CB",
                            Padding = new Thickness(4, 2),
                            Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                            BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                            Cursor = new Avalonia.Input.Cursor(Avalonia.Input.StandardCursorType.Hand),
                            VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                            FontSize = 12
                        };
                        ToolTip.SetTip(copyUser, "Copy username");
                        string capName = userName;
                        copyUser.Click += async (_, _) =>
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                var top = TopLevel.GetTopLevel(this);
                                if (top?.Clipboard != null)
                                {
                                    await top.Clipboard.SetTextAsync(capName);
                                    ShowNotification("Copied: " + capName, "success");
                                }
                            }
                            catch { }
                        };
                        row.Children.Add(copyUser);
                        row.Children.Add(Tb(userName + "  -  " + when + "  (" + days + "d ago)", size: 13, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas")));
                        list.Children.Add(row);
                    }
                    if (profiles.Count > show)
                        list.Children.Add(Tb("... +" + (profiles.Count - show) + " more profile folders", size: 12, opacity: 0.7));
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    status.Text = "C$\\Users unavailable: " + ex.Message;
                }
            }, DispatcherPriority.Background);
        }

        private void RenderComputerActions(StackPanel panel, DirectoryEntry de, AdNode node)
        {
            int uac = 0;
            try { uac = (int)(de.Properties["userAccountControl"]?.Value ?? 0); } catch { }
            bool disabled = (uac & 0x2) != 0;
            string nodeDn = node.Dn;
            string nodeName = node.Name;
            bool wasDisabled = disabled;

            // Domain controller? SERVER_TRUST_ACCOUNT machine flag, or the
            // Domain Controllers (516) / RODC (521) primary groups. A DC can
            // never be disabled - the toggle below enforces it twice.
            bool isDcComputer = (uac & 0x2000) != 0;
            if (!isDcComputer)
            {
                try
                {
                    int pgid = Convert.ToInt32(de.Properties["primaryGroupID"]?.Value ?? 0);
                    isDcComputer = pgid == 516 || pgid == 521;
                }
                catch { }
            }

            // Content goes into the shared outer card (same height as Logged-in users)
            panel.Children.Add(Tb("Actions", size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));

            var body = panel; // render directly into the outer card body

            // Resolve host early for connectivity + RDP
            string rdpHost = nodeName;
            try
            {
                string? dns = de.Properties["dNSHostName"]?.Value?.ToString();
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dns)) rdpHost = dns.Trim();
                else
                {
                    string? sam = de.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString();
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sam)) rdpHost = sam.Trim().TrimEnd('$');
                }
            }
            catch { }
            string rdpHostCap = rdpHost;
            string resolveHostCap = ResolveRdpHost(rdpHostCap);

            // Quick connectivity: ping + SMB/RDP ports (green/red dots)
            var connPanel = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8) };
            var connBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Check connectivity",
                Classes = { "action" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                MinWidth = 140
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(connBtn, "Ping host and test TCP ports: 445 (SMB/C$), 3389 (RDP)");
            var connStatus = Tb("Ping / ports not checked yet.", size: 12, opacity: 0.85, wrap: true);
            var connDetails = new StackPanel { Spacing = 3 };
            Border StatusDot(bool ok) => new Border
            {
                Width = 10,
                Height = 10,
                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(5),
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(ok
                    ? Color.FromRgb(76, 175, 80)
                    : Color.FromRgb(229, 115, 115)),
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 8, 0)
            };
            StackPanel StatusLine(bool ok, string text)
            {
                var row = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 0 };
                row.Children.Add(StatusDot(ok));
                row.Children.Add(Tb(text, size: 12, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas")));
                return row;
            }
            connBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                connBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                connStatus.Text = "Checking " + resolveHostCap + "...";
                connDetails.Children.Clear();
                string host = resolveHostCap;
                try
                {
                    var pingResult = await Task.Run(() => TryPingHost(host));
                    string pingLine = pingResult.Ok
                        ? "Ping: OK  " + pingResult.Ms + " ms" + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(pingResult.Detail) ? "" : "  (" + pingResult.Detail + ")")
                        : "Ping: FAIL  " + pingResult.Detail;
                    connDetails.Children.Add(StatusLine(pingResult.Ok, pingLine));

                    var ports = new (int Port, string Label)[]
                    {
                        (445, "SMB / C$"),
                        (3389, "RDP")
                    };
                    foreach (var (port, label) in ports)
                    {
                        bool open = await Task.Run(() => TryTcpPort(host, port));
                        connDetails.Children.Add(StatusLine(open,
                            "Port " + port + " (" + label + "): " + (open ? "OPEN" : "closed / filtered")));
                    }
                    connStatus.Text = "Checked " + host + " at " + DateTime.Now.ToString("HH:mm:ss");
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    connStatus.Text = "Check failed: " + ex.Message;
                }
                finally { connBtn.IsEnabled = true; }
            };
            connPanel.Children.Add(connBtn);
            connPanel.Children.Add(connStatus);
            connPanel.Children.Add(connDetails);
            body.Children.Add(connPanel);

            // Wrap so buttons stay inside the Actions card (half-width panel)
            var compBtnRow = new WrapPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            Thickness btnMargin = new Thickness(0, 0, 8, 8);
            var toggleBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = disabled ? "Enable Computer" : "Disable Computer",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 120,
                Margin = btnMargin
            };
            toggleBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                try
                {
                    using var fresh = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + nodeDn);
                    int cur = (int)(fresh.Properties["userAccountControl"].Value ?? 0);
                    if (wasDisabled)
                    {
                        fresh.Properties["userAccountControl"].Value = cur & ~0x2;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        // Hard gate: a domain controller can never be disabled
                        // (user request 2026-10-27). Re-checked on the live
                        // entry - the page may be stale.
                        int pgid = 0;
                        try { pgid = Convert.ToInt32(fresh.Properties["primaryGroupID"].Value ?? 0); } catch { }
                        if ((cur & 0x2000) != 0 || pgid == 516 || pgid == 521)
                        {
                            ShowNotification("Disable blocked: " + nodeName + " is a domain controller. Domain controllers cannot be disabled.", "error");
                            return;
                        }
                        fresh.Properties["userAccountControl"].Value = cur | 0x2;
                    }
                    fresh.CommitChanges();
                    ShowNotification(wasDisabled ? $"Computer enabled: {nodeName}" : $"Computer disabled: {nodeName}", "success");
                    ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                }
                catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Toggle failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
            };
            if (isDcComputer && !disabled)
            {
                // No Disable button on a domain controller - a note instead.
                // (If one is ever found already disabled, the Enable button
                // above still shows so it can be brought back.)
                compBtnRow.Children.Add(Tb("Domain controller - it cannot be disabled.", size: 12, opacity: 0.8, wrap: true, margin: btnMargin));
            }
            else
            {
                compBtnRow.Children.Add(toggleBtn);
            }

            var rdpBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Remote Desktop",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 120,
                Margin = btnMargin
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(rdpBtn, "RDP to " + rdpHostCap
                + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_rdpUsername) ? "" : " as " + _rdpUsername));
            rdpBtn.Click += (_, _) => LaunchRdp(rdpHostCap);
            compBtnRow.Children.Add(rdpBtn);

            var cShareBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "C$",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 80,
                Margin = btnMargin
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(cShareBtn, "Open \\\\" + rdpHostCap + "\\C$ in File Explorer");
            cShareBtn.Click += (_, _) => OpenComputerAdminShare(rdpHostCap);
            compBtnRow.Children.Add(cShareBtn);

            var shadowBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Shadow Session",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 120,
                Margin = btnMargin,
                IsEnabled = false
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(shadowBtn, "Checking for active sessions on " + rdpHostCap + "...");
            shadowBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                shadowBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                try
                {
                    var active = await Task.Run(() => GetActiveRemoteSessions(resolveHostCap));
                    if (active.Count == 0)
                        active = await Task.Run(() => GetActiveRemoteSessions(rdpHostCap));
                    if (active.Count == 0)
                    {
                        ShowNotification("No active session to shadow on " + rdpHostCap + ".", "error");
                        ToolTip.SetTip(shadowBtn, "No active session to shadow");
                        return;
                    }
                    if (active.Count == 1)
                        LaunchShadowRdp(rdpHostCap, null, active[0].Id);
                    else
                        await ShowShadowSessionPickerAndLaunchAsync(rdpHostCap, active);
                }
                finally
                {
                    // Re-query enable state
                    try
                    {
                        var active2 = await Task.Run(() => GetActiveRemoteSessions(resolveHostCap));
                        shadowBtn.IsEnabled = active2.Count > 0;
                        ToolTip.SetTip(shadowBtn, active2.Count > 0
                            ? active2.Count + " active session(s)  -  click to shadow"
                            : "No active session to shadow");
                    }
                    catch { shadowBtn.IsEnabled = false; }
                }
            };
            compBtnRow.Children.Add(shadowBtn);
            // Probe sessions so button is only usable when someone is logged on
            {
                string probeHost = resolveHostCap;
                string probeHost2 = rdpHostCap;
                Task.Run(async () =>
                {
                    try { await Task.Delay(250); } catch { }
                    var active = GetActiveRemoteSessions(probeHost);
                    if (active.Count == 0)
                        active = GetActiveRemoteSessions(probeHost2);
                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        shadowBtn.IsEnabled = active.Count > 0;
                        ToolTip.SetTip(shadowBtn, active.Count > 0
                            ? active.Count + " active session(s)  -  click to shadow"
                            : "No active session to shadow on " + rdpHostCap);
                    });
                });
            }

            var rebootBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Reboot",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 120,
                Margin = btnMargin
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(rebootBtn, "Restart " + rdpHostCap + " and wait until it responds to ping");
            AdNode rebootNodeCap = node;
            rebootBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                rebootBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                try { await RebootComputerAndWaitAsync(rdpHostCap, rebootNodeCap); }
                finally { rebootBtn.IsEnabled = true; }
            };
            compBtnRow.Children.Add(rebootBtn);

            var delProfBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "DelProf2",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 120,
                Margin = btnMargin
            };
            ToolTip.SetTip(delProfBtn,
                "DelProf2 (Helge Klein) - delete inactive Windows user profiles on this PC.\n\n"
                + "Only profiles that are not currently loaded are removed.\n"
                + "Also cleans stale ProfileList SID.bak registry entries.\n\n"
                + "Command: DelProf2.exe /c:COMPUTER /u /i\n"
                + "  /c  remote computer\n"
                + "  /u  unattended (no confirmation)\n"
                + "  /i  ignore errors, continue\n\n"
                + "Copies DelProf2.exe to C:\\Windows\\System32 on the target if missing,\n"
                + "from the Active Directory Explorer software share. Live output is shown.");
            delProfBtn.Click += (_, _) => RunDelProf2OnHost(rdpHostCap, node);
            compBtnRow.Children.Add(delProfBtn);

            // Delete is never offered for protected computers / domain
            // controllers, and is withdrawn when the machine turns out to be
            // online (background probe, same pattern as the Shadow button).
            // The delete dialog re-checks both at click time, fail closed.
            var deleteNote = Tb("", size: 11, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 0), visible: false);
            bool compProtected = false;
            try { compProtected = IsProtectedAdObject(de); } catch { compProtected = true; }
            if (compProtected)
            {
                deleteNote.Text = "Delete is unavailable: this is a protected computer or a domain controller.";
                deleteNote.IsVisible = true;
            }
            else
            {
                var deleteCompBtnTop = new Button
                {
                    Content = "Delete Computer",
                    Classes = { "action" },
                    MinWidth = 120,
                    Margin = btnMargin,
                    Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(176, 96, 96)),
                    Foreground = Brushes.White
                };
                deleteCompBtnTop.Click += (_, _) => ConfirmAndDeleteObject(nodeDn, nodeName, "Computer", node);
                compBtnRow.Children.Add(deleteCompBtnTop);
                string probeHost = resolveHostCap;
                Task.Run(() =>
                {
                    bool online = false;
                    try
                    {
                        var addrs = System.Net.Dns.GetHostAddresses(probeHost);
                        online = addrs != null && addrs.Length > 0;
                    }
                    catch { }
                    if (!online)
                    {
                        try { online = TryPingHost(probeHost, 700).Ok; } catch { }
                    }
                    if (online)
                    {
                        Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                        {
                            deleteCompBtnTop.IsVisible = false;
                            deleteNote.Text = "Delete is unavailable while this computer is online (it has a DNS address or answers a ping).";
                            deleteNote.IsVisible = true;
                        });
                    }
                });
            }
            body.Children.Add(compBtnRow);
            body.Children.Add(deleteNote);

            body.Children.Add(Tb("Move to OU", weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, margin: new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 2), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));

            var ouBox = new TextBox
            {
                Width = 320,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                Watermark = "Search OU name..."
            };
            var ouSuggest = new StackPanel { Spacing = 3 };
            var ouScroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0),
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                Content = ouSuggest,
                MaxHeight = 260,
                Height = 260,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                IsVisible = false
            };
            ouBox.TextChanged += async (_, _) =>
            {
                string raw = ouBox.Text?.Trim() ?? "";
                ouSuggest.Children.Clear();
                if (raw.Length < 1)
                {
                    ouScroll.IsVisible = false;
                    return;
                }
                try
                {
                    // Fill/refresh the OU cache off the UI thread; drop the result
                    // if the text has moved on while the search was running.
                    var cache = await Task.Run(() => EnsureOuCache());
                    if (!string.Equals(ouBox.Text?.Trim() ?? "", raw, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                        return;
                    string q = raw.ToLowerInvariant();
                    int n = 0;
                    foreach (var (ouName, ouDn) in cache)
                    {
                        if (ouName.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0
                            && ouDn.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0)
                            continue;
                        if (n++ >= 15) break;
                        var contentSp = new StackPanel { Spacing = 1 };
                        contentSp.Children.Add(MakeIconLine("OU", ouName, 13, 13, bold: false));
                        contentSp.Children.Add(Tb(ouDn, size: 11, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true));
                        var b = new Button
                        {
                            Content = contentSp,
                            Classes = { "suggestItem" },
                            HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                            HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left
                        };
                        string capDn = ouDn;
                        string capName = ouName;
                        b.Click += async (_, _) =>
                        {
                            bool moved = await ConfirmAndMoveToOuAsync(node, capDn, capName);
                            if (!moved) return;
                            ouBox.Text = "";
                            ouSuggest.Children.Clear();
                            ouScroll.IsVisible = false;
                            ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                        };
                        ouSuggest.Children.Add(b);
                    }
                    ouScroll.IsVisible = ouSuggest.Children.Count > 0;
                    // Keep focus/scroll on the search box - do not jump the page to the bottom
                }
                catch { ouScroll.IsVisible = false; }
            };
            body.Children.Add(ouBox);
            body.Children.Add(ouScroll);
        }


    }
}
'@
$mwRemoteCs = @'
// ADE - MainWindow partial class: RDP, shadow RDP, VNC, reboot and remote-session tools.
// Split from MainWindow.axaml.cs for maintainability (2026-10-26):
// same partial class, same namespace - no behavior change.
using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.DirectoryServices;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Net.Security;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Security.Principal;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Avalonia;
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Controls.Primitives;
using Avalonia.Input;
using Avalonia.Interactivity;
using Avalonia.Layout;
using Avalonia.Markup.Xaml;
using Avalonia.Media;
using Avalonia.Threading;

namespace __APPNAME__
{
    public partial class MainWindow : Window
    {
        private readonly List<System.Diagnostics.Process> _launchedRdpProcesses = new();
        private readonly object _rdpProcLock = new();
        private readonly HashSet<string> _rdpCmdKeyHosts = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        private System.Diagnostics.Process? StartMstsc(string arguments)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Process? proc = null;
            try
            {
                // UseShellExecute=false so we get a real process handle to track/kill
                var psi = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = "mstsc.exe",
                    Arguments = arguments,
                    UseShellExecute = false,
                    CreateNoWindow = false
                };
                proc = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psi);
            }
            catch
            {
                // Fallback if direct start fails
                try
                {
                    proc = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = "mstsc.exe",
                        Arguments = arguments,
                        UseShellExecute = true
                    });
                }
                catch { }
            }
            if (proc != null)
            {
                lock (_rdpProcLock)
                {
                    _launchedRdpProcesses.RemoveAll(p =>
                    {
                        try { return p.HasExited; } catch { return true; }
                    });
                    _launchedRdpProcesses.Add(proc);
                }
                try
                {
                    proc.EnableRaisingEvents = true;
                    proc.Exited += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        lock (_rdpProcLock)
                        {
                            try { _launchedRdpProcesses.Remove(proc); } catch { }
                        }
                    };
                }
                catch { }
            }
            return proc;
        }

        private void CleanupLaunchedRdpProcesses()
        {
            List<System.Diagnostics.Process> copy;
            lock (_rdpProcLock)
            {
                copy = _launchedRdpProcesses.ToList();
                _launchedRdpProcesses.Clear();
            }
            foreach (var p in copy)
            {
                try
                {
                    if (!p.HasExited)
                        p.Kill(entireProcessTree: true);
                }
                catch { }
                try { p.Dispose(); } catch { }
            }

            // Clear TERMSRV credentials we stored via cmdkey
            List<string> hosts;
            lock (_rdpProcLock)
            {
                hosts = _rdpCmdKeyHosts.ToList();
                _rdpCmdKeyHosts.Clear();
            }
            foreach (string host in hosts)
            {
                try
                {
                    var del = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = "cmdkey.exe",
                        Arguments = "/delete:TERMSRV/" + host,
                        UseShellExecute = false,
                        CreateNoWindow = true,
                        RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                        RedirectStandardError = true
                    });
                    del?.WaitForExit(3000);
                }
                catch { }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Normalize host for RDP: strip trailing $, optionally append DNS suffix.</summary>
        private string ResolveRdpHost(string host)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host)) return "";
            host = host.Trim().TrimEnd('$');
            if (host.Contains('.')) return host;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_dnsRoot))
                return host + "." + _dnsRoot.Trim().TrimStart('.');
            return host;
        }

        /// <summary>Store TERMSRV credentials via cmdkey so mstsc uses admin account.</summary>
        private void ApplyRdpCredentials(string host)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host)) return;
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_rdpUsername) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_rdpPassword))
                return;
            try
            {
                // Clear any stale entry first
                try
                {
                    var del = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = "cmdkey.exe",
                        Arguments = "/delete:TERMSRV/" + host,
                        UseShellExecute = false,
                        CreateNoWindow = true,
                        RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                        RedirectStandardError = true
                    });
                    del?.WaitForExit(5000);
                }
                catch { }

                var add = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = "cmdkey.exe",
                    Arguments = "/generic:TERMSRV/" + host
                        + " /user:" + _rdpUsername
                        + " /pass:" + _rdpPassword,
                    UseShellExecute = false,
                    CreateNoWindow = true,
                    RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                    RedirectStandardError = true
                });
                add?.WaitForExit(8000);
                lock (_rdpProcLock) { _rdpCmdKeyHosts.Add(host); }
            }
            catch { }
        }

        /// <summary>Open Explorer to \\host\C$ (admin share). Uses Windows stored credentials when available.</summary>
        private void OpenComputerAdminShare(string host)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host)) return;
            host = ResolveRdpHost(host.Trim().TrimEnd('$'));
            string unc = @"\\" + host + @"\C$";
            try
            {
                // Best-effort: map with cmdkey-style credentials via net use if RDP admin set
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_rdpUsername) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_rdpPassword))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var del = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                        {
                            FileName = "net.exe",
                            Arguments = "use \"" + unc + "\" /delete /y",
                            UseShellExecute = false,
                            CreateNoWindow = true,
                            RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                            RedirectStandardError = true
                        });
                        del?.WaitForExit(5000);
                    }
                    catch { }
                    try
                    {
                        var add = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                        {
                            FileName = "net.exe",
                            Arguments = "use \"" + unc + "\" /user:" + _rdpUsername + " \"" + _rdpPassword.Replace("\"", "\\\"") + "\"",
                            UseShellExecute = false,
                            CreateNoWindow = true,
                            RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                            RedirectStandardError = true
                        });
                        add?.WaitForExit(15000);
                    }
                    catch { }
                }

                System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = "explorer.exe",
                    Arguments = unc,
                    UseShellExecute = true
                });
                ShowNotification("Opening " + unc, "info");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowNotification("Could not open " + unc + ": " + ex.Message, "error");
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Normal (non-shadow) RDP with configured admin credentials.</summary>
        private void LaunchRdp(string host)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host)) return;
            host = ResolveRdpHost(host);
            try
            {
                ApplyRdpCredentials(host);
                StartMstsc("/v:" + host);
                ShowNotification("RDP -> " + host
                    + (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_rdpUsername) ? "" : " (as " + _rdpUsername + ")"), "info");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowNotification("RDP launch failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Admin shadow RDP into the user's active session without consent prompt.
        /// Resolves session ID via qwinsta / quser (prefer matching username + Active).
        /// Uses Settings RDP admin credentials via cmdkey. Falls back to normal RDP.
        /// </summary>
        private async Task ShowShadowSessionPickerAndLaunchAsync(
            string host,
            List<(int Id, string User, string State, string Name)> sessions)
        {
            if (sessions == null || sessions.Count == 0)
            {
                ShowNotification("No active session to shadow on " + host + ".", "error");
                return;
            }
            if (sessions.Count == 1)
            {
                LaunchShadowRdp(host, null, sessions[0].Id);
                return;
            }

            var tcs = new TaskCompletionSource<int?>();
            var dlg = new Window
            {
                Title = "Select session to shadow",
                Width = 480,
                SizeToContent = SizeToContent.Height,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                CanResize = false
            };
            StyleDialogWindow(dlg);
            var root = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(20), Spacing = 10 };
            root.Children.Add(Tb("Multiple active sessions on " + host + ". Choose one:", size: 13, wrap: true));
            var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };
            int? selected = null;
            foreach (var s in sessions)
            {
                string label = "#" + s.Id + "  "
                    + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(s.User) ? "(no user)" : s.User)
                    + "  [" + s.State + "]"
                    + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(s.Name) ? "" : "  " + s.Name);
                var b = new Button
                {
                    Content = label,
                    Classes = { "action" },
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                    HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                    MinHeight = 34
                };
                int idCap = s.Id;
                b.Click += (_, _) =>
                {
                    selected = idCap;
                    tcs.TrySetResult(idCap);
                    dlg.Close();
                };
                list.Children.Add(b);
            }
            root.Children.Add(list);
            var cancel = new Button { Content = "Cancel", Classes = { "action" }, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right, MinWidth = 100 };
            cancel.Click += (_, _) => { tcs.TrySetResult(null); dlg.Close(); };
            root.Children.Add(cancel);
            dlg.Closed += (_, _) => tcs.TrySetResult(selected);
            dlg.Content = root;
            try { await dlg.ShowDialog(this); } catch { try { dlg.Show(); } catch { } }
            int? pick = await tcs.Task;
            if (pick.HasValue && pick.Value > 0)
                LaunchShadowRdp(host, null, pick);
        }

        private async Task RebootComputerAndWaitAsync(string host, AdNode? refreshNode = null)
        {
            host = ResolveRdpHost(host);
