            string shortHost = host.Contains('.') ? host.Split('.')[0] : host;
            bool sure = await PromptYesNoAsync(
                "Reboot computer?",
                "Restart " + host + " now?\n\n"
                + "Users will be disconnected. ADE will wait until the PC responds to ping again.");
            if (!sure) return;

            TextBlock st;
            ProgressBar bar;
            var dlg = ShowProgressWindow("Rebooting " + shortHost, "Sending restart command...", out st, out bar);
            try
            {
                bool sent = await Task.Run(() =>
                {
                    try
                    {
                        // shutdown.exe remote reboot
                        var psi = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                        {
                            FileName = "shutdown.exe",
                            Arguments = "/m \\\\" + host + " /r /t 5 /f /c \"Restart initiated from Active Directory Explorer\"",
                            UseShellExecute = false,
                            RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                            RedirectStandardError = true,
                            CreateNoWindow = true
                        };
                        using var p = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psi);
                        if (p == null) return false;
                        p.WaitForExit(20000);
                        string err = (p.StandardError.ReadToEnd() ?? "") + (p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd() ?? "");
                        // exit 0 = success; some systems return non-zero with still working
                        if (p.ExitCode == 0) return true;
                        // fallback short name
                        if (!string.Equals(host, shortHost, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        {
                            psi.Arguments = "/m \\\\" + shortHost + " /r /t 5 /f /c \"Restart initiated from Active Directory Explorer\"";
                            using var p2 = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psi);
                            if (p2 == null) return false;
                            p2.WaitForExit(20000);
                            return p2.ExitCode == 0;
                        }
                        return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(err);
                    }
                    catch { return false; }
                });

                if (!sent)
                {
                    st.Text = "Failed to send restart command. Check admin rights / RPC.";
                    await Task.Delay(2000);
                    return;
                }

                st.Text = "Restart command sent. Waiting for " + shortHost + " to go offline...";
                // Wait until ping fails (up to 90s) then until it returns (up to 10 min)
                bool wentOffline = false;
                for (int i = 0; i < 45; i++)
                {
                    var ping = await Task.Run(() => TryPingHost(host, 1200));
                    if (!ping.Ok)
                    {
                        wentOffline = true;
                        break;
                    }
                    st.Text = "Waiting for offline... (" + ((i + 1) * 2) + "s)";
                    await Task.Delay(2000);
                }

                st.Text = wentOffline
                    ? "Host is offline. Waiting for it to come back..."
                    : "Still responding (fast reboot or ping blocked). Waiting for stable online...";

                bool online = false;
                int maxWait = 150; // 5 minutes @ 2s
                for (int i = 0; i < maxWait; i++)
                {
                    var ping = await Task.Run(() => TryPingHost(host, 1500));
                    if (ping.Ok)
                    {
                        // require 2 successful pings
                        await Task.Delay(1500);
                        var ping2 = await Task.Run(() => TryPingHost(host, 1500));
                        if (ping2.Ok)
                        {
                            online = true;
                            st.Text = "Online again (" + ping2.Ms + " ms). Reboot finished.";
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    st.Text = "Pinging " + shortHost + "... (" + ((i + 1) * 2) + "s)";
                    await Task.Delay(2000);
                }

                if (online)
                    ShowNotification(shortHost + " is back online.", "success");
                else
                    ShowNotification(shortHost + " did not respond to ping within the wait period.", "error");

                await Task.Delay(1200);
            }
            finally
            {
                try { dlg.Close(); } catch { }
            }

            if (refreshNode != null)
            {
                try { ShowHelpDeskInfo(refreshNode); } catch { }
            }
        }

        private void LaunchShadowRdp(string host, string? preferredUser = null, int? forcedSessionId = null)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host)) return;
            string shortHost = host.Trim().TrimEnd('$');
            host = ResolveRdpHost(shortHost);
            preferredUser = (preferredUser ?? "").Trim();
            // Normalize preferred user to short name
            if (preferredUser.Contains('\\')) preferredUser = preferredUser.Split('\\').Last();
            if (preferredUser.Contains('@')) preferredUser = preferredUser.Split('@')[0];

            try
            {
                ApplyRdpCredentials(host);

                // Collect candidate sessions: (Id, UserName, State, SessionName)
                var sessions = new List<(int Id, string User, string State, string Name)>();
                void ParseSessionLines(string raw)
                {
                    foreach (string line in (raw ?? "").Split(new[] { '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries))
                    {
                        string t = line.TrimStart();
                        if (t.Length == 0) continue;
                        if (t.StartsWith("SESSIONNAME", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                        if (t.StartsWith("USERNAME", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                        if (t.StartsWith(">")) t = t.Substring(1).TrimStart();

                        var m = System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Match(
                            t,
                            @"^(?<name>\S*)\s+(?:(?<user>\S+)\s+)?(?<id>\d+)\s+(?<state>Active|Disc|Disconnected|Listen|Conn|Connected|Idle)",
                            System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
                        if (!m.Success)
                        {
                            var tokens = t.Split((char[]?)null, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
                            int stateIdx = -1;
                            for (int i = 0; i < tokens.Length; i++)
                            {
                                string s = tokens[i];
                                if (s.Equals("Active", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                    s.Equals("Disc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                    s.Equals("Disconnected", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                    s.Equals("Listen", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                    s.Equals("Conn", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                    s.Equals("Connected", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                { stateIdx = i; break; }
                            }
                            if (stateIdx < 1) continue;
                            if (!int.TryParse(tokens[stateIdx - 1], out int sidFb)) continue;
                            if (sidFb <= 0) continue;
                            string userFb = "";
                            if (stateIdx >= 2 && !int.TryParse(tokens[stateIdx - 2], out _))
                                userFb = tokens[stateIdx - 2];
                            sessions.Add((sidFb, userFb, tokens[stateIdx], tokens[0]));
                            continue;
                        }

                        int sid = int.Parse(m.Groups["id"].Value);
                        if (sid <= 0) continue;
                        string user = m.Groups["user"].Success ? m.Groups["user"].Value : "";
                        if (user.Equals("Active", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                            user.Equals("Disc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                            user.Equals("Disconnected", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            user = "";
                        sessions.Add((sid, user, m.Groups["state"].Value, m.Groups["name"].Value));
                    }
                }

                // Try short name and FQDN for qwinsta/quser
                foreach (string target in new[] { host, shortHost }.Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var psiQ = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                        {
                            FileName = "qwinsta.exe",
                            Arguments = "/server:" + target,
                            UseShellExecute = false,
                            RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                            RedirectStandardError = true,
                            CreateNoWindow = true
                        };
                        using var q = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psiQ);
                        if (q != null)
                        {
                            string raw = q.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd() + "\n" + q.StandardError.ReadToEnd();
                            q.WaitForExit(12000);
                            ParseSessionLines(raw);
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }
                    if (sessions.Count == 0)
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            var psiU = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                            {
                                FileName = "quser.exe",
                                Arguments = "/server:" + target,
                                UseShellExecute = false,
                                RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                                RedirectStandardError = true,
                                CreateNoWindow = true
                            };
                            using var u = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psiU);
                            if (u != null)
                            {
                                string raw = u.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd() + "\n" + u.StandardError.ReadToEnd();
                                u.WaitForExit(12000);
                                // quser: USERNAME SESSIONNAME ID STATE ...
                                foreach (string line in raw.Split(new[] { '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries))
                                {
                                    string t = line.TrimStart();
                                    if (t.StartsWith("USERNAME", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                                    if (t.StartsWith(">")) t = t.Substring(1).TrimStart();
                                    var tokens = t.Split((char[]?)null, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
                                    if (tokens.Length < 3) continue;
                                    // Find first integer as ID
                                    for (int i = 1; i < tokens.Length - 1; i++)
                                    {
                                        if (int.TryParse(tokens[i], out int sid) && sid > 0)
                                        {
                                            string state = tokens[i + 1];
                                            sessions.Add((sid, tokens[0], state, i > 1 ? tokens[1] : ""));
                                            break;
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }
                    if (sessions.Count > 0) break;
                }

                static string ShortUser(string u)
                {
                    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(u)) return "";
                    if (u.Contains('\\')) u = u.Split('\\').Last();
                    if (u.Contains('@')) u = u.Split('@')[0];
                    return u;
                }

                bool IsActiveState(string st) =>
                    st.Equals("Active", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    || st.Equals("Connected", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    || st.Equals("Conn", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                // Prefer forced session, then active user match, then any active
                int sessionId = -1;
                string matchInfo = "";

                if (forcedSessionId.HasValue && forcedSessionId.Value > 0)
                {
                    sessionId = forcedSessionId.Value;
                    var forced = sessions.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Id == sessionId);
                    if (forced.Id > 0)
                        matchInfo = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(forced.User) ? forced.Name : forced.User + " / " + forced.Name)
                            + " [" + forced.State + "]";
                    else
                        matchInfo = "session " + sessionId;
                }
                else
                {
                    var activeOnly = sessions.Where(s => IsShadowableSession(s.Id, s.User, s.State, s.Name)).ToList();
                    if (activeOnly.Count == 0)
                    {
                        ShowNotification("No active session to shadow on " + host + ".", "error");
                        return;
                    }
                    if (activeOnly.Count > 1 && string.IsNullOrEmpty(preferredUser))
                    {
                        // Picker on UI thread
                        var pickList = activeOnly;
                        string hostForPick = host;
                        Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() => _ = ShowShadowSessionPickerAndLaunchAsync(hostForPick, pickList));
                        return;
                    }
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(preferredUser))
                    {
                        var userMatch = activeOnly.FirstOrDefault(s =>
                            ShortUser(s.User).Equals(preferredUser, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                        if (userMatch.Id > 0)
                        {
                            sessionId = userMatch.Id;
                            matchInfo = userMatch.User + " / " + userMatch.Name;
                        }
                    }
                    if (sessionId < 0)
                    {
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(preferredUser))
                        {
                            // A specific user was requested but no active session matches
                            // them. NEVER fall back to another person's session: taking
                            // no-consent control of the wrong desktop is a serious incident.
                            ShowNotification("No active session for " + preferredUser + " on " + host
                                + ". Shadow cancelled - ADE will not shadow a different user.", "error");
                            return;
                        }
                        var anyActive = activeOnly[0];
                        sessionId = anyActive.Id;
                        matchInfo = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(anyActive.User) ? anyActive.Name : anyActive.User + " / " + anyActive.Name);
                    }
                }

                if (sessionId > 0)
                {
                    bool policyOverridden = TrySetRemoteShadowNoConsent(host, out var restoreActions);
                    // Also try short name for registry if FQDN failed
                    if (!policyOverridden && !string.Equals(host, shortHost, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        policyOverridden = TrySetRemoteShadowNoConsent(shortHost, out restoreActions);

                    // Win11 needs a brief pause so remote Shadow registry is observed
                    try { System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(600); } catch { }
                    // Prefer FQDN/short host that credentials were stored against
                    string args = "/v:" + host + " /shadow:" + sessionId + " /control /noConsentPrompt";
                    StartMstsc(args);

                    if (policyOverridden)
                    {
                        ShowNotification("Shadow RDP -> " + host + " session " + sessionId
                            + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(matchInfo) ? "" : " (" + matchInfo + ")")
                            + " | no-consent set (restores in ~90s)", "info");
                        string hostCap = host;
                        var restore = restoreActions;
                        Task.Run(async () =>
                        {
                            await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(90));
                            try { RestoreRemoteShadowValues(hostCap, restore); } catch { }
                        });
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        ShowNotification("Shadow RDP -> " + host + " session " + sessionId
                            + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(matchInfo) ? "" : " (" + matchInfo + ")")
                            + " | could not set no-consent via remote registry", "info");
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    ShowNotification("No active session to shadow on " + host + ".", "error");
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowNotification("RDP launch failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
            }
        }

        // Shadow = 2 means "Full Control without user's permission"
        private const int ShadowFullControlNoConsent = 2;
        private static readonly string[] ShadowRegPaths =
        {
            @"SOFTWARE\Policies\Microsoft\Windows NT\Terminal Services",
            @"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows NT\CurrentVersion\Terminal Server",
            @"SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\Terminal Server\WinStations\RDP-Tcp",
            @"SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\Terminal Server"
        };

        /// <summary>
        /// Temporarily set remote Shadow policy to allow no-consent control.
        /// Returns restore actions (path + previous value or delete flag).
        /// Requires admin rights on target and Remote Registry service.
        /// </summary>
        private bool TrySetRemoteShadowNoConsent(string host, out List<(string Path, object? OldValue, bool Existed)> restore)
        {
            // Local list so nested local functions do not capture the out parameter (CS1628)
            var localRestore = new List<(string Path, object? OldValue, bool Existed)>();
            bool anyOk = false;
            try
            {
                // Win11 often has Remote Registry stopped; try to enable/start it (needs admin on target)
                try
                {
                    void RunSc(string args)
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            var psi = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                            {
                                FileName = "sc.exe",
                                Arguments = args,
                                UseShellExecute = false,
                                CreateNoWindow = true,
                                RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                                RedirectStandardError = true
                            };
                            using var p = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psi);
                            p?.WaitForExit(8000);
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }
                    RunSc("\\\\" + host + " config RemoteRegistry start= demand");
                    RunSc("\\\\" + host + " start RemoteRegistry");
                    System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(400);
                }
                catch { }

                using var hklm = Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey.OpenRemoteBaseKey(
                    Microsoft.Win32.RegistryHive.LocalMachine, host);

                void SetShadowDword(string path)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        using var key = hklm.OpenSubKey(path, writable: true)
                                       ?? hklm.CreateSubKey(path, true);
                        if (key == null) return;
                        object? old = null;
                        bool existed = false;
                        try
                        {
                            old = key.GetValue("Shadow");
                            existed = old != null;
                        }
                        catch { }
                        key.SetValue("Shadow", ShadowFullControlNoConsent, Microsoft.Win32.RegistryValueKind.DWord);
                        localRestore.Add((path, old, existed));
                        anyOk = true;
                    }
                    catch { }
                }

                foreach (string path in ShadowRegPaths)
                    SetShadowDword(path);

                try
                {
                    using var stations = hklm.OpenSubKey(
                        @"SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\Terminal Server\WinStations", writable: false);
                    if (stations != null)
                    {
                        foreach (string sub in stations.GetSubKeyNames())
                        {
                            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sub)) continue;
                            string path = @"SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\Terminal Server\WinStations\" + sub;
                            SetShadowDword(path);
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch { }

                try
                {
                    SetShadowDword(@"SOFTWARE\Policies\Microsoft\Windows NT\Terminal Services\Client");
                }
                catch { }
            }
            catch
            {
                anyOk = false;
            }
            restore = localRestore;
            return anyOk;
        }

        private void RestoreRemoteShadowValues(string host, List<(string Path, object? OldValue, bool Existed)> restore)
        {
            if (restore == null || restore.Count == 0) return;
            try
            {
                using var hklm = Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey.OpenRemoteBaseKey(
                    Microsoft.Win32.RegistryHive.LocalMachine, host);
                foreach (var item in restore)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        using var key = hklm.OpenSubKey(item.Path, writable: true);
                        if (key == null) continue;
                        if (item.Existed && item.OldValue != null)
                            key.SetValue("Shadow", item.OldValue, Microsoft.Win32.RegistryValueKind.DWord);
                        else
                            key.DeleteValue("Shadow", throwOnMissingValue: false);
                    }
                    catch { }
                }
            }
            catch { }
        }

        private void LaunchVnc(string host)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host)) return;
            host = host.Trim();
            string viewer = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_vncViewerPath) ? DefaultVncViewerPath : _vncViewerPath;
            try
            {
                if (!File.Exists(viewer))
                {
                    ShowNotification("VNC viewer not found: " + viewer, "error");
                    return;
                }
                // RealVNC Viewer: vncviewer.exe <hostname>
                var psi = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = viewer,
                    Arguments = host,
                    UseShellExecute = true,
                    WorkingDirectory = Path.GetDirectoryName(viewer) ?? ""
                };
                System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psi);
                ShowNotification("Launching VNC to " + host, "info");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowNotification("VNC launch failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Half-width panel: query qwinsta for interactive sessions on the computer,
        /// list logged-on users, and offer Logoff.
        /// </summary>
        private void RenderComputerActiveSessionsPanel(StackPanel panel, string host)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host)) return;
            string resolveHost = ResolveRdpHost(host);
            string shortHost = host.Trim().TrimEnd('$');

            // Content goes into the shared outer card (same height as Actions)
            panel.Children.Add(Tb("Logged-in users", size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));
            var status = Tb("Querying sessions on " + resolveHost + "...", size: 12, opacity: 0.75);
            panel.Children.Add(status);
            var list = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4 };
            var listScroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Content = list,
                MaxHeight = 280,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled,
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 8, 0)
            };
            panel.Children.Add(listScroll);
            var refreshBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Refresh sessions",
                Classes = { "action" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0)
            };
            panel.Children.Add(refreshBtn);

            void LoadSessions()
            {
                list.Children.Clear();
                status.Text = "Querying sessions on " + resolveHost + "...";
                refreshBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                string hostCap = resolveHost;
                string shortCap = shortHost;
                Task.Run(() =>
                {
                    var sessions = QueryRemoteSessions(hostCap);
                    if (sessions.Count == 0 && !string.Equals(hostCap, shortCap, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        sessions = QueryRemoteSessions(shortCap);

                    Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
                    {
                        refreshBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                        list.Children.Clear();
                        // Only interactive user sessions (skip services / Listen)
                        var users = sessions
                            .Where(s => s.Id > 0
                                && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.User)
                                && !s.State.Equals("Listen", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            .OrderBy(s => s.User, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            .ToList();

                        if (users.Count == 0)
                        {
                            status.Text = sessions.Count == 0
                                ? "Could not query sessions (offline, or access denied)."
                                : "No users currently logged in.";
                            return;
                        }

                        status.Text = users.Count + " user session(s) on " + hostCap;
                        foreach (var s in users)
                        {
                            var row = new Border
                            {
                                Padding = new Thickness(8, 6),
                                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(4),
                                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(28, 128, 128, 128)),
                                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                            };
                            var rowBody = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4 };
                            var top = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Spacing = 8 };
                            top.Children.Add(Tb(s.User, size: 13, weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center, fontFamily: new FontFamily("Consolas")));
                            string st = (s.State ?? "").ToUpperInvariant();
                            bool ok = st.Contains("ACTIVE") || st.Contains("CONN");
                            top.Children.Add(MakeBadge(string.IsNullOrEmpty(st) ? "SESSION" : st, ok ? "statusOk" : "statusWarn"));
                            rowBody.Children.Add(top);
                            rowBody.Children.Add(Tb("Session " + s.Id
                                    + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(s.Name) ? "" : "  |  " + s.Name), size: 11, opacity: 0.7));

                            int sid = s.Id;
                            string userCap = s.User;
                            string serverCap = hostCap;
                            var sessBtnRow = new StackPanel
                            {
                                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                                Spacing = 8,
                                Margin = new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 0)
                            };
                            var msgBtn = new Button
                            {
                                Content = "Message",
                                Classes = { "action" },
                                Padding = new Thickness(10, 4),
                                FontSize = 12
                            };
                            ToolTip.SetTip(msgBtn, "Send a popup message to this user on " + serverCap);
                            msgBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
                            {
                                string? text = await PromptTextDialogAsync(
                                    "Message " + userCap,
                                    "Message to display on " + serverCap + " for " + userCap + ":");
                                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text)) return;
                                msgBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                                string result = await Task.Run(() => SendRemoteMessage(serverCap, userCap, text.Trim()));
                                msgBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                                if (result.StartsWith("OK", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                    ShowNotification("Message sent to " + userCap, "success");
                                else
                                    ShowNotification("Message failed: " + result, "error");
                            };
                            var logoffBtn = new Button
                            {
                                Content = "Logoff",
                                Classes = { "action" },
                                Padding = new Thickness(10, 4),
                                FontSize = 12
                            };
                            logoffBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
                            {
                                bool yes = await ConfirmDialogAsync(
                                    "Confirm logoff",
                                    "Log off " + userCap + " from " + serverCap + " (session " + sid + ")?\n\nUnsaved work may be lost.");
                                if (!yes) return;
                                logoffBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                                ShowNotification("Logging off " + userCap + "...", "info");
                                string result = await Task.Run(() => LogoffRemoteSession(serverCap, sid));
                                if (result.StartsWith("OK", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                {
                                    ShowNotification("Logged off " + userCap + " from " + serverCap, "success");
                                    await Task.Delay(1500);
                                    LoadSessions();
                                }
                                else
                                {
                                    logoffBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                                    ShowNotification("Logoff failed: " + result, "error");
                                }
                            };
                            sessBtnRow.Children.Add(msgBtn);
                            sessBtnRow.Children.Add(logoffBtn);
                            rowBody.Children.Add(sessBtnRow);
                            row.Child = rowBody;
                            list.Children.Add(row);
                        }
                    });
                });
            }

            refreshBtn.Click += (_, _) => LoadSessions();
            LoadSessions();
        }

        private List<(int Id, string User, string State, string Name)> QueryRemoteSessions(string host)
        {
            var sessions = new List<(int Id, string User, string State, string Name)>();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host)) return sessions;

            void Parse(string raw)
            {
                foreach (string line in (raw ?? "").Split(new[] { '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries))
                {
                    string t = line.TrimStart();
                    if (t.Length == 0) continue;
                    if (t.StartsWith("SESSIONNAME", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                    if (t.StartsWith("USERNAME", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                    if (t.StartsWith(">")) t = t.Substring(1).TrimStart();

                    var m = System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Match(
                        t,
                        @"^(?<name>\S*)\s+(?:(?<user>\S+)\s+)?(?<id>\d+)\s+(?<state>Active|Disc|Disconnected|Listen|Conn|Connected|Idle)",
                        System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
                    if (m.Success)
                    {
                        int sid = int.Parse(m.Groups["id"].Value);
                        if (sid <= 0) continue;
                        string user = m.Groups["user"].Success ? m.Groups["user"].Value : "";
                        if (user.Equals("Active", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            || user.Equals("Disc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            || user.Equals("Disconnected", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            || user.Equals("Listen", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            user = "";
                        sessions.Add((sid, user, m.Groups["state"].Value, m.Groups["name"].Value));
                        continue;
                    }

                    var tokens = t.Split((char[]?)null, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
                    int stateIdx = -1;
                    for (int i = 0; i < tokens.Length; i++)
                    {
                        string s = tokens[i];
                        if (s.Equals("Active", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            || s.Equals("Disc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            || s.Equals("Disconnected", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            || s.Equals("Listen", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            || s.Equals("Conn", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            || s.Equals("Connected", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            || s.Equals("Idle", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        { stateIdx = i; break; }
                    }
                    if (stateIdx < 1) continue;
                    if (!int.TryParse(tokens[stateIdx - 1], out int sidFb) || sidFb <= 0) continue;
                    string userFb = "";
                    if (stateIdx >= 2 && !int.TryParse(tokens[stateIdx - 2], out _))
                        userFb = tokens[stateIdx - 2];
                    sessions.Add((sidFb, userFb, tokens[stateIdx], tokens[0]));
                }
            }

            try
            {
                var psi = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = "qwinsta.exe",
                    Arguments = "/server:" + host,
                    UseShellExecute = false,
                    RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                    RedirectStandardError = true,
                    CreateNoWindow = true
                };
                using var q = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psi);
                if (q != null)
                {
                    string raw = q.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd() + "\n" + q.StandardError.ReadToEnd();
                    q.WaitForExit(15000);
                    Parse(raw);
                }
            }
            catch { }

            if (sessions.Count == 0)
            {
                try
                {
                    var psi = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = "quser.exe",
                        Arguments = "/server:" + host,
                        UseShellExecute = false,
                        RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                        RedirectStandardError = true,
                        CreateNoWindow = true
                    };
                    using var u = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psi);
                    if (u != null)
                    {
                        string raw = u.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd() + "\n" + u.StandardError.ReadToEnd();
                        u.WaitForExit(15000);
                        foreach (string line in raw.Split(new[] { '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries))
                        {
                            string t = line.TrimStart();
                            if (t.StartsWith("USERNAME", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                            if (t.StartsWith(">")) t = t.Substring(1).TrimStart();
                            var tokens = t.Split((char[]?)null, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
                            if (tokens.Length < 3) continue;
                            for (int i = 1; i < tokens.Length - 1; i++)
                            {
                                if (int.TryParse(tokens[i], out int sid) && sid > 0)
                                {
                                    sessions.Add((sid, tokens[0], tokens[i + 1], i > 1 ? tokens[1] : ""));
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch { }
            }
            return sessions;
        }

        private static bool IsShadowableSession(int id, string user, string state, string name)
        {
            if (id <= 0) return false;
            // Session 0 / services / listeners are not interactive
            string nm = (name ?? "").Trim();
            string st = (state ?? "").Trim();
            string us = (user ?? "").Trim();
            if (nm.Equals("services", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return false;
            if (nm.Equals("rdp-tcp", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(us)) return false;
            if (st.Equals("Listen", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return false;
            if (st.Equals("Conn", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(us)) return false;
            // Only Active / Connected are safe to shadow (Disc causes "session is not connected")
            if (!(st.Equals("Active", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                  || st.Equals("Connected", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                return false;
            return true;
        }

        private List<(int Id, string User, string State, string Name)> GetActiveRemoteSessions(string host)
        {
            var all = QueryRemoteSessions(host);
            string shortHost = (host ?? "").Trim();
            if (shortHost.Contains('.'))
                shortHost = shortHost.Split('.')[0];
            if (all.Count == 0 && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(shortHost)
                && !string.Equals(shortHost, host, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                all = QueryRemoteSessions(shortHost);

            var active = new List<(int Id, string User, string State, string Name)>();
            foreach (var s in all)
            {
                if (IsShadowableSession(s.Id, s.User, s.State, s.Name))
                    active.Add(s);
            }
            return active;
        }

        private string SendRemoteMessage(string host, string username, string message)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(username) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(message))
                return "Host, user, and message are required.";
            try
            {
                // msg.exe username /server:host message text
                // Quote message for cmd parsing
                string msgArg = message.Replace("\"", "'");
                var psi = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = "msg.exe",
                    Arguments = username + " /server:" + host + " " + msgArg,
                    UseShellExecute = false,
                    RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                    RedirectStandardError = true,
                    CreateNoWindow = true
                };
                using var p = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psi);
                if (p == null) return "Failed to start msg.exe";
                string stdout = p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd();
                string stderr = p.StandardError.ReadToEnd();
                p.WaitForExit(15000);
                if (p.ExitCode == 0) return "OK";
                string err = (stderr + " " + stdout).Trim();
                return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(err) ? ("msg exit " + p.ExitCode) : err;
            }
            catch (Exception ex) { return ex.Message; }
        }

        private string LogoffRemoteSession(string host, int sessionId)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host) || sessionId <= 0)
                return "Invalid host or session id.";
            try
            {
                // logoff <id> /server:<host>
                var psi = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = "logoff.exe",
                    Arguments = sessionId + " /server:" + host,
                    UseShellExecute = false,
                    RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                    RedirectStandardError = true,
                    CreateNoWindow = true
                };
                using var p = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psi);
                if (p == null) return "Failed to start logoff.";
                string stdout = p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd();
                string stderr = p.StandardError.ReadToEnd();
                p.WaitForExit(30000);
                if (p.ExitCode == 0)
                    return "OK";
                // Fallback: rwinsta (reset session)
                var psi2 = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = "rwinsta.exe",
                    Arguments = sessionId + " /server:" + host,
                    UseShellExecute = false,
                    RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                    RedirectStandardError = true,
                    CreateNoWindow = true
                };
                using var p2 = System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(psi2);
                if (p2 == null)
                    return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(stderr) ? stdout : stderr;
                string err2 = p2.StandardError.ReadToEnd() + p2.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd();
                p2.WaitForExit(30000);
                if (p2.ExitCode == 0) return "OK";
                string msg = (stderr + " " + stdout + " " + err2).Trim();
                return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(msg) ? ("exit code " + p.ExitCode) : msg;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return ex.Message;
            }
        }

        private static string FormatUptimeSpan(TimeSpan span)
        {
            if (span.TotalSeconds < 0) span = TimeSpan.Zero;
            if (span.TotalDays >= 1)
            {
                int d = (int)span.TotalDays;
                return d + " day" + (d == 1 ? "" : "s") + " "
                    + span.Hours + " hr" + (span.Hours == 1 ? "" : "s");
            }
            if (span.TotalHours >= 1)
                return (int)span.TotalHours + " hr "
                    + span.Minutes + " min";
            if (span.TotalMinutes >= 1)
                return (int)span.TotalMinutes + " min";
            return Math.Max(0, (int)span.TotalSeconds) + " sec";
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Remote uptime via WMI Win32_OperatingSystem.LastBootUpTime (uses RDP admin creds when set).
        /// </summary>
        private string QueryRemoteUptimeText(string host)
        {
            host = (host ?? "").Trim();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(host)) return "";

            string? QueryOne(string target, bool withCreds)
            {
                try
                {
                    var opts = new System.Management.ConnectionOptions
                    {
                        EnablePrivileges = true,
                        Impersonation = System.Management.ImpersonationLevel.Impersonate,
                        Authentication = System.Management.AuthenticationLevel.PacketPrivacy,
                        Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3)
                    };
                    if (withCreds)
                    {
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_rdpUsername) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_rdpPassword))
                            return null;
                        opts.Username = _rdpUsername;
                        opts.Password = _rdpPassword;
                    }

                    var scope = new System.Management.ManagementScope(@"\\" + target + @"\root\cimv2", opts);
                    scope.Options.Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3);
                    scope.Connect();
                    var query = new System.Management.ObjectQuery("SELECT LastBootUpTime FROM Win32_OperatingSystem");
                    using var searcher = new System.Management.ManagementObjectSearcher(scope, query);
                    searcher.Options.Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3);
                    foreach (System.Management.ManagementObject mo in searcher.Get())
                    {
                        string dmtf = mo["LastBootUpTime"]?.ToString() ?? "";
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dmtf)) continue;
                        DateTime boot = System.Management.ManagementDateTimeConverter.ToDateTime(dmtf);
                        var span = DateTime.Now - boot;
                        return FormatUptimeSpan(span) + "  (since " + boot.ToString("g") + ")";
                    }
                }
                catch { }
                return null;
            }

            foreach (string target in new[] { host, host.Contains('.') ? host.Split('.')[0] : host }
                .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(target)) continue;
                string? text = QueryOne(target, withCreds: true);
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text)) return text!;
                text = QueryOne(target, withCreds: false);
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text)) return text!;
            }
            return "";
        }

        private static (bool Ok, long Ms, string Detail) TryPingHost(string host, int timeoutMs = 1500)
        {
            try
            {
                using var p = new System.Net.NetworkInformation.Ping();
                var reply = p.Send(host, timeoutMs);
                if (reply != null && reply.Status == System.Net.NetworkInformation.IPStatus.Success)
                    return (true, reply.RoundtripTime, reply.Address?.ToString() ?? "");
                string st = reply?.Status.ToString() ?? "no reply";
                return (false, 0, st);
            }
            catch (Exception ex) { return (false, 0, ex.Message); }
        }

    }
}
'@
$mwGroupCs = @'
// ADE - MainWindow partial class: group detail page - membership, member-of, exports, rename, remove undo.
// Split from MainWindow.axaml.cs for maintainability (2026-10-26):
// same partial class, same namespace - no behavior change.
using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.DirectoryServices;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Net.Security;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Security.Principal;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Avalonia;
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Controls.Primitives;
using Avalonia.Input;
using Avalonia.Interactivity;
using Avalonia.Layout;
using Avalonia.Markup.Xaml;
using Avalonia.Media;
using Avalonia.Threading;

namespace __APPNAME__
{
    public partial class MainWindow : Window
    {
        private void RenderGroupMemberManagement(StackPanel panel, DirectoryEntry de, AdNode node)
        {
            var members = GetAllMultiValues(de, "member");
            try
            {
                var sidBytes = de.Properties["objectSid"]?.Value as byte[];
                if (sidBytes != null && sidBytes.Length >= 8)
                {
                    int rid = sidBytes[sidBytes.Length - 4]
                            | (sidBytes[sidBytes.Length - 3] << 8)
                            | (sidBytes[sidBytes.Length - 2] << 16)
                            | (sidBytes[sidBytes.Length - 1] << 24);
                    using var searcher = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + _domainDn))
                    {
                        Filter = $"(&(objectCategory=person)(objectClass=user)(primaryGroupID={rid}))",
                        SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                        PageSize = 500,
                        PropertiesToLoad = { "distinguishedName", "cn", "sAMAccountName", "name", "objectClass" }
                    };
                    using var results = searcher.FindAll();
                    foreach (SearchResult sr in results)
                    {
                        string dn = (Prop(sr, "distinguishedName"));
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn) && !members.Any(m => string.Equals(m, dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                            members.Add(dn);
                    }
                }
            }
            catch { }

            string groupDn = node.Dn;
            string groupName = node.Name;

            // Dn, DisplayName, Sam, Given, Surname, IsGroup, SearchBlob
            // Resolved in parallel: one sequential LDAP bind per member made
            // big groups crawl. Each iteration uses its own DirectoryEntry,
            // and results land in per-index slots, so order is preserved.
            var memberArr = new (string Dn, string Display, string Sam, string Given, string Surname, bool IsGroup, string SearchBlob)[members.Count];
            Parallel.For(0, members.Count, new ParallelOptions { MaxDegreeOfParallelism = 16 }, i =>
            {
                string mDn = members[i];
                string display = mDn;
                string sam = "";
                string given = "";
                string surname = "";
                bool isGroup = false;
                try
                {
                    using var mde = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + mDn);
                    mde.RefreshCache(new[] { "objectClass", "cn", "name", "displayName", "sAMAccountName", "givenName", "sn" });
                    display = mde.Properties["displayName"]?.Value?.ToString()
                           ?? mde.Properties["name"]?.Value?.ToString()
                           ?? mde.Properties["cn"]?.Value?.ToString()
                           ?? mDn;
                    sam = mde.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                    given = mde.Properties["givenName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                    surname = mde.Properties["sn"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                    if (mde.Properties.Contains("objectClass"))
                        foreach (object oc in mde.Properties["objectClass"])
                            if (string.Equals(oc?.ToString(), "group", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            { isGroup = true; break; }
                }
                catch
                {
                    var cn = mDn.Split(',').FirstOrDefault(p => p.StartsWith("CN=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                    if (cn != null) display = cn.Substring(3);
                }
                string blob = string.Join(" ", display, sam, given, surname, mDn);
                memberArr[i] = (mDn, display, sam, given, surname, isGroup, blob);
            });
            var memberInfo = memberArr.OrderBy(x => x.IsGroup ? 0 : 1).ThenBy(x => x.Display, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();

            var selectedMap = new Dictionary<string, (string Display, string Sam, bool IsGroup, CheckBox Cb)>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            var selectedList = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4 };
            var selectedHeader = new TextBlock { Text = "Selected (0)", FontSize = 15, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold };
            var memberRows = new List<(string Dn, string Display, string Sam, string SearchBlob, bool IsGroup, Border Row, CheckBox Cb)>();
            var memberStack = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };

            void RefreshSelectedPanel()
            {
                selectedList.Children.Clear();
                selectedHeader.Text = $"Selected ({selectedMap.Count})";
                foreach (var kv in selectedMap.OrderBy(k => k.Value.Display, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    string iconType = kv.Value.IsGroup ? "Group" : "User";
                    string label = kv.Value.Display;
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(kv.Value.Sam))
                        label += "  (" + kv.Value.Sam + ")";
                    var row = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto") };
                    row.Children.Add(MakeIconLine(iconType, label, 14, 13));
                    var uncheck = new Button
                    {
                        Content = MakeMdi("mdi-close", 12),
                        Padding = new Thickness(6, 2),
                        Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                        BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                        FontSize = 12
                    };
                    string dnKey = kv.Key;
                    uncheck.Click += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        if (selectedMap.TryGetValue(dnKey, out var info))
                        {
                            info.Cb.IsChecked = false;
                            selectedMap.Remove(dnKey);
                            RefreshSelectedPanel();
                        }
                    };
                    Grid.SetColumn(uncheck, 1);
                    row.Children.Add(uncheck);
                    selectedList.Children.Add(new Border
                    {
                        Child = row,
                        Padding = new Thickness(4, 3),
                        CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(4),
                        Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(40, 128, 128, 128))
                    });
                }
            }

            void ApplyMemberFilter(string q)
            {
                q = (q ?? "").Trim();
                foreach (var row in memberRows)
                {
                    bool match = q.Length == 0 ||
                        row.SearchBlob.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
                    row.Row.IsVisible = match;
                }
            }

            // ---- ADD MEMBERS (top) ----
            var addCard = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Padding = new Thickness(10),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            var addInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };
            addInner.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Add members", FontSize = 16, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold });
            addInner.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Live search users or groups (username, first, last, or group name). Click a result to add (shows 3).", FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.75, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap });
            var addBox = new TextBox
            {
                Watermark = "Search users or groups to add...",
                MinHeight = 34,
                FontSize = 14,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            var addSuggest = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };
            var addSuggestHost = new Border
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(4),
                IsVisible = false,
                MinHeight = 40,
                MaxHeight = 160,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(100, 140, 140, 160)),
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(40, 80, 80, 100)),
                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(6),
                Child = new ScrollViewer
                {
                    Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0),
                    AllowAutoHide = false,
                    Content = addSuggest,
                    MaxHeight = 150,
                    VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto
                }
            };
            addBox.TextChanged += async (_, _) =>
            {
                string raw = addBox.Text?.Trim() ?? "";
                addSuggest.Children.Clear();
                if (raw.Length < 1)
                {
                    addSuggestHost.IsVisible = false;
                    return;
                }
                try
                {
                    string esc = EscapeLdap(raw);
                    // LDAP on a background thread so typing never blocks the UI;
                    // stale results are dropped if the text moved on.
                    var hits = await Task.Run(() =>
                    {
                        var found = new List<(string Dn, string Name, string Sam, bool IsGroup)>();

                        // Search groups
                        try
                        {
                            using var gSearcher = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + _domainDn))
                            {
                                Filter = $"(&(objectClass=group)(|(sAMAccountName={esc}*)(cn={esc}*)(name={esc}*)))",
                                SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                                SizeLimit = 10,
                                PropertiesToLoad = { "distinguishedName", "cn", "sAMAccountName", "name", "displayName" }
                            };
                            using var gResults = gSearcher.FindAll();
                            foreach (SearchResult sr in gResults)
                            {
                                string dn = (Prop(sr, "distinguishedName"));
                                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn)) continue;
                                if (string.Equals(dn, groupDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                                if (members.Any(m => string.Equals(m, dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))) continue;
                                if (found.Any(h => string.Equals(h.Dn, dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))) continue;
                                string name = sr.Properties["cn"]?[0]?.ToString()
                                           ?? sr.Properties["name"]?[0]?.ToString()
                                           ?? dn;
                                string sam = (Prop(sr, "sAMAccountName"));
                                found.Add((dn, name, sam, true));
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }

                        // Search users
                        try
                        {
                            using var uSearcher = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + _domainDn))
                            {
                                Filter = $"(&(objectClass=user)(objectCategory=person)(|(sAMAccountName={esc}*)(cn={esc}*)(givenName={esc}*)(sn={esc}*)(displayName={esc}*)))",
                                SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                                SizeLimit = 10,
                                PropertiesToLoad = { "distinguishedName", "cn", "sAMAccountName", "name", "displayName", "givenName", "sn" }
                            };
                            using var uResults = uSearcher.FindAll();
                            foreach (SearchResult sr in uResults)
                            {
                                string dn = (Prop(sr, "distinguishedName"));
                                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn)) continue;
                                if (members.Any(m => string.Equals(m, dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))) continue;
                                if (found.Any(h => string.Equals(h.Dn, dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))) continue;
                                string name = sr.Properties["displayName"]?[0]?.ToString()
                                           ?? sr.Properties["cn"]?[0]?.ToString()
                                           ?? sr.Properties["name"]?[0]?.ToString()
                                           ?? dn;
                                string sam = (Prop(sr, "sAMAccountName"));
                                found.Add((dn, name, sam, false));
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }

                        return found;
                    });
                    if (!string.Equals(addBox.Text?.Trim() ?? "", raw, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                        return;
                    int shown = 0;
                    foreach (var hit in hits.OrderBy(h => h.IsGroup ? 0 : 1).ThenBy(h => h.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        if (shown >= 3) break;
                        string iconType = hit.IsGroup ? "Group" : "User";
                        string kind = hit.IsGroup ? "Group" : "User";
                        var content = new StackPanel { Spacing = 1 };
                        content.Children.Add(MakeIconLine(iconType, hit.Name + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hit.Sam) ? "" : "  (" + hit.Sam + ")"), 14, 13, bold: true));
                        content.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = hit.Dn, FontSize = 11, Opacity = 0.7, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap });
                        var btn = new Button
                        {
                            Content = content,
                            Classes = { "suggestItem" },
                            HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                            HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left
                        };
                        string capDn = hit.Dn;
                        string capCn = hit.Name;
                        bool capGroup = hit.IsGroup;
                        btn.Click += (_, _) =>
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                using var group = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + groupDn);
                                using var target = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + capDn);
                                group.Invoke("Add", new object[] { target.Path });
                                group.CommitChanges();
                                ShowNotification($"Added {(capGroup ? "group" : "user")} \"{capCn}\" to {groupName}", "success");
                                addBox.Text = "";
                                addSuggest.Children.Clear();
                                addSuggestHost.IsVisible = false;
                                ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                            }
                            catch (Exception ex)
                            {
                                string msg = ex.Message ?? "";
                                if (msg.IndexOf("already", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                                    ShowNotification($"\"{capCn}\" is already a member of this group.", "error");
                                else
                                    ShowNotification("Add failed: " + msg, "error");
                            }
                        };
                        addSuggest.Children.Add(btn);
                        shown++;
                    }
                    addSuggestHost.IsVisible = shown > 0;
                    if (shown == 0)
                    {
                        addSuggest.Children.Add(Tb("No matching users or groups found (or already members).", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, margin: new Thickness(6)));
                        addSuggestHost.IsVisible = true;
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    addSuggest.Children.Clear();
                    addSuggest.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Search error: " + ex.Message, FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.8, Margin = new Thickness(6) });
                    addSuggestHost.IsVisible = true;
                }
            };
            addInner.Children.Add(addBox);
            addInner.Children.Add(addSuggestHost);
            addCard.Child = addInner;

            // ---- Members (left card) | Selected + Actions (right card) ----
            var topSplit = new Grid
            {
                ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,12,*"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top
            };

            // LEFT card: Members
            var leftInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            leftInner.Children.Add(Tb($"Members ({memberInfo.Count})", size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));
            var memberFilter = new TextBox
            {
                Watermark = "Filter by username, first, or last name...",
                MinHeight = 34,
                FontSize = 14,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            memberFilter.TextChanged += (_, _) => ApplyMemberFilter(memberFilter.Text ?? "");
            leftInner.Children.Add(memberFilter);

            if (memberInfo.Count == 0)
            {
                memberStack.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "(no members or insufficient rights)", Opacity = 0.7 });
            }
            else
            {
                int rowIdx = 0;
                foreach (var item in memberInfo)
                {
                    string mDn = item.Dn;
                    string display = item.Display;
                    string sam = item.Sam;
                    bool isGroup = item.IsGroup;
                    string label = display;
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sam))
                        label += "  (" + sam + ")";
                    var cb = new CheckBox
                    {
                        Content = MakeIconLine(isGroup ? "Group" : "User", label, 14, 13),
                        FontSize = 13,
                        Margin = new Thickness(4, 2),
                        HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                    };
                    cb.IsCheckedChanged += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        if (cb.IsChecked == true)
                            selectedMap[mDn] = (display, sam, isGroup, cb);
                        else
                            selectedMap.Remove(mDn);
                        RefreshSelectedPanel();
                    };
                    var rowBorder = new Border
                    {
                        Child = cb,
                        Padding = new Thickness(2, 1),
                        CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(4),
                        Background = (rowIdx++ % 2 == 0) ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(28, 128, 128, 128)) : Brushes.Transparent
                    };
                    string openDn = mDn;
                    rowBorder.DoubleTapped += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            using var mde = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + openDn);
                            string name = mde.Properties["name"]?.Value?.ToString()
                                       ?? mde.Properties["cn"]?.Value?.ToString()
                                       ?? openDn;
                            string type = "User";
                            if (mde.Properties.Contains("objectClass"))
                                foreach (object oc in mde.Properties["objectClass"])
                                    if (string.Equals(oc?.ToString(), "group", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { type = "Group"; break; }
                                    else if (string.Equals(oc?.ToString(), "computer", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { type = "Computer"; break; }
                            ShowHelpDeskInfo(new AdNode { Name = name, Dn = openDn, Type = type });
                        }
                        catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Open failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
                    };
                    memberStack.Children.Add(rowBorder);
                    memberRows.Add((mDn, display, sam, item.SearchBlob, isGroup, rowBorder, cb));
                }
            }

            // Member list fills remaining space; final height synced to right card (Actions bottom)
            var memberScroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0),
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                Content = memberStack,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Visible,
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
            };
            var memberListBox = new Border
            {
                Child = memberScroll,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(100, 140, 140, 160)),
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(30, 80, 80, 100)),
                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(6),
                Padding = new Thickness(4),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch
            };

            var leftGrid = new Grid
            {
                RowDefinitions = new RowDefinitions("Auto,Auto,*"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            var leftTitle = leftInner.Children[0];
            var leftFilterCtrl = leftInner.Children[1];
            leftInner.Children.Clear();
            Grid.SetRow(leftTitle, 0);
            Grid.SetRow(leftFilterCtrl, 1);
            Grid.SetRow(memberListBox, 2);
            leftGrid.Children.Add(leftTitle);
            leftGrid.Children.Add(leftFilterCtrl);
            leftGrid.Children.Add(memberListBox);

            var leftCard = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Child = leftGrid,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
                Padding = new Thickness(12),
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1)
            };

            // RIGHT card: Grid so Actions stay pinned to bottom-right always
            var rightGrid = new Grid
            {
                RowDefinitions = new RowDefinitions("Auto,Auto"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top
            };

            var selectedBlock = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            selectedBlock.Children.Add(selectedHeader);
            selectedBlock.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Checked users and nested groups appear here.", FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.75, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap });
            var selectedScroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0),
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                Content = selectedList,
                Height = 160,
                MaxHeight = 160,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Visible,
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
            };
            var selectedListBox = new Border
            {
                Child = selectedScroll,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(100, 140, 140, 160)),
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(30, 80, 80, 100)),
                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(6),
                Padding = new Thickness(4),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Height = 168,
                MaxHeight = 168,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top
            };
            selectedBlock.Children.Add(selectedListBox);
            Grid.SetRow(selectedBlock, 0);
            rightGrid.Children.Add(selectedBlock);

            // Actions pinned to bottom of right card
            var actionsBar = new Border
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 10, 0, 0),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Bottom,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(0, 1, 0, 0),
                BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(60, 128, 128, 128))
            };
            var actionsInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            actionsInner.Children.Add(Tb("Actions", size: 14, weight: FontWeight.Bold, margin: new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));
            var opStatus = Tb("", size: 12, opacity: 0.9, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4));
            var removeBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Remove selected from group",
                Classes = { "action" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                FontSize = 13
            };
            var stopBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Emergency stop",
                Classes = { "action" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                FontSize = 13,
                IsEnabled = false
            };
            var undoBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Undo last remove",
                Classes = { "action" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                FontSize = 13
            };
            // Enable undo if a matching undo record exists for this group
            try
            {
                var pending = LoadGroupRemoveUndo();
                undoBtn.IsEnabled = pending != null && string.Equals(pending.GroupDn, groupDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && pending.Members.Count > 0;
                if (undoBtn.IsEnabled)
                    undoBtn.Content = $"Undo last remove ({pending!.Members.Count})";
            }
            catch { undoBtn.IsEnabled = false; }

            stopBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                try { _groupOpCts?.Cancel(); } catch { }
                opStatus.Text = "Stop requested - finishing current item...";
                ShowNotification("Emergency stop requested.", "info");
            };

            removeBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                if (selectedMap.Count == 0) { ShowNotification("No members selected.", "info"); return; }
                var snapshot = selectedMap.Select(kv => (Dn: kv.Key, Display: kv.Value.Display, Sam: kv.Value.Sam)).ToList();
                int total = snapshot.Count;
                removeBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                undoBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                stopBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                _groupOpCts?.Cancel();
                _groupOpCts = new CancellationTokenSource();
                var token = _groupOpCts.Token;
                int ok = 0, fail = 0, skipped = 0;
                var removed = new List<(string Dn, string Display)>();
                var failures = new List<string>();
                try
                {
                    using var group = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + groupDn);
                    int i = 0;
                    foreach (var item in snapshot)
                    {
                        i++;
                        if (token.IsCancellationRequested)
                        {
                            skipped = total - i + 1;
                            opStatus.Text = $"Stopped. Removed {ok}/{total}. Skipped {skipped}.";
                            break;
                        }
                        opStatus.Text = $"Removing {i} of {total}: {item.Display}" + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Sam) ? "" : $" ({item.Sam})");
                        await Task.Delay(15); // yield so UI/status/stop can update
                        try
                        {
                            group.Invoke("Remove", new object[] { "LDAP://" + item.Dn });
                            ok++;
