                            removed.Add((item.Dn, item.Display));
                        }
                        catch (Exception ex)
                        {
                            fail++;
                            failures.Add(item.Display + ": " + (ex.Message ?? "error"));
                        }
                    }
                    string commitError = "";
                    try { group.CommitChanges(); }
                    catch (Exception cex) { commitError = cex.Message; }
                    if (removed.Count > 0)
                    {
                        SaveGroupRemoveUndo(new GroupRemoveUndo
                        {
                            Timestamp = DateTime.UtcNow,
                            GroupDn = groupDn,
                            GroupName = groupName,
                            Members = removed.Select(r => new GroupRemoveUndoMember { Dn = r.Dn, Display = r.Display }).ToList()
                        });
                        undoBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                        undoBtn.Content = $"Undo last remove ({removed.Count})";
                    }
                    string msg = $"Removed {ok} of {total} from {groupName}";
                    if (fail > 0) msg += $", {fail} failed";
                    if (skipped > 0) msg += $", {skipped} skipped (stopped)";
                    if (commitError.Length > 0)
                    {
                        msg += " (warning: the final commit reported an error - re-open the group to verify)";
                        ShowNotification("Commit warning: " + commitError, "error");
                    }
                    opStatus.Text = msg;
                    ShowNotification(msg, ok > 0 && fail == 0 && skipped == 0 ? "success" : "info");
                    if (failures.Count > 0 && failures.Count <= 5)
                        foreach (var f in failures) ShowNotification(f, "error");
                    else if (failures.Count > 5)
                        ShowNotification(failures.Count + " removals failed - see status.", "error");
                    if (ok > 0 || skipped > 0)
                        ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    opStatus.Text = "Remove failed: " + ex.Message;
                    ShowNotification("Remove failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                }
                finally
                {
                    removeBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                    stopBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                }
            };

            undoBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                var pending = LoadGroupRemoveUndo();
                if (pending == null || pending.Members.Count == 0)
                {
                    ShowNotification("Nothing to undo.", "info");
                    undoBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                    return;
                }
                if (!string.Equals(pending.GroupDn, groupDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    ShowNotification($"Last remove was on a different group ({pending.GroupName}). Open that group to undo.", "info");
                    return;
                }
                removeBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                undoBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                stopBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                _groupOpCts?.Cancel();
                _groupOpCts = new CancellationTokenSource();
                var token = _groupOpCts.Token;
                int ok = 0, fail = 0, skipped = 0;
                int total = pending.Members.Count;
                var remaining = new List<GroupRemoveUndoMember>();
                try
                {
                    using var group = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + groupDn);
                    int i = 0;
                    foreach (var m in pending.Members)
                    {
                        i++;
                        if (token.IsCancellationRequested)
                        {
                            skipped = total - i + 1;
                            // Keep everything not yet attempted for the next try
                            for (int k = i - 1; k < pending.Members.Count; k++)
                                remaining.Add(pending.Members[k]);
                            break;
                        }
                        opStatus.Text = $"Undoing {i} of {total}: re-adding {m.Display}";
                        await Task.Delay(15);
                        try
                        {
                            group.Invoke("Add", new object[] { "LDAP://" + m.Dn });
                            ok++;
                        }
                        catch { fail++; remaining.Add(m); }
                    }
                    string commitError = "";
                    try { group.CommitChanges(); }
                    catch (Exception cex) { commitError = cex.Message; }
                    if (remaining.Count == 0)
                        ClearGroupRemoveUndo();
                    else if (remaining.Count != total || fail > 0)
                    {
                        // Keep exactly the members that failed or were never attempted
                        pending.Members = remaining;
                        SaveGroupRemoveUndo(pending);
                    }
                    string msg = $"Undo: restored {ok} of {total} to {groupName}";
                    if (fail > 0) msg += $", {fail} failed";
                    if (skipped > 0) msg += $", {skipped} skipped (stopped)";
                    if (commitError.Length > 0)
                    {
                        msg += " (warning: the final commit reported an error - re-open the group to verify)";
                        ShowNotification("Commit warning: " + commitError, "error");
                    }
                    opStatus.Text = msg;
                    ShowNotification(msg, ok > 0 ? "success" : "error");
                    ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    opStatus.Text = "Undo failed: " + ex.Message;
                    ShowNotification("Undo failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                }
                finally
                {
                    removeBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                    stopBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                }
            };

            var selectAllBtn = new Button { Content = "Select all", Classes = { "action" }, FontSize = 13, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            selectAllBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                foreach (var row in memberRows)
                    if (row.Row.IsVisible)
                        row.Cb.IsChecked = true;
            };
            var clearBtn = new Button { Content = "Clear selection", Classes = { "action" }, FontSize = 13, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            clearBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                foreach (var kv in selectedMap.Values.ToList())
                    kv.Cb.IsChecked = false;
                selectedMap.Clear();
                RefreshSelectedPanel();
            };
            actionsInner.Children.Add(opStatus);
            actionsInner.Children.Add(removeBtn);
            actionsInner.Children.Add(stopBtn);
            actionsInner.Children.Add(undoBtn);
            actionsInner.Children.Add(selectAllBtn);
            actionsInner.Children.Add(clearBtn);
            actionsBar.Child = actionsInner;
            Grid.SetRow(actionsBar, 1);
            rightGrid.Children.Add(actionsBar);

            var rightCard = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Child = rightGrid,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
                Padding = new Thickness(12),
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 16)
            };

            Grid.SetColumn(leftCard, 0);
            Grid.SetColumn(rightCard, 2);
            topSplit.Children.Add(leftCard);
            topSplit.Children.Add(rightCard);

            // Keep left card height exactly equal to right card (ends at last action button)
            void SyncMemberColumnHeight()
            {
                double h = rightCard.Bounds.Height;
                if (h > 1)
                {
                    leftCard.Height = h;
                    leftCard.MaxHeight = h;
                    leftCard.MinHeight = h;
                }
            }
            rightCard.SizeChanged += (_, __) => SyncMemberColumnHeight();
            leftCard.AttachedToVisualTree += (_, __) =>
            {
                Avalonia.Threading.Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(SyncMemberColumnHeight, Avalonia.Threading.DispatcherPriority.Background);
            };

            // Outer wrap: Add members + Members + Selected
            var membersInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 10 };
            membersInner.Children.Add(addCard);
            membersInner.Children.Add(topSplit);
            var membersWrap = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Padding = new Thickness(12),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 16),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Child = new StackPanel
                {
                    Spacing = 10,
                    Children =
                    {
                        Tb("Members of this group", size: 18, weight: FontWeight.Bold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left),
                        Tb("Users and groups that belong to this group.", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true),
                        membersInner
                    }
                }
            };
            panel.Children.Add(membersWrap);

            // Member of: same three-panel layout (add / list / selected)
            RenderGroupMemberOfManagement(panel, de, node);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Member of section for a group: Add to parent group, list of groups this group belongs to,
        /// and selected + remove actions. Same layout as the Members section above.
        /// </summary>
        private void RenderGroupMemberOfManagement(StackPanel panel, DirectoryEntry de, AdNode node)
        {
            string groupDn = node.Dn;
            string groupName = node.Name;

            var parentDns = GetAllMultiValues(de, "memberOf");
            var parentInfo = new List<(string Dn, string Display, string Sam, string SearchBlob)>();
            foreach (string pDn in parentDns)
            {
                string display = pDn;
                string sam = "";
                try
                {
                    using var pde = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + pDn);
                    pde.RefreshCache(new[] { "cn", "name", "displayName", "sAMAccountName" });
                    display = pde.Properties["displayName"]?.Value?.ToString()
                           ?? pde.Properties["name"]?.Value?.ToString()
                           ?? pde.Properties["cn"]?.Value?.ToString()
                           ?? pDn;
                    sam = pde.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                }
                catch
                {
                    var cn = pDn.Split(',').FirstOrDefault(p => p.StartsWith("CN=", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                    if (cn != null) display = cn.Substring(3);
                }
                string blob = string.Join(" ", display, sam, pDn);
                parentInfo.Add((pDn, display, sam, blob));
            }
            parentInfo = parentInfo.OrderBy(x => x.Display, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();

            // ---- ADD TO GROUP (top) ----
            var addCard = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Padding = new Thickness(10),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            var addInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6 };
            addInner.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Add to group", FontSize = 16, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold });
            addInner.Children.Add(Tb("Live search groups. Click a result to make this group a member of it (shows 3).", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true));
            var addBox = new TextBox
            {
                Watermark = "Search groups to join...",
                MinHeight = 34,
                FontSize = 14,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            var addSuggest = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };
            var addSuggestHost = new Border
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(4),
                IsVisible = false,
                MinHeight = 40,
                MaxHeight = 160,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(100, 140, 140, 160)),
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(40, 80, 80, 100)),
                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(6),
                Child = new ScrollViewer
                {
                    Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0),
                    AllowAutoHide = false,
                    Content = addSuggest,
                    MaxHeight = 150,
                    VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto
                }
            };
            addBox.TextChanged += async (_, _) =>
            {
                string raw = addBox.Text?.Trim() ?? "";
                addSuggest.Children.Clear();
                if (raw.Length < 1)
                {
                    addSuggestHost.IsVisible = false;
                    return;
                }
                try
                {
                    string esc = EscapeLdap(raw);
                    // LDAP on a background thread so typing never blocks the UI;
                    // stale results are dropped if the text moved on.
                    var hits = await Task.Run(() =>
                    {
                        var found = new List<(string Dn, string Name, string Sam)>();
                        using var gSearcher = new DirectorySearcher(new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + _domainDn))
                        {
                            Filter = $"(&(objectClass=group)(|(sAMAccountName={esc}*)(cn={esc}*)(name={esc}*)))",
                            SearchScope = SearchScope.Subtree,
                            SizeLimit = 10,
                            PropertiesToLoad = { "distinguishedName", "cn", "sAMAccountName", "name" }
                        };
                        using var gResults = gSearcher.FindAll();
                        foreach (SearchResult sr in gResults)
                        {
                            string dn = (Prop(sr, "distinguishedName"));
                            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dn)) continue;
                            if (string.Equals(dn, groupDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                            if (parentDns.Any(m => string.Equals(m, dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))) continue;
                            if (found.Any(h => string.Equals(h.Dn, dn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))) continue;
                            string gname = sr.Properties["cn"]?[0]?.ToString()
                                        ?? sr.Properties["name"]?[0]?.ToString()
                                        ?? dn;
                            string gsam = (Prop(sr, "sAMAccountName"));
                            found.Add((dn, gname, gsam));
                        }
                        return found;
                    });
                    if (!string.Equals(addBox.Text?.Trim() ?? "", raw, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                        return;
                    int shown = 0;
                    foreach (var hit in hits.OrderBy(h => h.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        if (shown >= 3) break;
                        var content = new StackPanel { Spacing = 1 };
                        content.Children.Add(MakeIconLine("Group", hit.Name + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hit.Sam) ? "" : "  (" + hit.Sam + ")"), 14, 13, bold: true));
                        content.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = hit.Dn, FontSize = 11, Opacity = 0.7, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap });
                        var btn = new Button
                        {
                            Content = content,
                            Classes = { "suggestItem" },
                            HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                            HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left
                        };
                        string capDn = hit.Dn;
                        string capCn = hit.Name;
                        btn.Click += (_, _) =>
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                using var parent = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + capDn);
                                using var child = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + groupDn);
                                parent.Invoke("Add", new object[] { child.Path });
                                parent.CommitChanges();
                                ShowNotification("Added \"" + groupName + "\" to group \"" + capCn + "\"", "success");
                                addBox.Text = "";
                                addSuggest.Children.Clear();
                                addSuggestHost.IsVisible = false;
                                ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                            }
                            catch (Exception ex)
                            {
                                string msg = ex.Message ?? "";
                                if (msg.IndexOf("already", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                                    ShowNotification("\"" + groupName + "\" is already a member of \"" + capCn + "\".", "error");
                                else
                                    ShowNotification("Add failed: " + msg, "error");
                            }
                        };
                        addSuggest.Children.Add(btn);
                        shown++;
                    }
                    addSuggestHost.IsVisible = shown > 0;
                    if (shown == 0)
                    {
                        addSuggest.Children.Add(Tb("No matching groups found (or already a member).", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, margin: new Thickness(6)));
                        addSuggestHost.IsVisible = true;
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    addSuggest.Children.Clear();
                    addSuggest.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "Search error: " + ex.Message, FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.8, Margin = new Thickness(6) });
                    addSuggestHost.IsVisible = true;
                }
            };
            addInner.Children.Add(addBox);
            addInner.Children.Add(addSuggestHost);
            addCard.Child = addInner;

            // ---- Member of (left) | Selected + Actions (right) ----
            var selectedMap = new Dictionary<string, (string Display, string Sam, CheckBox Cb)>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            var selectedList = new StackPanel { Spacing = 4 };
            var selectedHeader = new TextBlock { Text = "Selected (0)", FontSize = 15, FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold };
            var memberRows = new List<(string Dn, string Display, string Sam, string SearchBlob, Border Row, CheckBox Cb)>();
            var memberStack = new StackPanel { Spacing = 2 };

            void RefreshSelectedPanel()
            {
                selectedList.Children.Clear();
                selectedHeader.Text = "Selected (" + selectedMap.Count + ")";
                foreach (var kv in selectedMap.OrderBy(k => k.Value.Display, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    string label = kv.Value.Display;
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(kv.Value.Sam))
                        label += "  (" + kv.Value.Sam + ")";
                    var row = new Grid { ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,Auto") };
                    row.Children.Add(MakeIconLine("Group", label, 14, 13));
                    var uncheck = new Button
                    {
                        Content = MakeMdi("mdi-close", 12),
                        Padding = new Thickness(6, 2),
                        Background = Brushes.Transparent,
                        BorderThickness = new Thickness(0),
                        FontSize = 12
                    };
                    string dnKey = kv.Key;
                    uncheck.Click += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        if (selectedMap.TryGetValue(dnKey, out var info))
                        {
                            info.Cb.IsChecked = false;
                            selectedMap.Remove(dnKey);
                            RefreshSelectedPanel();
                        }
                    };
                    Grid.SetColumn(uncheck, 1);
                    row.Children.Add(uncheck);
                    selectedList.Children.Add(new Border
                    {
                        Child = row,
                        Padding = new Thickness(4, 3),
                        CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(4),
                        Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(40, 128, 128, 128))
                    });
                }
            }

            void ApplyMemberFilter(string q)
            {
                q = (q ?? "").Trim();
                foreach (var row in memberRows)
                {
                    bool match = q.Length == 0 ||
                        row.SearchBlob.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
                    row.Row.IsVisible = match;
                }
            }

            var topSplit = new Grid
            {
                ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("*,12,*"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top
            };

            // LEFT: Member of list
            var leftTitle = Tb("Member of (" + parentInfo.Count + ")", size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center);
            var memberFilter = new TextBox
            {
                Watermark = "Filter by group name...",
                MinHeight = 34,
                FontSize = 14,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            memberFilter.TextChanged += (_, _) => ApplyMemberFilter(memberFilter.Text ?? "");

            if (parentInfo.Count == 0)
            {
                memberStack.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "(not a member of any groups, or insufficient rights)", Opacity = 0.7 });
            }
            else
            {
                int rowIdx = 0;
                foreach (var item in parentInfo)
                {
                    string label = item.Display;
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Sam))
                        label += "  (" + item.Sam + ")";
                    var cb = new CheckBox
                    {
                        Content = MakeIconLine("Group", label, 14, 13),
                        FontSize = 13,
                        Margin = new Thickness(4, 2),
                        HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                    };
                    string mDn = item.Dn;
                    string display = item.Display;
                    string sam = item.Sam;
                    cb.IsCheckedChanged += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        if (cb.IsChecked == true)
                            selectedMap[mDn] = (display, sam, cb);
                        else
                            selectedMap.Remove(mDn);
                        RefreshSelectedPanel();
                    };
                    var rowBorder = new Border
                    {
                        Child = cb,
                        Padding = new Thickness(2, 1),
                        CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(4),
                        Background = (rowIdx++ % 2 == 0)
                            ? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(28, 128, 128, 128))
                            : Brushes.Transparent
                    };
                    string openDn = mDn;
                    rowBorder.DoubleTapped += (_, _) =>
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            using var mde = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + openDn);
                            string gname = mde.Properties["name"]?.Value?.ToString()
                                        ?? mde.Properties["cn"]?.Value?.ToString()
                                        ?? openDn;
                            ShowHelpDeskInfo(new AdNode { Name = gname, Dn = openDn, Type = "Group" });
                        }
                        catch (Exception ex) { ShowNotification("Open failed: " + ex.Message, "error"); }
                    };
                    memberStack.Children.Add(rowBorder);
                    memberRows.Add((mDn, display, sam, item.SearchBlob, rowBorder, cb));
                }
            }

            var memberScroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0),
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                Content = memberStack,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Visible,
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
            };
            var memberListBox = new Border
            {
                Child = memberScroll,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(100, 140, 140, 160)),
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(30, 80, 80, 100)),
                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(6),
                Padding = new Thickness(4),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            var leftGrid = new Grid
            {
                RowDefinitions = new RowDefinitions("Auto,Auto,*"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            Grid.SetRow(leftTitle, 0);
            Grid.SetRow(memberFilter, 1);
            Grid.SetRow(memberListBox, 2);
            leftGrid.Children.Add(leftTitle);
            leftGrid.Children.Add(memberFilter);
            leftGrid.Children.Add(memberListBox);
            var leftCard = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Child = leftGrid,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
                Padding = new Thickness(12),
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1)
            };

            // RIGHT: Selected + Actions
            var rightGrid = new Grid
            {
                RowDefinitions = new RowDefinitions("Auto,Auto"),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top
            };
            var selectedBlock = new StackPanel { Spacing = 6, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            selectedBlock.Children.Add(selectedHeader);
            selectedBlock.Children.Add(Tb("Checked parent groups appear here.", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true));
            var selectedScroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 18, 0),
                AllowAutoHide = false,
                Content = selectedList,
                Height = 160,
                MaxHeight = 160,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Visible,
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
            };
            selectedBlock.Children.Add(new Border
            {
                Child = selectedScroll,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(100, 140, 140, 160)),
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(30, 80, 80, 100)),
                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(6),
                Padding = new Thickness(4),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Height = 168,
                MaxHeight = 168
            });
            Grid.SetRow(selectedBlock, 0);
            rightGrid.Children.Add(selectedBlock);

            var actionsBar = new Border
            {
                Padding = new Thickness(0, 10, 0, 0),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(0, 1, 0, 0),
                BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(60, 128, 128, 128))
            };
            var actionsInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8 };
            actionsInner.Children.Add(Tb("Actions", size: 14, weight: FontWeight.Bold, margin: new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0)));
            var opStatus = Tb("", size: 12, opacity: 0.9, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4));
            var removeBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Remove from selected groups",
                Classes = { "action" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                FontSize = 13
            };
            var undoBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Undo last remove",
                Classes = { "action" },
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                FontSize = 13
            };
            // Enable undo if a matching undo record exists for this group
            try
            {
                var pendingUndo = LoadGroupMemberOfUndo();
                undoBtn.IsEnabled = pendingUndo != null && string.Equals(pendingUndo.MemberDn, groupDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && pendingUndo.Parents.Count > 0;
                if (undoBtn.IsEnabled)
                    undoBtn.Content = $"Undo last remove ({pendingUndo!.Parents.Count})";
            }
            catch { undoBtn.IsEnabled = false; }
            removeBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                if (selectedMap.Count == 0) { ShowNotification("No groups selected.", "info"); return; }
                var snapshot = selectedMap.Select(kv => (Dn: kv.Key, Display: kv.Value.Display)).ToList();
                int total = snapshot.Count;
                removeBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                undoBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                int ok = 0, fail = 0;
                var removedFrom = new List<(string Dn, string Display)>();
                try
                {
                    int i = 0;
                    foreach (var item in snapshot)
                    {
                        i++;
                        opStatus.Text = "Removing from " + i + " of " + total + ": " + item.Display;
                        await Task.Delay(15);
                        try
                        {
                            using var parent = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + item.Dn);
                            parent.Invoke("Remove", new object[] { "LDAP://" + groupDn });
                            parent.CommitChanges();
                            ok++;
                            removedFrom.Add((item.Dn, item.Display));
                        }
                        catch { fail++; }
                    }
                    if (removedFrom.Count > 0)
                    {
                        SaveGroupMemberOfUndo(new GroupMemberOfUndo
                        {
                            Timestamp = DateTime.UtcNow,
                            MemberDn = groupDn,
                            MemberName = groupName,
                            Parents = removedFrom.Select(r => new GroupRemoveUndoMember { Dn = r.Dn, Display = r.Display }).ToList()
                        });
                        undoBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                        undoBtn.Content = $"Undo last remove ({removedFrom.Count})";
                    }
                    string msg = "Removed from " + ok + " of " + total + " group(s)";
                    if (fail > 0) msg += ", " + fail + " failed";
                    opStatus.Text = msg;
                    ShowNotification(msg, ok > 0 && fail == 0 ? "success" : "info");
                    if (ok > 0) ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    opStatus.Text = "Remove failed: " + ex.Message;
                    ShowNotification("Remove failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                }
                finally { removeBtn.IsEnabled = true; }
            };
            undoBtn.Click += async (_, _) =>
            {
                var pending = LoadGroupMemberOfUndo();
                if (pending == null || pending.Parents.Count == 0)
                {
                    ShowNotification("Nothing to undo.", "info");
                    undoBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                    return;
                }
                if (!string.Equals(pending.MemberDn, groupDn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    ShowNotification($"Last remove was on a different group ({pending.MemberName}). Open that group to undo.", "info");
                    return;
                }
                removeBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                undoBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                int ok = 0, fail = 0;
                int total = pending.Parents.Count;
                var remaining = new List<GroupRemoveUndoMember>();
                try
                {
                    int i = 0;
                    foreach (var p in pending.Parents)
                    {
                        i++;
                        opStatus.Text = $"Undoing {i} of {total}: re-adding {groupName} to {p.Display}";
                        await Task.Delay(15);
                        try
                        {
                            using var parent = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + p.Dn);
                            parent.Invoke("Add", new object[] { "LDAP://" + groupDn });
                            parent.CommitChanges();
                            ok++;
                        }
                        catch { fail++; remaining.Add(p); }
                    }
                    if (remaining.Count == 0)
                        ClearGroupMemberOfUndo();
                    else
                    {
                        pending.Parents = remaining;
                        SaveGroupMemberOfUndo(pending);
                        undoBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                        undoBtn.Content = $"Undo last remove ({remaining.Count})";
                    }
                    string msg = $"Undo: re-added {groupName} to {ok} of {total} group(s)";
                    if (fail > 0) msg += $", {fail} failed";
                    opStatus.Text = msg;
                    ShowNotification(msg, ok > 0 ? "success" : "error");
                    if (ok > 0) ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    opStatus.Text = "Undo failed: " + ex.Message;
                    ShowNotification("Undo failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                }
                finally { removeBtn.IsEnabled = true; }
            };
            var selectAllBtn = new Button { Content = "Select all", Classes = { "action" }, FontSize = 13, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            selectAllBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                foreach (var row in memberRows)
                    if (row.Row.IsVisible)
                        row.Cb.IsChecked = true;
            };
            var clearBtn = new Button { Content = "Clear selection", Classes = { "action" }, FontSize = 13, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            clearBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                foreach (var kv in selectedMap.Values.ToList())
                    kv.Cb.IsChecked = false;
                selectedMap.Clear();
                RefreshSelectedPanel();
            };
            actionsInner.Children.Add(opStatus);
            actionsInner.Children.Add(removeBtn);
            actionsInner.Children.Add(undoBtn);
            actionsInner.Children.Add(selectAllBtn);
            actionsInner.Children.Add(clearBtn);
            actionsBar.Child = actionsInner;
            Grid.SetRow(actionsBar, 1);
            rightGrid.Children.Add(actionsBar);

            var rightCard = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Child = rightGrid,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
                Padding = new Thickness(12),
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(1),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 0)
            };

            Grid.SetColumn(leftCard, 0);
            Grid.SetColumn(rightCard, 2);
            topSplit.Children.Add(leftCard);
            topSplit.Children.Add(rightCard);

            void SyncHeight()
            {
                double h = rightCard.Bounds.Height;
                if (h > 1)
                {
                    leftCard.Height = h;
                    leftCard.MaxHeight = h;
                    leftCard.MinHeight = h;
                }
            }
            rightCard.SizeChanged += (_, __) => SyncHeight();
            leftCard.AttachedToVisualTree += (_, __) =>
            {
                Avalonia.Threading.Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(SyncHeight, Avalonia.Threading.DispatcherPriority.Background);
            };

            var memberOfInner = new StackPanel { Spacing = 10 };
            memberOfInner.Children.Add(addCard);
            memberOfInner.Children.Add(topSplit);
            var memberOfWrap = new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Padding = new Thickness(12),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 16),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Child = new StackPanel
                {
                    Spacing = 10,
                    Children =
                    {
                        Tb("Member of", size: 18, weight: FontWeight.Bold, halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left),
                        Tb("Groups that this group belongs to (nested group membership).", size: 12, opacity: 0.75, wrap: true),
                        memberOfInner
                    }
                }
            };
            panel.Children.Add(memberOfWrap);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Export empty groups (already filtered to non-built-in) with selectable attributes.
        /// </summary>
        private void ShowEmptyGroupsExportDialog(List<(string Name, string Sam, string Dn, bool Protected)> groups)
        {
            // Safety: never export built-in / protected groups
            var toExport = groups.Where(g => !g.Protected).OrderBy(g => g.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();
            if (toExport.Count == 0)
            {
                ShowNotification("No non-built-in empty groups to export.", "info");
                return;
            }

            var dlg = new Window
            {
                Title = "Export empty groups",
                Width = 440,
                SizeToContent = SizeToContent.Height,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                CanResize = false
            };
            StyleDialogWindow(dlg);
            var root = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(20), Spacing = 10 };
            root.Children.Add(Tb("Export empty groups", size: 18, weight: FontWeight.Bold));
            root.Children.Add(Tb(toExport.Count.ToString("N0") + " non-built-in empty group(s). Built-in / system groups are excluded.", size: 13, opacity: 0.85, wrap: true));
            root.Children.Add(Tb("Select columns to include:", weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, margin: new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0)));

            var cbName = new CheckBox { Content = "Name", IsChecked = true, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbSam = new CheckBox { Content = "SamAccountName", IsChecked = true, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbDn = new CheckBox { Content = "DistinguishedName", IsChecked = true, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbDesc = new CheckBox { Content = "Description", IsChecked = true, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbType = new CheckBox { Content = "Group type (Security/Distribution)", IsChecked = true, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbCreated = new CheckBox { Content = "When created", IsChecked = false, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbChanged = new CheckBox { Content = "When changed", IsChecked = false, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbMail = new CheckBox { Content = "Mail", IsChecked = false, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbManaged = new CheckBox { Content = "Managed by", IsChecked = false, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbInfo = new CheckBox { Content = "Notes (info)", IsChecked = false, FontSize = 13 };
            foreach (var cb in new[] { cbName, cbSam, cbDn, cbDesc, cbType, cbCreated, cbChanged, cbMail, cbManaged, cbInfo })
                root.Children.Add(cb);

            var status = Tb("", size: 12, opacity: 0.85, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0));
            root.Children.Add(status);

            var btnRow = new StackPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                Spacing = 10,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0)
            };
            var cancelBtn = new Button { Content = "Cancel", MinWidth = 100, Classes = { "action" } };
            var exportBtn = new Button { Content = "Export", MinWidth = 100, Classes = { "action" } };
            cancelBtn.Click += (_, _) => dlg.Close();
            exportBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                bool any = (cbName.IsChecked == true) || (cbSam.IsChecked == true) || (cbDn.IsChecked == true)
                    || (cbDesc.IsChecked == true) || (cbType.IsChecked == true) || (cbCreated.IsChecked == true)
                    || (cbChanged.IsChecked == true) || (cbMail.IsChecked == true) || (cbManaged.IsChecked == true)
                    || (cbInfo.IsChecked == true);
                if (!any)
                {
                    status.Text = "Select at least one column.";
                    return;
                }
                exportBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                status.Text = "Building export...";
                try
                {
                    string CsvEsc(string? v) => "\"" + (v ?? "").Replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\"";
                    var headers = new List<string>();
                    if (cbName.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("Name");
                    if (cbSam.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("SamAccountName");
                    if (cbDn.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("DistinguishedName");
                    if (cbDesc.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("Description");
                    if (cbType.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("GroupType");
                    if (cbCreated.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("WhenCreated");
                    if (cbChanged.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("WhenChanged");
                    if (cbMail.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("Mail");
                    if (cbManaged.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("ManagedBy");
                    if (cbInfo.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("Notes");

                    bool needLdap = (cbDesc.IsChecked == true) || (cbType.IsChecked == true) || (cbCreated.IsChecked == true)
                        || (cbChanged.IsChecked == true) || (cbMail.IsChecked == true) || (cbManaged.IsChecked == true)
                        || (cbInfo.IsChecked == true);

                    var dataRows = new List<List<string>>();
                    foreach (var g in toExport)
                    {
                        // Double-check protection at export time
                        if (g.Protected) continue;

                        string name = g.Name, sam = g.Sam, dn = g.Dn;
                        string desc = "", gtype = "", created = "", changed = "", mail = "", managed = "", info = "";
                        if (needLdap)
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                using var de = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + dn);
                                de.RefreshCache(new[] {
                                    "name", "cn", "sAMAccountName", "description", "groupType",
                                    "whenCreated", "whenChanged", "mail", "managedBy", "info",
                                    "objectSid", "isCriticalSystemObject", "distinguishedName"
                                });
                                // Re-verify not built-in
                                byte[]? sid = de.Properties["objectSid"]?.Value as byte[];
                                object? crit = null;
                                try { if (de.Properties.Contains("isCriticalSystemObject")) crit = de.Properties["isCriticalSystemObject"]?.Value; } catch { }
                                string? checkDn = de.Properties["distinguishedName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? dn;
                                string? checkSam = de.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? sam;
                                if (IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(checkDn, sid, crit, checkSam))
                                    continue;

                                desc = de.Properties["description"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                                mail = de.Properties["mail"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                                managed = de.Properties["managedBy"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                                info = de.Properties["info"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                                try
                                {
                                    if (de.Properties["whenCreated"]?.Value is DateTime wc)
                                        created = wc.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm");
                                }
                                catch { }
                                try
                                {
                                    if (de.Properties["whenChanged"]?.Value is DateTime wch)
                                        changed = wch.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm");
                                }
                                catch { }
                                try
                                {
                                    if (de.Properties.Contains("groupType") && de.Properties["groupType"].Count > 0)
                                    {
                                        int gt = Convert.ToInt32(de.Properties["groupType"][0]);
                                        gtype = (gt & unchecked((int)0x80000000)) != 0 ? "Security" : "Distribution";
                                    }
                                }
                                catch { }
                            }
                            catch { }
                        }

                        var cells = new List<string>();
                        if (cbName.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(name);
                        if (cbSam.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(sam);
                        if (cbDn.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(dn);
                        if (cbDesc.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(desc);
                        if (cbType.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(gtype);
                        if (cbCreated.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(created);
                        if (cbChanged.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(changed);
                        if (cbMail.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(mail);
                        if (cbManaged.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(managed);
                        if (cbInfo.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(info);
                        dataRows.Add(cells);
                    }

                    string dir = Path.Combine(
                        Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments),
                        "ADE_Exports");
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
                    string file = Path.Combine(dir, "empty_groups_" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss") + ".csv");
                    var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
                    sb.AppendLine(string.Join(",", headers.Select(h => CsvEsc(h))));
                    foreach (var cells in dataRows)
                        sb.AppendLine(string.Join(",", cells.Select(c => CsvEsc(c))));
                    File.WriteAllText(file, sb.ToString(), new System.Text.UTF8Encoding(encoderShouldEmitUTF8Identifier: true));
                    status.Text = "Exported " + dataRows.Count + " groups.";
                    ShowNotification("Exported " + dataRows.Count + " empty groups to Documents\\ADE_Exports", "success");
                    try
                    {
                        System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                        {
                            FileName = file,
                            UseShellExecute = true
                        });
                    }
                    catch { }
                    dlg.Close();
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    status.Text = "Export failed: " + ex.Message;
                    ShowNotification("Export failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                    exportBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                }
            };
            btnRow.Children.Add(cancelBtn);
            btnRow.Children.Add(exportBtn);
            root.Children.Add(btnRow);
            dlg.Content = root;
            try { dlg.ShowDialog(this); } catch { dlg.Show(); }
        }

        private void ShowGroupExportDialog(string groupDn, string groupName)
        {
            var dlg = new Window
            {
                Title = "Export membership - " + groupName,
                Width = 480,
                Height = 520,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                CanResize = true,
                MinHeight = 360,
                MinWidth = 400
            };
            StyleDialogWindow(dlg);
            var root = new DockPanel { Margin = new Thickness(20) };
            var header = Tb("Select which columns to include in the CSV. Exports direct members of this group.", size: 13, opacity: 0.85, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 12));
            DockPanel.SetDock(header, Dock.Top);
            root.Children.Add(header);

            var btnRow = new StackPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                Spacing = 10,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 12, 0, 0)
            };
            var cancelBtn = new Button { Content = "Cancel", Classes = { "action" }, MinWidth = 100 };
            var exportBtn = new Button { Content = "Export", Classes = { "action" }, MinWidth = 100 };
            cancelBtn.Click += (_, _) => dlg.Close();
            DockPanel.SetDock(btnRow, Dock.Bottom);

            var body = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8 };
            body.Children.Add(Tb("Group: " + groupName, size: 14, weight: FontWeight.SemiBold));
            body.Children.Add(Tb("Columns", size: 13, weight: FontWeight.SemiBold, margin: new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0)));
            // Attribute checkboxes - common ones default on, optional off
            var cbSam = new CheckBox { Content = "Username (sAMAccountName)", IsChecked = true, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbDisplay = new CheckBox { Content = "Full name (displayName)", IsChecked = true, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbType = new CheckBox { Content = "Type (User / Group)", IsChecked = true, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbGroup = new CheckBox { Content = "Group name", IsChecked = true, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbDn = new CheckBox { Content = "Distinguished Name", IsChecked = false, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbGiven = new CheckBox { Content = "First name (givenName)", IsChecked = false, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbSn = new CheckBox { Content = "Last name (sn)", IsChecked = false, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbMail = new CheckBox { Content = "Email (mail)", IsChecked = false, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbTitle = new CheckBox { Content = "Title", IsChecked = false, FontSize = 13 };
            var cbDept = new CheckBox { Content = "Department", IsChecked = false, FontSize = 13 };
            foreach (var cb in new[] { cbSam, cbDisplay, cbType, cbGroup, cbDn, cbGiven, cbSn, cbMail, cbTitle, cbDept })
                body.Children.Add(cb);
            var status = new TextBlock { Text = "", FontSize = 12, Opacity = 0.85, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap, Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0) };
            body.Children.Add(status);

            exportBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                bool any = (cbSam.IsChecked == true) || (cbDisplay.IsChecked == true) || (cbType.IsChecked == true)
                    || (cbGroup.IsChecked == true) || (cbDn.IsChecked == true) || (cbGiven.IsChecked == true)
                    || (cbSn.IsChecked == true) || (cbMail.IsChecked == true) || (cbTitle.IsChecked == true)
                    || (cbDept.IsChecked == true);
                if (!any)
                {
                    status.Text = "Select at least one column.";
                    return;
                }
                exportBtn.IsEnabled = false;
                status.Text = "Building export...";
                try
                {
                    string CsvEsc(string? v) => "\"" + (v ?? "").Replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\"";
                    var headers = new List<string>();
                    if (cbSam.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("Username");
                    if (cbDisplay.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("FullName");
                    if (cbType.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("Type");
                    if (cbGroup.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("Group");
                    if (cbDn.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("DistinguishedName");
                    if (cbGiven.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("FirstName");
                    if (cbSn.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("LastName");
                    if (cbMail.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("Email");
                    if (cbTitle.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("Title");
                    if (cbDept.IsChecked == true) headers.Add("Department");

                    var rows = new List<List<string>>();
                    using (var gde = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + groupDn))
                    {
                        gde.RefreshCache(new[] { "member" });
                        var members = GetAllMultiValues(gde, "member");
                        foreach (string mDn in members)
                        {
                            string display = "", sam = "", given = "", sn = "", mail = "", title = "", dept = "";
                            bool isGroup = false;
                            try
                            {
                                using var mde = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + mDn);
                                mde.RefreshCache(new[] {
                                    "objectClass", "cn", "name", "displayName", "sAMAccountName",
                                    "givenName", "sn", "mail", "title", "department"
                                });
                                display = mde.Properties["displayName"]?.Value?.ToString()
                                       ?? mde.Properties["name"]?.Value?.ToString()
                                       ?? mde.Properties["cn"]?.Value?.ToString()
                                       ?? mDn;
                                sam = mde.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                                given = mde.Properties["givenName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                                sn = mde.Properties["sn"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                                mail = mde.Properties["mail"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                                title = mde.Properties["title"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                                dept = mde.Properties["department"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? "";
                                var oc = mde.Properties["objectClass"];
                                if (oc != null)
                                    foreach (object o in oc)
                                        if (string.Equals(o?.ToString(), "group", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                            isGroup = true;
                            }
                            catch
                            {
                                display = mDn;
                            }
                            var cells = new List<string>();
                            if (cbSam.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(sam);
                            if (cbDisplay.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(display);
                            if (cbType.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(isGroup ? "Group" : "User");
                            if (cbGroup.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(groupName);
                            if (cbDn.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(mDn);
                            if (cbGiven.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(given);
                            if (cbSn.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(sn);
                            if (cbMail.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(mail);
                            if (cbTitle.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(title);
                            if (cbDept.IsChecked == true) cells.Add(dept);
                            rows.Add(cells);
                        }
                    }

                    string safeName = string.Join("_", (groupName ?? "group").Split(Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars()));
                    string dir = Path.Combine(
                        Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments),
                        "ADE_Exports");
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
                    string file = Path.Combine(dir, safeName + "_members_" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss") + ".csv");
                    var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
                    sb.AppendLine(string.Join(",", headers.Select(h => CsvEsc(h))));
                    // Sort by first column
                    foreach (var cells in rows.OrderBy(r => r.Count > 0 ? r[0] : "", StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        sb.AppendLine(string.Join(",", cells.Select(c => CsvEsc(c))));
                    File.WriteAllText(file, sb.ToString(), new System.Text.UTF8Encoding(encoderShouldEmitUTF8Identifier: true));
                    status.Text = "Exported " + rows.Count + " members.";
                    ShowNotification("Exported " + rows.Count + " members to Documents\\ADE_Exports", "success");
                    try
                    {
                        System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                        {
                            FileName = file,
                            UseShellExecute = true
                        });
                    }
                    catch { }
                    dlg.Close();
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    status.Text = "Export failed: " + ex.Message;
                    ShowNotification("Export failed: " + ex.Message, "error");
                    exportBtn.IsEnabled = true;
                }
            };

            btnRow.Children.Add(cancelBtn);
            btnRow.Children.Add(exportBtn);
            root.Children.Add(btnRow);
            var bodyScroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                Content = body,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled
            };
            root.Children.Add(bodyScroll);
            dlg.Content = root;
            try { dlg.ShowDialog(this); }
            catch { dlg.Show(); }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// ADUC-style Rename Group dialog: group name (CN) and pre-Windows 2000 name (sAMAccountName).
        /// Theme-friendly (light and dark mode).
        /// </summary>
        private void ShowRenameGroupDialog(AdNode node)
        {
            if (node == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(node.Dn)) return;

            // Built-in / protected groups (incl. the domain-controller and
            // other default groups) can never be renamed (user request
            // 2026-10-27). Checked live at open; IsProtectedAdObject fails
            // closed, and the button is hidden for them as well.
            try
            {
                using var guard = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + node.Dn);
                if (IsProtectedAdObject(guard))
                {
                    ShowNotification("Rename blocked: \"" + node.Name + "\" is a built-in or protected group and cannot be renamed.", "error");
                    return;
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                ShowNotification("Rename blocked: could not verify whether \"" + node.Name + "\" is protected.", "error");
                return;
            }

            string groupName = "";
            string sam = "";
            try
            {
                using var de = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + node.Dn);
                de.RefreshCache(new[] { "cn", "name", "sAMAccountName" });
                groupName = de.Properties["cn"]?.Value?.ToString()
                         ?? de.Properties["name"]?.Value?.ToString()
                         ?? node.Name
                         ?? "";
                sam = de.Properties["sAMAccountName"]?.Value?.ToString() ?? node.SamAccountName ?? "";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowNotification("Could not load group for rename: " + ex.Message, "error");
                return;
            }

            TextBox MkField(string initial)
            {
                return new TextBox
                {
                    Text = initial ?? "",
                    MinHeight = 32,
                    FontSize = 13,
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                };
            }

            var tbName = MkField(groupName);
            var tbSam = MkField(sam);

            Control LabeledRow(string label, Control field)
            {
                var grid = new Grid
                {
                    ColumnDefinitions = new ColumnDefinitions("170,*"),
                    Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 10),
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
                };
                grid.Children.Add(Tb(label, size: 13, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 10, 0), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left, valign: VerticalAlignment.Center));
                Grid.SetColumn(field, 1);
                grid.Children.Add(field);
                return grid;
            }

            var body = new StackPanel
            {
                Spacing = 0,
                Margin = new Thickness(16),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
            };
            body.Children.Add(LabeledRow("Group name:", tbName));
            body.Children.Add(LabeledRow("Group name\n(pre-Windows 2000):", tbSam));

            var errTb = Tb("", size: 12, wrap: true, margin: new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0), foreground: new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(200, 80, 80)), visible: false);
            body.Children.Add(errTb);

            var dlg = new Window
            {
                Title = "Rename Group",
                Width = 420,
                MinWidth = 380,
                SizeToContent = SizeToContent.Height,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                CanResize = false,
                ShowInTaskbar = false
            };
            StyleDialogWindow(dlg);

            var okBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "OK",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 88,
                IsDefault = true
            };
            var cancelBtn = new Button
            {
                Content = "Cancel",
                Classes = { "action" },
                MinWidth = 88,
                IsCancel = true
            };
            cancelBtn.Click += (_, _) => dlg.Close();

            string originalDn = node.Dn;
            string originalCn = groupName;
            string originalSam = sam;

            okBtn.Click += (_, _) =>
            {
                errTb.IsVisible = false;
                string newName = (tbName.Text ?? "").Trim();
                string newSam = (tbSam.Text ?? "").Trim();

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(newName))
                {
                    errTb.Text = "Group name is required.";
                    errTb.IsVisible = true;
                    return;
                }
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(newSam))
                {
                    errTb.Text = "Pre-Windows 2000 group name (sAMAccountName) is required.";
                    errTb.IsVisible = true;
                    return;
                }
                if (newSam.Length > 256)
                {
                    errTb.Text = "Pre-Windows 2000 group name is too long.";
                    errTb.IsVisible = true;
                    return;
                }

                try
                {
                    using var fresh = new DirectoryEntry("LDAP://" + originalDn);
                    fresh.RefreshCache(new[] { "cn", "sAMAccountName", "name" });

                    if (!string.Equals(newSam, originalSam, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        fresh.Properties["sAMAccountName"].Value = newSam;

                    fresh.CommitChanges();

                    if (!string.Equals(newName, originalCn, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                    {
                        string EscapeRdn(string cn)
                        {
                            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(cn)) return cn;
                            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
                            foreach (char ch in cn)
                            {
                                if (ch == ',' || ch == '+' || ch == '"' || ch == '\\' ||
                                    ch == '<' || ch == '>' || ch == ';' || ch == '=' || ch == '\0')
                                    sb.Append('\\');
                                sb.Append(ch);
                            }
                            return sb.ToString();
                        }
                        string newRdn = "CN=" + EscapeRdn(newName);
                        fresh.Rename(newRdn);
                        try { fresh.CommitChanges(); } catch { }

                        try
                        {
                            string parentDn = originalDn.IndexOf(',') >= 0
                                ? originalDn.Substring(originalDn.IndexOf(',') + 1)
                                : originalDn;
                            node.Dn = newRdn + "," + parentDn;
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }

                    node.Name = newName;
                    node.SamAccountName = newSam;

                    ShowNotification("Group renamed: " + newName, "success");
                    dlg.Close();
                    ShowHelpDeskInfo(node);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    errTb.Text = "Rename failed: " + ex.Message;
                    errTb.IsVisible = true;
                }
            };

            var btnRow = new StackPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                Spacing = 10,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
                Margin = new Thickness(16, 8, 16, 16)
            };
            btnRow.Children.Add(okBtn);
            btnRow.Children.Add(cancelBtn);

            var root = new DockPanel { LastChildFill = true };
            DockPanel.SetDock(btnRow, Dock.Bottom);
            root.Children.Add(btnRow);
            root.Children.Add(body);
            dlg.Content = root;

            try { dlg.ShowDialog(this); }
            catch { try { dlg.Show(); } catch { } }
        }

        private void RenderGroupActions(StackPanel panel, DirectoryEntry de, AdNode node)
        {
            string nodeDn = node.Dn;
            string nodeName = node.Name;
            bool protectedGroup = IsBuiltInOrProtectedGroup(de);

            var actionCol = new StackPanel { Spacing = 8, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch };
            actionCol.Children.Add(Tb("Group Actions", size: 16, weight: FontWeight.Bold, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4), halign: HorizontalAlignment.Left));

            if (!protectedGroup)
            {
                var renameGroupBtn = new Button
                {
                    Content = "Rename",
                    Classes = { "action" },
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                    MinWidth = 160
                };
                ToolTip.SetTip(renameGroupBtn, "Rename group (name and pre-Windows 2000 name)");
                renameGroupBtn.Click += (_, _) => ShowRenameGroupDialog(node);
                actionCol.Children.Add(renameGroupBtn);
            }

            if (protectedGroup)
            {
                actionCol.Children.Add(Tb("Built-in / system group - rename and delete are disabled.", size: 12, opacity: 0.8, wrap: true));
            }
            else
            {
                var deleteGroupBtn = new Button
                {
                    Content = "Delete Group",
                    Classes = { "action" },
                    HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
                    MinWidth = 160,
                    Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(176, 96, 96)),
                    Foreground = Brushes.White
                };
                deleteGroupBtn.Click += (_, _) => ConfirmAndDeleteObject(nodeDn, nodeName, "Group", node);
                actionCol.Children.Add(deleteGroupBtn);
            }

            panel.Children.Add(new Border
            {
                Classes = { "card" },
                Child = actionCol,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 12)
            });
        }

        private sealed class GroupRemoveUndoMember
        {
            public string Dn { get; set; } = "";
            public string Display { get; set; } = "";
        }
        private sealed class GroupRemoveUndo
        {
            public DateTime Timestamp { get; set; }
            public string GroupDn { get; set; } = "";
            public string GroupName { get; set; } = "";
            public List<GroupRemoveUndoMember> Members { get; set; } = new();
        }
        private GroupRemoveUndo? LoadGroupRemoveUndo()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!File.Exists(UndoFile)) return null;
                string json = File.ReadAllText(UndoFile);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(json)) return null;
                return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<GroupRemoveUndo>(json);
            }
            catch { return null; }
        }
        private void SaveGroupRemoveUndo(GroupRemoveUndo data)
        {
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(UndoDir);
                var opts = new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true };
                File.WriteAllText(UndoFile, JsonSerializer.Serialize(data, opts));
            }
            catch { }
        }
        private void ClearGroupRemoveUndo()
        {
            try { if (File.Exists(UndoFile)) File.Delete(UndoFile); } catch { }
        }

        private sealed class GroupMemberOfUndo
        {
            public DateTime Timestamp { get; set; }
            public string MemberDn { get; set; } = "";
            public string MemberName { get; set; } = "";
            public List<GroupRemoveUndoMember> Parents { get; set; } = new();
        }
        private GroupMemberOfUndo? LoadGroupMemberOfUndo()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!File.Exists(MemberOfUndoFile)) return null;
                string json = File.ReadAllText(MemberOfUndoFile);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(json)) return null;
                return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<GroupMemberOfUndo>(json);
            }
            catch { return null; }
        }
        private void SaveGroupMemberOfUndo(GroupMemberOfUndo data)
        {
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(UndoDir);
                var opts = new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true };
                File.WriteAllText(MemberOfUndoFile, JsonSerializer.Serialize(data, opts));
            }
            catch { }
        }
        private void ClearGroupMemberOfUndo()
        {
            try { if (File.Exists(MemberOfUndoFile)) File.Delete(MemberOfUndoFile); } catch { }
        }

    }
}
'@
    $utf8Bom = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding $true
    function Write-Utf8Bom([string]$Path, [string]$Content) {
        [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($Path, $Content, $utf8Bom)
    }
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir ($Name + '.csproj')) ($projAva.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'Program.cs')         ($progCs.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'App.axaml')           ($appAxaml.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'App.axaml.cs')        ($appCs.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'MainWindow.axaml')    ($mwAxaml.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'MainWindow.axaml.cs') ($mwCs.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'MainWindow.SettingsVault.cs') ($mwSettingsVaultCs.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'MainWindow.Horizon.cs')       ($mwHorizonCs.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'MainWindow.ProfileFix.cs')    ($mwProfileFixCs.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'MainWindow.TreeNavigation.cs') ($mwTreeNavCs.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'MainWindow.Dashboard.cs')      ($mwDashboardCs.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'MainWindow.DetailPages.cs')   ($mwDetailCs.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'MainWindow.RemoteTools.cs')   ($mwRemoteCs.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
    Write-Utf8Bom (Join-Path $Dir 'MainWindow.GroupPages.cs')    ($mwGroupCs.Replace('__APPNAME__', $Name))
}
function New-Project([string]$ExePath, [string]$Name, [string]$Dir) {
    Invoke-DotNet -ExePath $ExePath -FailCode 4 -CliArgs @('new', 'console', '-n', $Name, '-o', $Dir)
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $Dir ($Name + '.csproj')))) {
        Throw-Code 4 'The template reported success but the project workspace could not be created.'
    }
}
function Publish-Project([string]$ExePath, [string]$Dir, [string]$Out) {
    Write-Info 'Running Windows-targeted package restore...'
    Invoke-DotNet -ExePath $ExePath -FailCode 4 -CliArgs @('restore', $Dir, '-r', 'win-x64', '--interactive', 'false')
    Write-Info 'Compiling and publishing self-contained binary...'
    Invoke-DotNet -ExePath $ExePath -FailCode 5 -CliArgs @(
        'publish', $Dir,
        '-c', 'Release',
        '-r', 'win-x64',
        '--self-contained', 'true',
        '-o', $Out,
        '/p:UseSharedCompilation=false',
        '/p:NodeReuse=false'
    )
}
function Start-PublishedApp([string]$Exe, [string]$WorkDir) {
    try {
        Start-Process -FilePath $Exe -WorkingDirectory $WorkDir
        return $true
    } catch {
        Write-Warn2 "Auto-launch failed: $($_.Exception.Message)"
        Write-Warn2 "Run manually: $Exe"
        return $false
    }
}
# ===================== MAIN =====================
$sw = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
try {
    $ProjectName = Get-SafeName $ProjectName
    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($BaseDir)) {
        $BaseDir = if ($PSScriptRoot) { $PSScriptRoot } else { (Get-Location).Path }
    }
    $ProjectDir  = Join-Path $BaseDir $ProjectName
    $PublishDir  = Join-Path $ProjectDir 'publish'
    Write-Host '================================================================' -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "  Building Active Directory Explorer (Material + fast Horizon): $ProjectName" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host '================================================================' -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Initialize-Tls
    $Script:StageName = 'Environment checks'
    Write-Stage '1/8' 'Initializing environment'
    Test-DiskSpace -Path $BaseDir
    $Script:StageName = 'Clean slate'
    Write-Stage '2/8' "Preparing clean workspace: $ProjectDir"
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ProjectDir) {
        if (-not (Remove-Folder -Path $ProjectDir)) {
            Write-Err2 "Could not delete directory."
            exit 7
        }
        Write-Ok 'Cleaned previous version.'
    }
    [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($ProjectDir) | Out-Null
    $Script:StageName = '.NET SDK detection/install'
    Write-Stage '3/8' 'Preparing .NET 8 SDK'
    $DotNetExe = Find-DotNetSdk
    if (-not $DotNetExe) {
        Install-DotNetSdk
        $DotNetExe = Join-Path $Script:DotnetDir 'dotnet.exe'
    }
    Set-DotNetEnv (Split-Path $DotNetExe)
    Write-Ok "Using .NET SDK: $(& $DotNetExe --version)"
    $Script:StageName = 'Project creation'
    Write-Stage '4/8' 'Creating project'
    New-Project $DotNetExe $ProjectName $ProjectDir
    $Script:StageName = 'Writing sources'
    Write-Stage '5/8' 'Writing Active Directory Explorer sources'
    Write-SourceFiles $ProjectDir $ProjectName
    $Script:StageName = 'Restore/build/publish'
    Write-Stage '6/8' 'Building self-contained application'
    Publish-Project $DotNetExe $ProjectDir $PublishDir
    $Script:StageName = 'Artifact verification'
    Write-Stage '7/8' 'Verifying output'
    $exe = Join-Path $PublishDir ($ProjectName + '.exe')
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $exe)) { Throw-Code 5 "EXE not found" }
    $sizeMb = [math]::Round((Get-Item $exe).Length / 1MB, 1)
    Write-Ok "Build successful - $sizeMb MB"
    $Script:StageName = 'Launch'
    if ($NoLaunch) {
        Write-Info "EXE location: $exe"
    } else {
        Write-Stage '8/8' 'Launching application'
        Start-PublishedApp $exe $PublishDir
    }
    Write-Host '================================================================' -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host '  SUCCESS - Active Directory Explorer Ready' -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host '================================================================' -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Ok "Project Folder : $ProjectDir"
    Write-Ok "Executable     : $exe"
    Write-Ok ("Build time     : {0:hh\:mm\:ss}" -f $sw.Elapsed)
    exit 0
}
catch {
    $code = 1
    if ($_.Exception.Message -match '^\[(\d)\]') { $code = [int]$Matches[1] }
    Write-Err2 "FAILED during $($Script:StageName)"
    Write-Err2 $_.Exception.Message
    exit $code
}
finally {
    if ($Script:InstallerPath -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Script:InstallerPath)) {
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $Script:InstallerPath -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    }
}
